## I / 一

===Original===
A Range Colt
===Chinese===
牧场上的小马驹

===Original===
It seemed like Mother Nature was sure agreeable that day when the little black colt came to the range world and tried to get a footing with his long wobblety legs on the brown prairie sod. Short stems of new green grass was trying to make their way up thru the last year’s faded growth, and reaching for the sun’s warm rays. Taking in all that could be seen, felt, and inhaled, there was no day, time, nor place that could beat that spring morning on the sunny side of the low prairie butte where Smoky the colt was foaled.

“Smoky” wouldn’t have fitted the colt as a name just then on account he was jet black, but that name wasn’t attached onto him till he was a four-year-old, which was when he first started being useful as a saddle horse. He didn’t see the first light of day thru no box stall window, and there was no human around to make a fuss over him and try to steady him on his feet for them first few steps. Smoky was just a little range colt, and all the company he had that first morning of his life was his watchful mammy.
===Chinese===
那匹小小的黑马驹来到牧场的世界，试着用他那几条晃晃悠悠的长腿，在棕色的草原草皮上站稳脚跟——大自然母亲那天瞧着真是和气极了。去年褪了色的枯草还铺在地上，新绿的短茎草芽正打底下使劲往上钻，去够太阳暖和的光线。把一切看得见、摸得着、闻得到的都算上，再没有哪个日子、哪个时辰、哪块地方，能赛得过那片低矮草原孤丘向阳一面的那个春天的早晨——马驹烟儿（Smoky）就是在那儿落地的。

「烟儿」这名字在当时跟这匹马驹半点也对不上号，因为他浑身是地道的漆黑；这名字是直到他四岁才安到他头上的，那正是他头一回当鞍马、派上真正用场的时候。他没有隔着单间马厩的窗户望见自己的头一线天光，身边也没有一个人围着他大惊小怪，扶着他站稳、走头几步。烟儿就是一匹牧场上的小马驹，他来到世上的头一个早晨，唯一的伴儿就是他那时刻留神的马妈妈。

===Original===
Smoky wasn’t quite an hour old when he begin to take interest in things. The warm spring sun was doing its work and kept a pouring warmth all over that slick little black hide, and right on thru his little body, till pretty soon his head come up kinda shaky and he begin nosing around them long front legs that was stretched out in front of him. His mammy was close by him, and at the first move the colt made she run her nose along his short neck and nickered. Smoky’s head went up another two inches at the sound, and his first little answering nicker was heard. Of course a person would of had to listen mighty close to hear it, but then if you’d a watched his nostrils quivering you could tell that’s just what he was trying to do.

That was the starting of Smoky. Pretty soon his ears begin to work back and forth towards the sound his mammy would make as she moved. He was trying to locate just where she was. Then something moved right in front of his nose about a foot; it’d been there quite a good spell but he’d never realized it before; besides his vision was a little dim yet and he wasn’t interested much till that something moved again and planted itself still closer.

Being it was right close he took a sniff at it. That sniff recorded itself into his brain and as much as told him that all was well. It was one of his mammy’s legs. His ears perked up and he tried nickering again with a heap better result than the first time.
===Chinese===
烟儿还不到一个钟头大，就开始对周围的东西上心了。暖和的春日太阳干着它的活儿，把暖流一股一股浇在那身油亮的小黑毛上，又一直渗进他小小的身子；没多会儿，他的脑袋晃晃悠悠地抬了起来，开始用鼻子拱那几条摊在他跟前的长前腿。马妈妈紧紧守在他身边，马驹一动，她就把鼻子顺着他的短脖颈蹭过去，低低嘶了一声。听见这声音，烟儿的头又抬高了足足两英寸，人们听见了他第一声细细的、回话似的低嘶。当然，谁都得竖起耳朵细听才听得见，可要是你瞧着他那直打颤的鼻孔，就明白他正是想要这么叫。

这就是烟儿的开头。没多久，他的两只耳朵就随着马妈妈走动时弄出的声响来回转悠开了——他正想弄清她到底在哪儿。接着，有个什么东西在他鼻子跟前约莫一英尺远的地方动了一下；那东西在那儿待了老半天了，只是他先前一直没理会；再说他的眼神还有点儿发蒙，他也提不起多大兴致——直到那东西又动了一下，还又把身子挪得近了些。

离得这么近，他就朝它闻了闻。这一闻在那小脑袋里记下了印子，差不多等于告诉他：一切安好。那是他马妈妈的一条腿。他耳朵竖了起来，又试着低嘶了一声，这回可比头一回中用多了。

===Original===
One good thing called for another and natural like he made a sudden scramble to get up, but his legs wouldn’t work right, and just about when he’d got his belly clear of the ground, and as he was resting there for another try at the rest of the way up, one of his front legs quivered and buckled at the elbow, and the whole works went down.

He layed there flat on his side and breathing hard. His mammy nickered encouragement, and it wasn’t long when his head was up again and his legs spraddled out all around him the same as before. He was going to try again, but next time he was going to be more sure of his *ground*. He was studying, it seemed like, and sniffing of his legs and then the earth, like he was trying to figger out how he was going to get one to stand up on the other. His mammy kept a circling around and a talking to him in horse language; she’d give him a shove with her nose then walk away and watch him.
===Chinese===
好事接着唤好事，他顺理成章地猛地一挣，想站起来；可那几条腿不听使唤，刚把肚皮撑离地面、正歇在那儿攒着劲儿要把后半程也撑上去的当口，一条前腿一哆嗦，肘弯儿一软，整套家什就塌了下去。

他侧身平躺在那儿，喘得厉害。马妈妈低嘶着给他打气，不多久他的头又抬了起来，四条腿照旧朝四面八方支棱着。他还想再试一回，不过下回他要对自己脚下的*地皮*更有数些。他像是在琢磨事儿，闻闻自己的腿，又闻闻土地，好像要盘算清楚，怎么才能拿这一个去站在另一个上头。马妈妈围着他一圈一圈地转，用马儿的话跟他说着；一会儿用鼻子推他一把，一会儿又走开去，望着他。

===Original===
The spring air, which I think is most for the benefit of all that’s young, had a lot to do to keep Smoky from laying still for very long. His vision was getting clearer fast, and his strength was coming in just as fast. Not far away, but still too far for Smoky to see, was little calves, little white-faced fellers a playing and bucking around and letting out walleyed bellers at their mammies, running out a ways and then running back, tails up, at a speed that’d make a greyhound blush for shame.

There was other little colts too all a cavorting around and tearing up good sod, but with all them calves and colts that was with the bunches of cattle or horses scattered out on the range, the same experience of helplessness that Smoky was going thru had been theirs for a spell, and a few hadn’t been as lucky as Smoky in their first squint at daylight. Them few had come to the range world when the ground was still covered with snow, or else cold spring rains was a pouring down to wet ’em to the bone.
===Chinese===
春天的空气——依我看，那多半是为着天下一切年幼的东西才有的——起了大作用，叫烟儿躺不安稳多久。他的眼神飞快地清楚起来，力气也飞快地跟着长。不远的地方——可对烟儿来说还太远、望不见——有一群小牛犊，一群白脸的小家伙，又蹦又跳地撒欢儿，瞪圆了眼珠朝各自的妈妈哗哗大叫，跑出去一截，又竖着尾巴跑回来，那速度能把灵缇犬臊个满脸通红。

还有别的小马驹，也全都撒着欢儿地腾跃，把好好的草皮踢得稀烂。不过，跟着散布在牧场上的牛群马群的这些牛犊和马驹，哪一个都熬过一段跟烟儿眼下正熬着的一模一样的、无依无靠的日子；还有几个，头一眼看见天光的时候，运气远不如烟儿。他们来到这牧场的世界时，地上还盖着雪，再不就是冷冰冰的春雨兜头浇下，把他们浇个透心凉。

===Original===
Smoky’s mother had sneaked out of the bunch a few days before Smoky came, and hid in a lonely spot where she’d be sure that no cattle nor horses or even riders would be around. In a few days, and when Smoky would be strong enough to lope out, she’d go back again; but in the meantime she wanted to be alone with her colt and put all her attention on him, without having to contend with chasing off big inquisitive geldings or jealous fillies.

She was of range blood, which means mostly mustang with strains of Steeldust or Coach throwed in. If hard winters come and the range was covered with heavy snows, she knowed of high ridges where the strong winds kept a few spots bare and where feed could be got. If droughts came to dry up the grass and water holes, she sniffed the air for moisture and drifted out acrost the plain which was her home range, to the high mountains where things was more normal. There was cougars and wolves in that high country, but her mustang instinct made her the “fittest.” She circled around and never went under where the lion was perched a waiting for her, and the wolf never found her where she could be cornered.
===Chinese===
烟儿出生前几天，他妈妈就悄悄溜出了马群，藏到一个冷清去处，在那儿她有把握见不着一头牛、一匹马，连个骑马的人也不会有。过几天，等烟儿壮实到能颠着小跑赶路了，她就回去；眼下呢，她只想跟马驹单独待着，把全部心思都放在他身上，省得费神去轰走那些好奇的大骟马，或是好妒忌的小母马。

她身上流的是旷野牧场的血，说白了，主要是野马（mustang）的血，又兑进去些斯蒂尔达斯特（Steeldust）或者拉车马的血统。要是碰上严冬、牧场让大雪封了，她知道该上哪些高岭——大风总会把那儿吹得露出几块光地，寻得着吃的。要是旱得草枯了、水坑见了底，她就迎着风闻出潮气，一路漂过那片她当家做主的平原，上到日子好过些的高山里去。那片高山里头有美洲狮，也有狼，可她那野马的天性让她成了「适者」。她总绕着道走，从不打美洲狮蹲着守她的崖底下过；狼也从来没能在什么把她逼进死角的去处寻着她。

===Original===
Smoky had inherited that same instinct of his mammy’s, but on that quiet spring morning he wasn’t at all worried about enemies. His mammy was there, and besides he had a hard job ahead that was taking all of his mind to figger out: that was to stand on them long things which was fastened to his body and which kept a spraddling out in all directions.

The first thing to do was to gather ’em under him and try again. He did that easy enough, and then he waited and gathered up all the strength that was in him. He sniffed at the ground to make sure it was there and then his head went up, his front feet stretched out in front of him, and with his hind legs all under him, he used all that strength he’d been storing up and pushed himself up on his front feet, his hind legs straightened up to steady him; and as luck would have it there was just enough distance between each leg to keep him up there. All he had to do was to keep them legs stiff and from buckling up under him, which wasn’t at all easy, cause getting up to where he was had used up a lot of his strength, and them long legs of his was doing a heap of shaking.
===Chinese===
烟儿承了他马妈妈的这份天性，不过在那个安安静静的春天的早晨，他一点儿也不为敌人操心。马妈妈就在身边；再说，他面前还摆着一桩要费尽心思去琢磨的难事呢——就是怎么站到那几条长家伙上去：它们拴在他身子上，还老朝四面八方乱支。

头一件事，是把它们收拢到身子底下，再试一回。这一步他做得挺利索；然后他就候着，把浑身的劲儿一点点攒足。他朝地上闻了闻，确认地还在那儿，然后头一扬，前腿朝前撑出去，后腿拢在身子底下，把攒下的那股子力气整个使出来，把自己撑上了前腿，后腿绷直了稳住身子——运气也真不错，几条腿彼此隔得恰好够开，正好把他撑在那上头。剩下的，就是把几条腿绷住、别叫它们在身下打弯——这可一点儿也不轻松，因为爬到这一步已经耗去了他好大力气，那几条长腿正抖得厉害。

===Original===
All would of been well maybe, only his mammy nickered “that’s a good boy,” and that’s what queered Smoky. His head went up proud as a peacock and he forgot all about keeping his props stiff and under him. Down he went the whole length of his legs, and there he layed the same as before.

But he didn’t lay long this time. He either liked the sport of going up and coming down or else he was getting peeved; he was up again, mighty shaky, but he was up sure enough. His mammy came to him. She sniffed at him and he sniffed back. Then nature played another hand and he nursed, the first nourishment was took in, his tummy warmed up and strength came fast. Smoky was an hour and a half old and up to stay.
===Chinese===
说不定本来一切都会好好的，偏偏他马妈妈低嘶了一声「真是个好孩子」——坏事的正是这个。烟儿的头骄傲得跟孔雀似的扬了起来，把绷住腿、撑住身子这茬儿忘了个一干二净。他顺着那几条腿的整个长度倒了下去，又照老样子躺在那儿了。

不过这回他没躺多久。要么是他喜欢上了这一起一倒的玩意儿，要么就是他有点儿恼了；反正他又起来了，晃得厉害，可到底是站住了。马妈妈朝他走过来。她闻了闻他，他也回闻了她。接着大自然又出了一手牌——他吃奶了。头一顿营养下了肚，肚皮暖起来，力气跟着就上来。烟儿来到世上一个半钟头，从此站定了。

===Original===
The rest of that day was full of events for Smoky. He explored the whole country, went up big mountains two feet high, wide valleys six or eight feet acrost, and at one time was as far as twelve feet away from his mammy all by himself. He shied at a rock once; it was a dangerous *looking* rock, and he kicked at it as he went past. All that action being put on at once come pretty near being too much for him and he came close to measuring his whole length on Mother Earth once again. But luck was with him, and taking it all he had a mighty good time. When the sun went to sinking over the blue ridges in the West, Smoky, he missed all the beauty of the first sunset in his life;﻿—he was stretched out full length, of his own accord this time, and sound asleep.

The night was a mighty good rival of what the day had been. All the stars was out and showing off, and the braves was a chasing the buffalo plum around the Big Dipper, the water hole of The Happy Hunting Grounds. But all that was lost to Smoky; he was still asleep and recuperating from his first day’s adventures, and most likely he’d kept on sleeping for a good long spell, only his mammy who was standing guard over him happened to get a little too close and stepped on his tail.
===Chinese===
那天余下的时光，对烟儿来说满是新鲜事。他把整片乡野探了个遍：翻过两英尺高的大山，蹚过六到八英尺宽的大山谷，有一回还独自一个跑到了离马妈妈十二英尺远的去处。有一回他让一块岩石吓了一跳——那是一块*瞧上去*危险的岩石，打旁边过的时候他还踢了它一脚。这么多热闹一齐上身，差一点儿就叫他吃不消，险些又一次把自己的整个身长量到大地母亲身上去。可运气站在他这边，统算起来，他玩得痛快极了。太阳朝西边青蓝的山脊沉下去的时候，烟儿把他这辈子头一场日落的美景整个错过了——这一回是他自己乐意的，四脚拉直躺了个舒展，睡得死沉。

那一夜，跟白天有得一比，谁也不让谁。满天星斗都出来卖弄，一伙印第安勇士正围着北斗七星，把那群野牛追得团团转——北斗七星，就是「极乐猎场」（The Happy Hunting Grounds）的那口水坑。可这一切烟儿都无缘得见；他还睡着，正从第一天的历险里缓劲儿，多半还能再睡上老长一阵——若不是守着他的马妈妈碰巧站得近了那么一点儿，一脚踩上了他的尾巴。

===Original===
Smoky must of been in the middle of some bad dream. His natural instinct might of pictured some enemy to his mind, and something that looked like a wolf or a bear must of had him cornered for sure. Anyway, when he felt his tail pinched that way he figgered that when a feller begins to *feel* it’s sure time to act, and he did. He shot up right under his mammy’s chin, let out a squeal, and stood there ready to fight. He took in the country for *feet* and *feet* around and looking for the enemy that’d nipped him, and finally in his scouting around that way he run acrost the shadow of his mammy. That meant but one thing, safety; and that accounted for and put away as past left room for a craving he’d never noticed in his excitement. He was hungry, and proceeded right then and there to take on a feed of his mammy’s warm, rich milk.

The sky was beginning to get light in the East, the stars was fading away and the buffalo hunters had went to rest. A few hours had passed since Smoky had been woke up out of his bad dream and there he was, asleep again. He’d missed his first sunset and now he was sleeping thru his first sunrise, but he was going to be prepared for that new day’s run, and the strength he was accumulating through them sleeps and between feeds would sure make him fit to cover a lot of territory.
===Chinese===
烟儿那会儿准是正做到什么噩梦的半截里。他的天性本能没准儿在脑子里给他画出了什么敌人，一个像狼又像熊的东西准是把他逼上了绝路。反正，等他觉得尾巴叫那么一夹，他就认定了：一个小家伙既然开始有了*感觉*，那就到了该动手的时候——他也真动手了。他从马妈妈的下巴底下猛地弹起来，尖嘶一声，站在那儿摆好了打架的架势。他把周围*几英尺*又*几英尺*的地方都扫量了一遍，搜寻那个咬了他的敌人；这么转悠着侦察了一阵，末了撞见了马妈妈的影子。这就只意味着一件事——安全；这桩事清了账、归了档，心里便腾出地方，盛一桩他在慌乱里一直没留神的馋劲儿。他饿了，于是当场就地吃上了一顿他马妈妈那温热浓香的奶。

东边的天开始放亮，星星渐渐隐去，猎野牛的人们都歇下了。烟儿叫噩梦惊醒过来已经过了几个钟头，他呢，又睡着了。头一场日落他错过了，这会儿他又把头一场日出睡过去了；可新一趟的行程他得养精蓄锐，那些觉里攒下的、两顿奶之间攒下的力气，保管叫他跑得了大片的地面。

===Original===
There wasn’t a move out of him till the sun was well up and beginning to throw a good heat. He stacked up on a lot of that heat, and pretty soon one of his ears moved, then the other. He took a long breath and stretched. Smoky was coming to life.﻿—His mammy nickered, and that done the trick; Smoky raised his head, looked around, and proceeded to get up. After a little time that was done and bowing his neck he stretched again. Smoky was ready for another day.

The big day started right after Smoky had his feed; then his mother went to grazing and moving away straight to the direction of some trees a mile or so to the south. A clear spring was by them trees, and water is what Smoky’s mammy wanted the most right then. She was craving for a drink of that cold water, but you’d never thought it by the way she traveled. She’d nose around at the grass and wait for spells, so as little Smoky could keep up with her and still find time to investigate everything what throwed a shadow.
===Chinese===
太阳升得老高、开始撒下一阵好热力之前，他一动也没动。他攒足了那一肚子热，不多久，一只耳朵动了动，跟着另一只也动了。他深深吸了口气，伸了个懒腰。烟儿醒过来了。——马妈妈低嘶了一声，这一下见了效；烟儿抬起头，四下里望了望，张罗着起身。过了一小会儿，事就成了，他把脖颈一弓，又伸了个懒腰。烟儿又准备好过新的一天了。

大日子打烟儿吃过奶就开了场；随后他妈妈吃起草来，一边吃一边径直朝南边一英里开外的一片树挪过去。那片树旁有一眼清泉，这会儿烟儿的马妈妈最想要的就是水。她馋那一口凉水，可你从她赶路的架势上一点儿也瞧不出来。她一路在草上东闻闻、西拱拱，时不时等上一等，好叫小烟儿跟得上她，还匀得出工夫去查访每一样会投下影子的东西。

===Original===
A baby cottontail had jumped up once right under his nose, stood there a second too scared to move, and pretty soon made a high dive between the colt’s long legs and hit for his hole; Smoky never seen the rabbit or even knowed he was there or he might of been running yet, cause that’s what he’d been looking for, an excuse to run. But he finally made up an excuse, and a while later as he brushed past a long dry weed and it tickled his belly, he let out a squeal and went from there.

His long legs tangled and untangled themselves as he run, and he was sure making speed. Around and around he went and finally lined out straight away from where his mammy was headed. She nickered for him and waited, all patience. He turned after a spell and headed for his mammy again the same as though he’d run acrost another enemy at the other end; and as he got close to his mammy he let out a buck, a squeal, a snort, and stopped﻿—he was sure some little wild horse.
===Chinese===
有一回，一只小棉尾兔就在他鼻子底下蹦了起来，愣了一秒钟，吓得不敢动弹，跟着就从马驹那两条长腿中间一个猛子扎过去，直奔自己的洞去了；烟儿压根儿没瞧见那兔子，连有它在都不知道——不然他没准儿这会儿还跑着呢，因为他等的就是这个：一个撒腿跑的由头。不过后来他自己寻了个由头：过了一阵，他擦着一株又长又干的枯草过去，草梢儿挠着了他的肚皮，他尖嘶一声，打那儿窜了出去。

他撒开腿跑，两条长腿缠了又解、解了又缠，那速度真叫快。他一圈又一圈地兜着，末了照直跑离了马妈妈去的那个方向。她朝他低嘶，等着，满满的都是耐性。过了一阵他折回来，又朝马妈妈跑去，那架势就像他在另一头又撞见了一个敌人；到了马妈妈跟前，他尥了一个蹶子、尖嘶一声、喷了个响鼻，站住了——他可真是一匹不折不扣的小野马。

===Original===
It took a couple of hours for them two to make that mile to the spring. The mother drank a lot of that good water, a few long breaths and drank some more till the thirst was all gone. Smoky came over and nosed at the pool, but he didn’t take on any of the fluid, it looked just like so much thin air to him, the same with the tender green grass that was beginning to grow in bunches everywhere; it was just growing for him to run on.

The rest of that day was pretty well used up around that one spot; adventures of all kinds was numerous for Smoky, and when he wasn’t stretched out and asleep there was plenty of big stumps in the cottonwood grove that could be depended on to give him the scare he’d be looking for.
===Chinese===
娘儿俩足足走了两个来钟头，才把去泉边的那一英里走完。妈妈喝足了那口好水，缓了几口长气，又喝了一气，直喝得一点儿不渴了才算完。烟儿凑过去，鼻子拱了拱水洼，可他一口也没沾——在他眼里，那玩意儿跟一汪稀薄的空气没什么两样；那到处一丛丛刚冒头的小嫩草也一样，草长在那儿，不过是给他跑着玩的。

那天余下的工夫，差不多都耗在那一处了；各色各样的历险在烟儿这儿多得很，只要他不是四脚摊开睡着，那片三角叶杨林子里有的是大树桩，保管能给他来上一吓——吓的还是他正巴望的那一种。

===Original===
But there was other things and more threatening than stumps which Smoky hadn’t as yet spotted, like for instance﻿—a big cayote had squatted and been watching him thru dead willow branches. He wasn’t at all interested in the action Smoky was putting into his play, and only wished the colt’s mammy would move away a little further when he would then take a chance and try to get him down﻿—colt meat was his favorite dish and he sure wasn’t going to let no chance slip by even if it took a whole day’s waiting for one to show itself.

A couple of chances had come his way but they was queered by Smoky’s mammy being too close, and he knowed better than show himself and get run down by them hoofs of hers. Finally, and when he seen his appetite wouldn’t win anything by sticking around that spot any longer, he took a last sniff and came out of his hiding place. Keeping the willows between him and the horses, he loped out till he was at a safe running distance and where he could see all around him, and there he squatted again, in plain sight this time. He hadn’t quite made up his mind as yet whether to go or stick around a while longer.﻿—Just about then Smoky spots him.
===Chinese===
可还有别的东西，比树桩更吓人的，烟儿一直没瞧见——比方说：一只大郊狼早就蹲下了，隔着枯柳条一直盯着他。烟儿玩耍里那些花样动作，它半点儿也不关心，只盼着马驹的妈妈再挪远一些，那时它就寻个机会下手，把他撂倒——马驹肉是它顶爱吃的一道菜，它是说什么也不肯让机会白白溜走的，哪怕为等一个机会白等上一整天。

机会倒是来过两回，可都让烟儿的马妈妈离得太近给搅黄了，它也懂事，不肯现出身来叫她那几只蹄子踩着跑。到后来，它看出再守在那儿，馋劲儿也捞不着什么了，便最后闻了一鼻子，打藏身处钻了出来。它让柳丛隔在自己和马群当中，一路小跑出去，直到跑出一段保险的、四下都看得见的距离，才又蹲下——这一回是在明面儿上了。它还没拿定主意：是走呢，还是再耗上一阵。——差不多就在这时，烟儿瞧见它了。

===Original===
To him, the cayote was just another stump, but more interesting than the others he’d kicked at, on account that this stump moved, and that promised a lot of excitement. With a bowed neck and kinked tail Smoky trotted up towards the cayote. The cayote just set there and waited and when the colt got to within a few feet from him, he started away and just fast enough so as the colt’s curiosity would make him follow. If he could only get the colt over the ridge and out of his mammy’s sight.

It all was only a lot of fun to Smoky, and besides he was bound to find out what was that grey and yellow object that could move and run and didn’t at all look like his mammy. His instinct was warning him steady as he went, but curiosity had the best of him, and it wasn’t till he was over the hill before his instinct got above his curiosity and he seen that all wasn’t well.
===Chinese===
在他眼里，那郊狼不过是又一个树桩，只是比他踢过的那些更有意思，因为这个树桩会动，会动就许诺下不少热闹。烟儿弓起脖子，尾巴拧了个卷儿，小跑着朝郊狼凑过去。郊狼只管蹲在那儿等着，等马驹凑到离它只剩几英尺，它才起身走开，步子不紧不慢，恰好吊着马驹的好奇心，叫他跟上来。要是能把马驹引过山脊、引出他妈妈的视线，可就好了。

这一切对烟儿来说不过是一桩大乐子；再说，他还非弄明白不可：那个灰不灰黄不黄、会动会跑、又一点儿不像他马妈妈的，到底是个什么东西。一路上，本能不住地给他递警告，可好奇心占了上风；直到翻过了山头，本能才压过了好奇，他也才看出事情不大对头。

===Original===
The cayote had turned and quicker than a flash made a jump for Smoky’s throat.﻿—The generations of mustang blood that’d fought the lobo and cougar, and which was the same blood that flowed in Smoky’s veins, is all that saved the colt. That inherited instinct made him do the right thing at the right time, he whirled quicker than lightning and let fly with both hind feet with the result that the cayote’s teeth just pinched the skin under his jaws. But even at that, he wasn’t going to get rid of his enemy (it was a sure enough enemy this time) that easy, and as he kicked he felt the weight of the cayote, and then a sharp pain on his hamstrings.

Smoky was scared, and he let out a squeal that sure made every living thing in that neighborhood set up and wonder; it was a plain and loud distress signal, and it was answered. His mammy shot up the hill, took in the goings on at a glance, and ears back, teeth a shining, tore up the earth and lit into the battle like a ton of dynamite.

The battle was over in a second, and with hunks of yellow fur a flying all directions it wound up in a chase. The cayote was in the lead and he stayed in the lead till a second hill took him out of sight.
===Chinese===
郊狼已经转过身，说时迟那时快，一个跃起直取烟儿的喉咙。——正是那一代代同狼和美洲狮搏杀过的野马血脉——也就是流在烟儿血管里的这同一股血——救下了这匹马驹。那份承袭来的本能叫他在对的时机做出了对的事：他比闪电还快地一转身，两只后蹄一齐飞出去，结果郊狼的牙只夹着了他下巴底下的皮。可饶是这样，他也没那么容易甩掉这个敌人（这一回可是个货真价实的敌人）；他踢出去的时候，觉出了郊狼坠在蹄上的分量，跟着后腿腱上便是一阵尖锐的疼。

烟儿吓坏了，放出了一声尖叫，那一声保准叫左邻右舍一切活物都坐起来发了愣；那是一声响亮又明白的求救信号，而且得到了回应。他的马妈妈冲上山坡，一眼看清了是怎么回事，耳朵朝后一贴，牙齿一龇，刨得土花四溅，像一吨炸药似的轰进了战团。

战斗一秒钟就完了事：黄色的毛一块一块朝四面八方乱飞，末了变成一场追击。郊狼一路领先，也一路把领先守到第二道山丘把它挡得没了影儿。

===Original===
Smoky was glad to follow his mammy back to the spring and on to the other side a ways. He didn’t shy at the stumps he passed on the way, and the twig that tickled his tummy didn’t bring no play. He was hungry and tired, and when the first was tended to and his appetite called for no more he lost no time to picking out a place to rest his weary bones. A thin stream of blood was drying on one of his hind legs, but there was no pain, and when the sun set and the shadow of his mammy spread out over him he was sound asleep, and maybe dreaming of stumps, of stumps that moved.

When the sun came up the next morning, Smoky was up too, and eyes half closed was standing still as the big boulder next to him and sunned himself. A stiff hind leg was a reminder of what happened the day before, but the experience was forgotten far as dampening his spirits was concerned, even the stiffness wouldn’t hold him back from whatever the new day would hold. He’d always remember the cayote, and from then on never mistake him for a stump, but that sure wasn’t going to take any play out of him.
===Chinese===
烟儿乐得跟着马妈妈回到泉边，又朝那一头走了一截。路过树桩，他不再闪躲；挠着肚皮的小树枝，也逗不出他半点儿玩兴。他又饿又乏；头一样安顿好了、胃口也不再讨了之后，他赶紧挑了块地方，安顿他那一把乏透了的骨头。一条后腿上正干着一道细细的血痕，可并不疼；太阳落下去、马妈妈的影子罩上他的时候，他早已睡得死沉——没准儿正梦见树桩，梦见会走的树桩呢。

第二天早晨太阳一露头，烟儿也起来了，眯缝着眼站在那儿一动不动，跟身边那块大圆石一个样，晒着他那份太阳。一条发僵的后腿提醒着他昨天出的事，可要说泄气，那点经历早忘到脑后了；就连那点僵劲儿，也拦不住他去接新一天里不管什么样的营生。他记住了那只郊狼，打那以后再不会把它错当成树桩；可要说叫他少了玩兴，那是万万不能的。

===Original===
He was two days old now and strength had piled up fast, he felt there was no trail too long for him and when the sun was a couple of hours high that morning and his mother showed indications that she wanted to drift he sure wasn’t dragging along behind. The stiffness gradually went out of his hind leg as he traveled, and by the afternoon of that day he was again shying at everything and sometimes even shying at nothing at all.

They kept a traveling and traveling, and it seemed like to Smoky that the trail was getting pretty long after all. They skirted the flat along the foot of the mountains, crossed one high ridge, and many creeks, and still his mother was drifting on. She wouldn’t hardly even stop for him to nurse, and Smoky was getting cranky, and tired.

The pace kept up till the sun was well on its way down, when it slackened some and finally the mother went to grazing. A short while later Smoky was layed out full length and dead to the world.
===Chinese===
他这会儿两天大了，力气攒得飞快，他觉得天底下没有哪条道是他走不下来的。那天太阳升起两个钟头的时候，他妈妈露出想挪窝赶路的意思，他可是说什么也不肯在后头拖拖拉拉。走着走着，后腿那点僵劲儿渐渐散了，到那天下午，他又见什么闪什么了——有时候连什么都没有，他也要闪一下。

他们走啊走啊，走了老远还在走，烟儿都觉得这趟路未免也太长了。他们沿着山根绕过平地，翻过一道高岭，蹚过一条又一条小河，他妈妈还是一个劲儿地往前漂。她连停下来让他吃口奶都难得肯，烟儿上了脾气，也乏了。

这步子一直撑到太阳老西，才松了些，末了他妈妈总算吃起草来。没多大工夫，烟儿四脚拉直躺下，睡得不省人事。

===Original===
Smoky didn’t know and didn’t care much just then, but his mammy was headed back to her home range, where there was lots of horses and other little colts for him to play with; and when late that night she lined out again traveling steady he wasn’t in any too good a humor.

Finally it seemed like they’d got there, for his mammy after watering at a creek went to grazing at the edge of some big cottonwoods; she showed no indications of wanting to go any further. Right there Smoky was willing to take advantage of the chance and recuperate for all he was worth. The sun came up, but Smoky was in the shade of the cottonwoods what was beginning to leaf out. He slept on and a twitching ear once in long spells is all that showed he was still alive.
===Chinese===
烟儿那会儿不知道，也不大在乎——其实他马妈妈这是回她自个儿的地盘去，那儿的马多得很，还有别的小马驹陪他玩；当天夜里很晚，她又一门心思、稳稳当当上了路，他心里可不大痛快。

末了，看样子总算到了：他妈妈在一条河边喝过水，便在几棵大三角叶杨的边上吃起草来；她没有半点再往前走的意思。烟儿巴不得趁这个机会，可着劲儿把自己缓过来。太阳升起来了，可烟儿待在刚开始抽芽的杨树荫里，一觉接着一觉地睡，隔老半天耳朵才抖一下，表明他还活着。

===Original===
That day never seen much of him; once in a while he’d get up and nurse but right away after he’d disappear again and stretch out flat on the warm earth.

He kept that up till way in the middle of the next night, and it was well towards morning before he felt like he was all horse again.
===Chinese===
那一天里他没露几面；偶尔起来吃口奶，一转眼又没了影，平平地摊在那片暖烘烘的地上。

这么一睡就睡到第二天半夜里老深，快挨着天亮，他才觉得自己又重新是一匹整马了。

===Original===
He come out of it in fine shape though, and he was stronger than ever. His vision was taking more territory too, and he was getting so he could see near half as far as his mammy could. She was the first to see the bunch of range horses trailing in to water early that morning. Smoky heard her nicker as she recognized the bunch and it drawed a heap of interest as to what she was nickering about, for he was right there alongside of her and he couldn’t see nothing for her to nicker at, but pretty soon he could hear the horses as they trailed towards him. His ears straightened towards the sound and a while later he could make out the shapes of ’em. Smoky just kind of quivered at the sight of so many that looked like his mammy. He was all interested, but at the same time, and even though his instinct told him that all was well, he had no hankering to leave his mammy’s side till he knowed for sure just what was up.

The mother watched the bunch coming closer with ears pointed straight ahead, but soon as some of the leaders discovered little Smoky there was a commotion and they all begin crowding in to get a look at and greet the newcomer, about which time the mother layed her ears back. It was a warning that none of ’em come too close.
===Chinese===
不过缓过来之后，他出落得精神极了，比先前更壮。他的眼界也扩了地盘，慢慢地，他差不多能望到他马妈妈能望到的一半那么远了。那天一早，头一个望见那群进泉边喝水的牧场马的，是她。她认出马群时低嘶了一声，烟儿听见了，对她嘶的什么起了老大的好奇，因为他明明就在她身边，却什么可嘶的东西也看不见；可不多久，他就听见马群朝他这边走来的动静了。他两耳朝那声音竖得笔直，又过了一阵，他能认出他们的影子了。一眼望见这么多跟他马妈妈一个模样的，烟儿浑身上下直打颤。他满心好奇；可同时——哪怕本能告诉他一切安好——他不弄个水落石出，也不肯离开马妈妈身边半步。

妈妈望着马群越走越近，两耳笔直朝前支着；可领头的那几匹一发现小烟儿，队伍里就炸了窝，全都挤上来要看一看新来的、跟他见个礼——差不多就在这当口，妈妈把耳朵朝后一放。那是个警告：谁也别靠得太近。

===Original===
Little Smoky’s knees was a shaking under him at the sight of so many of his kind; he leaned against his mammy half afraid, but his head was up far as he could get it and facing ’em and showed by the shine in his eyes that he liked the whole proceeding mighty well at that. He rubbed nostrils with a strange gelding which was braver than the rest and dared come close, and when that gelding was nipped at by his mammy he had a mighty strong hankering to help her along just for fun, and nip him himself.

The preliminary introduction took a good hour, and the mother stood guard; not for fear that any of ’em would harm Smoky, but she wanted it understood from the start that he was her little colt and she had the say over him. It finally *was* understood, but it took all that day and part of the next for the bunch to get used in having the new little feller around and quit making a fuss over him.
===Chinese===
一下子见了这么多同类，小烟儿的膝盖底下直打晃；他半是害怕地倚着马妈妈，脑袋却扬到了不能再高，正对着他们，眼里放出的光亮表明，这整场热闹他偏偏还喜欢得很。有一匹生骟马比其余的都胆大，敢凑上前来，同他碰了碰鼻子；等那骟马挨了他妈妈一口咬，烟儿心里痒痒得很想帮上一把——纯粹为了好玩，也想自己叼他一口。

这场见面礼足足行了一个钟头，妈妈一直在旁边守着——倒不是怕谁会伤害烟儿，而是她要打头里就把话挑明：他是她的小马驹，只有她能做他的主。这层意思最后*确实*叫大家弄明白了；不过马群又花了一整天加半天的工夫，才习惯了这个新来的小家伙在跟前，才不再围着他大惊小怪。

===Original===
They was all jealous of one another and fought amongst themselves to be the only one near him, and his mother, of course she’d declared herself from the start, and it was took for granted from all around that her place in Smoky’s heart couldn’t be considered, and all knowed better than try and chase her away from him. Fillies and old mares, young geldings and old ponies and all, had it out as to which was the most fit to tag along and play with Smoky and keep a watchful eye over him along with his mammy. All wanted the job, but a big buckskin saddle horse who all the time had been the boss of the herd took it to hand to show them that *he* would be the all around guardeen for Smoky, and second only to his mammy. He delivered a few swift kicks, pounded on some ribs, left teeth marks on shiny hides, and after taking one last look and making sure that all was persuaded, grazed out towards Smoky who by his mammy had watched the whole proceeding with a heap of interest.

There was three other little colts in the bunch besides Smoky, and each time one of them little fellers came the buckskin horse had to whip the bunch so as he’d have the say over the newest one. Now Smoky was the newest one, and the buckskin horse had first rights as an outsider once again. He was an old horse full of scars showing where he’d had many a scrap; there was saddle marks on his back and at one time he had been a mighty fine cowhorse. Now he was pensioned; he’d more than earned a rest and all he had to do for the rest of his life was to pick out good feed grounds for the winter, shady places and tenderest green grass for the summer, and his other interest in life was them little colts that came in spring time.
===Chinese===
他们个个争风吃醋，互相较劲，都想当唯一守在他身边的那一个。至于他妈妈——她打头里就亮明了身份，大家也就理所当地认了：她在烟儿心里的位置没得争，谁都识相，不去想把她从他身边撵走。小母马和老母马、小骟马和老矮马，全体较开了劲，看谁最有资格跟烟儿一块儿玩、陪他马妈妈一块儿照看他。个个都想要这份差事；可一匹高大的兔褐色（buckskin）鞍马——他一向是马群的头儿——出面教训大家：*他*才是烟儿的全权保护人，地位仅次于他马妈妈。他快快地踢了几蹄，捶了几下肋条，在油亮的皮毛上留下几个牙印儿，然后末了环视一圈，见个个都被说服了，便朝烟儿这边吃着草踱过来——烟儿在马妈妈身边，把这整个过程从头到尾看了个津津有味。

除了烟儿，马群里还有三匹别的小马驹；每回来一匹新的小家伙，这匹兔褐马都得把全群揍服帖一回，好让他对新来的那一匹说一不二。眼下烟儿成了最新的一个，兔褐马便又当了一回优先的外人。他是匹老马，浑身是疤，处处显出他打过多少回架；背上留着鞍痕，早年间他也是一匹顶呱呱的牧牛马。如今他算退了休；这份清闲他挣得足足的，后半辈子要干的，无非是冬天挑几片好草场，夏天寻几处凉快地界、几口最嫩的青草；他另一桩心思，就是每年开春来的那些小马驹。

===Original===
Smoky’s mother was young, at least ten years younger than the buckskin horse, but the buckskin was like a colt compared to her when it come to be playful. She had the responsibility of Smoky and while she let him play with her, kick or bite at her, she never played with him and once in a while if he’d get too rough she’d let him know about it. She loved little Smoky with all her heart and would of died for him any time, and her main interest was to see that she kept in condition so that Smoky would never be stunted by lacking of rich milk. She had no time for play.

And that’s where the old buckskin came in. Him and Smoky was soon acquainted, in a short while they was playing, Smoky would kick at him while the big buckskin nipped him easy and careful along the flank, then he’d run away from him, and the little colt had a lot of fun chasing that big hunk of horseflesh all over the country. The rest of the bunch would watch the two play and with no effort to hide how jealous they felt.
===Chinese===
烟儿的妈妈还年轻，比兔褐马至少小十岁；可要论贪玩，兔褐马在她跟前倒像匹马驹。她肩上担着烟儿，所以由着他在她跟前玩、朝她踢、朝她咬，她自己却从不跟他玩；偶尔他闹得过了头，她也会给他点颜色瞧。她爱小烟儿爱到了心坎里，什么时候为他死都肯；她顶要紧的心思，就是把自己保养好，别让烟儿缺了香浓的奶、亏了长劲。她没有闲工夫玩。

这正是老兔褐马的用武之地。他和烟儿没多久就厮熟了，一小会儿工夫俩人就玩开了：烟儿朝他踢，大兔褐马就轻轻巧巧、小心翼翼地在他胁腹上叼一口，跟着撒腿跑开，小马驹满乡野追着那一大坨马肉跑，追得乐不可支。马群其余的都瞧着他们俩玩，一点儿也不掩饰自己心里的醋劲儿。

===Original===
Smoky’s mother kept her eye on the buckskin, but never interfered, she knowed, and it was only when Smoky came back to her, tired and hungry, that she put her ears back and warned him to keep away.

It took a few days before the buckskin would allow any of the other horses to get near Smoky, and then he had no say about it for he found that Smoky had his own ideas about things, and if he wanted to mingle in with the other horses that was his business, and all the buckskin could do then was to try and keep the other horses away. That was quite a job, specially if Smoky wanted to be with them. So the buckskin finally had to give it up and do the best he could which was to see that none of ’em done him any harm. But none of ’em had any intentions of doing the little colt any harm, and as it was it looked like Smoky had ’em all buffaloed. He’d tear in after some big horse like he was going to eat him up and all that big horse would do was to scatter out like the devil was after him.
===Chinese===
烟儿的妈妈一直拿眼睛瞟着兔褐马，却从不插手——她心里有数——只有烟儿玩累了、饿急了跑回她身边来的时候，她才把耳朵一贴，警告他离远点儿。

兔褐马过了好几天才容许别的马靠近烟儿——到后来这事儿也由不得他了，因为他发现烟儿对事情自有主意：他想跟哪些马混，那是他自己的事，兔褐马能做的，顶多是拦着别的马别凑上来。这可是件苦差事，尤其是烟儿自己想跟他们混的时候。于是兔褐马末了只得撒手，尽人事听天命——看着别让谁伤着他。可谁也没存心要伤这匹小马驹；实情倒像是烟儿把他们全镇住了。他冲着哪匹大马冲过去，那架势像要一口吞了他，那匹大马能做的，就是撒开丫子跑，活像魔鬼撵着他似的。

===Original===
Smoky was the boss and pet of the herd for a good two weeks and then one day, here comes another little feller, a little bay colt just two days old and trailing in alongside his mammy. Smoky was left in the background and witnessed the same fuss and commotion that was done over him that morning by the creek. The buckskin horse once again fought his way in that new little feller’s heart, and right away he forgot Smoky.

But Smoky never seen anything wrong to that, he went on to playing with every horse that would have him and it wasn’t long till he picked up with a young fillie and afterwards went to mingling with other young colts.
===Chinese===
烟儿足足当了两个礼拜马群的头儿和心尖子；后来有一天，又来了一个小家伙——一匹枣红色的小马驹，才落地两天，跟在自己妈妈身边走了进来。烟儿被撂在了一边，眼睁睁看着那天早上在河边为他行过的那套热闹和忙乱，原样又演了一遍。兔褐马再一次一路打进那新来的小家伙心里，一转身就把烟儿忘了个干净。

可烟儿半点儿没觉得有什么不对，照旧哪匹马肯陪他玩就跟哪匹玩；没多久他搭上了一个小母马，跟着又跟别的小马驹们混在了一起。

===Original===
From then on Smoky had more freedom, he could go out a ways without having some big overgrowed horse tagging along, but he never went far and if he did he always came back a heap faster than when he started out. But them spring days was great for Smoky; he found out a lot of things amongst which was, that grass was good to eat, and water mighty fine to drink when the day was hot. He seen cayotes again and the bigger he got the less he was afraid of ’em till he finally went to chasing every one of ’em he’d see.

Then one day he run acrost another yellow animal. That animal didn’t look dangerous, and what’s more it was hard for Smoky to make out just what it was, and he was bound to find out. He followed that animal plum to the edge of some willows, and the queer part of it was that animal didn’t seem at all in a hurry to get away, it was mumbling along and just taking its time and Smoky was mighty tempted to plant one front foot right in the middle of it and do some pawing, but as luck would have it he didn’t have the chance, it’d got in under some willows and all that was sticking out was a part of the animal’s tail. Smoky took a sniff at it without learning anything outside that it shook a little. There didn’t seem to be no danger, so the next sniff he took was a little closer, and that done the trick. Smoky let out a squeal and a snort as he felt his nostrils punctured in half a dozen places with four-inch porcupine quills.
===Chinese===
打那以后，烟儿自在了许多：他可以出去蹓跶一截，身后不用再跟着哪匹傻大个儿的马；可他也跑不远，真跑远了，回来的那趟总比出去那趟快得多。那些春日的日子，对烟儿真是好日子；他弄明白了不少事，其中就有：草是好吃的，大热天里水是顶好喝的。他又见着郊狼了，个头越长越大，胆子也越长越壮，到后来，见一只他就追一只。

后来有一天，他又撞上了一种黄色的动物。那东西瞧着不凶，再说烟儿也实在认不出它是个什么，可他非弄清楚不可。他跟着那东西一路走到一片柳丛边上；怪就怪在，那东西一点儿也不急着跑，嘴里咕咕哝哝，慢条斯理地磨蹭着。烟儿心里痒痒，直想一前蹄子踏到它当腰，好好刨它一阵，可他运气不坏，这机会没捞着：那东西钻进了柳棵子底下，露在外头的只剩一小截尾巴。烟儿凑上去闻了闻，没闻出个所以然，只觉得它微微抖了抖。瞧着没什么危险，于是第二鼻子凑得更近了些——这一下可就见了效。烟儿尖嘶一声，喷出一个响鼻——他的鼻孔叫四英寸长的豪猪刺，扎了六七个眼儿。

===Original===
But Smoky was lucky, for if he’d been a couple of inches closer there’d been quills rammed into his nose plum up to his eyes, which would’ve caused a swelling in such size that he couldn’t of been able to eat and most likely starve to death. As it was there was just a few of them quills in his nostrils, and compared to the real dose he might of got, it was just a mild warning to him. Another lesson.

It was a few days later when he met another strange animal, or strange animals, for there was many of ’em. He didn’t get much interest out of them somehow, but while they was handy maybe it was just as well for him to have a close look at one. Besides he had nothing else to do, and his mammy wasn’t far away.
===Chinese===
不过烟儿是走运的：他要是再凑近两英寸，那些刺就得捅进他的鼻子、一直顶到眼睛跟前，肿得他没法吃东西，多半得活活饿死。实际上，刺在他鼻孔里的就这么几根；比起他本来可能挨的那一整份，这不过是一回客客气气的警告。又长了一回记性。

过了几天，他又遇上了一种陌生的动物——或者说一群陌生的动物，因为它们多得是。不知怎么，他对它们提不起多大兴致；不过既然它们就在手边，不妨就凑近了仔细瞧瞧其中一头。再说他也闲着没事，马妈妈又离得不远。

===Original===
His instinct had no warning to give as he strutted towards the smallest one of the strangers which he’d picked to investigate. He wasn’t afraid of this animal and this animal didn’t seem afraid of him so Smoky kept a getting closer till one was within a couple of feet of the other. Both Smoky and this stranger was young, and mighty inquisitive, and neither as yet knowed that they’d sure be seeing plenty of each other’s kind as they get older, that they’ll be meeting thru the roundups at the “cutting grounds,” on “day-herd” and on “night guard,” on the long, hot, and dusty trails. A cowboy will be riding Smoky then and keeping a whole herd on the move, a whole herd of the kind that little Smoky was so busy investigating that day. They’ll be full grown then, and there’ll be other young ones to take the place of them that’s trailed in to the shipping point.

But Smoky wasn’t as yet worried or even thought on what was to come, neither was the little white-faced calf he was exchanging squints with; and when the critter called her long-eared, split-hoofed baby to her side, Smoky just kicked up his heels, put his head down, and bucked and crowhopped all the way to where his mammy and the rest of the bunch was grazing.
===Chinese===
他大摇大摆地朝那群生客里个头最小的一头走去——他挑中的正是这一头——一路上，本能没给过他半点警告。他不怕这动物，这动物瞧着也不怕他，于是烟儿越凑越近，直到两下里只隔一两英尺。烟儿和这个生客都还年幼，都好奇得厉害；两个谁也还不知道，等日后长大，彼此这一号的会面可多着呢——围拢牛群的「分群场」上要碰头，「日牧」上、「夜哨」上要碰头，在那一条条又长又热、尘土飞扬的赶牛道上也要碰头。到那时，会有个牧牛人骑在烟儿背上，赶着一整群牲口往前走——赶的正是一整群跟小烟儿那天忙着查访的这种一个模样的。到那时他们全长大了，自然另有年轻的一茬来顶班，把那些被赶去装车站的换下来。

不过烟儿这会儿既不担心，也压根儿没想过往后的事——跟他面对面互相瞪眼的这头小白脸牛犊，也一样没想过。后来那家伙把她那长耳朵、豁蹄子的小娃娃唤到身边去了，烟儿也就踢了踢后蹄，脑袋一低，又尥蹶子又打蹦儿地一路跑回他马妈妈和马群大伙儿吃草的地方去了。

## II / 二

===Original===
Smoky Meets the Human
===Chinese===
烟儿初见人类

===Original===
The long spring days followed by the warmer days of middle summer had took away all signs of snow excepting where the peaks was highest and the canyons deep and narrow. Up there was crusted hunks still holding out against the sun and hugging the shady sides of rocky ledges, and leaving out moisture that kept the springs and creeks running to the flats below.

The grass was greener up there, the flies wasn’t so bad, and besides there was always a breeze and sometimes a wind which made things mighty cooling, especially in the shade of the twisted pines scattered over the country where Smoky, his mammy, and the bunch was ranging.
===Chinese===
漫长的春日过去，仲夏更暖和的日子跟着到来，把雪的痕迹收拾了个干净，只有峰顶最高、峡谷又深又窄的地方还留着例外。高头还存着些结了硬壳的雪块，仍在跟太阳较劲，贴着石崖背阴的那一面，慢慢渗出湿气，养得山泉和溪水一路淌到下头的平地里去。

高处的草更绿，苍蝇也没那么闹人；再说那儿总有一股小风，有时还来上一阵大风，把一切吹得凉快极了——尤其是在歪脖子松树的树荫底下。这些松树零零散散地长在烟儿、他马妈妈和马群游荡的那片乡野上。

===Original===
That high, rocky, and rough territory had a lot to do in the makings of Smoky. Playing down the steep ridges, where shale rock made the footing slippery and mighty uncertain, had took all the wobble and shake out of his legs. They fit to his body more and rounded up in size, so as they looked like they really belonged to him. His hoofs had long ago lost their pink soft shell and turned to steel grey and were near as hard and tough as steel itself; and the way he’d buck and play down a rocky canyon and jump over down timber, may not of compared with a mountain goat for sureness, but he more than made up for that in speed and recklessness, and somehow he’d always hit the bottom right side up.

It was in one of them wild scrambles down a mountain side one day that Smoky near run into a cinnamon cub which had been curled up and sleeping on top of a big stump. Smoky stood in his tracks for a second, and in that second the cub fell off the stump with a snarl and lit a running on the other side.
===Chinese===
那片又高又多石、坎坷不平的山地，对烟儿长成个什么模样可是出了大力。在陡峭的山梁上撒欢儿——页岩碎石把落脚处弄得又滑又没个准——把他四条腿里那份晃荡和打颤全给磨没了。腿跟身子越发合衬，尺寸也长得匀实，瞧上去真像是打他身上正经长出来的。他那四只蹄子早就褪掉了粉红的软壳，转成了铁灰色，差不多跟钢铁本身一样又硬又结实；他一路连蹦带跳冲下乱石峡谷、腾越倒木的那副本事，论脚下的稳当兴许比不过山羊，可论速度和不管不顾，他远远赚了回来——而且不知怎么的，落到谷底时他回回都是正着身子的。

有一天，就在这么连滚带爬地冲下一面山坡的当口，烟儿差点儿撞上一只肉桂色的小熊崽，那小家伙原本蜷在一个大树桩顶上睡觉。烟儿在原地站了一秒，就这一秒的工夫，小熊崽呜地一声咆哮，从树桩上栽了下来，一落地就朝另一边撒腿跑了。

===Original===
The action of the cub is what decided Smoky whether to stand still, turn back and hightail it, or follow and investigate; but his curiosity was still with him, and bowing his neck he paced high and mighty on the trail of the hairy puzzle.

Over dead timber he went, sailed acrost washes, and ducked under branches. He was gaining and would of kept the chase up for quite a spell, only, and just when things was getting real interesting, there was a crash, and to his right a dust and a commotion which sounded like a landslide. In half a second more, a big round brown head showed itself thru a tangle of broken limbs and underbrush, Smoky got a glimpse of two small eyes afire, long white teeth a gleaming, and when all the sudden apparition was backed by a roar that near shook the mountains, Smoky left. He tore a hole in the earth as he turned tail, and he wasn’t pacing high and mighty as he made distance and raced back towards his mammy and safety.
===Chinese===
小熊崽这一动，反倒替烟儿拿定了主意——是原地站着，是回头逃命，还是跟上去瞧个究竟；反正他的好奇心还使着劲儿呢。只见他把脖子一弓，昂首阔步、神气十足地踏上了追踪那团毛茸茸的谜的路。

他跨过枯倒木，飞身跃过干涸的冲沟，又低头钻过一根根枝桠。眼看越追越近，这场追逐本还能痛痛快快续上好一阵，偏偏——正到热闹处，忽听轰隆一声巨响，右边腾起一片烟尘，那动静听着像塌了半坡山。不过半秒，一颗又大又圆的棕色脑袋从压断的枝桠和灌木丛里探了出来，烟儿瞥见两只喷火的小眼睛、一口闪着白光的长牙；这团凭空冒出来的怪物再配上差点儿震塌群山的一声吼——烟儿跑了。他掉头那一霎，蹄子简直在地上刨出个坑；一路放开阔步跑回马妈妈和安稳那儿去的工夫，可再不见半点昂首阔步的派头了。

===Original===
His heart was thumping fit to bust as he cleared the timber and got out in open country, and for the life of him he couldn’t figger out how that little bunch of fur he’d been chasing could turn out into such a scenery-tearing cyclone as what he’d got a glimpse of. He’d never reckoned the little cub had a mammy too.

But Smoky was learning fast, and along with his own experiences he learned from his mother just what was what in the timber and on the flats;﻿—like another time on the foothills, his mammy was in the lead and him following close behind on a hot dusty trail towards a shady spot. Of a sudden there was a rattling sound, and just as sudden his mammy left the trail as though she’d been shot. Instinct made Smoky do the same and none too soon, for on the left just a foot or so off the trail was a wriggling thing that’d just struck, and missed to reach his ankle by an inch.
===Chinese===
冲出树林、跑到开阔地上，他的心还咚咚地撞，险些要炸开；他打死也想不明白，自己一路追的那一小团毛，怎么就变成了方才瞥见的那么一个撕天裂地的龙卷风。他压根儿没料到，那只小熊崽也有个妈妈。

不过烟儿学得快。除了自己亲身领教的，他还跟着妈妈学会了林子里、平地上什么是什么——好比还有一回在山麓丘陵上，马妈妈在前头带路，他紧贴在后头，走在一条晒得发烫、尘土扑面的道上，奔一处荫凉地去。冷不丁响起一阵咯咯的脆响，马妈妈像中了枪似的猛地蹿出了那条道。天性叫烟儿也照做了，而且做得正是时候——就在左边、离道不过一英尺的地方，盘着个正蠕动的家伙，刚扑了一口，离他的踝子骨只差一英寸。

===Original===
Smoky stood off at a safe distance and snorted at it as it coiled up ready. Somehow he had no hankering to go stick his nose nowhere near or take a sniff at the grey and dirty yellow colored rattler, and when his mammy nickered for him to follow there was a warning in her nicker; he took another look at the snake. He’d remember, and do the same as his mother had done whenever the rattling sound would be heard again.

Taking in all, Smoky was getting mighty wise along with being mighty lucky in getting that wisdom. Scratches is about all he ever packed out of any scramble, and scratches didn’t count with him. His hide was getting tough and the blood that flowed in his veins wasn’t from a heart that’d peter out very easy.
===Chinese===
烟儿隔着安全的距离站定，朝那东西打了个响鼻，只见它正盘起身子，蓄着劲儿。不知怎么，他一点儿也不想去把鼻子凑近，去闻那条灰里透着土黄的响尾蛇；马妈妈低嘶着唤他跟上，嘶声里带着警告，他又看了那蛇一眼。他会记住的——往后再听见这咯咯的脆响，他就照妈妈这一回做的做。

统算下来，烟儿越长越精明，也实在运气好，精明得没吃什么亏。哪一回连滚带爬，他顶多带走几道划伤，划伤在他这儿不算回事。他的皮越长越结实，他血管里流的血，也不是打一颗动不动就泄气的心脏里泵出来的。

===Original===
The little horse was having a great time up in that high country, and if he’d seen more of life, he’d most likely wondered how long it all was going to last. It would of struck him as too good to last much longer, but as it was, Smoky took in all that life could give and enjoyed it to the limit. He never passed anything which had him wondering for fear of missing something. If a limb cracked anywheres within hearing distance he’d perk his ears towards the sound and seldom would go on till he found out just why that limb cracked that way. He’d follow and pester the badger till it’d hunt a hole; he’d circle around a tree and watch the bushy tailed squirrel as it’d climb up out of his reach. Skunks had crossed his trail too, but somehow, the atmosphere around ’em would sort of dampen his curiosity and he always kept his distance.

Smoky had met and had experiences with all the range country’s wild animals excepting the lion and the wolf. His mammy kept clear of the territory where them outlaws ranged, and if by scent the bunch suspicioned them two as neighbors, they’d drift, or else keep on the lookout till the others had drifted. Smoky met them too and had scrambles with ’em, but that came later in his life, and it’s a good thing it was later, for I most likely wouldn’t be telling about Smoky now.
===Chinese===
这匹小马在高地上的日子过得痛快极了。他要是个见过世面的，多半要犯嘀咕，这样的日子还能有多长；这日子好得简直不像能长久——可眼下呢，烟儿只管把生活给的一切照单全收，享受到了头儿。凡是叫他起疑的东西，他从没有放过去不管的，生怕错过了什么。只要在他听得见的范围里哪根枝桠咔嚓一响，他就把耳朵支棱过去，不弄清那根枝桠为什么偏偏那么断，轻易不肯挪窝。他会追着獾纠缠，直到人家钻回洞里；他会绕着一棵树打转，仰头瞧那条大毛尾巴的松鼠爬到他够不着的高处。臭鼬也从他走的道上横穿过，可不知怎么，那一带四周围的空气总会浇熄他几分好奇，他向来离得远远的。

牧场乡野上的野兽，烟儿差不多全打过交道、领教过了，只差美洲狮和狼这两个。他马妈妈向来绕开那两个亡命徒游荡的地界；马群要是凭气味疑心这两位就住在左近，就换个地界漂走，再不就日夜提防，直到那两位自己挪了窝。烟儿后来也遇上了他们，也跟他们交过手，可那是他往后日子的事了——幸亏是往后，不然的话，这会儿我说不定就讲不成烟儿的故事了。

===Original===
The first big event of Smoky’s life came when he was four months old. There was nothing to tell him anything would happen, no dark skies nor ill winds to threaten or warn, and as it was, the little feller was just in the steady motion of keeping one end of himself clear of the few flies that was around. That short tail of his was working like a pendulum, he was standing up and asleep, the breeze blowed thru his mane, and that same breeze made a sort of lullaby as it passed thru the branches of the big pine that shaded him and his mammy.

His mammy was asleep too, and so was the rest of the bunch, and when the cowboy that was riding up the canyon spotted ’em he knowed he could get above ’em and be where he could start ’em down before any of the bunch would see him.
===Chinese===
烟儿平生头一件大事，落在他四个月大的时候。没有什么告诉他要出事——没有阴沉的天，也没有邪风来吓唬、来报信；这小家伙当时正一门心思匀着劲儿，用那条短尾巴把身边那几只苍蝇从身子这头赶到那头。尾巴摆得跟钟摆似的，他站着就睡熟了。小风穿过他的鬃毛，又穿过替他和马妈妈遮凉的那棵大松树的枝桠，呜呜地哼出几分摇篮曲的意思。

他马妈妈也睡了，群里其余的伙计也都睡了。那个顺着峡谷骑上来的牛仔一眼望见他们，心里就有了数：他能绕到上头，占住先机，赶在马群里谁都没瞧见他之前，把他们朝下撵。

===Original===
It was a mighty good thing he done that, for soon as one of the bunch got wind of him and raised a head, there was a snort, they came to life and was on the run in a split second. Down the side of the canyon they went, a cloud of dust and the cowboy following.

Smoky was right with the bunch from the start. He stampeded with the leader, and once in his life it never came to him to wonder what it was all about; he just run and plum forgot to investigate.
===Chinese===
他这一手可是干对了——马群里刚有一匹闻见他的气味、扬起头来，就听得一声响鼻，全群霎时活了过来，一眨眼就跑了起来。他们顺着峡谷一侧冲下去，身后腾起一团烟尘，牛仔紧追不舍。

打从头儿起，烟儿就跟马群寸步不离。他跟着领头的一起惊了群；平生头一遭，他压根儿顾不上琢磨这一切是为了什么——光顾着跑，把看个究竟这茬儿忘了个精光。

===Original===
Tails was a popping as the horses slid off the mountain, jumped off ledges and sailed acrost washouts. Loosened rocks bumped against boulders, boulders crashed into dead hanging timber, and pretty soon a landslide brought up the rear; but even that was too slow. The ponies and the cowboy behind ’em hit the bottom of the canyon first, and when the slide reached that spot and filled the canyon with ten feet of boulders, timber, and dirt, the whole wild bunch was half a mile away and kicking up dust on the foothills at the edge of the flat.

It was away out on the flat and where the dust wasn’t so thick that Smoky took a back slant over his withers and got his first sight of the human. The way his mammy and the rest of the bunch acted, the way they run and tried to dodge or leave that human behind, sure put the impression in Smoky’s mind that here was a different kind of animal, the kind that no horse would stop to fight or argue with but instead run away from, if it was possible.
===Chinese===
马群从山坡上出溜下来，从岩台上蹦下去，飞身跨过塌垮的沟壑，一条条尾巴甩得噼啪作响。震松的碎石撞上巨石，巨石砸进悬着的枯树，没多会儿，一场滑坡在后头压了阵——可连它也嫌自己慢。小马们和跟在后面的牛仔先一步到了谷底；等滑坡赶到那儿，把十英尺厚的巨石、木头和泥土填进峡谷的时候，这一整帮野家伙已经在半英里开外，在平地边上的山麓丘陵上踢腾起新的烟尘了。

一直跑到外面开阔的平地上、烟尘淡下去的地方，烟儿才回头，从自己耆甲上边瞟了一眼，平生头一回望见了人类。瞧他马妈妈和群里别的伙计那副做派——没命地跑，一门心思要闪开、要把那个人类甩在身后——这准在烟儿心里印下了一个理儿：这是另一路动物，是哪种马都不会停下来跟它斗一斗、讲讲理的动物，能跑就只管跑。

===Original===
But it didn’t seem possible, for the rider was still right on their tails, and stayed there till he drove ’em into the long wings of big log corrals, which to Smoky seemed like trees growing sideways instead of up and down. But the little horse knowed that there was no going thru them trees. He stuck close as he could to his mammy’s side. She and the bunch milled around for a spell around the big pen, the big gate closed on ’em, and wild eyed, the bunch turned and faced a bowlegged, leather covered, sunburnt human.

Smoky shivered as he watched that strange crethure get off one of his kin, a horse just like any of the bunch him and his mammy was running with, all excepting for that funny hunk of leather on his back. Pretty soon the human fumbled around a while and then that hunk of leather was pulled off, the horse was turned loose, shook himself, and walked towards Smoky and the bunch.
===Chinese===
可瞧这架势是跑不掉了——那骑马的仍旧死死咬在他们尾巴后头，一路把他们撵进大原木畜栏（corral）那两道长长的拢翼。在烟儿眼里，那就像是横着长的树，不朝天上长，朝两边长。可小马心里明白，这堆「树」是穿不过去的。他使出浑身解数，紧紧贴住马妈妈的身子。她跟全群围着大栏转了好一阵，大栏门随后在他们身后关上了——马群瞪圆了眼，转过身，正面对上一个罗圈腿、浑身裹着皮子、让太阳烤得黢黑的人类。

烟儿瞧着那怪家伙从一匹同类身上爬下来，浑身直打哆嗦。那匹马跟他和他马妈妈一起奔跑的这一群里的任何一匹没什么两样，唯独背上驮着那么一块古怪的皮子。没多会儿，那个人类摸索了一阵，那块皮子被卸了下来；那匹马得了自由，抖了抖身子，朝烟儿和马群走了过来。

===Original===
The colt was stary-eyed and never missed a thing; and soon as the loose horse came his way he took a sniff at his sweaty hide for some kind of a clue as to just what had been setting on him all thru that long run. The sniff left him more puzzled than ever, and forgetting the horse he put all his attention on the crethure which was standing up and on two legs.

There’d been a lot of lightning up in the mountains where Smoky had been ranging that summer; he’d seen some fires up there too. That lightning and them fires was great puzzles to the colt, and when he seen the human make a swift move with a paw, and then seen a fire in one of them paws, and later on, smoke coming out of the mouth, it all made things more than ever impossible for him to figger out. He stood petrified, and watched.
===Chinese===
马驹眼睛瞪得溜圆，什么都逃不过他。那匹撒了缰的马一走近，他就凑上去闻他汗津津的皮毛，想寻点儿线索，弄清那么长一路的狂奔里，究竟是个什么东西一直骑在他背上。这一闻，把他闻得更糊涂了；他把那匹马忘在脑后，把全部心思都放到那个用两条腿站立的怪家伙身上去了。

那年夏天，烟儿游荡的群山里雷电打过好些回；山上的几场火他也见过。那雷电和那些火，对马驹都是大大的谜。这会儿他又瞧见那个人类的爪子飞快地一挥，跟着那爪子里就攥出了火；再过一会儿，那嘴里还冒出烟来——这一切加在一块儿，叫他越发没法儿想明白了。他愣在那儿跟块石头似的，看着。

===Original===
Pretty soon, them same paws that’d held the fire, reached down and picked up a coil of rope, a loop was made, and then the human walked towards him and the bunch. At that move the bunch tore around the corral and raised the dust; then Smoky heard the hiss of a rope as it sailed over past him and the loop settled on one of the ponies’ heads. The pony was stopped and led out to the hunk of leather on the ground; it was cinched on him the same as it’d been on the other horse, and when the human climbed on is when Smoky first set eyes on one of his kind in a fight with the two-legged crethure.

It was a great sight to the colt. He’d seen some of his bunch play and kick often, and he’d done a lot of that himself, but he’d never seen any get in the position and tear things up the way that pony was doing. He knowed that pony was fighting, bucking for all he was worth, and doing his daggonedest to shed that sticking and ill built wonder that was on top of him. Smoky watched and shook when he heard the pony beller. He’d never heard one of his kind make that noise before, and he knowed without wondering just what the beller meant. He remembered doing near the same that time when the cayote had nipped him in the ham strings.
===Chinese===
没多会儿，那双方才还攥着火的爪子又伸下去，拾起一盘绳子，挽出个套扣，接着那个人类就朝他和马群走来。这一动，马群绕着畜栏疯跑起来，蹬得烟尘四起；跟着烟儿听见绳子破空的一声嘶响从身边掠过，绳扣落在了一匹小马的头上。那匹小马被拽停，被牵到地上那块皮子跟前；皮子照方才那匹马的样子给他勒紧了；等那个人类跨上去——就在那一霎，烟儿平生头一回，亲眼看见自己的同类跟那两条腿的怪家伙搏斗。

这场面马驹可开了眼了。自己群里伙计们的撒欢尥蹶子他常常见，他自己也尥过不知多少回，可他从没见过谁能摆出那么个架势、把四下里搅个底朝天，像那匹小马这会儿干的这样。他知道那匹马是在拼命，在使出吃奶的劲儿尥蹶子，豁出一切要把背上那个黏在身上、怎么长都不对劲的怪物甩下去。听见那匹马拖长了声音的吼叫，烟儿一边看一边发颤。他从没听过哪个同类发出这样的声音，可他不用琢磨就知道这吼叫是什么意思——他记得郊狼咬他后腿腱那一回，他自己也叫得跟这差不离。

===Original===
Smoky’s eyes was blazing as he watched on thru the fight, and the pony’s hard jumps dwindled down to crowhops and then a stop. He watched the man as he got off the horse, opened the gate, lead the horse out and after closing it, watched him ride on and out of sight. It wasn’t till then that he came back to himself and it come to his mind to investigate the kind of place it was that cooped him in. He rubbed noses with his mammy and went to scouting around the big corral. Long strands of mane which had caught in slivers of the logs told him there’d been lots of horses here before; sniffs at the ground and more sniffs at pieces of calves’ ears that’d been cut while earmarking reminded him of the critter he’d seen while he was only a couple of weeks old. Many calves had been branded in the big corral, and with all them signs which was plain enough reading to Smoky it only made him all the more suspicious and spooky.

He was trying to get up enough nerve to go near and take a sniff at a pair of chaps hanging on the corral gate, when he noticed a dust, and under it a band of horses being hazed towards the corral he was in. With that band was a half dozen riders or more, and the sight of them made Smoky hightail to his mammy’s side in a hurry. Once there, he took in all that could be seen and watched the riders drive the horses thru the gate and turn ’em in with his bunch. There was a lot of dust, milling around, and confusion, for there was now near two hundred head of horses in the one big corral; but to Smoky all that company was mighty welcome, they meant more protection, he could hide better in that big bunch and be able to always keep some of the horses between him and them two-legged crethures.
===Chinese===
烟儿瞪着冒火的眼睛看完了这场搏斗；那匹马狠劲的腾跳衰成了原地的小蹦跳，末了彻底停了。他瞧着那个人下了马，开了栏门，把马牵出去，回手掩上门，骑上马走远，走出了视线。直到那时他才回过神来，想起该考察考察这个把他们圈起来的究竟是个什么地方。他跟马妈妈蹭了蹭鼻子，就去大畜栏里四处探查。挂在原木毛刺上的长长鬃毛告诉他，这儿从前关过好多马；在地上闻一闻，再对着几小片剪耳记时铰下来的牛犊耳朵闻一闻——这又叫他想起自己才两三个星期大时见过的那类牲口。多少牛犊都在这大栏里挨过烙；这一切在烟儿读来明明白白，只是越发叫他疑心重重、心里发毛。

他正攒着胆子想凑近去，闻一闻挂在栏门上的一条皮套裤（chaps），忽然望见一股烟尘，烟尘底下一队马正被朝着他待的这张畜栏轰过来。跟着那队马的还有六七个骑手，兴许更多——一瞧见他们，烟儿撒腿就奔回了马妈妈身边。到了那儿，他把能瞧见的全瞧在眼里：骑手们赶着那些马穿过栏门，把他们并进了自己这群。烟尘滚滚，马团团打转，乱成一锅粥——这一张大栏里如今快关了两百匹马了；可对烟儿来说，这么多伴儿是顶顶受欢迎的——伴儿多就是护身的多，他在这大群里能藏得更严实，随时都能隔上几匹马，把自己跟那些两条腿的怪家伙隔开。

===Original===
He kept hid as well as he could while the bunch milled around the corral, and in a short while, as he watched thru the horses’ legs, he seen where on the outside and close to the pen a fire was started, long bars of iron was passed thru between the logs and one end of ’em sticking in the hot blaze. Then, pretty soon a commotion was stirred, and the bunch went to racing around the corral and snorting. Many was cut out into another corral, till there was only about fifty left, mostly young colts about Smoky’s age, and a few quiet old mares.

Smoky had no chance to hide, and as he seen the bowlegged humans uncoil long ropes and heard the loops whiz past him at the speed of a bullet, terror struck in his heart and he was ready to leave the earth. He heard some of the colts squeal as they was snared, throwed, and tied down, and that sure didn’t help to ease the fear that’d took hold of him.
===Chinese===
马群在栏里团团转的当口，他尽着本事把自己藏严实。没大工夫，他从马腿的缝隙里望见，栏外紧贴着围栏生起了一堆火，一根根长铁条打原木缝里递进来，一头戳进了旺火里。跟着没多会儿就闹腾起来了：马群绕着栏狂奔，响鼻打个不停。许多马被剔出去赶进了另一张栏，到最后这张栏里只剩五十来匹，多半是跟烟儿差不多大的小马驹，外加几匹安安静静的老母马。

烟儿这回没了藏身的份儿。他眼见那些罗圈腿的人类抖开一条条长绳，听见绳圈子带着子弹般的呼啸从身边掠过——恐惧攥住了他的心，吓得他魂儿都要出窍。他听见有的马驹被套住、被摔倒、被捆住时发出的尖叫，这对攥住他的那股恐惧，实在没起半点儿缓解的作用。

===Original===
He was doing his best and keeping as far out of reach as he could but it seemed like them crethures was everywhere, and no place where them long ropes couldn’t reach. It was during one of his wild scrambles for a get away that Smoky heard the close hiss of a rope, and like a snake coiled itself around both his front legs, he let out a squeal, and in another second he was flat to the ground and four feet tied up.

Smoky figgered the end of the world had come as he felt the human touch him, and if it’d been in his blood to faint away, he’d done it easy; but as it was he never missed a thing. He seen one of the crethures run towards him with a hot iron, smelled burning hair and hide﻿—it was his own that burned, but it felt cool and there was no pain, for he was at the stage where the searing iron was no worse than a touch from the human hand. But there’s an end to all, whether it’s good or bad, and pretty soon, Smoky felt the ropes come off his legs, a boost to let him know that all was over, and when he stood up and run back to the bunch, there was a mark on his slick hide that was there for life﻿—as the brand read, the little horse belonged to the  (Rocking R) outfit.
===Chinese===
他使出了全身的解数，能躲多远躲多远，可那些怪家伙好像哪儿都有，没有一处是那长绳够不着的。就在他一次没命夺路乱窜的当口，烟儿听见绳子嘶地一声到了耳边——像条蛇似的，一下子缠住了他的两条前腿。他尖叫了一声；再一秒钟，他已经平拍在地上，四只蹄子全给捆住了。

人手一碰他，烟儿只当天塌地陷、世界到了头。他血里要是有昏死过去的本事，这会儿早使出来了；可事实是，他一样也没落下。他看见一个怪家伙拿着烧红的烙铁朝他跑来，闻见了毛皮烧焦的气味——烧的是他自己的毛皮，可那感觉很凉，也不疼，因为他正是在那么个年纪：滚烫的烙铁，也不过跟人手碰一下一样。可凡事都有个头儿，好的坏的都一样。没多会儿，烟儿觉出腿上的绳子褪了下去，又被托了一把——这是告诉他，一切都过去了。等他站起来跑回马群，他那油亮的皮毛上已经带上了一块记号，一辈子的记号——照烙印上读出来的，这匹小马从此归了摇椅R（Rocking R）牧场。

===Original===
It was all a mighty great relief to Smoky and the other colts when the branding come to an end, the bunch all put back together, and when the colts found their mammies all was turned out and free again, free to go back to the high mountain range, or run on the flats.

Smoky’s mammy took the lead, and after the rest of the bunch was thru parleying with the strange horses, they joined in with her and the colt and all strung out for the foothills. The next day they all was up in high country again and everything of the day before was forgotten, forgotten, all excepting with Smoky and the other little colts. They still remembered some, on account that it had all been mighty new to ’em, and besides, the sting of the fresh brand was there on their left thigh to remind.
===Chinese===
打烙印的活计一收场，马群重新归拢到一块儿，马驹们也各自寻着了马妈妈——对烟儿和其他小马驹来说，这真是天大的解脱。他们全被放了出去，又自由了：自由地回高山牧场去，或是到平地上撒欢。

烟儿的马妈妈打了头。等群里其余的伙计跟那些生马寒暄完了，就跟着她和小马驹汇成一股，鱼贯朝山麓丘陵去了。第二天，他们又全都上了高山地界，头一天的事都忘啦，忘啦——只有烟儿和其他小马驹没全忘。他们还记得一些：一来那些事对他们实在太新鲜，二来，新烙印的灼痛还在他们左胯上提着醒儿呢。

===Original===
But as the days went by, and new things happened right along to draw Smoky’s interest in life, the happenings at the corral was gradually left behind like a bad dream; the burn healed quick and left a neat brand all of which growed right with him.

Fall came, skies clouded and the rains was getting cold, and each time it cleared up again it was a little colder, the sun wasn’t making as high a circle and was steady losing some of its heat, and when after a few mornings’ frost the skies clouded again and the wind blowed a light snow over the high pinnacles, the bunch gradually ranged lower and lower, till, when they reached the foothills and finally the flats, the first of the winter had set in and it was time for ’em to drift to their winter range.
===Chinese===
可是日子一天天过去，新鲜事一件接着一件，牵着烟儿对生活的兴头，畜栏里那一场渐渐丢在了脑后，像一场噩梦；烫伤好得快，留下一个利利索索的烙印，往后就随着他一块儿长。

秋天来了，天阴下来，雨也一场比一场凉；每回放晴，都又冷上一分，太阳绕的圈子矮了下去，热气也一天天丢。几个霜晨过后，天又阴了，风把一阵细雪刮过高高的峰尖，马群游荡的地界便一寸寸往低处挪；等他们到了山麓丘陵、又到了平地，冬天已经开了头，该是他们漂去冬牧场的时节了。

===Original===
Their winter range was low ridges and benches that raised up in the middle of the prairie. There was steep ravines where willows and cottonwoods growed in big patches, the shelter of them was mighty fine when the cold north winds blowed and the howling blizzard made every living thing hunt a hole. Tall grass was there too, and could always be reached by pawing for it. In quiet winter days, when the sun came out and the wind went down, the bunch could always leave their shelter and find places on the ridges where the winds had swept the snow away, and where the grass was in plain sight.

Drifting acrost that flat open country and investigating that new winter territory had kept Smoky’s eyes, ears, and nostrils mighty busy. There’d been a lot to keep him looking, listening, and sniffing. Every buffalo wallow, coulee, and rise had kept his senses on hair trigger edge, and when the first snow had come, he’d enjoy that too. It made him want to buck and play as it fell on his withers and rump, and along with the cold weather that’d turned the range brown and then white, he was finding more ambition to keep on the jump. He wasn’t looking for shade no more.
===Chinese===
他们的冬牧场，是大草原当中隆起的低岭和阶地。陡峭的沟壑里，柳树和三角叶杨一片一片地长；赶上北风发狠、暴风雪嚎得天下活物都找洞钻的时候，那里面藏身是好极了。高草也有，刨开雪总够得着。冬日里安静的日子，太阳一出来、风一歇，马群就能离开藏身处，到岭上去寻那些被风扫净了雪、青草明摆在眼前的地方。

漂过那片平坦开阔的乡野、考察那片新的冬日地界，把烟儿的眼睛、耳朵和鼻孔忙了个够呛。要看的、要听的、要闻的实在不少。每一个野牛打滚的洼坑、每一道冲沟、每一处隆起，都把他的感官绷在一触即发的弦上；头一场雪下来的时候，他也乐得受用。雪片落在他的耆甲和屁股上，撩得他想尥蹶子撒欢；加上天一冷，牧场先黄后白，他的兴头越发足，一天到晚不肯消停。他再不找什么树荫了。

===Original===
If Smoky minded the cold he sure didn’t show it, and if you could of felt his warm hide and seen how thick the hair had growed on it, and how long, you’d never wondered why it was that the cold raw winds never fazed him. Mother Nature had seen to that and brought on the winter gradual, till, when the time come for it to set in, Smoky was well prepared. He was packing a natural fur coat on a good thick hide, and with an inch of tallow for a lining, and along with the rich, thick blood which he kept in good circulation he was mighty able to compete with the snows and freezing weather, and was never found to hunt shelter till the blizzard blowed over the ridges from the north.

He pawed snow for his feed that winter, for it had been quite a few months before when he found that his mammy’s milk wasn’t quite enough, and later turned out to be just a taste, and finally, she give him to understand that he was weaned. There was no arguing with her, and Smoky knowed better than try, so he pawed and hunted for grass like a big horse. He et snow and could stay away from water as long as any of the bunch, and even though he lost some of his roundness thru the worst of the winter, you couldn’t of noticed it on account of his hair being so long.
===Chinese===
烟儿要是把冷放在心上，那他可真是一点儿没露出来。你只要摸一摸他那暖烘烘的皮毛，看看上面的毛长得有多厚、有多长，就再不会奇怪，阴冷刺骨的风怎么就奈何不了他。这都是大自然母亲打点好的：她把冬天一步一步带进来，等冬天真正扎下根的时候，烟儿早已准备停当。他裹着一件天生的皮袄，衬在一层厚实的皮子上，里头还絮了一英寸厚的膘油；再加上一身又浓又稠的活血，周身循环得旺旺的，跟大雪酷寒较量起来绰绰有余——暴风雪不从北边翻过山梁压下来，你休想见他寻地方避风。

那个冬天，他刨雪觅食——其实早在好几个月前，他就发现马妈妈的奶不太够吃了，后来渐渐只剩个零嘴儿，到末了，她让他明白：他断奶了。跟她没什么好争的，烟儿也识相，不去自讨没趣，于是就像匹大马似的刨雪寻草。他吃雪，不喝水能跟群里的任何一匹赛着熬；冬天最难过的那阵子，他身上掉了几分圆实，可他毛长，你压根儿瞧不出来。

===Original===
Being that Smoky was still quite a privileged character it helped him considerable thru them long winter months, if he’d see some big horse dig down into a special good grassy spot; he’d take advantage of his standing and chase the big horse away. He looked mighty wicked as he put his ears down, showed his teeth, and delivered a side kick, and the big horse would *act* scared to death, and get away from the dangerous Smoky in a hurry. There was only one in the bunch that wouldn’t scare worth a bit, and that was his mammy. He could paw in the same hole with her and maybe steal a bunch of grass right from under her nose, but there was no chasing *her* away. Most likely there was no such intentions in Smoky’s mind anyhow, for the little horse did think an awful lot of that mammy of his; and even though she never played with him, and even nipped him for some things he’d do, he knowed if a showdown ever come she’d fight to a finish for him.

So, as the snows piled high and the ravines filled with drifts, Smoky went on and passed the hard of the winter in near the same carefree reckless way he’d passed the summer before. Of course, pawing for his feed the way he had to was taking some of his energy, but he’d manage to reserve some for play, and many is the time when you’d see the bunch a pawing all intent to reaching the grass, you’d see Smoky tearing up clouds of light snow and a playing for all he was worth. Other colts would join him, and pretty soon the young ones would have the white scenery all tracked the same as if a thousand head of horses had stampeded thru.
===Chinese===
话说烟儿在那群里多少还是个有头脸的角色，这帮他熬过了那些漫长的冬月——哪匹大马要是刨开一片特别好的草窝，他就仗着自己的身份，把人家轰走。他把耳朵朝后一贴，龇出牙，再补上一记侧踢，那副凶相可真够瞧的；大马便*装*出吓破了胆的样子，急急躲开这个惹不起的烟儿。全群只有一位轰也轰不走，那就是他马妈妈。他可以跟她同一个坑里刨草，兴许还能从她鼻子底下偷走一口，可要说轰走*她*，那是休想。多半烟儿心里也压根儿没这个念头，因为这小马对他那位马妈妈实在是看得很重；虽说她从不跟他玩，他有些作为还挨过她的咬，可他知道，真到了摊牌的那一天，她会为他血战到底。

就这样，雪越积越高，沟壑让积雪填满，烟儿照旧往前过活，跟头年夏天一样没心没肺、天不怕地不怕，熬过了冬天最苦的那一截。当然，天天这么刨雪觅食，多少耗他些力气，可他总还匀得出一些来玩。多少回你都会瞧见：马群正埋头刨雪、一心够着底下的草，烟儿却在那儿扬起一团一团的细雪，撒着欢儿可劲儿地闹。别的马驹也加入进来，没多会儿，小家伙们就把那一白天地踩得满是蹄印，活像有一千匹马从中间惊群跑过去似的。

===Original===
The winter wore on that way, no events came to shake the quiet and peace of that part of the range, only, one day a rider had showed up against the skyline. Smoky had been the only one to see him on account he was a little ways from the bunch and where he could see around a point. With the sight of that rider Smoky remembered ropes, a corral and human hands; and he sashayed back to the bunch fast as his legs could carry him.

Finally, the first sign of spring came. Smoky couldn’t appreciate it very much on account that the warm winds which was starting the snow to melting only left him weak and lazy. His blood hadn’t started to thin down as yet, and for the first short spell in his life, he had no hankering to crowhop around and play.
===Chinese===
冬天就这么一天天挨过去，没有什么事儿来搅动牧场那一角的清静，只有一天，一个骑马的人在天边线上冒了出来。全场只有烟儿瞧见了他，因为那会儿他离群有一截，正占着能望过一处山嘴的位置。那骑手的影子一入眼，烟儿就想起了绳子、畜栏和人手；他撒开四条腿，能跑多快跑多快，一溜烟儿回了群。

终于，春天的头一个征候来了。烟儿却没什么心思领受，因为那阵化雪的暖风只叫他浑身发软、犯懒。他一身冬血还没换稀下来，平生头一小段日子里，他提不起半点儿兴致蹦跶着撒欢。

===Original===
Then a few weeks later the bare earth begin to show in big spots and on the sunny side of the buttes green grass begin to shoot up. That new green grass tasted mighty good to Smoky; it tasted so good that the dry feed he’d wintered on and which could now be got without pawing for, was only stepped on in hunting for them first blades of green. Nothing but that would do, and as it was still scarce and hard to find that early in the year, he covered a lot of territory and got very little feed.

But the rest of the bunch was afflicted the same way; the long dry grass wasn’t good enough no more, and consequences is the bunch lost some weight. But Mother Nature was on hand there again; she knowed that’s what the bunch needed to condition ’em for the change of season, and sure enough, pretty soon the warm weather didn’t leave ’em so drowsy no more, and as the grass kept a growing, and finally got to be everywhere, on the ridges as on the flats, the bunch perked up again; the long winter hair was loosening and big hunks of it was left wherever they rolled.
===Chinese===
过了几个星期，光地开始一大片一大片地露出来，孤丘向阳的一面，绿草冒出了头。那新绿草在烟儿嘴里香极了；香得他连吃了一冬的干草——如今不用刨雪就够得着的干草——都只配踩在蹄子底下，为的是去寻头几茬绿芽。非那个不罢休，可一年里这么早的时节，绿草又稀又难寻，于是他跑了一大片子地，肚子却没落着多少食。

其实群里别的伙计也都害着一样的毛病；长长的干草再不招人待见，结果全群都掉了些分量。不过大自然母亲又照应到了：她知道，全群正需要这么一场，好把身子调理得换得了季。果不其然，没多会儿，暖和天就不叫他们那么犯困了；草一个劲儿地长，末了岭上平地到处都是，马群重新抖擞起来；长长的冬毛松了，他们滚到哪儿，一坨一坨的毛就落在哪儿。

===Original===
Smoky’s winter coat had faded to a brown at the first sign of spring, and now that the warmer weather had come and green grass was a plenty, there was another color showed where he’d shed off the long hair. It was what we call “mouse color” only maybe darker; no more of the slick black hair that decorated his hide the summer before could be seen, the change of color had showed itself around his ears and flanks but it wasn’t till winter came that the real change had took place and turned him to a grayish mouse color.

His head and legs was a little darker than his body and showed brown, and with that little blaze face of his a looming up, he made a mighty pretty picture, a picture of the kind once you see you never forget; for Smoky was perfect any way you looked at him and it seemed like as you sized him up that the other of his kind hadn’t been played square with and some of their good points stole away so as Smoky would be the perfect little horse.
===Chinese===
刚见春意那阵儿，烟儿的冬毛就褪成了褐色；如今暖和天来了，绿草管够，他褪去长毛的地方又透出了另一种颜色。那就是我们说的「鼠灰色」，兴许还要深些；头年夏天装扮他一身皮毛的油亮黑毛再见不着了。变色先从耳朵边和腹侧显出来，可真正的变化要到冬天才落定，把他变成一匹灰扑扑的鼠色马。

他的头和腿比身子深些，泛着褐色；加上他那道小小的白章脸往上一衬，整个儿是一幅好看极了的小画——那样的画，你只要见过一回，一辈子忘不掉。因为烟儿不管你从哪头端详都无可挑剔；你打量他的时候简直会觉得，他的同类们准是受了亏待，各自的好处被偷走了些，好凑成烟儿这匹十全十美的小马。

===Original===
Smoky had never thought of his good looks and strong body, his good looks was only a sign of his good health. He felt it all and used it to the limit for his own benefit and for whatever fun his strength and energy could afford him. That never lacked, and if he layed down it was seldom because he was tired, it was more thru a hint from Mother Nature for him to hold on a while and store up on life and more strength.

The spring rains came and went, and each time after each spell of moisture the grass was a little taller and the country greener, the sun kept a getting warmer too, and some days was already hot.
===Chinese===
烟儿从没想过自己长相好、身子骨结实——好长相不过是他好底子的一个记号。这一切他都受着，并且用到极限：为自己沾光，也为他那身力气和精神换得来的种种快活。力气和精神从来不缺；他要是躺下了，十有八九不是因为累，多半是大自然母亲递了个话儿，叫他缓一缓，攒一攒，好添些生气、添些力气。

春雨来了又去；每落一场，草就高一截，乡野就绿一分。太阳一天天越晒越暖，有些日子已经热了。

===Original===
It was during one of these hot days that Smoky’s mother disappeared. Smoky had been snoozing in the shade of a creek bank and it wasn’t till quite a while after he got up and started grazing that he noticed she was gone. The bunch had been drifting back for the summer range and was at the foothills of the big range, the big flat below was an easy place to spot any moving object on, but Smoky couldn’t find hide nor hair of that mammy of his. He trotted around the bunch and, nickering, investigated for a spell. She couldn’t be found.

He took another look at the country around, and nickering in kind of wonder, he went to grazing again. Somehow he wasn’t fidgety as he should of been, maybe he had a hunch that her disappearing that way was necessary and that all was hunkydory. Anyway Smoky never missed any sleep, or feed, or play while she was gone; things went on just the same, and the little horse’s hide was getting slicker every day.
===Chinese===
就是赶着这么一个热天，烟儿的妈妈不见了。烟儿一直在溪岸的荫凉里打盹，等他起来啃上草、过了好一阵，才发觉她没了影儿。马群本是漂回夏牧场去，已经到了大山场的山麓丘陵；底下那片大平地，什么活物一动就瞧得见，可烟儿连他那位马妈妈的毛尖儿都没找着一根。他绕着马群小跑，一边低嘶一边寻了好一阵。哪儿也寻不见。

他又把四下的乡野望了一遍，带着几分纳罕低嘶了两声，接着又低头吃草去了。不知怎么，他并没有像该有的那样焦躁；兴许他心里有个预感，觉得她那么走开是必要的，一切都会安妥。反正她不在的这些日子，烟儿觉没少睡、草没少吃、欢没少撒；日子照旧往前过，小马的皮毛一天比一天油亮。

===Original===
A few days passed, and then one morning the big buckskin horse that was still in the bunch perked up his ears, nickered, and loped out towards the flat. A horse was out there and coming towards the bunch, alongside the horse was a little moving object.

Smoky and the bunch stood in their tracks and watched. Pretty soon Smoky noticed something familiar in that lone horse coming towards him, but that little object a tagging along puzzled him, and head up, he trotted out a ways to investigate. Then it all came to him, for the lone horse was none other than his mammy.
===Chinese===
又过了几天，一天早晨，群里那匹大兔褐马忽然竖起耳朵，低嘶一声，朝平地那边小跑出去。外面有一匹马正朝马群走来，那马身边还跟着一个小小的、会动的东西。

烟儿和马群站在原地望着。没多会儿，烟儿觉出那匹独自走来的马有点儿眼熟，可旁边跟着的那个小东西叫他犯迷糊；他昂起头，小跑出去一截，要去看个究竟。这下全明白了——那匹马不是别人，正是他马妈妈。

===Original===
He lit out on a run a nickering as he went till he got to within a few feet of her, and then he got a slant at the object a tagging alongside, a brand new little wobblety legged colt it was, shining black, and awful timid at the sight of so many strangers. It was Smoky’s new little brother.

Smoky couldn’t keep his nose off the baby, and his mammy had to cock one ear back at him the same as to say “careful, Son”; but Smoky was careful, and as his mammy went on to join the bunch, he followed and the big buckskin brought up the rear. From then on Smoky ranked second.
===Chinese===
他撒腿飞奔过去，一路低嘶个不停，跑到离她只剩几英尺的地方，才斜眼瞧清了旁边跟着的那个小东西——一匹崭新的小马驹，四条腿还晃晃悠悠的，黑得发亮，一眼看见这么多生面孔，怕得不行。这是烟儿新添的小弟弟。

烟儿的鼻子就没离开过那个小不点儿，马妈妈只好把一只耳朵朝后别着对着他，那意思是说「轻着点儿，孩子」。可烟儿到底是轻着点儿的。马妈妈上前归了群，他跟在后头，大兔褐马殿了后。打从那时起，烟儿屈居第二了。

## III / 三

===Original===
Where the Trails Fork

===Chinese===
道儿分岔的地方

===Original===
Middle summer had come, the day was hot and still; even up amongst the high peaks and where the snow was making a last stand the heat was strong, for the sun was shooting straight down and the crags could give no more shade. Up on a rocky trail of that country a small bunch of range horses was drifting one behind the other and following the leader﻿—the leader was Smoky’s mammy, the new little black colt right at her heels and next the blaze-faced, mouse colored, yearling, Smoky. A little further back was a big buckskin horse and there followed eight or ten others which made up the rest of the bunch.

They all trailed along seemed like headed for nowheres in particular. They passed under wind-twisted trees and right on thru the shade they’d give. Cool streams wasn’t even sniffed at, and the long stems of grass that was everywhere wasn’t at all noticed; they was all just drifting and maybe only hitting out for another special good part of the high range.﻿—A feller watching ’em would of figgered that something or other had started ’em on the move, maybe a rider had been spotted that morning which had kettled ’em into a run, or else cougars might of been too numerous for comfort.

===Chinese===
仲夏到了，天又热又闷；连高高的群峰上头、残雪做最后挣扎的地方，暑气也一样逼人——太阳直直地射下来，嶙峋的山石再也腾不出一块荫凉。在那片乡野的一条石头道上，一小群牧场马正一匹跟着一匹、随着领头的漂着走——领头的正是烟儿的马妈妈，新添的小黑马驹紧跟在她脚跟后头，再往后，便是那匹白章脸、鼠灰色的一岁驹（yearling）——烟儿。稍后一点是一匹大兔褐马，跟着又有八匹十匹的，凑齐了这一群其余的伙计。

他们一路鱼贯而行，瞧那样子，也没个特别要去的地方。他们从风拧歪的树下走过，径直穿过树影投下的荫凉。清凉的溪水，他们连鼻子都没伸过去闻一下；遍地高高的草茎，也没谁放在心上；他们只是漂着走，兴许不过是奔着高地上另一块顶好的草场去。——要是有谁瞧着他们，准会琢磨出个所以然来：许是那天早上望见了骑马的人，被撵得跑了一阵；再不就是美洲狮闹得太多，叫他们待着不踏实。

===Original===
The little bunch kept a trailing along till they came to where the trail branched and the leader took the lower one. The little black colt and all the rest followed, all excepting the mouse color yearling. The upper trail had drawed that one’s interest, and nothing would do but what he had to investigate it for a ways. He kept his nose on the ground as he went and sniffed for clues of anything that might be of interest to him, he could see the bunch below and he figgered on cutting across to ’em soon as his curiosity was satisfied.

===Chinese===
这一小群马一路跟下去，走到道路分岔的地方，领头的选择了下头那条。小黑马驹和其余的都跟了去，唯独那匹鼠灰色的一岁驹没跟。上头那条岔道勾住了他的心思，说什么他也要顺着它查探上一程。他一路把鼻子贴着地，嗅着一切可能让他来劲的线索；他望得见下头的马群，盘算着等好奇心填满了，就抄近路斜插过去，归到他们里头去。

===Original===
Ahead of him a ways and above the trail was a big granite boulder a good ten feet high. A scrub mahogany had found root in a crack of the big rock and was spreading its branches well over it and making a good shade. In that shade and mighty hard to notice, was an object, a long, flat, dark buckskin object, which looked a lot like part of the rock. It was stretched out full length and seemed like without life only maybe for the tip of its long, round tail which was jerking up and down. The round head raised an inch at the sound of hoofs on the rocky trail, the ears flattened and the yellow eyes turned jet black at the sight of Smoky, the mouse colored yearling.

===Chinese===
在他前头不远、道的上坡一侧，立着一块足有十英尺高的大花岗岩。一株矮桃花心木（scrub mahogany）在大石的裂缝里扎了根，把枝桠大大方方铺张开去，在石上造出一片好荫凉。就在那片荫凉里，有个极难留意的东西——一个长长的、扁扁的、深兔褐色的东西，瞧着跟岩石本身没两样。它直挺挺伸展开去，像是没了性命，只有那条长圆尾巴的尾巴尖儿，还在一上一下地抽动。石头道上响起蹄声，那颗圆脑袋抬起了一英寸；等看清来的是那匹鼠灰色的一岁驹烟儿，它的耳朵便贴伏下去，黄眼睛转成了漆黑。

===Original===
Smoky was coming right on the trail and would pass to within a couple of feet of the big rock that was the mountain lion’s game hunting perch, many a deer he’d pounced onto and killed from that perch; and not far away from that spot was bones scattered around which showed where he’d drug his victims and et his fill. Wolves, cayotes, and other varmints had cleaned up what the big lion would leave and the result was white bones a shining to the sun.

The lion had a big territory which he claimed as his, but in all that rough country there was no better place than the one he was now getting ready to spring from. He’d got meat from that spot when he failed at others, and the trail he overlooked was tracked with many hoofs, hoofs of all the kind that ranged up there﻿—it was a main trail to a main pass.

===Chinese===
烟儿正顺着道走来，离那块大岩最近处不过两三英尺——那岩正是美洲狮行猎的瞭望台，多少只鹿都是他从那上头扑下来咬死的；离那处不远，四下散落着一堆骨头，标出了他拖拽猎物、吃个肚圆的地方。狼、郊狼，还有别的野东西，把大猫吃剩的收拾得干干净净，结果就只剩一片白骨，在太阳底下发亮。

这头美洲狮有一大片自认是自家地盘的疆域，可在这片高低坎坷的乡野里，再没有一处比他此刻蓄劲待扑的这个位置更好了。在别处扑空的时候，他打这里头搞到过肉；他俯瞰的这条道上印满了蹄印，各式各样在高地走动的蹄子都打这儿过——那可是一条通往大山口的主道。

===Original===
Why Smoky’s mother didn’t take that trail can’t be explained much; may be it was instinct that warned her, and then again she might of got a glimpse of the tall rock and past experience made her turn to the left, but anyway she and her young colt and the rest of the bunch was safe and had left Smoky till he was thru investigating and ready to catch up with ’em.

Smoky kept on a coming and edging closer to the rock; he nosed every twig and stone along the trail till he got to within a few feet of the spot where the lion would spring. The lion wasn’t a stretched out shadow no more. He still looked like part of the rock and fitted pretty well with the stump of the scrub mahogany, but he was in a position that sure tallied up with all what was about to happen. He was ready, and still as the rock he was on, and the quiver of his long tail was a plenty to show that his wiry frame and brain was sure together and intent on one thing.

===Chinese===
烟儿的妈妈为什么没走那条道，说不大清楚；也许是直觉提醒了她，也许她又瞟见了那块高岩，从前的经历叫她朝左边拐了。反正她和她的小驹子、连同群里其余的伙计都平安无事，把烟儿落在后头，由他查探个够，再赶上去归队。

烟儿只管往前走，一步一步蹭近那块岩石；他把道上的每一根细枝、每一块石头都嗅了个遍，直到离美洲狮蓄劲要扑的那处只剩几英尺。美洲狮再不是一个摊平的影子了。他瞧着仍像岩石的一部分，跟那株矮桃花心木的树桩也贴补得挺合缝，可他如今这副架势，分明是同即将发生的一切对上了榫。他预备停当，静得跟身下的岩石一样；只有那条长尾巴一阵一阵地颤，足够说明他精瘦的身架和脑袋正拧成一股绳，一心只惦着一件事。

===Original===
Another foot ahead and Smoky would be seeing his last of daylight. The colt had one leg raised to make that last step when there’s a rattling buzz comes from the foot of the rock; a four foot rattlesnake stretched out and reaches for Smoky’s nose and that one leg which was raised to go forward went back instead. It was all that saved him.

===Chinese===
再往前一英尺，烟儿就要看这辈子最后的天光了。小驹子正抬起一条腿、要迈这最后一步，忽然岩脚底下响起一阵咯咯的嗡鸣；一条四英尺长的响尾蛇窜身探出，直奔烟儿的鼻子——那条抬起来要往前迈的腿，撤了回去。救他命的，就这一下。

===Original===
The lion had figgered on his victim a jumping to one side at his leap, and he’d allowed for that, but the way it happened this time was that the snake caused Smoky to jump away just as he’d started which was a little too soon according to the lion’s figgering, and what’s more Smoky went to the wrong direction about a foot with the result that he just got his claws full of Smoky’s mane and no more. He scrambled in mid air and done his best to get a hold in Smoky’s neck but even with all the action he put in his trying he struck mostly air, and then hard ground.

===Chinese===
