美洲狮原先盘算好，猎物会在他跃起时朝一边闪，这一层他也留了余量；偏偏这一回，是那条蛇催得烟儿在他刚起跳的当口就闪开了——照美洲狮的算法，早了那么一点儿；更要紧的是，烟儿闪的方向还不对，偏出去一英尺上下，结果是狮爪里只捞到满满一把鬃毛，再没别的。他在半空里急抓乱挠，拼了命想抠住烟儿的脖子，可任凭他把浑身解数使尽，扑到的大半是空气，跟着，便是硬邦邦的地面。

===Original===
Smoky never waited to see if that flying shadow of sharp claws was after him or not; he’d started at the sound of the rattler and had kept a moving mighty fast ever since. A few feet of drop in the scenery only helped him make more speed and the shortcut from the trail he’d left to the trail his mammy and the bunch was on was covered in no time.

He lit in the bunch a running, and the bunch getting a hint from his wild eyed actions that all wasn’t well started a running too and for a ways they all went as though the devil was after ’em.

===Chinese===
烟儿才不等在那儿，看那团带利爪的飞影追不追他；咯咯声一响他就动了身，打那以后就没歇过蹄子。地势往下矮了几英尺，反倒帮他跑出更快的速度；从他撇下的那条道，到他马妈妈和马群走的那条道，这条近路一眨眼就跑到了头。

他一头扎进马群里，跑着的蹄子还没收住；马群瞧他瞪圆了眼的那副疯劲儿，知道出了岔子，也跟着跑了起来，有一程子大家跑得像是魔鬼撵在后头似的。

===Original===
But the devil (if that ain’t too mild a name for the lion) wasn’t after ’em. He knowed the colt had too much speed for him and never even thought of following him, and as it was he was just a lashing himself with his long tail and mad clear thru at the thought of missing such a nice fat yearling colt as Smoky was.

===Chinese===
可那魔鬼（要是拿这字眼称呼美洲狮还不算太轻的话）并没有追。他知道那驹子的脚程不是他追得上的，压根儿没起过跟下去的念头；他只是拿长尾巴抽打着自己，一想到放跑了烟儿这么一身好膘的一岁驹子，气得从里到外没一处不着火。

===Original===
From that day on Smoky dodged high rocks unless he could see the top of ’em; pine trees with stout lower limbs had him a circling too, or any other place where a lion could perch on and spring from. The little horse was gradually getting so he was satisfied to be more with the bunch and not do so much investigating; besides he’d got first hand acquaintance with most all that prowled the range, and everything in general was getting to be less of a puzzle to him.

===Chinese===
打那天起，烟儿见了高岩就躲，除非能把岩顶瞧个明白；底下枝桠粗壮的松树，也惹得他绕着圈儿走；凡是美洲狮能蹲上去、又能扑下来的地方，一概如此。这匹小马渐渐变得情愿多跟马群待在一处，不那么到处查探了；再说，牧场上出没的东西，他差不多都亲身领教过一遍，万事万物在他眼里，也一个个不那么像谜了。

===Original===
It all kept a getting to be less of a puzzle to him till finally there come a time when Smoky got so he thought he knowed it all. He figgered he had the world by the tail and with a downhill push. Like all the other colts of his age he was just where conceit had the best of him; he got strong headed and full of mischief, and then’s when the older horses figgered him to be a regular pest and began knocking on him.

===Chinese===
这些事在他眼里越来越不像谜，到后来竟有那么一天，烟儿自个儿觉着什么都懂了。他掂量着，这世界叫他攥住了尾巴，还是顺坡往下推的架势。跟他同岁的马驹一样，他正走到自满占了上风的关口；他犟了头，一肚子淘气，也就在这时候，老马们把他当成了正经的祸害，动手敲打他。

===Original===
He was getting to be of size that could stand knocks too. They all took turns at him and pounded on his ribs every chance they had thru the rest of that summer, and tried to set him where he belonged; but it was slow work and Smoky was still getting away with some of the bluffs when the first snows came. He was ornery all that winter, and even though none of the horses would let him steal the grass they pawed up, he aggravated ’em a lot by making ’em think he would; and when they’d kick at him, and miss, there was some more about his actions that sure let ’em know he was getting away with something.

===Chinese===
他的个头，也长到了挨得起敲打的份上。那年夏天剩下的日子里，他们轮班上阵，一逮着机会就朝他两肋捶上几下，想把他捋回他该待的位置上去；可这活儿出息得慢，初雪落下来的时候，烟儿使的一些唬人招数还照样得逞。整个一冬他都难缠得很；哪怕没有哪匹马真肯让他抢走自己刨出来的草，他也老是摆出一副要抢的架势，把他们气得够呛；他们朝他踢、又踢了个空的时候，他那一副做派更叫他们断定：这小子，又占到什么便宜了。

===Original===
Then one day a strange horse showed up on the skyline and joined the bunch. A strange horse is always sort of timid when first joining a new bunch that way; and Smoky took advantage of that to show there was at least one he had buffaloed﻿—he run the stranger around and around and kept a nipping him on the rump till the old pony was on the point of leaving and hunt new territory. That sport lasted off and on for a few days, and then one day the older horse turned and lit into Smoky. There was no battle, for Smoky was just running a bluff; and at the first turn of the events he evaporated and kept on evaporating till the stranger got cooled down a bit. After that Smoky kept his distance and acted willing to let the stranger stay with the bunch.

===Chinese===
后来有一天，天边线上冒出一匹生马，进了马群。生马乍进一个新群，总有几分怯场；烟儿就钻这个空子，好显摆群里至少还有一匹是他唬住过的——他撵着那生马一圈又一圈地跑，一口一口咬人家的屁股，咬得那匹老马险些要离群另找地界去了。这把戏断断续续玩了几天，后来有一天，那匹老马转过身来，冲着烟儿发作了。谈不上什么大战，因为烟儿本来就是在使诈；风向刚一转，他就化没了影儿，一路化下去，直到生马消了消气才算罢。打那以后，烟儿跟他保持着距离，做出一副情愿让他留在群里的样子。

===Original===
The winter wore on that way, and as Smoky was met hard at every ornery thing he’d do, it all got to finally leave an impression on him, and he gradually lost *some* of his conceit and hard headedness. But Spring came, other seasons and all kinds of weather followed, and it wasn’t till Smoky was a three year old that he really come anywheres near to living up to good range horse etiquette. There was so much life wrapped up in that pony’s hide that it was mighty hard for him to settle down and behave, and even as a three year old he sometimes had to bust out and do things that wasn’t at all proper and which made the old horses set their ears back and show their teeth.

===Chinese===
那个冬天就这么熬了过去。烟儿每使一回坏，都挨了硬邦邦的回敬，到头来这一切终于在他身上留了印子，他的自负和犟劲儿，渐渐磨掉了那么几分。可春天来了，别的季节、各样的天气跟着轮转，直到烟儿满了三岁，他才真正算得上沾着了好牧场马规矩的边儿。这匹马的皮囊里裹着的生活力实在太多，要他安分下来、懂点规矩，实在是难；即便到了三岁，他有时还得发作一回，干些顶不像话的事，惹得老马们把耳朵朝后一背，龇出牙来。

---

===Original===
The start of Smoky’s third year was all to his favor﻿—the spring rains was warmer than on average, the green grass shot up half an inch to the day, and more than met up with the hard to satisfy appetite which was his. Consequences is, when he shed off his long winter coat he was slicker and rounder than ever and looked like he was wrapped up in fine mouse colored silk. His blazed face loomed up snow white and to match his trim ankles. He was a picture to make any cowboy miss a few heart beats as he sometimes raced acrost the prairie sod and with head and tail up showed off the qualities that stuck out at his every move.

But to the bunch, all them qualities and good points of Smoky’s was lost and not at all noticed. His mammy or any of the others would of thought just as much of him if he was just an ordinary horse or even an ill built scrub. They’d all liked him better if he wasn’t so ornery and didn’t need so much convincing, for Smoky was getting to be of a size and temper along with it where it was mighty hard for some to try to eddicate him and *show* that they could.

===Chinese===
烟儿这第三个年头的开局，事事都顺着他——春雨比往年暖，青草一天能蹿半英寸，把他那张难喂饱的嘴，伺候得绰绰有余。结果是，等他脱下长长的冬毛，他比以往什么时候都更油光、更滚圆，瞧着就像裹了一身细密的鼠灰色绸子。他的白章脸衬得雪也似的白，正配他那四只利落的蹄踝。他时而掠过草原草皮飞奔一场，昂着头、翘着尾，一举一动里透出的好本事藏都藏不住——那模样，够叫随便哪个牛仔看漏好几拍心跳。

可在在马群眼里，烟儿的这些本事、这些好品相，全都白搭，没有谁放在心上。他就算只是匹平平常常的马，甚至是匹架子歪斜的劣马，他马妈妈也好、其余的谁也好，照样只把他看得那么重。要是他不这么难缠、不用费这么多口舌去开导，大家伙儿本可以更喜欢他些——因为烟儿的个头连同脾气都长到了这么个份上，有些马再想调教他、再想*露一手*自己还压得住他，实在是难了。

===Original===
His eddication kept on though for there was still a few that packed a convincing hoof, but them few was dwindling down fast and Smoky was steady getting where he could hold his own with most any of ’em, till finally, and after many showdowns, there came a time when there was only two left in the bunch that he wouldn’t stop and argue with, them two was his mammy and the big buckskin.

Smoky felt some superior and mighty proud then for a while; and it’s a good thing he was a little wiser and quieter and not so full of mischief no more or he’d sure dealt them ponies misery; as it was he was now willing to leave them alone if they’d do the same by him.

===Chinese===
不过调教并没有停，因为群里还剩那么几匹，蹄子上带着说服人的分量；只是那几匹也快折损光了。烟儿稳稳地长到跟群里几乎随便哪匹都能打个平手的境地；到最后，经过许多回摊牌，有那么一天，群里只剩下两匹，他不会再停下来跟他们较劲的了——那两匹，一匹是他马妈妈，一匹是那匹大兔褐马。

有那么一阵子，烟儿自觉高马一等，得意得很。好在他这会儿也多了几分明白、安静了些，不像先前那样满肚子坏水，不然那些马可真要有罪受；眼下呢，只要他们不来惹他，他也乐得两不相扰。

===Original===
Things went on that way for some time and as the days went by, the bunch was getting to be more willing to accept Smoky as a full size range horse with brains according. None tried to eddicate him no more, and if once in a while he showed young blood and some foolishness, they was all careful to overlook it. Of course Smoky was wise enough to keep away from his mammy and the buckskin at them times.

Peace was with the little bunch; all had some understanding and every horse knowed his ground. It was all so peaceful that Smoky felt it and it all begin to wear on him to the point where he felt like tackling the big buckskin, just to start something﻿—then relief came one day and scattered that peaceful monotony from hell to breakfast.

===Chinese===
日子就这么过了一阵。随着一天天过去，马群越来越情愿把烟儿当作一匹成年的牧场马看待，承认他也有了相配的脑子。没有谁再想调教他；偶尔他血气上头、犯点儿糊涂，大家也都小心地只当没瞧见。当然啦，赶上那种时候，烟儿自己也识趣，躲开他马妈妈和那匹兔褐马远远的。

这一小群马安享着太平；彼此都有了些默契，每一匹马都清楚自己的斤两。这份太平太平得过了头，连烟儿都觉出来了，心里渐渐磨得发痒，痒到想去找大兔褐马较量一场，就为挑起点事儿——后来有一天，解闷儿的来了，把那份太平的单调，搅了个底朝天。

===Original===
It all happened as the little bunch, strung out, was heading for water. Smoky’s mammy was in the lead as usual, and she was the first to turn the point of a ridge and find herself to within a few yards of a big black stud. Smoky was close second on the sight, and somehow as he snorted at the long-maned thick-jawed black a hunch came to him that peace had come to a sudden end.

===Chinese===
事情就出在这一小群马排成一溜、正奔水去的当口。烟儿的马妈妈照旧打头，她头一个拐过一道山梁的梁尖，一抬眼，离一匹黑亮的大种公马（stud）只剩几步远了。烟儿第二个望见；也不知怎么，他朝那匹长鬃厚颚的黑马打了个响鼻的工夫，心里就冒出个预感：太平日子，到头了。

===Original===
He stood in his tracks kinda doubtful as to what to do and watched the black cloud of horseflesh, he’d let the stallion make the first false move﻿—Proud as a peacock came the black, mane and tail a waving and stepping high. His little bunch of mares and colts had stayed back at the first sight of the strange ones, and was now watching the proceedings of the meeting.

That meeting impressed the young horses a whole lot, the white of their eyes showed with interest as the stud came up to within a few paces of the new bunch, stopped, and with a powerful neck bowed to a half circle, ears pointed ahead, and eyes a shining, stood and sized up the strangers.

===Chinese===
他原地站着，一时拿不定主意，眼睛盯着那团乌云似的马身子——他打算让种公马先出第一个错招——黑马开过来了，骄傲得跟孔雀似的，鬃毛尾巴一齐翻飞，蹄子抬得老高。他自己那一小群骒马（mares）和马驹，头一眼望见这群生面孔时就留在了后头，这会儿正远远瞧着这场会面怎么往下走。

那场会面给年轻马们留下了极深的印象。种公马走到离这群新面孔只剩几步远的地方站住，脖子粗壮有力，弓成一道半圆，耳朵朝前点着，眼睛炯炯放光，站在那儿把生马们上下打量——他们一个个瞪大了眼白，瞧得入了神。

===Original===
He’d had plenty of experience in meeting strange bunches that way before which all left him kinda cautious, for many a time he’d left quicker than he’d come, and lost some hide to an older stud what was more up to the game of fighting; and he soon learned that it wasn’t a wise idea to ram into a strange bunch and go to appropriating mares without first investigating what kind of a leader that bunch had.

He’d got wised up in many ways thru them meetings, and he learned to be some careful. He’d also learned to handle his hoofs and teeth till there hadn’t been any stud on that range that’d been able to whip him the last three years﻿—he’d evened up scores.

===Chinese===
像这样跟生群打照面，他先前有的是经验，桩桩件件都教他留了心眼——有多少回，他来得慢、走得快，还叫岁数更老、更谙熟打架门道的种公马揭走几块皮；他很快就学明白了：不先摸清一个群的领头的是什么来路，就冒冒失失撞进去收编骒马，可不是聪明主意。

经过这些会面，他在好些方面开了窍，学会了多少稳重些。他也把蹄子和牙齿上的功夫练到了家，近三年来，那片牧场上再没有哪匹种公马赢得了他——新旧账目，他都算清了。

===Original===
Smoky hadn’t moved, and as the stud still kept a standing in one spot with no indication of wanting to start anything, he got restless. Pretty soon it came to his mind that the stallion was leery of starting anything. It was a big mistake, but Smoky’d had no way of knowing better. The big buckskin did know better and if Smoky had noticed, he’d seen him out there on the far side of the bunch, and willing to keep neutral.

A move from the black stud decided Smoky. He’d stepped close to his mammy and nostril to nostril was exchanging sniffs with her when she let out a squeal and struck at him, all of which the stallion didn’t pay any attention to. But right about then Smoky landed on him, or, *at him*, for his striking front feet and bared sharp teeth missed him, missed him just enough to be a clean miss.

===Chinese===
烟儿没动窝。那匹种公马却老在一个地方站着，一点要挑事的意思都瞧不出来，他等得心里发起毛来。没多会儿他就认定：这种公马是怯场，不敢挑事。这个误会可大了，只是烟儿无从知道底细。大兔褐马倒知道底细——烟儿要是留了神，本可以瞧见他远远站在马群另一头，一副袖手旁观、两不相帮的架势。

黑种公马一动，烟儿拿定了主意。他凑到马妈妈身边，鼻孔对着鼻孔正跟她互相嗅着，她忽然尖叫一声，朝他踢了一蹄子——这些种公马全没放在眼里。可就在这节骨眼上，烟儿朝他扑了过去——或者说，*朝他扑了一下*——因为那双扬起的前蹄和龇出的利牙全落了空，偏偏空得干脆利落，一星半点儿也没沾着他。

===Original===
Smoky had never reckoned with the fighting qualities of a stallion, and he couldn’t figger out how it was he’d struck just thin air when he was so sure his enemy had been right there in front of him and within easy reaching distance; and what’s more that puzzled him was that the stallion never offered to show fight when he landed at him so furious. Instead he’d just got out of the way of his rush, kept his ears ahead, and went on sizing up the bunch the same as if nothing had happened. Smoky felt like he hadn’t even been noticed, and the actions of the stud said plainer than words “fool kid.”

A swift kick in the ribs couldn’t of done any better towards putting Smoky down a peg or two, and that simple quick move of the stud’s went a long ways to show what could of happened if he’d been in mind to fight. All that left Smoky kinda uncertain as to how to proceed. He didn’t know whether to go back and try it again or let things rest for a spell till another chance showed up.

===Chinese===
种公马打架的斤两，烟儿从没掂量过。他想不明白：明明拿准了仇人就在眼前、一伸蹄子就够得着的地方，怎么扑过去只扑了个空；更叫他纳闷的是，他扑得那么凶，种公马却压根儿不接招。人家只是闪身让开他这一冲，耳朵照旧朝前点着，继续打量马群，好像什么都没发生过似的。烟儿觉着自己压根儿没被人放在眼里，而种公马那一副做派，比说话还明白：「傻孩子。」

要挫一挫烟儿的锐气，朝他肋上飞快来一蹄子，效果也不过如此；种公马这轻描淡写的一让，够叫人看清：他真要是存心动手，事情能是什么光景。这一来，烟儿倒没了主意，不知往下怎么进行才好。是回去再试一回，还是先搁一搁，等下回机会自己露头——他拿不定。

===Original===
In the meantime the black stud had found out that there was none in that bunch he’d need to watch, and head down to the ground, ears back, he started cutting out the geldings, keeping the mares and fillies to put in with the bunch he already had. That was a harder job than it might sound here, for none of the geldings wanted to be cut out of the bunch they’d been with so long, and even though they went out easy enough they’d turn back as the stud would be cutting out another and would have to be headed off and cut out again and again.

===Chinese===
这当口，黑种公马已经摸清了：这一群里，没有哪匹是他需要提防的。他把脑袋低到地皮，耳朵朝后一背，动手把骟马们从群里往外剔，把骒马和小母马留下，并进他自己已有的那一群里去。这活儿可比听上去难办——骟马们没有一个情愿离开相处了这么久的群；虽说被往外剔的时候走得挺痛快，可等种公马转身去剔下一匹的工夫，他们又拐了回来，只好一回又一回地拦头截住、重新往外剔。

===Original===
Then the big buckskin which had been neutral all this time finally got riled up at being separated from the mares that way and when the stud headed for him he stood his ground. A few seconds more and there was buckskin and black hair a sailing in the air, then hoofs a pounding away which would of kept up with machine-gun fire for speed, only the pounding wasn’t sounding so sharp; it was hitting something solid, and there wasn’t many misses.

Finally out of the dust that was stirred there came a streak of buckskin and right close to it was a streak of black, away from the herd they went, and pretty soon the black stud came back shaking his head the same as to let every horse know that he wasn’t going to stand for no foolishness.

===Chinese===
到这时，一直两不相帮的大兔褐马，到底叫这么着跟骒马们拆开给惹火了；种公马朝他逼过去，他站在原地没退。没几秒钟，兔褐毛和黑毛就在半空里乱飞，跟着蹄子噼里啪啦砸响起来，那频率赶得上一挺机关枪，只是砸出来的动静不那么脆——它砸的是实打实的物件，而且十下里难有一下落空。

末了，从腾起的尘土里蹿出一道兔褐色的影，紧贴着它是一道黑影；两道影子一前一后，离群远去。没多会儿，黑种公马独自回来了，一路甩着脑袋，那样子等于告诉每一匹马：谁要再胡闹，他可不答应。

===Original===
There was one more to be put out of the bunch, he was that mouse colored gelding, Smoky. He’d got in while the stud was chasing away the buckskin, he’d stood alongside his mammy and watched the fight, and there was a light in his eyes that showed he was ready to start another battle if it was necessary, but he sure wasn’t going to be put out, without he was convinced it could be done﻿—he wasn’t built that way.

===Chinese===
群里还剩最后一匹要往外剔的——就是那匹鼠灰色的骟马，烟儿。种公马追打兔褐马那阵子，他乘机归了群，站在马妈妈身旁，从头到尾看完了那场架；他眼睛里燃着一点光，表明必要的话，他随时乐意再开一场。可要把他往外剔，没那么容易——不叫他实实在在信服这事办得到，他决计不走——他生来就不是那副坯子。

===Original===
The stallion spots him there and never went thru no preliminaries nor tried to scare him out with just a look; he dived right into him and Smoky met him halfways. That battle was short and wicked and Smoky managed to land some good hard kicks, kicks that’d knocked the wind out of any ordinary horse and sent ’em a sprawling; but the stallion wasn’t no ordinary horse and them kicks only shook him a little and made him all the madder. He’d fought too many hard battles to let any gelding faze him and besides he was in the habit of winning.

===Chinese===
种公马一眼瞥见他在那儿，也不来什么开场客套，也没打算光凭一个眼色把他吓退；他一头扎进去就开打，烟儿也迎上去半个照面。那一场架，打得又短又狠。烟儿结结实实踢中了几蹄——那几蹄搁在随便哪匹寻常马身上，都得踢得人家闭气趴下；可这匹种公马不是寻常马，那几蹄只晃了他一晃，反倒把他气得更凶。恶仗他打得太多太多了，一匹骟马动摇不了他；再说，赢，早成了他的老习惯。

===Original===
His chance came when Smoky turned to land a couple more hard ones. The stud was broadside to the gelding, and as the hard ones came, he just reared up out of the reach of ’em, made a big lunge to one side and coming down he made a quick grab and fastened his teeth in Smoky’s withers. When Smoky pulled away and the stud’s teeth snapped together there was some of his silky hide between ’em.

Smoky squealed and kicked some more, then he whirled and faced the stud, figgering on doing some damage with teeth and front hoofs. Just about then the stud whirled too and planted his two hind hoofs smack bang into Smoky’s ribs. There was an echo which sounded like a steam engine ramming into a stone wall. That echo was followed with a mighty grunt as Smoky was lifted off his feet and throwed out a ways to a staggering standstill.

===Chinese===
他的机会来了——烟儿转过身，要再补上两记狠的。种公马正侧身对着这匹骟马，眼看狠蹄子到了，他干脆前身立起，躲出那两蹄的够处，朝一边猛地一个大扑，落下来时顺势疾咬一口，牙关扣进了烟儿的耆甲。烟儿往回一拽，种公马上下牙一磕——磕在中间的，是烟儿一片绸子似的皮。

烟儿尖叫着又踢了几蹄，跟着旋过身子，正面对住种公马，打算用牙和前蹄讨回点儿伤。也就在这当口，种公马也一个旋身，两只后蹄不偏不倚，结结实实踹进烟儿的肋。一声闷响，听着像一台蒸汽机车撞上了石墙。闷响过后是重重一声哼唷——烟儿四蹄离地，飞出去一截，踉踉跄跄才算站定。

===Original===
Smoky was in a daze, his vision was dim, and maybe it was all instinct that warned him of the dark cloud that’d turned and was now a tearing down on him. Anyway something made him move in a hurry; all the strength that was left in him was used to make distance away from the black devil which now looked to Smoky like a big centipede, it had so many legs.

His life depended in the speed he could make, and Smoky was running, running like he’d never run before. It seemed like there was no shaking the mad stud, and just when he was on the point of giving in and make a last stand for his life that destroying hunk of horseflesh left him﻿—When Smoky stopped, looked back, and seen the stud hightail it back to the mares, he had no hankering to follow; he was convinced.

===Chinese===
烟儿懵了，眼前发花；那团乌云已经掉了头，正一路撕着地皮朝他压下来——提醒他的，兴许全凭天性。反正有什么东西催着他赶紧挪窝；他把周身剩下的力气全使了出来，只求离那个黑魔王远一点儿——黑魔王这会儿在烟儿眼里，活像条大蜈蚣，腿实在太多了。

他这条命，全押在他能跑出的脚程上了。烟儿在跑，跑得这辈子从没这么跑过。那匹疯种公马像是怎么也甩不脱；眼看他就要认栽、要豁出性命背水一战了，那团毁灭性的大肉团忽然撇下了他——等烟儿收住脚、回过头，瞧见种公马翘着尾巴奔回骒马那边去了，他一点儿跟着去的念头也没有。他服了。

---

===Original===
The next few days that followed was mighty aimless to Smoky, him and the big buckskin had formed a pardnership in that time and the two wandered around like they was lost and didn’t care where they went. They covered a lot of territory, passed up a lot of good grassy hollows and shady places, but they kept a drifting on. They grazed as they drifted and natural like followed up the canyons and crossed over the high passes that’d been the summer range of Smoky’s mammy and the bunch.

===Chinese===
接下来那几天，烟儿过得没个准头。他和那匹大兔褐马在那阵子结成了伴儿，两个像迷了路似的四处漂泊，漂到哪儿算哪儿。他们走了大片的地方，错过了不少草旺的洼地、荫凉的好去处，却只管一路漂下去。他们边漂边吃草，顺着天性沿峡谷往上走，翻过一道道高山口——那些高处，原是烟儿马妈妈和马群的夏牧场。

===Original===
They came acrost other little bunches, but it seemed like in each of ’em there was a wild eyed thick-jawed stud come out ready to kick the daylight out of ’em if any symptoms of them wanting to trail in with the bunch was showed.

In their roaming around they passed other geldings which like themselves had been kicked out of the stud bunches; the meeting with them was just plain “howdedo’s” and each and all passed on and headed their own wandering way.﻿—All would be hunkydory again for the buckskin if he could find another bunch to run with where there was mares and little colts. He had a mighty strong failing for the little fellers and most any bunch would do if there was only a few of them in; but with Smoky, it was his mammy he missed most, his brother, and the other colts he’d growed up with.

===Chinese===
他们也撞见过别的小群，可每一群里，都有一匹瞪圆了眼、厚下颚的种公马迎出来，摆出一副架势：只要他们露出半点想跟着入群的苗头，就把他们连魂儿一块儿踢出去。

一路漂泊，他们也遇到过别的骟马，都是跟他们一样从种公马群里被踢出来的；见了面，不过一声普普通通的「你好你好」，便各走各的漂泊路。——对兔褐马来说，只要能再寻一个有骒马、有小驹子的群一起过日子，一切就又都安妥了。他对小家伙们有一份改不掉的痴心，随便哪个群都行，只要里头有那么几匹小驹子就好；可烟儿这边，最惦记的是他马妈妈、他弟弟，还有一同长大的那些马驹。

===Original===
No other bunch would do as well, and the nicker he’d send echoing acrost canyons and over ridges every once in a while was just for them certain few.

===Chinese===
换哪个群都不顶事；他隔三差五送出一声低嘶，任它荡过峡谷、翻过山梁——那一声，单单是嘶给那么几个的。

===Original===
Smoky’s mammy had no choice when that black stallion came and scattered them out to his liking that way. She was made to join that little bunch of his and she knowed better than try to do different; she knowed she’d only lose some hide in any attempt to get away and that in the long run she’d have to do as he pointed out.

She was wise to the range and the ways of her kind, and even though she was as strong for Smoky as Smoky was for her she didn’t miss him so much as he did her. She felt in a way that he was now big and mighty able to take care of himself, and then there was other youngsters which called for all her attention. But it was different with Smoky; she was his mammy and there was none other that could take her place. He’d growed up at her side and even though other little colts had come she was and always would be the mother he knowed when he was wobblety legged and needed her.

===Chinese===
那匹黑种公马跑来、照他的心意把他们拆散重编的时候，烟儿的马妈妈没有别的路可挑。她被迫并进了他那一个小群，她也明白，犯不上试着另走一条道；她知道，但凡想逃，只得多揭掉几块皮，而且归根结底，还得照他指的道走。

她通晓这片牧场，也通晓自己同类的路数。虽说她对烟儿的心，跟烟儿对她的心一样重，可她没有他惦记她那样惦记他。她多多少少觉得，他如今长大了，尽照看得住自己；再说还有别的小家伙，桩桩件件都少不了她操心。可烟儿那边不一样；她是他的马妈妈，谁也顶不了她的位子。他是在她身边长大的，后来纵然又添了别的马驹，可他四条腿还打晃、正用得着她的那阵子认下的妈妈，是她——往后也永远是。

===Original===
Then one day and as time had wore on in lonesomeness that way, there came a short break in the monotony which helped Smoky forget some. Him and the buckskin had run acrost a little bunch of mares﻿—there was some little colts in the bunch, and a stud, a young stud.

The big buckskin sized up the stud the same as he’d sized all the others he’d met, and as this young feller came up full of pride and confidence to meet the two strangers, the big buckskin found a flaw in him﻿—the flaw was nothing more or less than just *youth*, he showed it in every move he made and every action.﻿—From past experience the buckskin had figgered youth and ignorance to go together, and that’s what made it interesting.

===Chinese===
就这样，日子在孤单里磨着。后来有一天，单调里裂开了一道短短的口子，帮烟儿忘掉了几分愁。他和兔褐马撞上了一小群骒马——群里带着几匹小驹子，还有一匹种公马，一匹年轻的种公马。

大兔褐马把这匹种公马打量了一遍，跟打量他先前后后遇过的所有对手一样。那小年轻满怀着骄傲和自信，迎上来会他们两个生面孔，大兔褐马却在他身上寻出了一个破绽——那破绽不是别的，正是*年轻*二字，他一招一式、一个动静里都带着它。——凭老经验，兔褐马掂量出年轻和无知向来结伴同行，而这，正是有意思的地方。

===Original===
Interesting by the fact that thru youth and ignorance the young stallion wouldn’t maybe be able to compete against the fighting ability of the buckskin. The younger horse hadn’t as yet fought many battles; that the buckskin could feel at a glance of him. He didn’t turn away like he’d done before﻿—as the stallion came on he just stood in his tracks and watched him. Smoky was doing the same.

There’s bowed necks as the three touch nostrils, there’s some squealing and striking and then a kick is planted﻿—the young stud had started things.

===Chinese===
有意思，就在于凭着这份年轻和无知，这匹小种公马多半敌不过兔褐马打架的真功夫。这匹年轻的马还没有打过多少仗；这一点，兔褐马瞅一眼就掂出来了。他没有像从前那样掉头躲开——种公马往这边来，他只管站在原地瞧着。烟儿也是一样。

三个脖子一弓，鼻尖碰了鼻尖；跟着是几声尖叫、几记扬蹄，再跟着，一蹄踹实了——先动手挑事的，是那匹小种公马。

===Original===
Smoky had caught the kick, which left him out a ways. In the meantime the buckskin followed up the lead and went at it from there. It was all a mighty fair exchange from the start, kicks and bites was averaging pretty well on both parties, and for a young horse that chestnut stud was sure doing well. All might of come to a draw and both fighters might of quit about the same time, if it hadn’t been for Smoky.

===Chinese===
那一蹄踹在烟儿身上，把他踹出去一截。这当口，兔褐马顺着这一手接了下去，从这儿正式开了场。打从头儿起，两边你来我往，踢和咬清算得不相上下；一匹年轻的马打到这个份上，那匹栗色种公马（chestnut）实在算打得不赖。若不是烟儿横在里头，这一场多半要打个平手，两位对手也多半要差不多同时收手。

===Original===
Smoky, which had got to be pretty thick with the buckskin, and had been a good pardner of his thru their lonesome roamings, found it mighty natural like wanting to help when trouble came that way; besides he was holding a grudge against the stud for kicking him the way he did, and all them things together kinda had him worked up to mix in.

===Chinese===
烟儿跟兔褐马早就打得火热，孤单漂泊的这些日子又一直是他称职的伙伴；麻烦这么找上门来，他觉得想搭把手再自然不过；再说，那匹种公马先前那么踹他，这笔账他还记着呢。这几样凑到一块儿，就不由得把他架起来，非要掺和进去不可。

===Original===
His chance came as the chestnut whirled to plant a hard one on the buckskin’s ribs. There was only a few feet between Smoky and the stud right then and double action started from there. The stud felt hard hitting hoofs and teeth a getting him from both sides and the punishment he received all at once wouldn’t of been worse if he’d a lit in a stack of wild cats.

===Chinese===
机会来了——栗色的那匹旋过身，要朝兔褐马的肋上钉一记狠的。那工夫，烟儿跟种公马中间只隔着几英尺，两面的战事就从那儿开了头。种公马只觉得又狠又硬的蹄子和牙，从两边一齐招呼上身；一口气吃下的这顿好打，比一头栽进野猫堆里也轻不到哪儿去。

===Original===
It was then that it come to his mind, and sudden, that he should let up on the fighting and start to do some running if he wanted to keep hisself all in one piece. Smoky and the buckskin kept a pounding on him and a helping along on the good hunch till finally it was all made mighty plain. The chestnut picked himself up as best and quick as he could, and made a leap out of reach of the too many wicked hoofs and teeth, and tore up the earth for a change of scenery. The two pardners done their best to escort him on his way.

But as that day came to an end and as the sun passed over and beyond the blue ridges, Smoky and his pardner could see a lone horse outlined against the sky; the chestnut was following. He followed ’em and the bunch they’d chased him out of for three days, and once he started a fight to win back what he’d lost. He just lost more hide and won nothing but another boost out of that territory.﻿—Smoky and the big buckskin had handed him the same medicine another stud had handed them.

===Chinese===
就在那时，他突然开了窍：要想囫囵个儿地保住自己，就别打了，改跑吧。烟儿和兔褐马一个劲儿地捶他、成全他这个好念头，直到把道理敲打得明明白白。栗色马拼尽气力、以最快的速度撑起身子，一跃跳出那太多狠蹄利牙的够处，刨起一路土尘，只为换一片新天地。两位搭档尽了全力，一路「护送」他上路。

可那一天到了头、太阳落过蓝黢黢的山梁那一边的时候，烟儿和他的伙计望得见天幕上勾着一匹孤马的影子——栗色马跟上来了。他跟着他们、跟着那个他们把他撵出来的群，足足跟了三天；有一回他还开了一仗，想把丢了的东西夺回来。结果不过又多赔了几块皮，赢到手的，只有再被轰出那片地界一趟。——烟儿和大兔褐马喂他吃的那服药，正是当初别的种公马喂他们的那一服。

===Original===
The days that followed was mighty peaceful to the big buckskin, and Smoky seemed some contented too, he was gradually getting used to being away from his mother and new young fillies and colts he was running with made it all a heap easier to forget. Then again, the knocks he’d got ever since that day when things had been so peaceful with his mammy, when he just figgered he’d have to start something to bust up the monotony of that peace, all took the mischief out of him. The fight with the black stallion, the lonesome ramblings with the buckskin, and the other fights with the chestnut stud all helped eddicate him and shape him into a full sized, serious thinking gelding. It didn’t take so much to keep him contented no more, and somehow or other he was seeing a heap more in life.

===Chinese===
后来的日子，对大兔褐马来说太平极了，烟儿看上去也安生了些。他渐渐习惯了离开妈妈，加上同群新结识的年轻小母马和马驹，这一切都叫遗忘省了许多事。再说，打从他马妈妈身边那个太平日子起——正是他掂量着非得挑起点事儿、好破一破那份太平的单调的那一天起——他挨过的那些敲打，早把他一身淘气敲了出去。跟黑种公马的那一仗，跟兔褐马孤单的漂泊，还有跟栗色种公马的另外几仗，都一并调教了他，把他塑成了一匹成年的、会正经思量的骟马。如今要叫他知足，用不着那么些花样了；而且不知怎么，他从生活里瞧出的门道，也多出了一堆。

===Original===
That’s the way things stood with Smoky that summer; him and the buckskin ranged high up in the mountains with the little bunch of mares and colts, they all snoozed and grazed thru the days and done the same thru the nights. A little play was brought on once in a while by some of the young colts and Smoky and the buckskin was always the steady victims of them. Them two older horses was colts themselves at them times and the way they’d all nip one another and then sashay around hell bent for election, a human would wonder at the care Smoky and the buckskin was taking so that the colts would feel winners in all they’d start.

===Chinese===
那年夏天，烟儿的日子就是这个光景。他和兔褐马跟着那小群骒马和马驹，把游荡的高处安在了深山里；白天大家打盹、吃草，夜里照样打盹、吃草。隔三差五，哪几匹小驹子要惹出一场玩耍，烟儿和兔褐马永远是那稳稳当当的受害者。赶上那种时候，这两匹老马自己也成了小驹子；瞧他们你轻咬我一口、我轻咬你一口、又一路没命地撒欢儿的那股劲儿，再想想烟儿和兔褐马处处留着分寸、好叫驹子们觉得回回都是他们赢了的那份细心，人真要啧啧称奇。

===Original===
Summer passed, the grass had gradually turned to a yellow brown and the leaves of the aspens begin banking up on the edges of the streams; fall had come, and one day the bunch started a grazing steady lower and lower till a few days later the foothills was reached. It was there that Smoky took the lead and headed for the winter range where his mammy had put him thru that first year. The big buckskin followed till, glancing back over his withers he noticed that the mares and colts had left off and branched out another direction. The buckskin stood in his tracks, watched Smoky line out straight ahead, and then looked back at the mares again. For the time being he wasn’t sure whether to go on with his pardner or turn back to the bunch. It was hard for him to decide, he wanted to go with Smoky and still them little colts sure had a mighty holt on his heartstrings. It was just about as he was doing the hardest figgering when one of them little fellers came out of the bunch a ways and nickered for him. That little nicker decided things for the buckskin, he answered it and loped back to join with the other little fellers and the mares.

===Chinese===
夏天过去，草渐渐转成黄褐色，山杨（aspen）的叶子开始在溪流边上积起一堆堆；秋天来了。有一天，马群开始一路越吃越低，几天之后就到了山麓丘陵。就在那儿，烟儿领了头，直奔他头一年跟着马妈妈去过的那片冬牧场。大兔褐马跟了一程，回头从自己耆甲上掠过一瞥，才发现骒马和马驹们已经住了脚，朝另一个方向岔出去了。兔褐马站在原地，望着烟儿照直往前牵成一条线，又回头望望骒马们。一时间，他拿不准是跟着伙计走下去，还是折回群里去。这抉择对他太难了：他想跟烟儿走，可那些小驹子，实实在在攥着他的心弦呢。正赶上他掰着最难的那笔账的时候，一个小家伙从群里走出来几步，朝他低嘶了一声。就这一声低嘶，替兔褐马定了局——他应了一声，小跑着回去，归了那群小家伙和骒马。

===Original===
Smoky went on straight ahead. Maybe he was thinking strong, thinking that he’d see his mammy again on that winter range. Anyway, it never come to him to look back and see if the bunch was following him, and finally when it did come to him that he was drifting on alone, he stopped and looked around in a sort of vacant stare, his instinct had been controlling him and was taking him back to his home range. But when he found himself alone that way it all left him surprised at first and then doubtful as to what to do. He was mighty attached to that buckskin, the little colts, and the bunch in general.

He looked at the far away hills of his range and he seemed like to think on the subject for quite a spell, then of a sudden his head went up, a loud nicker went out and away in the distance he could hear an answer﻿—the answer had come from his pardner, the buckskin.

===Chinese===
烟儿照直往前走。兴许他正想得殷切，想着在那片冬牧场上，又能见着他马妈妈了。反正他压根儿没想起来回头看看马群跟上没有；直到最后觉出自己是独自一个往前漂，他才站住，带着一种发怔的茫然四下里张望——方才一直是他的天性在掌着舵，把他往家乡的牧场带。可一旦发觉自己这么孤零零一个，他先是一惊，跟着就没了主意。他是那样舍不得那匹兔褐马、那些小驹子，舍不得这一群马。

他望了望自己牧场上远远的山峦，这桩事仿佛琢磨了好一阵；忽然，他的头昂了起来，一声响亮的低嘶送了出去；远处，他听得见一声回应——回应来自他的伙计，那匹兔褐马。

===Original===
Smoky nickered again and loped back to the bunch. He’d come to feel that it didn’t matter so much which range he wintered on, he was a big horse now, and a few ridges to the north or south of that range he was raised in didn’t make much difference.﻿—An old mare had took the lead and from then on Smoky just followed side by side with the buckskin. A little colt nipped him in the flanks, and all was well.

===Chinese===
烟儿又嘶了一声，小跑着回了群。他已经想开了：在哪片牧场过冬，没那么要紧；他如今是匹大马了，老家牧场往北或往南差上几道山梁，也没什么大不了的。——一匹老骒马接了头，打那儿以后，烟儿就跟兔褐马并排走在群里。一匹小驹子在他肋腹上轻轻咬了一口，一切都安好。

## IV / 四

===Original===
The End of a Rope

===Chinese===
一条绳子的尽头

===Original===
Snow layed heavy on the range that winter, grass was hard to get at, and the little bunch of ponies that tracked the low hills which raised up on the prairies was finding themselves doing a lot of rustling and pawing, and getting very little feed. Bunches of cattle followed ’em wherever they went and rooted with their nose for the few blades of dry grass them horses had pawed the snow off of and left.

Hay couldn’t be bought that winter and the stockmen found themselves where they had to take a chance and pull their cattle thru with whatever little hay the dry summer before could let ’em have. Cattle had been in fine shape that fall, but as the snow kept a piling and a drifting and covering up the feed the tallow kept a dwindling away from under the critters’ hides and lean ribs begin to show more and more thru the long winter hair.

===Chinese===
那年冬天，大雪沉甸甸地压在牧场上，草难得够得着；那一小群在草原上拔起的矮丘间走动的小马，发觉自己整日里奔波刨雪，到嘴的草料却少得可怜。成群的牛跟定了他们，他们走到哪儿就跟到哪儿，拿鼻子拱着地，抢食马儿们刨开积雪、剩下没吃尽的几缕干草。

那年冬天干草买不着，牧场主们只得碰碰运气，靠前一个旱夏能留给他们的那一点点干草，硬拽着牛群往前熬。那年入秋时牛的膘情还挺好，可雪越下越厚、越积越深，把草料一层层盖住，牲口皮底下的板油便一天天消减下去，瘦棱棱的肋骨从长长的冬毛底下越透越分明。

===Original===
Then came a time as the blizzards blowed and regardless of what all the stockmen done (which was to the limit of what any human can do) when mounds of white begin to show here and there in that part of the range. Underneath them white mounds was the dead carcass of a critter. Some was dug up by the varmints, cayotes was licking their chops; and to make things worse, there appeared three big grey wolves on the skyline one day.

===Chinese===
后来到了刮暴风雪的时候，任凭牧场主们怎么折腾（凡人力所能及的，他们全都使到了），牧场上还是东一处西一处地鼓起了白色的雪堆。那些白堆子底下，是牲口倒毙的尸首。有的叫野东西刨开了，郊狼舔着嘴巴直咂滋味；更糟的是，有一天，天边上冒出了三条大灰狼。

===Original===
Smoky and the big buckskin horse was the first to see the wolves. Their ears was towards ’em as the three outlaws of the range trotted along and then stopped to look at the horses.

Smoky had never seen a wolf before, but the big old buckskin had seen too many of ’em and had scars to show for his meetings with the kind. He let a loud snort at the sight of the three grey shapes and from that Smoky got a hunch that these was more to be reckoned with than the cayotes he’d chased when he was a yearling. He had a hankering to go and give them a chase too, but the nervous way the buckskin was acting kinda warned him that it’d be best for him to stick with the bunch.

===Chinese===
烟儿和那匹大兔褐马头一个望见了狼。这三条牧场上的亡命徒一路小跑、又停下打量马群的时候，两匹马的耳朵一直朝那边支棱着。

烟儿从前没见过狼，可那匹老兔褐马见得太多了，身上还带着跟这一路货打交道的疤。一望见那三条灰影，老马就打了个响亮的响鼻；烟儿由此觉出，这几个家伙，比他一岁驹时候追着玩的郊狼难对付得多。他心里也痒痒，想过去撵他们一场，可兔褐马那副紧张劲儿多少是在提醒他：最好还是紧跟着大伙儿。

===Original===
The weak and dying cattle is what had really drawed the wolves, of course they would just as soon tackle a strong animal as a weak one but the scent that scattered over the range from the dead stock and which would reach no less sensitive a nose than theirs was a lot to their liking, and they’d just drifted in to investigate.

It was below them to touch any of the carcasses they’d passed, for these was old wolves well up to the game of killing, and nothing but fresh meat would do. A good fat yearling or two year old colt is what came highest and most to their tastes, and when they skirted that ridge and spotted that little bunch of ponies in the draw below, it was the sight of them that reminded their appetites how long ago it was since they’d et last, and they’d traveled a long ways.

===Chinese===
真正把狼招来的，是那些衰弱将死的牛。当然，壮口弱口他们都照样下得去嘴，可死牲口的气味散遍了牧场，一路飘进他们那再灵不过的鼻子，正中下怀——他们便是闻着味儿漂过来，探看一番的。

他们一路经过的那些尸首，自己是瞧不上口的——这几条是杀生的老手，非鲜肉不要。一身好膘的一岁驹、两岁驹，才最合他们的胃口，顶招他们惦记；等他们绕着山脊兜过去，望见下面洼地里那一小群马，正是这一眼，提醒了他们的肚子：打上回吃饱算起，已经过去多久了，何况他们又跑了老远的路。

===Original===
But it was still daylight, and according to their natural way of doing things they’d wait till night come before making the kill. They skirted on and out of sight of the horses, nosed the snow and the air to make sure that the coast was clear, and after another look at the country so they’d know it when they returned, the wolves trotted on. They showed what old timers they was as they circled well away from a carcass for fear of a trap. They’d had their toes pinched in the steel jaws, scars showed where bullets had grazed ’em, and one was still packing a piece of lead which a cowboy had fired at him from a long shot with a 30﻿–﻿30.

===Chinese===
可这会儿还是大白天，照他们天生的路数，得等天黑下来才动杀机。他们接着绕行，走出了马的视线，嗅嗅积雪又嗅嗅空气，确认四下里没人没险，再把这片乡野重新打量一遍，好记熟了，回来时认得路，然后才一路小跑着去了。他们远远兜开一具尸首，生怕底下埋着夹子——单看这份小心，就知道都是老江湖。他们的脚趾头在钢夹子里吃过苦头，身上留着子弹擦过的疤，还有一条至今揣着一粒铅弹——那是一个牛仔老远用一杆 30–30 步枪射在他身上的。

===Original===
The big buckskin back there in the draw knowed their way, and it showed in his action. He’d quit pawing for grass and instead put all his attention to the tops of the ridges that was all around him and the bunch. The way them three wolves had sized up the bunch and then disappeared had made him restless and mighty spooky; and finally that draw got to be too much of a hole for him, too good a place for an enemy to come into without being seen till that enemy was too close.

The older mares showed a lot of spookiness too, which all got Smoky riled up so that he begin acting the same; and when the buckskin took the lead out of the draw to where a good look of the country around could be got, the whole bunch was mighty anxious to follow; even the little colts seemed to have the hunch that something was up; the white of their eyes showed and they stuck mighty close to their mammies’ side.

===Chinese===
洼地里那匹大兔褐马识得他们的路数，这从他的一举一动里都看得出来。他不再刨雪找草，把全副心思都放到四下围着他和马群的那一圈山脊顶上。那三条狼把马群上下打量一番、随即消失无踪的做派，弄得他心神不宁、疑神疑鬼；到后来，他只觉得这洼地实在太像个坑——敌人打这儿摸进来，等你望见时已经逼得太近——实在太是个埋伏的好地方了。

几匹老骒马也满眼露着惊惶，这又把烟儿撩得烦躁起来，跟着一个模样；等兔褐马领头走出洼地、奔向四野望得真切的高处时，全群都急巴巴地跟了上去；连小驹子们像是也觉出了要出事，眼白都翻了出来，死死贴在自家马妈妈身侧不肯挪开。

---

===Original===
A big moon came up and the light of it reflected a path that shined on the crusted snow. The air was mighty still, still with the cold that’d gripped the range and made everything that lived and carried hoofs come to a stand, so that no air would be stirred; a breeze at that temperature would of froze stiff every standing animal in that territory.

Smoky, the buckskin, and the bunch stood on a knoll where they could see well around ’em. They looked like petrified or froze there so still they all stood; there was no sign of life from ’em excepting for an ear that moved once in a while and which was on the job to catch any sound that might come from near or far.

===Chinese===
一轮大月亮升了起来，月光映出一条道，亮堂堂地铺在结了壳的雪上。空气静极了——那是攥住了整个牧场的严寒逼出来的静，逼得一切有性命、长蹄子的都停驻下来，一丝风也不搅动；那样的温度里，哪怕起一阵微风，也能把这片地界上站着的牲口个个冻成硬邦邦的。

烟儿、兔褐马和马群立在一座小圆丘上，四下里都望得见。他们站得那么定，像是叫法术钉住了、冻住了；全身上下见不着一点活气，只有一只耳朵偶尔转一转——那只耳朵一直在当着班，捕捉远近任何一点可能的动静。

===Original===
The “yip, yip,” and howl of a cayote was heard, another answered, and pretty soon them two filled the air with their serenading﻿—. The echo of that hadn’t quite died down when the long, drawed out, and mournful howl of a wolf made that of the cayotes seem like a joke. The little bunch of horses on the knoll hadn’t blinked an eye while the cayotes was serenading, but at the sound of what followed, every head in the bunch went up, every ear pointed towards the sound, and the buckskin with a few others snorted.

Restlessness had got in the bunch. Smoky started out a ways and came back; then pretty soon, and keeping as close together as they could, they all begin moving. They moved on like shadows, and like more shadows three grey shapes had took up their trail.

===Chinese===
郊狼「叽叽、叽叽」的叫声和嚎声传了过来，另一只应了一声，不多时，这两个就把满天的空气唱满了。那回声还没落尽，一声又长又慢、悲悲切切的狼嚎起来了，把郊狼的小夜曲衬得像个玩笑。郊狼唱的时候，圆丘上那一小群马眼皮都没眨一下；可后头这一声起处，满群的脑袋一齐扬了起来，满群的耳朵齐齐指向声源，兔褐马带着另外几匹打响了鼻。

不安钻进了马群。烟儿走出去一截，又折了回来；不多时，大家尽量挨得紧紧的，一齐挪动起来。他们像影子一样往前移，而在这些影子后头，又有三条灰影，像更多的影子，缀上了他们的踪迹。

===Original===
The big buckskin had stayed in the rear of the bunch and he was first to notice the wolves. A loud whistling snort was heard from him as he landed in the middle of the bunch and kettled ’em into a stampede and the run for their lives. The cold air was split forty ways and crusted hunks of snow was sent a flying as the ponies all wild eyed broke their way thru the drifts at the edge of a ridge and run on towards the big flat.

Smoky had stampeded with the rest and kept pretty well up in the lead thru the run, but now that his blood was warming up in plowing thru the deep snow, and being that that blood was circulating more free up his neck and into his brain, it all put somewhat of a different light on the subject. That brain of his was all het up, on hair trigger with the waking up the run was giving it, and pretty soon something hatched up in there that made Smoky slow down till the bunch went past and ahead of him.﻿—He was wanting to see what was all fired dangerous about them wolves so as to make the bunch run that way.

===Chinese===
大兔褐马一直殿在群后，头一个察觉了狼。一声尖啸似的响鼻从他那儿炸开——他一头扎进马群当中，把大伙儿兜拢成一场狂奔、一场逃命的大跑。冷风叫他们劈成了四十道，结壳的雪块四面八方地飞，瞪圆了眼的小马们撞开山脊边的雪堆，朝那片大平地奔去。

烟儿跟着大伙儿一路狂奔，全程都稳稳跑在很靠前的位置；可这一路深雪里蹬踏下来，血渐渐跑热了，那血又顺着脖子涌得更欢、灌进脑子，这桩事在他眼里便换了个光景。他那颗脑子整个热了起来，奔跑把它唤醒，它就架上了一触即发的弦；不多时，里头孵出个念头，让烟儿放慢下来，直到马群从他身旁过去、把他甩在前头。——他要弄明白，那几条狼到底有什么了不得的凶险，值得让大伙儿这么个跑法。

===Original===
The big buckskin was the last to pass Smoky. He was busy keeping two little colts just a few months old from lagging behind too far, bucking the deep snow at the speed the bunch was making was beginning to show on ’em and it was taking a lot of persuading from the big horse to keep them little fellers on the move.

The wolves was steady catching up with the bunch and the attack would of took place some sooner if it hadn’t been for Smoky. His lagging behind had fooled the wolves into thinking that the mouse colored gelding had quit and was ready to make his last stand. It had been Smoky’s intention to wait for the killers and paw the daylight out of ’em, but as the three rushed in on him he figgered it a good idea to postpone the pawing for a while and do a little running till he was some acquainted with their ways and tricks.

===Chinese===
大兔褐马最后一个打烟儿身旁过。他正忙着照应两匹才几个月大的小驹子，别叫他们落得太远——照全群那个脚程顶着深雪跑，已经开始在这两个小家伙身上显出劲儿来了，全靠老马好说歹说，才催得这两个小家伙儿不停蹄。

狼一步步追上了马群，要不是烟儿，这场扑杀本来还会来得更早。他落在后头，倒把狼懵住了，只当这匹鼠灰色骟马认了命，要作最后一搏了。烟儿原本的打算，正是候着这几个杀才，拿蹄子把他们眼里的天光都刨出来；可三条狼朝他一拥而上，他又觉得不妨先缓一缓这顿刨，先跑上一小程，等摸熟几分他们的路数和伎俩再说。

===Original===
Head and tail up and fire in his eyes he lined out and *led the wolves away from the bunch*. They’d figgered on making him their victim on account he was the handiest, but as the chase kept up they found the gelding had a powerful lot of speed left in him. In the meantime Smoky had somehow lost all hankering of stopping and fight it out with ’em. There was something about the three hungry looking crethures that kept him a moving, and his instinct was warning him strong that he should keep some safe distance between him and them.

He was doing that the best he could and as the running kept up and the wolves couldn’t get any closer they finally figgered they was wasting their time. May be he got to looking too old and tough for ’em and calculated they’d rather have younger and more tender meat; besides he was leading ’em straight away from the bunch which might make ’em lose their chances of getting anything at all.

===Chinese===
他扬着头、翘着尾，眼里蹿着火苗，撒开四蹄跑成一条线，*把狼从马群身边远远引开*。狼原盘算着拿他当头一顿，谁叫他离得最近、最顺手；可这一追下去，才发现这匹骟马肚里还存着好大一注快脚。这其间，烟儿也不知怎的，把停下来跟他们拼个你死我活的瘾头全丢了。这三个一脸饿相的怪家伙身上有种东西，逼得他只顾往前跑；直觉在高声警告他：得在自己跟他们之间，留出一段稳妥的距离。

这他尽力办到了；跑着跑着，狼再没能靠近半分，末了他们认定这是白费工夫。也许是嫌他瞧着太老、太筋道，他们盘算着还是年轻细嫩的肉更可口；再说，他正领着他们笔直地离开马群，再跟下去，怕是连一口都别想捞着了。

===Original===
Smoky’s play of leading the wolves off that way had been a great relief to the bunch and mostly the young colts; they’d had a chance to slow down some and get their second wind, and when the killers showed up on their trail once again they was all more able to sashay on and keep from reach of their tearing fangs.

When Smoky found that the wolves had left him and turned back towards the bunch, it was his natural instinct to turn too and follow up in their tracks. He had a hunch somehow that he’d be needed there and he hadn’t altogether lost the hope of a chance of taking apart at least one of the outlaws.

===Chinese===
烟儿这么把狼引开的招数，对马群是天大的解脱，对小驹子们尤其如此；他们得了空缓一缓、匀出第二口气来，等那几个杀才重新缀上踪迹时，个个都更有力气接着赶路，不掉进那撕咬的獠牙够得着的地方去了。

烟儿发觉狼撇下了他、掉头朝马群去了，天性立时叫他转过身，顺着他们的踪迹追了上去。他不知怎的预感到那边用得着他；再说，至少把那帮亡命徒里的一个拆散的机会，他也还没全然死心。

===Original===
It was a long and mighty hard run back till he caught up with the bunch again, but Smoky wasn’t the horse he was for nothing. He made it in near as good a time as the wolves themselves, and he got there just as the wolves circled around past the buckskin and headed for one of the colts he’d been hazing.

The buckskin hadn’t hardly been noticed; the wolves had passed him up as too old, specially when there was such as the young colts which could be got easy. The old horse had watched ’em catch up with the bunch and go past him for a younger victim. He had no way to know that they didn’t want him, and he could of kept well in the lead of the bunch if he’d wanted to, but he’d made hisself guardian over the little colts and he couldn’t for the life of him have left ’em behind. Of course the little fellers’ mammies would of fought for ’em too but they was at the stage where they felt every horse was for himself; they’d scared into a stampede and was all a running for their own lives.

===Chinese===
回头再追上马群，是又长又苦的一程，可烟儿这匹马不是白当的。他几乎赶出了狼自己的脚程，赶到时，正撞上狼群兜着圈子掠过兔褐马，直奔他先前照应着的一匹小驹子去。

兔褐马几乎没被放在眼里；狼把他略了过去，嫌他太老——何况还有小驹子这样现成好抓的在。老马眼睁睁看着他们追上马群、越过他扑向更年轻的牺牲品。他无从知道人家根本不要他；他要是想，本可以稳稳跑在群前头，可他既已自任了小驹子们的守护人，说什么也不能把他们撂在后头。当然，小家伙们的马妈妈也会为他们拼命，可她们正处在那个觉得马各顾各命的节骨眼上；炸了群，个个都在逃自己的命。

===Original===
The old buckskin knowed wolves; he knowed they had their eye on him and it was best to keep neutral till they’d got over being watched of every move he’d make; and as the three greys passed him and was gaining on the scared little colts he kept to one side and watched. It was just as the leader made a leap for one of the little fellers’ ham strings that the big buckskin came to life, made a leap too, and went to fighting at the risk of his own life.

The wolves hadn’t looked for no such move from him. They’d got over watching and figgered he was far behind and had put all their attention on dragging down the victim they’d picked. It was a mighty big surprise for them when from behind the big buckskin landed on the second wolf and buried him in the snow while on his way to the first. A good sized hoof came down just as that first wolf turned his head to meet the fighting buckskin. That hoof connected with his lower jaw as he made the turn and left that jaw hanging limp and plum useless.﻿—When the old pony looked back for the other wolves there was long grey hairs sticking between his teeth.

===Chinese===
老兔褐马懂狼；他知道他们把眼睛盯在他身上，最好的法子是不动声色，等他们把监视他一举一动的那份心思松懈下去；三条灰狼打他身旁掠过、朝吓破了胆的小驹子逼近时，他偏在一旁瞧着。就在头狼腾身扑向一个小家伙后腿筋的当口，大兔褐马霍然活了过来，也腾身一跃，豁出性命跟他们干了起来。

狼万没想到他会来这一手。他们早收了监视的心，只道他远远落在后头，把全副精神都放在扑倒挑中的猎物上。背后大兔褐马凌空落下，半路里把第二条狼踩进了雪窝——这给他们的是老大一个意外。头狼刚扭头迎战，一只大号的蹄子当头砸到，正正磕在他的下颌上，磕得那下巴松垮垮地耷拉着，全然没了用处。——等老马回头再寻别的狼，牙缝里还夹着几撮长长的灰毛。

===Original===
It was about then when Smoky arrived on the scene. He’d come up right behind the buckskin and when the second wolf picked himself up out of the snow and made a grab which would of been the death of the old horse, Smoky done a side swipe that was quicker than chained lightning. A hind hoof came up and caught that wolf right under a front leg close to the body and took that leg off of him like it’d been a tooth pick;﻿—another horse that’d come up from behind and hadn’t been reckoned with.

It was during this commotion of biting and kicking mixture of buckskin and mouse colored horseflesh and flying grey wolves that the third and only able wolf disappeared into thin air. Them two fighting ponies had took away all his appetite for colt meat and left a hankering only to be gone from the reach of their destroying hoofs. Three of his kind could of competed with the mad ponies if their attention had been on them from the start, but that’s where the slip had been made, and as it was that lone wolf didn’t feel at all equal of resuming what the leader of the pack had started.﻿—He left.

===Chinese===
也就在这当口，烟儿赶到了。他从兔褐马背后逼了上来；第二条狼从雪里挣起身，一口咬向老马——那一口原是要了老马命的——烟儿横里一记侧踢，快过连了环的闪电。一只后蹄扬起，不偏不倚正落在那条狼一条前腿的腋根底下，把那条腿卸了下来，利索得像掰断一根牙签；——又是一匹打背后杀到、没被算在账上的马。

就在兔褐色和鼠灰色的马肉、又咬又踢的混战、四下乱飞的灰狼搅作一团的乱劲里，第三条——也是唯一一条还齐全能战的——狼凭空没了影。这两匹杀性正浓的小马，把他对小驹子肉的胃口全打没了，只给他留下一股念想：快逃出那毁灭性的铁蹄之外。若是打一开头他们三个就把心思放在这两匹疯马身上，凭同类的数目原可以跟他们较量较量；坏就坏在这一步失了算。事到如今，这孤零零的一条狼，自觉说什么也接不下头狼挑起的那一摊了。——他走了。

---

===Original===
The moon faded away into the sky; break of day had come. Out on the flat the little bunch of ponies was knee deep in the snow and a pawing away for the grass that was underneath, there wasn’t a scratch on nary a hide to show that any had ever seen a wolf; but if Smoky and his pardner the buckskin hadn’t been in that little bunch there would of been another story to tell. The little colt which was so busy digging up feed for himself, and plum ignorant of the close call he’d had, would of been amongst the missing and just easing the appetites of three grey wolves; and who knows but what a couple more colts might of been killed along with him, for once a wolf gets a taste of warm blood there’s no telling how far he’ll go.

===Chinese===
月亮淡进了天里；破晓来了。大平地上，那一小群马立在深及膝盖的雪里，刨着底下的草，哪一张皮上也没有一道抓痕，能表明他们见过什么狼；可要是那一小群里没有烟儿和他伙计兔褐马，要讲的可就是另一回事了。那匹正忙着给自己刨草吃、对擦身而过的鬼门关全然不知的小驹子，早该上了失踪的名册，正好给三条灰狼垫垫饥；谁说得准呢，兴许还有一两匹驹子陪他一道送命——狼一旦尝过了热血，他还能走多远，没谁说得准。

===Original===
The “yip, yip” and howl of a cayote sounded off from the hills, and gradual as the sun came up big clouds showed over the skyline from the northwest and seemed like headed to meet and kill that sun’s warm rays. By noon that day a blizzard had come and the little bunch of ponies faced it on the way back to the shelter of the hills from where they’d left in their run for life.

The howl of a lone wolf was heard that night, and away off to the south there came an answer, an answer that was more drawed out and mournful than any that’d ever been heard. Smoky snorted, but with the buckskin, only his head went up, his ears pointed towards the sound. He knowed wolves and he knowed they wouldn’t be back, not that night.

===Chinese===
郊狼「叽叽、叽叽」的叫声和嚎声又从山里响起来。太阳渐渐升高，西北边天际涌出大团大团的云，瞧那架势，是要迎上去掐灭太阳那几缕暖和的光。到那天晌午，暴风雪来了，那一小群马顶着它，往山坳的藏身处走——他们逃命时正是打那儿跑出来的。

那天夜里传来一声孤狼的嚎，老远的南边起了一声回应——那回应拖得比谁听过的都长、都哀。烟儿打了个响鼻；兔褐马呢，只把头扬了扬，耳朵指向声源。他懂狼，他知道他们不会回来了，至少那晚不会。

===Original===
The blizzard hung on for a day and filled the ravines with deep drifts; then the wind died and it settled down to a slow falling snow. There was more white mounds where that snow had covered the carcasses of dead critters, but amongst them mounds there was one that wasn’t made by any of the bellering grass eating kind.﻿—A big gray wolf layed there, a broke jaw had been the cause of his death.

(Some months later a cowboy run down and roped a three legged wolf and remarked as he looked close to where a front leg was missing, how “it must of been an awful wicked bullet to’ve took that leg off so neat.”)

===Chinese===
暴风雪缠了一整天，把沟沟壑壑填满了深深的积雪；随后风息了，转为一场慢悠悠飘落的雪。那场雪盖住了一批倒毙牲口的尸首，白堆子又添了几座；可那些堆子当中，有一座不是出自哪只会叫唤的食草牲口。——一条大灰狼躺在那儿；要了他命的，是一只断裂的下颌。

（几个月后，一个牛仔追上一条三条腿的狼，用绳套住了，凑近端详那条前腿没了的地方，感叹说：「能把一条腿卸得这么干净利落，那颗子弹准是个邪性透顶的家伙。」）

===Original===
The already long winter dragged and hung on like it never was going to quit, snow was deep, and even though the sun climbed higher and stayed longer there seemed to be no more heat from it than there’d been two months before and when it was at its lowest. The ponies was having a hard time and as the feed kept a getting to be harder to reach right along they was steady losing on weight and strength. The roundness that’d been theirs a few months before was all gone and instead they showed lean and slab sided.

===Chinese===
这本已够长的冬天还拖着赖着，像永远不打算收场；雪深得很，太阳虽说爬得高了、待得久了，可它那点热乎气，竟不比两个月前、它最低的时候多出一丝。小马们的日子难熬，草料一天比一天更难够着，他们的膘和力气也就一路往下掉。几个月前还浑圆滚壮的身子，如今那股圆润全没了，只剩精瘦瘪塌的肋腔。

---

===Original===
Finally, and after it seemed there’d be no end to the rough weather, there came a break; it turned warmer, and some time later the snow begin to sag and then melt on the sunny side of the hills, gradually, and after what seemed weeks instead of days the grass showed in plain sight more and more till the time come when the ponies didn’t have to paw for their feed no more. Then after a while there was green stems showing thru the dry grass.﻿—The dangers of the winter was over.

The range had turned from white to brown and then green and the little bunch of ponies begin to perk up considerable. The winter hair was a slipping, their eyes showed more bright, and pretty soon ribs begin to disappear under layers of fat and glossy hides. Then to make this new green world as great and wonderful as the winter before had been hard and cruel, there begin to appear brand new little colts in the bunch, all slick little fellers and full of play. And as the bunch drifted to the open prairie they came acrost little calves, their little white faces a shining in the sun.

===Chinese===
末了，在坏天气像是永无尽头的时候，终于裂开一道口子；天转暖了，过了些日子，向阳坡上的雪开始塌陷、消融，慢慢地，草越露越多、望得真真切切；约莫熬过了几个星期而非几天，便到了小马们再不用刨雪觅食的时候。又过一阵，干草底下冒出了青绿的草茎。——冬天的凶险过去了。

牧场从白变褐、又从褐变绿，那一小群马也肉眼可见地精神起来。冬毛一片片脱着，眼睛越来越亮，不多时，肋骨便渐渐隐没在一层层膘肉和油光水滑的皮毛底下。跟着，仿佛是要叫这个崭新的绿色世界，同前一个又硬又狠的冬天一样了不起、一样叫人称奇似的，马群里开始添了一匹匹崭新的小驹子，个个皮光水滑、浑身是玩闹的劲头。马群漂向开阔的草原时，迎面遇上了一群群小牛犊，一张张小白脸在太阳底下闪亮。

===Original===
Smoky had more than kept with all the changes to the good; he showed it in every move he made, and as him and the old buckskin (which had got young again) played around and showed jealous over the new colts, it made a sight that was complete in all that life could give.

There was months of peace that way; the little bunch roamed the prairies not at all seeming to care where sunup found ’em. Tall green feed was a plenty and everywheres, clear swift mountain streams slowed down on the flats and furnished moisture for the big cottonwood that reached out in the sky and made cool shade, and as it was, time was just let slip by and enjoyed only as a free range horse with little colts for company can enjoy it.

===Chinese===
烟儿何止跟上了这些向好的变化；他的每一步都把这好处亮了出来。他和老兔褐马（这老伙计又还了童）在旁边闹腾，对着新来的小驹子还争风吃醋——那景象，把生命所能给的圆满，凑了个齐齐整整。

就这样过了几个月太平日子；那一小群马在草原上漫游，日头出来时在哪儿撞见他们，全不当回事。高高的青草又多又遍野，清冽湍急的山溪到了平地上缓下来，滋养着那些把枝桠伸进天里、搭出一方荫凉的大三角叶杨。日子就这么滑过去，那份受用，只有带着小驹子们作伴的、自由自在的牧场马，才受得起。

===Original===
It was more thru habit than heat that the little bunch drifted on up the foothills one day and then higher in the mountain. May be they liked the breeze up there better, or the change of feed, or maybe it was that too many riders had been showing up off and on and which kinda disturbed ’em.

But them riders couldn’t be dodged that easy, and one day for a whole half hour there was one within a half a mile of ’em a setting on his horse, field glasses in his hands and looking at the little bunch as they fanned themselves on a high ridge plum ignorant of the eyes that was on ’em.

===Chinese===
有一天，这一小群马又往山麓上漂，再往山里更高处去——这多半是习性使然，不为避暑。也许是他们更喜欢上面的风，也许是想换换草口，又也许是骑马人来来去去出没得太勤，叫他们有些不安生。

可那些骑马人不是那么容易躲开的。有一天，整整半个钟头，有一个就待在离他们不到半英里的地方，坐在马背上，手里端着望远镜，望着这一小群马——他们正在一道高高的山脊上给自己扇着风，对盯在他们身上的那双眼睛浑然不觉。

===Original===
That rider had spotted the mouse colored blaze-faced gelding, and at the sight of him let out a whistle of surprise of seeing such a horse. He’d rode a little closer then and watched that horse some more. He’d of come still closer only he didn’t want to kettle the bunch and make ’em suspicious, besides he’d just wanted to locate where that horse was running so he’d know where to find him when he wanted to.﻿—He was one of the Rocking R men.

Smoky had stood the whole watchful spell without a hunch of it; and as him and the little bunch started a grazing on up the mountain there was nothing further away from his mind than the thought of a human on his trail. Of course there wasn’t any human on his trail that day, but there would be soon, for the way that rider talked that night, and described Smoky to the bronco buster of the outfit, all indicated that it wouldn’t be long when the little horse would be finding himself in a high pole corral.

===Chinese===
那个骑马人相中了这匹白章脸的鼠灰色骟马，一望见他，就惊得吹了声口哨——世上竟有这样的马。他于是又骑近了些，把这匹马多瞧了一阵。他本想再近些，只是不愿兜拢马群、惹他们起疑；再说，他只想摸清这匹马活动的地界，往后要用他时，好知道上哪儿去找。——他是摇椅R牧场的人。

烟儿受了人家这整场窥测，却一丝风声都没觉出来；他和那一小群马边吃边往山上蹭去的时候，心里离得最远的念头，就是有人打上了他的主意。当然，那天还没有人真缀上他的踪，可快了——那骑马人当晚一路上的话，连同他把烟儿向营里那位驯野马手（bronco buster）形容的那一番，都表明要不了多久，这匹小马就会发现自己落进一座高高的木杆畜栏里了。

===Original===
Smoky was now a four year old going on five, the age when most all range geldings are run in and broke to either saddle or harness, for use on the range or to fit ’em for market. The little horse’d had a good long time of freedom and if he was kept with the outfit he’d get more, but his time for usefulness had come. The free roaming of the hills and flats was past for a while till the work he’d be cut out for was done, and Smoky’s experience from his colt days on till now would go on with more learning and experiences with the human.

Smoky’s waking up to realizing them things came sudden and all mighty unexpected. A long legged rider on a long legged horse had showed up on a ridge above him and the bunch; there’d been a lot of territory covered in mighty fast time as all lit into a run and they was hazed down onto the flats and then into long pole wings towards the corral; then first thing Smoky knowed he was penned in, he couldn’t go no further. A big gate was closed and all around him was big cottonwood bars.

===Chinese===
烟儿这会儿四岁、快满五岁了——牧场上的骟马到了这个岁数，多半都要赶进圈里，调教上鞍或者上套，好在牧场上使唤，或是调理妥当送去市场。这匹小马已经过了好长一段自由日子；若是留在营里，往后还有得是，可他派用场的时辰到了。山里平地上的自在漫游，得暂且告一段子，等派给他的活计做完再说；而烟儿从驹子时候直到如今攒下的阅历，也将接着添上新的一课——跟人类打交道的新学问、新历练。

烟儿对这些事儿的醒悟，来得又突然又万万没想到。一个长腿骑手骑着一匹长腿马，出现在他和马群上方的山脊上；一大片地界在飞快的工夫里就被跑遍了——马群炸了窝，被兜撵到平地上，又赶进通向畜栏的两道长长的木杆翅子里；跟着，烟儿还没回过神，就发现自己被圈死了，再也没处可去。大门一关，四下里全是三角叶杨的大栏杆。

===Original===
In another pen joining the one Smoky was in there was other horses, all geldings and along about Smoky’s size and age. The gate between was opened and Smoky was cut out of his bunch by that same long legged rider that’d run him in, and put thru that gate to join them other geldings. The gate was closed again after him.

Smoky peeked thru the bars and watched the rider open the outside gate and leave out the bunch he’d run with. He watched one of the mares take the lead and in a long lope, head back to the high territory from where they’d come, he watched the little colts running to keep up and then he seen the big buckskin tagging along. His pardner and all was leaving him amongst strange horses, in a high corral, and not far away was a human which to Smoky was ten times worse than any wolf.

===Chinese===
紧挨着烟儿这座圈的另一座圈里还有马，清一色的骟马，个头岁数都跟烟儿相仿。当中那道门开了，还是那个把他赶进来的长腿骑手，把他从自家群里剔了出去，赶过那道门，合到那些骟马里头。门在他身后又关上了。

烟儿从栏杆缝里望着那骑手打开外头的门，把他一起跑过来的那群马放了出去。他望见一匹骒马领了头，放开大步小跑，朝他们来时的高地回去；他望见小驹子们一路小跑着紧追；随后，他看见大兔褐马跟在后头。他的伙计，连同整个一群，就这么把他留在一群陌生马里、留在一座高栏圈里，而离不远的地方还有一个人——在烟儿眼里，那比什么狼都凶上十倍。

===Original===
He nickered, and there was a sound to it that made the buckskin stop, look back, and nicker an answer. The old horse stood there a while kinda like he was waiting, but pretty soon he started again and caught up with the bunch. The old buckskin knowed humans, he’d packed many a one of ’em on many a long ride; his freedom had been handed back to him for the good work he’d done. He’d experienced what Smoky was going thru now and knowing what he did it was all plain to him just what was up. There was no use of him waiting.

Smoky watched him and the bunch disappear in a cloud of dust and out of sight. If only there was no bars holding him it wouldn’t take him long and he could still catch up with ’em, but﻿—He was brought back to hard facts by the squeak of the heavy gate as it was pulled open, and the cowboy walked in with a long coil of rope on his arm.

===Chinese===
他低嘶了一声，那嘶声里带着点东西，叫兔褐马停住了脚，回头望了望，低嘶着应了一声。老马在那儿站了一阵，像是在等什么，可不多时他又动了身，赶上了马群。老兔褐马识得人类；多少个人、多少趟长路，他都驮过；他是立了功，自由才被还到手的。烟儿眼下受的这份罪，他从前一一经历过，凭他知道的那一切，眼下是怎么一回事，他心里亮堂得很。等，是没用的。

烟儿望着他和马群消失在一团尘土里，再也望不见。要是没有栏杆拦着，用不了多大工夫，他还能追上他们，可是——沉重的大门吱呀一声拉开，把他拽回到硬邦邦的现实里：一个牛仔走了进来，胳膊上搭着一大盘长绳。

===Original===
Smoky let out a snort at the sight of the human and tore up the earth for the far side of the corral. Natural fear of the crethure had a hold on him and once against the solid bars he turned and quivering faced what he felt was his worst enemy.

If Smoky could only of knowed, there’d been a lot of suffering which he wouldn’t had to’ve went thru on account of that fear; if he’d only knowed that right then that human was just admiring him for all he was worth and that doing the little horse any harm was the furthest thing away from his mind. But the wild gelding had no way of knowing, and every word that human was saying sounded to him like the growl of a flesh tearing animal, and every move was a step closer to the victim;﻿—he was the victim.

===Chinese===
一望见人，烟儿就打了个响鼻，刨着蹄子直窜到畜栏最远的一头。对这怪家伙的天生惧怕攥住了他；一抵到结结实实的栏杆，他便转过身，浑身发着抖，面对他认定的头号死敌。

烟儿要是知道就好了——单为这份害怕，他本可以免掉多少罪受；他要是知道就好了：这会儿那个人正把他里里外外赏鉴个够，而伤害这匹小马的念头，在那人心里远得不能再远。可这匹野骟马无从知道。那人说的每一个字，落在他耳朵里都像撕肉的野兽在低吼；那人的每一步，都是朝牺牲品逼近的一步；——他，就是那牺牲品。

===Original===
The cowboy well understood his kind. He’d been raised on the backs of such as him and he was making his living by gentling that kind and making good saddle horses out of ’em; and as he stood there, his eyes taking in every move the mouse colored gelding was making, there was a smile showed under the stetson. That smile was just for the glad way he felt as he sized him up and seen where he was all saddle horse, not the kind that’d fit in harness and to be shipped to farming countries. He was glad to know that he’d be the first to touch that pony’s hide, and as he kept his eye on the gelding, at the same time shaking out a loop, he felt there’d be no end of patience for such a horse as that one looked to be.

===Chinese===
这牛仔太懂他们这一路了。他就是在这样的马背上摔打大的，如今靠的也是把这一路马驯出个好脾气、调教成好鞍马来谋生。他站在那儿，眼睛把鼠灰色骟马的每一个动作都收了进去，斯泰森（stetson）帽檐底下露出一丝笑。那笑，是打心眼里的痛快：他把这马一打量，看出这是匹十足的鞍马坯子，不是那号上套送农区使唤的料。想到头一个上手摸这马皮毛的将是自己，他更觉着高兴；他眼睛盯着骟马，手上抖开一个绳圈，心里觉得，对这样一匹瞧着如此的马，耐心有的是，没有底。

===Original===
His loop ready he walked towards the gelding. Smoky watched him come and that pony’s actions showed where he just wanted to shrink away to nothing and disappear; but he stayed full size, and seen where his next best move was to just move, and away to most any old place. The other geldings scattered as the human came on, and Smoky piled in amongst ’em full speed to the other side of the corral; about that time he heard the hiss of a rope and that thing which he likened to a snake coiled up and right around both his front feet.

Them front feet was jerked away from under him as he sailed in the air and tried to get away, and then he made a circle in the atmosphere and came down to earth flat on his side. He no more than hit the ground when he tried to get up. He tried it again and again, and as the cowboy talked to him and advised him to ease up on the fighting, Smoky turned a wild eyed look his way and snorted.

===Chinese===
绳圈备好了，他朝骟马走去。烟儿望着他过来，那副做派分明是想把自己缩没了、消失掉才好；可他尺寸照旧，缩不成零，便看出下一手最好的棋还是走——走，随便是哪儿，老远的就行。人一逼近，别的骟马轰地散开，烟儿全速扎进他们堆里，直奔畜栏另一头；约莫就在那当口，他听见绳子破空的嘶声——那条他当作蛇的东西，一圈，正正套上了他的两只前蹄。

前蹄猛地被抽走，他人悬在半空还想挣，跟着在大气里划了半个圈，侧身平平地砸到了地上。蹄子刚沾地他就想爬起来。他试了一遍又一遍；牛仔一面跟他说话，劝他省着点儿劲儿别斗了，烟儿只把一双狂野的眼甩向那边，打了个响鼻。

===Original===
“Now lay down and be good,” says that cowboy. “I sure don’t want to skin up that pretty hide of yours.”

===Chinese===
「好了，趴下，乖乖的，」那牛仔说。「我真不想蹭破你这张漂亮皮。」

===Original===
Smoky did lay down; he had to, for in another few seconds his four feet was tied together. He breathed hard as he layed there plum helpless. His mind wasn’t working no more, his heart was a thumping fit to bust, and the racing of the blood thru his body only stampeded his brain. He was past trying to figger out how he was throwed so easy and then held down where he could move only his head. No cougar or bear could of made him so helpless; he could of fought with them; but with this human it all seemed like he had no chance, and the mystery of that human’s power is what put the fear in him, a fear the likes of which was a heap worse than he’d felt if he’d been cornered by a thousand bears, cougars, and wolves.

In a dazed way he seen the cowboy bend over him; a knee touched his neck and the muscles along there quivered the same as if a snake’s fangs had been feeling for a holt. A hand touched his ear and another his forehead; there was no pain but if there had been the little horse would of never felt it.

===Chinese===
烟儿确实趴下了；他不得不趴——再过几秒钟，他四只蹄子已经给捆作了一堆。他躺在那儿，彻底没了辙，喘得粗重。脑子不再转了，心咚咚地擂，擂得快要炸开；浑身的血横冲直撞，反倒把他脑子里最后一点章法也冲炸了营。他已顾不上去琢磨，自己怎么摔得这么容易、又怎么摁得只剩脑袋能动。美洲狮也好，熊也好，都没能叫他这么束手无策——跟他们他尚且能斗一斗；可在这人手里，他像是全无半点机会，而这人那股力量里的神秘，正是叫他害怕的根由——这份怕，比被一千头熊、美洲狮和狼堵在死角里的那种怕，还要沉得多。

晕晕乎乎里，他看见牛仔朝他俯下身来；一只膝盖抵上他的脖颈，那里的肌肉一阵抽颤，就像蛇的毒牙正探着下口的地方。一只手碰上他的耳朵，另一只手抚上他的额头；没有疼——就算有，这匹小马也决计觉不出来了。

===Original===
Pretty soon a hackamore was slipped on his head, he felt the rawhide “bosal” around his nose, and then the “feador” rope around his neck, all the while the crethure was making a low, and *somehow* not aggravating noise. It was a talking to him.

The cowboy gave his forehead a couple of rubs, then stood up and walked around to the pony’s feet. Smoky felt the tight ropes loosening up from around his ankles and pulled away; his feet was free but his mind was confused a lot and he still layed there; then he felt a pull on the hackamore rope.

===Chinese===
不多时，一副笼头（hackamore）套上了他的头，他觉出生牛皮的「波萨尔」（bosal）扣拢了他的鼻子，跟着是绕上脖子的「费阿多」（feador）绳——这全过程里，那怪家伙一直低低地弄出一种*不知怎的*并不惹他上火的声响。那是人在跟他讲话。

牛仔在他额头上搓揉了两把，站起身，绕到小马的蹄子那边。烟儿觉出踝上勒紧的绳松了开来，便把蹄子抽开；蹄子是自由了，可他脑子里还乱作一团，仍旧躺在那儿；随后，他觉出笼头绳上一紧。

===Original===
“Come on up and stand on your legs,” says that cowboy﻿—and Smoky came to life.

===Chinese===
「来，起来，用你自己的腿站好，」那牛仔说——烟儿一下子活了过来。

===Original===
He came to life a pawing and rearing and a snorting. His feet was free and he could handle ’em again; he did handle ’em and put in all the strength and action he had a trying to pull away from the cowboy which was holding him with a long rope.﻿—There’s some talk of the skill that’s showed between the angler and the trout, but the skill that was brought out from that hundred and fifty pound cowboy a holding that eleven hundred pounds of kinky, wild horseflesh was past talking about, and beyond the figgering out of any human that’s not up to the trade of bronco busting.

===Chinese===
他活过来便刨蹄、直立、打响鼻。蹄子自由了，又归他使唤了；他也真使唤开了，把浑身的力气和本事全填进去，死命要从那牛仔手里挣脱——那人正用一根长绳拽着他。——钓鱼人和鳟鱼之间如何斗智，人们常有得谈；可这一百五十磅的牛仔，手里稳稳牵着一千一百磅倔头倔脑的野马肉所显出的功夫，就远不是谈得尽的了，也远不是没吃过驯野马这碗饭的人所能想得明白的了。

===Original===
The cowboy played his rope and held his horse; he’d held many like him before and most all had fought the same as Smoky was now fighting. That pony’s eyes was afire as he seen there was no chance for any get away even when he was on his feet. He couldn’t at all shake that two-legged hunk of terror, and as he snorted and fought the rope that still held fast around his head and neck, he begin to tire some; and came a time when as the cowboy stood still a few yards away he stood still too, and legs wide apart, sweat a dripping from his slick hide, he took in a breathing spell.

===Chinese===
牛仔放收着绳，稳稳拿住他的马；这样的马他从前拿住过许许多多，几乎个个都像烟儿眼下这样斗过一场。这匹小马眼里烧着火——他看明白了，哪怕站起身，也再无半点逃路。那个两条腿的、活脱一团恐怖的家伙，他怎么也甩不脱；他打着响鼻，跟仍旧牢牢锁在头颈上的绳子搏斗，渐渐有些乏了；到后来，牛仔在几步开外站定，他也站定了，四腿撇开，汗水从油光的皮毛上滴下来，他喘匀了一口气。

===Original===
He stood there as he watched the cowboy back away and let the rope slide thru his hands; he watched him open the gate and get the saddle horse that’d been left to stand on the other side, seen him get on that horse and then pick up the slack of the rope that was holding him. There was thirty feet of it between him and the mounted human, and when that rope was tossed a little, as the rider circled around him, Smoky made a leap and shaking his head like trying to slip what held him, he headed straight on for the open gate.

But once past it Smoky was jerked to a fighting standstill, he hadn’t as yet reckoned that a rope could hold him﻿—. The gate was closed after him and the rider had went thru and then Smoky felt some slack. He took advantage of that and started out full speed again; he was out of the corral and in the open, the rope that was still on him was only felt and wasn’t holding him from lining out.

===Chinese===
他站在那儿，望着牛仔倒退着放绳，绳从那人手掌里滑过去；望着他打开门，牵过原先拴在外头的那匹鞍马，跨上去，又收起牵着他的绳的余头。他和那骑在马上的人之间，隔着三十英尺绳子；骑手绕着他兜圈、绳子轻轻一抛的一瞬，烟儿腾身一跃，甩着脑袋像要甩脱锁着他的东西，直奔敞开的大门冲去。

可刚冲过门，烟儿就被猛地拽定，只剩挣跳的份儿——他还没算到，一根绳子竟也拴得住他。门在他身后关上，骑手穿门而过，跟着烟儿觉出绳上松了些。他抓住这个空子，撒开蹄子又全速跑起来；他出了畜栏，到了开阔地，身上那根绳只是还在、还觉着分量，却拦不住他撒开四蹄了。

===Original===
A shallow creek bottom full of tall green feed was by that corral and Smoky headed down it; any place would do so long as he could run and keep a distance between him and that rider; but that run wasn’t to last long, once again he felt the rope tighten till he was brought to a stop, and facing the rider once more, watched him get off his horse and fasten the end of the long rope to a log.

“Well, little horse,” says the cowboy as he stood there and watched him for a spell, “don’t play too rough with this rope, the better you treat it the better it’ll treat you,” and with that he got on his horse and rode off towards the corrals where more broncs waited for the same eddication that Smoky had just got.

===Chinese===
畜栏旁边有一条浅溪的溪床，长满了高高的青草，烟儿顺着它一头扎了下去；只要能跑、能把跟那骑手的距离拉开，去哪儿都行；可这一跑没能跑多久——绳子又是一紧，把他拽停了；再一转身面对那骑手，他望着人家下了马，把长绳的末端拴在一根圆木上。

「唉，小马儿，」那牛仔站在那儿瞧了他一阵，说道：「跟这绳子别耍得太野；你待它好点儿，它也就待你好点儿。」说完，他上了马，朝那排畜栏驰去——还有好些野马在那儿等着，等烟儿方才领过的同一堂调教。

===Original===
That long soft and thick cotton rope, and that log which held Smoky, was the means of his first learnings as to ways for usefulness to the human. The more he’d fight that rope and try to get away from it the more he’d learn that his fighting and tearing was of *no use*. That rope was on the job steady and to learn him to turn as he run and hit the end of it, it would take the stiffness out of his neck and there’d come a time when he’d give to a pull from either side without fighting and wanting to be convinced that it could be done. The log which the rope was tied to was part of the teaching apparatus, heavy enough to hold the pony, and even though it could be dragged around some Smoky couldn’t get very far with it.

The little horse realized somehow as he sized up the contraption that the end had come to all he’d enjoyed with the freedom he’d had﻿—cool shades, clear streams, and grassy ranges to all roam on as he pleased had been took away from him; he didn’t know what was to come next but he did know that he was on that creek bottom, close to corrals, and there to stay for a spell.

===Chinese===
那根又长又软又粗的棉绳，连同那根拴住烟儿的圆木，就是他头一堂课的教具——教他学怎样对人派用场。他越跟那绳斗、越想挣脱它，就越明白：他的又撕又挣，全是*无用功*。那绳子时时刻刻在岗；要教会他一边跑一边拐弯、一头撞上绳的尽头，好把他脖子里的犟劲儿撞散——总有一天，从哪边拽他，他都会顺着劲儿让过去，不再又斗又闹、非要人证明给他看这办得到。拴绳的那根圆木也是这套教具的一部分，沉得足以拽住小马；虽说多少能叫他拖得挪一挪，可拖着它，烟儿是走不出多远的。

这匹小马把这套家伙什儿打量了一番，不知怎的明白了：他连同他的自由一道享有过的一切，都到头了——荫凉、清溪，还有任他随意漫游的草场，全从他身上收走了；接下来还有什么，他不知道；可他确实知道，他如今在这条溪床上，挨着一片畜栏，而且，要在这儿待上一阵子了。

## V / 五

===Original===
The Bronc Twister Steps Up

===Chinese===
驯野马手挑大梁

===Original===
A cloud of dust was hanging on over the big corrals where Clint, the bronc twister of the Rocking R outfit, was busy starting raw broncs under the saddle and “twisted” ’em in shape for good saddle stock. It was long, hot, and hard days for that cowboy as he wrestled with the slick, fat, and snorty ponies and convinced ’em that they all could be led, rode, and handled according to the way he seen fit; but Clint was used to that; he’d been at it for years with nary a rest or relief from the work that was beginning to tell on him.

===Chinese===
一团烟尘悬在那几座大畜栏上空，迟迟不散——克林特，摇椅R牧场的驯野马手（bronc twister），正在栏里忙着给一匹匹生野马上鞍开驯，「拧」出个规整模样，好正经充作鞍马使唤。那牛仔跟这些油光水滑、膘肥体壮、动辄打响鼻的小马纠缠角力，说服他们一个个都能牵、能骑、能照他看着合适的法子摆布——这样的日子又漫长、又炎热、又熬人；可克林特惯了；他干这一行已经好些年，没歇过一口气，而这份渐渐在他身上显出后果的营生，一刻也没饶过他。

===Original===
He’d take ten broncs at a time and soon as he’d took the rough off them ten he’d turn ’em over as broke and run in ten more raw, wild ponies. Each green colt was rode every day if even only for half an hour, and gradually learned to behave under the saddle. There was a few that wouldn’t learn to behave, but the Rocking R outfit had good men and all them ponies was put in to their work whether they was good or bad.

===Chinese===
他一次接手十匹野马，等这十匹的粗野劲儿一去，便把他们当驯好的交出去，再圈进十匹新的生马。每匹新驹每天都骑一骑，哪怕只骑半个钟头，让他们渐渐学会鞍下守规矩。也有那么几匹怎么也学不会规矩的，可摇椅R牧场有的是好手，不管马好马坏，一匹匹都给安进了各自的活计里。

===Original===
Clint had been with the layout for near two years, and in that time had broke to ride somewheres around eighty head of horses. He’d broke many more for other outfits and never made an outlaw. If one did turn outlaw once in a while it was because of that pony’s natural instinct to be that way, but Clint handled and rode ’em all just the same﻿—if a perticular horse couldn’t be learned it sure wasn’t his fault and none had better try to learn that same horse *anything*.

===Chinese===
克林特在这处牧场干了快两年，这两年里经他手驯得能骑的马，前后算来约有八十匹。他给别家牧场驯过的还要多得多，从没调教出一匹劣马（outlaw）。偶尔真出一匹劣马，那也是那马天生就那路性子使然，克林特照旧把他们一一调教、一一骑坐——倘若有哪匹马实在教不会，那断然不是他的错，而且往后谁也别想再教会那匹马*任何*东西。

===Original===
As has been said before, bronc fighting was beginning to tell on Clint﻿—none of them ponies he’d broke had spared him, and instead they’d called on for all that was in him. Many had tried to tear him apart and scatter him in the dust of the big corrals; hoofs had come like greased lightning and took hunks off his batwing chaps, teeth had took a few shirts off his back, and as he’d climbed on one after another of these wild, kinky ponies they most all tried to see if they could move the heart of him from one side of his body to the other.

===Chinese===
正像前头说过的，同野马的这番缠斗，渐渐在克林特身上讨起债来——他驯过的那些小马，没有一匹对他手下留情，反倒把他浑身的本事都讨了去。多少匹马想过要把他撕开扯碎、撒进大畜栏的尘土里；蹄子挟着电光石火飞来，从他蝙蝠翼皮套裤上削走一块块片子；牙口从他背上撕下过好几件衬衫；而当他一匹接一匹爬上这些狂野倔拗的小马时，他们几乎个个都想试试：能不能把他那颗心从身子这一边挪到那一边去。

===Original===
There was many times when he was layed up with dislocated shoulders, ribs broke and legs the same. From the root of his hair to the toes in his made to order boots there was signs, if not seen they was felt, where some horse had twisted, broke, or shook something loose. Each happening had come more or less separate, and healed some in time, but as some kept a repeating off and on there was some parts of him which never got strong again; and as time went on, and as Clint said, “he was beginning to feel loose like an old clock and figgered that some day some bucking hunk of horseflesh would take the *tick* out of him and scatter him out so that none of the parts would never be found again.”

===Chinese===
有多少回，他肩头脱了臼、肋骨断了、腿也一样断过，只能躺下将息。从发根到那双定做靴子里的脚趾头，处处都有印记——看不见的，也摸得着——哪处的什么是叫哪匹马拧过、断过、震松过的。每一桩都多多少少是单来的，也都多少随时光愈合了；可有几处老伤断断续续总复发，身上便有些部位再没结实起来；日子一久，照克林特自己的话说，「他开始觉得自己松垮得像座老钟，寻思着早晚有一天，哪一疙瘩尥蹶子的马肉会把他那声*滴答*一举卸走，把他抖散了架，散得他那些零件永远再凑不齐。」

===Original===
Clint had started riding rough ones long before he quit growing and that’s the condition he was in at thirty, an old man, far as riding was concerned. The horses of the same big outfit he’d rode for was worked on the average of only four months in the year, and in them four months the broke horses was rode only four or five hours once every three days. That might show some of the difference in the work the cowboy and the cowhorse does with a real cow outfit.﻿—The men go to pieces young and early and the ponies stay fat﻿—but there was no grudge for there’s nobody in the world likes to see and ride a fat strong horse more than does the cowboy.

===Chinese===
克林特还没停止长个儿，就早早骑上这些烈性子马了；到三十岁，论骑术，他已经是个老头儿了。他效力的这家大牧场，马匹一年平均只使役四个月；这四个月里，驯好的马每三天才骑一回，一回不过四五个钟头。这多少能看出些差别来：在一支地道的牧牛班子里，牛仔和牧牛马各干的是什么活。——人是年纪轻轻就早早垮了，小马却一直膘肥体壮——可谁也不记这份账，因为天底下再没有谁，比牛仔更爱瞧、更爱骑一匹肥壮结实的好马了。

===Original===
They’ll keep the ponies fat and feeling good, and some of them horses find it hard to behave and will try to jar loose the eye teeth of their riders. The cowboy wants ’em that way though﻿—it’s a pride of his to have a kinky horse under him that’s feeling good rather than some gentle old plug that’s leg weary. That all gets him in time, but there’s a grin on his face when that time comes, a grin from the pride of knowing that he never was seen on no horse that was against the principle of a cowboy to ride.

===Chinese===
牛仔们自会把小马养得肥肥的、舒舒坦坦的，而有些马偏守不住规矩，还想把骑他的人的虎牙都颠松。不过牛仔要的就是这个——胯下是一匹精神头十足的倔马，总比一匹腿脚乏透了的温吞老驛马更叫他面上有光。这一切早晚要讨到他头上的；可等到那一天，他脸上还挂着笑呢——那是打骄傲里挂出来的笑：他知道自己从没让人瞧见骑过哪匹违背牛仔骑乘道义的马。

===Original===
Like with Clint, horses was the life of him. He loved ’em for all he was worth and the greatest pleasure in the world for him was in just being with a corral full of ’em, handling ’em and feeling of their hides. The satisfaction he’d get out of seeing some four year old colt learn the things he’d teach meant a heap more to him than the wages he drawed for that work; and there was times as he’d be breaking some right brainy gelding and watch the horse pick up fast on the eddication he’d give him, when he’d feel real attached to the pony. He’d hate to give him up when the time came for all half broke horses to be turned over to the roundup wagons and where more teaching in the handling of the critter begin.

===Chinese===
就拿克林特来说，马就是他的命。他掏出全部心神去爱他们，天底下顶大的快活，莫过于守着一栏的马，摆弄他们，摸摸他们的皮毛。眼看一匹四岁驹把他教的那些一样样学会，那份满足，比他干这活计领的工钱贵重得多；有时他驯着某匹脑子灵光得很的骟马，瞧那马把他给的调教一点就透，心里就真对这马生出依恋来。到所有半驯的马都得交出去、归到围拢车队（roundup wagons）手里、接着受更进一步调教的时节，他总舍不得撒手。

===Original===
“I feel sort of married to them kind of ponies” he’d say, “and I sure don’t hanker to part with ’em just when we’re beginning to get along good together, but” he’d go on “I guess as long as I’ll be breaking horses this way I can’t get too sensitive.”

===Chinese===
「我跟这类小马，简直像成了亲，」他常这么说，「正赶上咱俩刚要处得热乎就分手，我实在不忍；不过呢，」他又接着说，「我寻思着，只要我还这么靠驯马过活，就不能太婆婆妈妈。」

===Original===
But Clint kept a being sensitive that way, and he never was happy when he’d see riders coming in on him and then ride away hazing a bunch of the broncs he’d “started.” “Some day,” he was heard to say once “I’m going to meet a horse I’ll really get married to, and then there’ll be things a popping.”

===Chinese===
可克林特偏就这么婆婆妈妈着，每逢瞧见骑手们朝他而来，又轰着他「开了驯」的那批野马扬长而去，他心里从没痛快过。「早晚有一天，」有一回叫人听见他说，「我会碰上一匹真正要跟我成亲的马，到那时候，可就有热闹瞧了。」

===Original===
Clint would have such a liking for some of them ponies that he’d forget and didn’t want to think that they belonged to the company and not to him. He was just hired to break ’em. He’d reason that out often but that reasoning never fazed the hankering he felt and that’s how come when he run in the mouse colored gelding he begin to do some tall figgering.

===Chinese===
克林特对其中某些小马的喜欢，能喜欢到忘形，不愿去想：这些马是牧场的产业，不是他的，他不过是受雇来驯他们的。这层道理他时常想个通透，可道理归道理，压不住心里那股馋劲儿；所以等他把那匹鼠灰色骟马赶进栏来，他就动开了大念头，一圈圈盘算起来。

===Original===
He had a hunch when he first set eyes on that pony that he’d met the horse which would start “things a popping” when any rider showed up to claim all that’s half broke. Clint had dreamed of such a horse as the mouse colored gelding but he’d never expected to see one really living, that pony had got holt of his heart strings from the start, and as he watched thru the bars of the corral out to where the horse was picketed he felt him to be the kind he’d steal if he couldn’t buy, and if he could neither steal nor buy he’d work for.

===Chinese===
头一眼望见那匹马，他心里就有了谱：等哪天骑手们前来认领这批驯到一半的马，真正会闹出「热闹」来的，就是这一匹。这样一匹鼠灰色骟马，克林特做梦都梦见过，可从没指望真能见着一匹活的。这马打一开头就攥住了他的心弦；他隔着畜栏的栏杆，望向外头马儿拴着的地方，只觉得这马——买不成，他就偷；偷也偷不成，买也买不成，他就替人家做工去换。

===Original===
It’d been two days since he’d run him in and put him on the picket rope outside the corral a ways, and in them two days Clint had been mighty fearful lest somebody rode up on him, seen the horse and took possession of him as private saddle stock for the superintendent or some other what owned shares in the outfit and liked pretty horses that way. Clint wanted that horse mighty bad and he was just leery something would happen so he’d be took away from him, but as he’d reason some he was less worried and he’d wind up by saying as he’d take another peek towards the gelding. “They’d have to let me break him first, and before anybody else gets him I’ll sure make an outlaw out of that horse.”

===Chinese===
