他把这马赶进栏、拴到栏外不远的拴马绳（picket rope）上，已经两天了。这两天里克林特一直提心吊胆，生怕有谁骑马撞上来，看上了这马，把他收了去，充作总管或者哪位在牧场里占着股份、又偏偏好这一口骏马的人物的私房坐骑。克林特想这马想得不行，只怕出个什么岔子，马让人家从他手里领走；可他宽解自己几句，心也就松了些，末了总要再朝那骟马望上一眼，说一句：「总得先让我把他驯完吧；等旁人接手之前，我保管把这家伙调教成一匹劣马。」

===Original===
That was no way for Clint to feel maybe, but that’s sure enough the way he figgered on doing rather than lose the horse to anybody else;﻿—that feeling was past skin deep with him and that I think excuses him some.

===Chinese===
克林特这么想，兴许不像话；可与其把这马输给旁的什么人，他打定的主意确确实实就是这个。——这份心思在他身上早已越过了皮毛一层，我想，这也多少能替他开脱。

===Original===
In the two days that Clint’d had the horse up, there was no chance passed where he could show his feelings and win that pony’s confidence﻿—if the picket rope tangled him up too much Clint was right there to untangle him and each time the gelding fought less when he came. That pony was gradually losing his fear of being et up or tore apart by the human and pretty soon he felt as Clint came and went that each visit from that crethure brought some comfort in a way.

===Chinese===
马被克林特收上来的这两天，凡能表表心意、赢取这小马信任的机会，他一个也没放过——拴马绳要是把马缠得太紧，克林特立时赶到跟前替他解开，而来一趟，骟马的挣扎便轻一分。那小马对人的恐惧——怕被吃掉、怕被撕碎——正一点点消散；不多久他便觉出来，那怪家伙每来一趟，多少都捎着一点舒服。

===Original===
It was on the second evening and when the day’s work was all done that Clint made his way from the bunk house to where the gelding was picketed. He went up to within a rope’s length of the horse, rolled a smoke, and stood there watching him.

===Chinese===
到第二个晚上，一天的活计全收了尾，克林特便从工棚（bunk house）踱到骟马拴着的地方。他走到离马只剩一条绳远近的地方，卷了一支烟，就那么站着端详他。

===Original===
“Smoky,” he says, “you’re some horse”﻿—Clint hadn’t hardly realized he’d spoke a name, he was too busy watching and admiring that pony’s every move, so as it was that name came unconscious like to the cowboy, and it was used and repeated from then on as natural as though that name had been thought and decided on.

===Chinese===
「烟儿（Smoky），」他开口道，「你可真是匹好马」——克林特自己几乎没察觉他已经说出了一个名字，他正忙着盯住那小马的一举一动、满心赞叹呢；这名字就这么不知不觉溜上了牛仔的嘴，打那以后便叫开了、沿用下来，自然得仿佛它是左思右想、郑重定下的一般。

===Original===
He’d named many horses and had always let the name come to him either by the color, size, or shape of each horse, and sometimes by the way they acted. He’d called one tall rangy horse “Shorty” and another low built small horse “Skyhigh.” Often the name didn’t at all fit the horse in that way but there was some reason there, the same as there was a mighty good reason to call the mouse colored gelding “Smoky.”

===Chinese===
他给许多马起过名字，向来是让名字自己找上门——凭每匹马的毛色、个头、身形，有时凭他们的做派。他管一匹高大颀长的马叫「矮子」，管另一匹低矮细小的马叫「通天高」。名字常常跟马这么对不上号，可总有那么点缘由在；正如管这匹鼠灰色骟马叫「烟儿」，缘由就再好不过了。

===Original===
He did look like a rounded shiny cloud of grey smoke, and as he held his ground and watched the cowboy, he acted as though he might live up to his name and really go up in smoke﻿—his acquaintance with the human hadn’t been very long and he wasn’t as yet any too confident.

===Chinese===
他实在像一团滚圆发亮的灰烟；他钉在原地望着牛仔的那副样子，仿佛真要不负这名声，索性化作一缕青烟上了天——他跟人类相识的日子还短，眼下底气也还不足。

===Original===
Clint could tell as he watched just what was going on in that pony’s think tank; he could still see fear in his eyes, but mixed in with that fear was a lot of nerve that showed fight. He knowed that pony would fight and make himself hard to handle, and he’d of been mighty disappointed not to’ve seen them signs in the horse. It was only natural that any of his kind should act this way and he figgered the wilder the spirit the bigger and more worth while would the winning be.﻿—He would take his time, do a good job and turn Smoky from a wild raw bronc into a well broke and eddicated cowhorse.

===Chinese===
克林特一边看，一边就摸得清这小马脑子里转的什么念头；那双眼睛里恐惧还在，可与恐惧掺在一处的，还有一大股透着敢斗的胆气。他知道这马会斗、会叫人难摆弄，要是连这点苗头都瞧不见，他反倒要大失所望。他的同类凡这般表现，本是天经地义；他心里盘算：性子越野，收服过来才越有分量、越值当。——他要不慌不忙，稳稳当当干一场，把烟儿从一匹野生的生马，调教成一匹驯透了的、懂规矩的牧牛马。

===Original===
He took a few steps closer and Smoky backed away to the end of the rope﻿—he snorted when he found he couldn’t back no further and pawed at the rope as the cowboy kept a coming still closer and closer. Clint took his time but came on steady and a talking the while till he finally got within a couple of feet of the horse and where he could touch him. Hanging on to the rope with the right hand he reached out with the left and touched him easy between the eyes. Smoky flinched and snorted but he stood it﻿—he stood it for quite a spell and felt the hand rubbing on his forehead and working up and up towards his ears.

===Chinese===
他又朝前挪了几步，烟儿便退，一直退到绳头——发觉再退无可退，他打了个响鼻，又刨那条绳，牛仔却还一个劲儿往近里凑。克林特不急，脚步却稳，嘴里一路说着话，末了终于进到离马不过一两英尺、伸手可及的地方。他右手攥定绳子，左手探出去，轻轻按在马的两眼之间。烟儿一哆嗦、打了个响鼻，可他忍住了——忍了好一阵子，由着那只手在额头上揉搓，一寸一寸往耳朵那边挪。

===Original===
Clint had just about got to one of them ears when Smoky rubbed his nose along the cowboy’s sleeve, took a sniff, and then of a sudden nipped him on the arm. That had happened to him before many a time and he’d been ready for it with the result that the pony got only a piece of shirt and no flesh.

===Chinese===
克林特眼看着就要够着其中一只耳朵了，烟儿却把鼻子顺着牛仔的袖子蹭上去，嗅了一下，冷不丁照他胳膊咬了一口。这种事他从前经得多了，早有防备，结果小马只叼走一片衬衫，没沾着肉。

===Original===
“Now, don’t be so daggoned ornery,” says that cowboy as he kept a rubbing the same as though nothing had happened, “I only want to reach between them ears and touch that knowledge bump of yours.”

===Chinese===
「行了，别这么死倔死倔的，」那牛仔一边说，一边照旧揉搓，仿佛什么都没发生过，「我不过是想够到那两只耳朵中间，碰碰你那块智慧疙瘩。」

===Original===
Finally he did reach the bump and rubbed around there a spell. Smoky struck once, Clint dodged the front hoof and kept a rubbing. He rubbed past the left ear and down his neck till the withers was reached, the mane was worked on and all the knots in it untangled. The little horse quivered and flinched every once in a while but the rubbing process went on till Smoky begin showing symptoms that he could stand it all easy enough.

===Chinese===
他到底够着了那块疙瘩，围着揉了好一阵。烟儿炮了一蹄子，克林特闪过那只前蹄，手底下没停。他揉过左耳，顺着脖颈往下，一直揉到耆甲，又拾掇起鬃毛，把里头的结一个个解开了。小马时不时打个哆嗦、缩一下，可揉搓的工序照旧进行，直到烟儿渐渐露出苗头：这一切他满受得住，受得挺自在。

===Original===
In the meantime Clint talked to him like he’d never took time to talk to another horse before, and if Smoky could of understood he’d knowed by that talk just what was ahead for him; but Smoky wasn’t thinking on what was ahead﻿—the present had him worried enough as it was, and he was kept busy watching every move that human was making.

===Chinese===
这工夫，克林特对他絮叨开了，那份耐性是从前不曾给过任何一匹马的；烟儿若是听得懂，也就从这些话里明白了自己前头等着的是什么；可烟儿顾不上想前头——光眼面前这一摊就够他提心吊胆的了，他忙不迭地盯住那个人的一举一动。

===Original===
Smoky had lost considerable wildness during the two days on the picket rope. He’d learned there was no use in fighting the rope that held him, that it was best to turn when he came to the end of it, and gradually he was getting used to have that rope touch him here and there and he’d quit kicking at it. He was more familiar with that than with the human who put him there, but the rope done the trick of getting him used to having anything touch him﻿—it kinda broke him to stand the touch of the hand.

===Chinese===
在拴马绳上过的这两天，烟儿的野性子褪去了不少。他明白了跟拴住自己的绳子斗没用，明白了到了绳头最好转身，也渐渐惯了那绳子东碰一下西碰一下，不再朝它尥蹶子。比起把他拴在那儿的人，他跟绳子倒更熟些；可正是绳子立了功，让他惯于被东西触碰——多少算替那只手开了路，教他受得住手的抚触。

===Original===
He was learning to stand that well enough too, but the movements of that hand had to be just right, not too quick and no jabbing done or there’d be a scattering of something mighty quick.

===Chinese===
这个他也在学着消受，学得还挺像样；只是那只手的动作得恰到好处——不能太快，不许乱戳，不然转眼就得有什么东西炸窝飞散。

===Original===
“I’m sure making a lot of fuss over you,” says Clint as he rubs on past the withers and along his back a ways. “If you was just an ordinary bronc you’d be missing most of this attention and you’d be finding yourself in the corral with me on top of you by tomorrow, and turned in the ‘Remuda’ by another month, but I got a scheme up on account of me liking you the way I do: I’m going to take my time and make you my private top horse and when that’s done I’ll have every cowboy in the country jealous of me for having such a horse as you’re going to turn out to be.”

===Chinese===
「我可真是把你当宝贝伺候，」克林特一边说，一边揉过耆甲，顺着脊背往上摩挲了一截。「你要只是匹寻常野马，这份优待多半就轮不上，明儿你就得在畜栏里驮着我了，再过一个月，就编进『雷穆达』（Remuda）里去喽。可我喜欢你这副品相，心里盘算着一件事：我要不紧不慢来，把你调教成我私人的头号坐骑；等功成那天，你出落成的那等好马，得叫这一带每个牛仔都眼红我。」

===Original===
With Clint’s scheming that way there was a good chance of him winning out, and gradually, steady, the eddication of Smoky started in. That cowboy called on for all he knowed in the profession of horse breaking and used it all with a lot of time to shape out Smoky the way he wanted him. No company time was used on the horse on account Clint felt it wouldn’t been doing the square thing, “cause” as he says “it’ll be bad enough if I have to steal him.”﻿—Of course Clint wouldn’t steal that horse or no other one, but he felt like he’d sure do something out of the ordinary rather than let Smoky go to any other rider.

===Chinese===
克林特既这么盘算，赢面就很大；一点一点、稳稳当当，烟儿的调教开了场。那牛仔把驯马这一行里他懂的本事全数搬了出来，全押上大把的时光，要把烟儿塑成他心里想的那副品性。牧场的工时倒是一点没搭进去——克林特觉得那样不够光明，「因为，」照他说，「真到非偷他不可的时候，那才够难看的呢。」——当然，克林特断不会偷这马，也不会偷任何一匹；可他心里有数：宁干一桩出格的事，也绝不让烟儿落到旁的骑手手里。

===Original===
Every evening after that last meal of the day was over, Clint would be down in the creek bottom with Smoky. What went on there showed some of what Clint really thought of the mouse colored gelding, and there was no disappointed look on his face when dark made him return to the bunk house.

===Chinese===
打那以后，每天晚饭一收，克林特准下到小溪谷底烟儿那儿去。那底下发生的事，多少透出克林特对这匹鼠灰色骟马存的真实心思；每回天黑把他赶回工棚，他脸上都寻不出一点失望。

===Original===
Smoky had been on the picket rope about a week. In that time Clint had kept his eye on him thru the day while working in the corral and spent a couple of hours with him every evening. The little horse had got used to the rope and wouldn’t pay no attention to it no more, but as for the cowboy he was just neutral; it was hard for him to shed off the fear of the human and which he’d inherited﻿—that human was still a mighty big mystery to him even after a week’s acquaintance. It’d done him no harm but his wild instinct kept a warning him to expect most anything. The power that two-legged crethure had over him kept him leery and watching for the next move, whatever that would be﻿—and that’s why Smoky was still neutral, his confidence for the human hadn’t come to the top as yet and not a move did that cowboy make which he didn’t see.

===Chinese===
烟儿在拴马绳上待了约莫一个礼拜。这些天里，克林特白天在畜栏干活，眼角始终挂着他，每天傍晚又陪他一两个钟头。小马早惯了那条绳，再不拿正眼瞧它；可对牛仔，他还端着中立；那份对人的恐惧是承袭来的，一时半会儿抖落不掉——就算处了一个礼拜，那个人在他眼里仍旧是老大一个谜。人没伤害过他，可野性的本能一径提醒他：什么都得防着。那两条腿的怪家伙攥在他头上的威权，叫他提着心、盯住下一手，不管那下一手是什么——所以烟儿还端着中立：他对人的信任还没浮上水面，而那牛仔的一举一动，没有一样逃出过他的眼。

===Original===
“You sure got your eye on me, aint you, little horse?” Clint would say, “but that’s the way I want you to be,” he’d go on, “for the more you watch the more you’ll see and the quicker you’ll learn.”

===Chinese===
「你可真是一双眼老盯着我，是不是啊，小家伙？」克林特总这么逗他，「不过我要的就是你这样，」他又接着说，「你越看，见得就越多；见得越多，学得就越快。」

===Original===
Smoky did watch and see and learn, and then one evening Clint untied the long picket rope from the log and started leading him towards the corral; the little horse was broke to lead by then and he followed easy enough. His heart was a thumping in wonder of what was due to happen as the cowboy led him thru the big pole gate. He stepped high and careful and his eyes took in everything that looked suspicious﻿—a slicker hanging over one side of the corral made him snort and try to pull away. Clint talked to him, and kept on a leading him thru another gate into another smaller and round corral. A big snubbing post stuck up in the center of it and by that post was a big brown and shiny hunk of leather; it was Clint’s saddle.

===Chinese===
烟儿果然看、果然见、果然学。后来有一天傍晚，克林特把长拴马绳从木桩上解下来，牵着他往畜栏走；小马这时已驯得肯让人牵了，跟得挺顺当。牛仔领他穿过那道大木杆门时，他心里咚咚直跳，猜不透等着自己的是什么。他蹄子抬得高高的、落得细细的，两只眼睛把一切看着可疑的东西都收进去——栏边搭着的一件雨披（slicker）骇得他打响鼻、直往后挣。克林特对他絮叨着，一路又领他穿过另一道门，进了另一座小些的圆栏。圆栏正中竖着一根粗大的拴马桩（snubbing post），桩边搭着一大块油亮的棕色皮子——那是克林特的鞍子。

---

===Original===
“Well now, little horse, the performance is about to begin, you’re going to get your first smell of saddle leather.” Clint had turned as he spoke and begin rubbing on Smoky’s forehead. For once since Smoky had been caught his attention wasn’t on the cowboy. That hunk of leather was drawing all his interest; and ears pointed straight at it, eyes a shining, he snorted his suspicions and dislike for the looks of the contraption that was laying there, waiting it seemed like to jump at him and eat him alive.

===Chinese===
「好了，小家伙，好戏就要开场了，你这就要头一回闻鞍皮的味儿了。」克林特说着转过身，开始在烟儿额头上揉搓。自打被捉住以来，头一回，烟儿的心思没放在牛仔身上。那块皮子把他的兴头全勾了过去；耳朵直直指向它，眼睛亮亮的，他冲着那件趴在地上的玩意儿打响鼻，把满腹的疑心和嫌恶都喷了过去——瞧那架势，就像正候着要扑上来把他活吞了。

===Original===
“Look, snort, and paw at it all you want,” says the cowboy. “You’ll get well acquainted with it before you get thru, and I won’t rush the acquaintance either.”

===Chinese===
「瞧吧，打响鼻吧，刨它个够，随你便，」牛仔说。「等你这关过完，有你跟它处熟的时候；这门交情，我也不催。」

===Original===
Clint didn’t. He kept Smoky to within a few feet of the saddle and grinning some at the pony’s actions, kept a rubbing him back of the ears while the investigation was going on. Smoky was for getting away from there but Clint was persuading him to stick around close, and there was nothing for him to do but just that.

===Chinese===
克林特果然没催。他把烟儿留在离鞍子几英尺的地方，瞧着小马的做派咧嘴直乐，一边在他耳后揉搓，任那场查验进行。烟儿一门心思要离开那儿，克林特却劝他贴着近处待着，他别无选择，只好照办。

===Original===
A move from the direction of that saddle right then would of queered things and made Smoky scatter, and Clint couldn’t of held him either for a ways, but the hunk of leather layed still, mighty still, and pretty soon it kinda lost its dangerous look to the little horse﻿—he begin looking around for other things in that corral which wouldn’t be to his liking and not seeing anything that was worth getting spooky at, Smoky begin watching the cowboy again.

===Chinese===
这时候那鞍子那边但凡起个动静，事情就砸了，烟儿准得炸开跑散，那一程克林特也拽不住他；可那块皮子躺着不动，纹丝儿不动，不多时在小马眼里，它那副凶相就淡了——他转而打量起栏里旁的不得他欢心的东西，见再没什么值得大惊小怪的，便又看上了牛仔。

===Original===
It was about then that Clint reached over and picked up the saddle slow and easy and drug it closer to Smoky. At the first move of the riggin’ the little horse snorted and backed away but Clint and the saddle kept a coming straight towards him, slow but steady. One side of the high corral finally was reached. Smoky had backed against it and couldn’t go no further. The cowboy, still hanging onto the rope that held his head, came on, saddle and all with him, and quivering with fear the little horse layed low. Feet straight out in front and head near to the ground he stayed there, and got another and different eddication with the saddle, this time it was dragging.

===Chinese===
约莫就在这时，克林特探身把鞍子拎起来，又慢又稳，往烟儿跟前拖。鞍具头一动，小马便打响鼻往后退，可克林特和鞍子照直朝他逼来，慢，却稳。末了，高栏的一边到了。烟儿背抵着栏板，再没处退了。牛仔手里仍攥着拴他脑袋的绳子，连人带鞍一齐赶到，小马吓得直哆嗦，趴伏了下去。前腿直直伸在前头，脑袋几乎贴地，他就在那儿僵着，又领了一课不一样的鞍上调教——这一回，是拖着来的。

===Original===
When Clint thought that had gone far enough and seen where Smoky had got over the worst of his fear he layed the saddle down again, and picking up an old saddle blanket he begin fanning the air with it, closer and closer to Smoky came the blanket as the fanning motion kept on, and stary-eyed the little horse watched. He struck at it and snorted a couple of times and he even tried to turn and kick, but the blanket came on till finally one corner of it grazed his side. He flinched and kicked and tried to jerk away but there was no dodging that spooky looking thing.

===Chinese===
等克林特觉得火候够了，瞧出烟儿最凶的那阵怕劲已经过去，他便把鞍子重新放下；又拾起一条旧鞍垫（saddle blanket），抡开来朝空气里扇。扇个不停，鞍垫便一寸寸逼向烟儿，小马瞪着发直的眼睛瞧。他朝它炮蹄子、打响鼻，有一两回甚至还拧身想踢，可鞍垫照样压过来，末了，一个角扫上了他的肋侧。他一哆嗦、一尥蹶子、拼命想挣开，可那副吓人模样的东西，是躲不掉的。

===Original===
Not a word was heard from the cowboy as the “sacking” went on, this was a part of the eddication that was necessary and which should be put thru mighty quiet. It was all a spooky enough performance to a raw bronc without adding on any talking, and even though the goings on scared the pony near out of his hide, that blanket done the trick of showing him that no matter how bad it looked it wasn’t going to hurt him, it was one mighty good thing to teach him general confidence in the cowboy and his riggin’.

===Chinese===
「麻袋操练」（sacking）进行当中，牛仔一声不吭；这是调教里少不了的一环，得在极静里过。对一匹生野马来说，光这套阵仗已经够吓人的了，再添上说话大可不必；这一通折腾虽然把小马吓得魂儿都快出了窍，那条鞍垫却立了功，让他看明白：这东西模样再凶，也伤不着他——这一手实在高明，教会了他笼统地信那牛仔和他的鞍具。

===Original===
Smoky fought like a cornered wolf and tried to get away, but he had no chance﻿—Clint had “sacked” many a bronc that’d fought as much and the cigarette between his lips noticed no change of spells between puffs. Smoky showed hate and fear of the human once again the same as when he was first caught, his instinct had warned him to expect most anything from that crethure, and he wasn’t surprised at the way things had turned;﻿—but that didn’t help any, he just wanted to sail clear over the corral and disappear.

===Chinese===
烟儿像条被逼进死角的狼一样死斗，想脱身，可他没半点机会——克林特「麻袋操练」过的野马多了，斗得比这凶的都有，他嘴唇间那支烟，一口接一口，节奏纹丝不变。烟儿对人又一次露出了恨和怕，同头一回被捉时一模一样；本能早提醒过他，那怪家伙什么都干得出来，事情落到这步田地，他并不意外；——可这也不顶用，他只想凌空飞过畜栏，逃得无影无踪。

===Original===
Thru all that fighting and goings on the sacking kept up in steady motion. Wherever the long blanket touched Smoky he flinched, and kicked at it and squealed. He was too scared to realize that there was no sting or any kind of a hurt felt. It was just the looks of the thing which had him going and his fighting instinct just had to answer every swish of that thing that circled around a leg one time and his neck the next.

===Chinese===
那场打斗、那通折腾里，「麻袋操练」始终匀着节奏进行。那条长鞍垫挨着烟儿哪儿，他哪儿就一哆嗦，朝它又踢又叫。他怕得太狠，意识不到那里头没有一下刺痛、没有一点伤。全是那东西的模样撵着他跑，他的斗性又不能不应战——那东西绕着他一条腿扫一圈，又绕着他脖子扫一圈，每一下他都得接着。

===Original===
Finally, and whether it was from being tired or fighting or that he was dazed past caring of what was going on, Smoky begin to let up; his kicks begin to get less wicked and his eyes lost some of the fiery look till came a time when he stood near still and he’d only flinch as the blanket kept a touching, going away, and touching him here and there and all over.

===Chinese===
到后来——也不知是乏了，是斗累了，还是已经懵得失了计较——烟儿渐渐松了劲；炮出去的蹄子没那么狠了，眼里的火光也淡了些，直到有那么一刻，他几乎站定不动了，只剩鞍垫一路触碰、走开、又在他身上各处轻轻挨上时，他才缩一缩。

===Original===
Clint noticing the little horse calming down remarked, “You’ll get so you’ll like it pretty soon.” But Smoky wasn’t showing no such symptom as yet, he was just standing it best as he could and that was all.

===Chinese===
克林特见小马安定了下来，搭话说：「不用多久，你就会喜欢上这个喽。」可烟儿眼下还没露半点这样的苗头，他不过是尽力熬着罢了。

===Original===
Both sides and all around Smoky went Clint with his blanket till the little horse finally even quit flinching. The cowboy then dropped the rope that was holding the horse and worked his blanket wilder than ever, that blanket was layed everywhere on that pony’s hide and around his legs, he layed it on the ground and drug it under him and all Smoky would do was to cock one ear and watch it, but he never moved. A half an hour before such a play would of sent him straight up.

===Chinese===
克林特举着鞍垫把烟儿的两边、周身全过了一遍，直到小马末了连哆嗦都不打了。牛仔这才撒开拴马的绳，把鞍垫耍得比先前更野：那垫子往马皮上处处落，往他腿边处处绕，铺到地上、从他肚皮底下拖过去——烟儿呢，不过支起一只耳朵瞧着它，身子一动没动。要搁半个钟头以前，这一套早把他惊得平地蹿上天了。

===Original===
Clint worked on for a while longer till he was sure there wasn’t a spot on that pony that’d flinch at the feel of the blanket, then he begin to notice that Smoky was finally getting so he kinda liked the performance, no flies could touch him while that was going on, and that blanket being pulled all over him that way seemed to kind of soothe some.

===Chinese===
克林特又练了好一阵，直到确信这马身上再没有一处沾了鞍垫会哆嗦；这时他方才留意到，烟儿末了竟有点喜欢上这套操练了——操练进行当中，苍蝇叮不着他，那条鞍垫满身拖来拂去，竟像有几分舒筋解乏的意思。

===Original===
It was about when Clint figgered he could do no more good in the way of sacking that he picked up his saddle again and came straight towards Smoky with it. The squeak of the riggin’ brought some interest from the horse, but Clint was careful to bring the old blanket with him and keep a fanning the same as to let him know that one was no worse than the other.

===Chinese===
约莫在克林特盘算着「麻袋操练」的功课已做足、再练无益的当口，他又拎起鞍子，照直朝烟儿走来。鞍具吱呀一响，勾起了马几分注意；克林特却小心地连旧鞍垫一并带来，照旧一路扇着，好叫他知道：这一样跟那一样，谁也不比谁凶。

===Original===
In the first saddling of most broncs Clint generally tied up one of their hind legs so as to hinder ’em from kicking the saddle out of his hands and at the same time learn ’em to stand still while that went on;﻿—a few of ’em he’d just hobble in front. And being that Smoky’d had more teaching than the average colt generally gets before first saddling, Clint figgered that just hobbling his front feet would do.

===Chinese===
头一回上鞍，克林特照例要把大多数野马的一条后腿拴起来，一防他们把鞍子从他手里踢飞，二来也趁便教他们在这当口站定别动；——有几匹，他只在前腿上打绊（hobble）。烟儿在上这一课之前受的调教，比寻常马驹到的份儿多，克林特盘算着，光绊住他的两只前蹄就够了。

===Original===
The sacking had helped a lot and Clint had no trouble fastening the rawhide hobbles around Smoky’s ankles. The pony snorted at him a little but stood still, for Clint was waving that blanket around as he worked. Once the hobbles was on he picked up his saddle and *eased* it up and on that pony’s back. Smoky had a hunch that something new was going on, something different than the sacking performance which he’d just went thru; but as nothing happened outside of the flapping of stirrup leathers and cinches he stood in one spot, only a quiver in the muscles along his shoulder showed how much alive he was, and how quick he could leave the earth if anything “goosed” him.

===Chinese===
「麻袋操练」帮了大忙，克林特没费什么劲就把生皮绊子拴上了烟儿的脚踝。小马朝他打了打响鼻，却站定不动——原来克林特一边干活，一边还抡着那条鞍垫。绊子一上好，他拎起鞍子，把它又轻又缓地*送*上了小马的背。烟儿隐隐觉出又有什么新节目开演了，跟他刚熬过的那场麻袋操练不是一码事；可除了镫革和肚带（cinch）的噼啪扑动，什么也没发生，他便原地站着；只有肩侧肌肉里一丝颤动，透出他活着有多真——一旦什么东西「拧」他一口，他离地就能有多快。

===Original===
Plenty of practice had made Clint past master at putting a saddle on a green colt, nothing happened to make Smoky want to move out of his tracks, and even when the cinch was reached for and drawed up under his belly he never batted an eye. The sacking had all been a mighty fine preliminary for all this that followed and cured the horse from scaring at everything that flapped on or around him.

===Chinese===
千锤百炼早把克林特练成了给新驹上鞍的宗师；烟儿这厢没出一点想挪窝的动静，连肚带伸手够着、在他肚皮底下勒紧时，他眼皮都没眨一下。先前那场「麻袋操练」，对后头这一切实在是顶好的预习，把他见着身上身侧有什么扑动就发毛的毛病，治好了。

===Original===
As it was Smoky hardly realized that he was saddled till Clint took the hobbles off his front feet and pulled him to one side. At that pull he felt something fastened to him and hanging on; that was a new kind of feeling to Smoky and it kettled him, down went his head and he lit in to bucking.

===Chinese===
就这样，直到克林特解掉他前蹄上的绊子、把他朝旁边一拉，烟儿才恍然觉出自己身上驮着鞍。经这一拉，他感到有个东西拴在自己身上、坠着不放；这对烟儿是种全新的感觉，把他闹懵了——脑袋一扎，他便撒开了尥蹶子。

===Original===
Clint had expected that, for no bronc likes the feel of the cinch no matter how loose it might be, and when Smoky bogged his head that way he was ready;﻿—he let the hackamore rope slide thru his hands for a ways and till he could get a good footing, then he give that rope a little flip and set down on it. That done the trick and it come daggone near upsetting the little horse, but Clint let out just enough slack and that saved him. He didn’t want to throw the horse but then he didn’t want to have that horse buck with an empty saddle either.

===Chinese===
这一手克林特早料到了——肚带再松，也没有哪匹野马爱那滋味；烟儿脑袋一扎，他已有了准备——他放笼头绳从掌心滑出去一截，直到站牢了脚跟，才把绳子轻轻一抖，拿身子坐住了它。这一招奏了效，险些把小马掀个底朝天，亏得克林特又放出一截活绳，才保住他没翻。他不想摔倒这马，可也不想让这马驮着空鞍尥蹶子。

===Original===
“Now Smoky,” says that cowboy as the horse jerked to his senses, turned and faced him, “I don’t want you to waste any of your energy that way, if you want to do any bucking you just wait till I get in the middle of you.”

===Chinese===
「行了，烟儿，」等那马猛地回过神、转过身来正对着他，牛仔才开腔，「我可不想你把劲儿白白糟蹋在这上头；你要真想尥蹶子，就等我骑到你脊梁正当间再说。」

===Original===
Smoky waited, but it wasn’t thru the talk the cowboy had handed him that he did wait; it was that he remembered how that rope had upset him that first day he was picketed to the log outside the corral; and he wasn’t hankering to be “busted” that way again.

===Chinese===
烟儿果然等了，可他等，并不是听了牛仔递过来的那一番话；是他记得头一天被拴在栏外木桩上时，那条绳子把他掀翻的那一下；他实在不想再那么挨一回「摔打」。

---

===Original===
There’s folks that’s read some on how horses are broke on the range, and from that reading they get the idea that the cowboy breaks the horses’ spirit, that it’s the only way a wild horse can be tamed. What I’ve got to say on the subject if that’s what’s believed, is that either them folks read something that’s mighty wrong, or else they got the wrong impression and misunderstood what they read; and breaking a horse the way he’s broke on the range is about the same on the animal as schooling is to the human youngster. The spirit of the wild horse is the same after years of riding as it was before he ever felt a rope, and there’s no human in the world wants to perserve that spirit in the horse like the cowboy does;﻿—he’s the one what knows better than anybody else that a horse with a broken spirit is no horse at all.

===Chinese===
有人读过一些牧场上是怎样驯马的，读着读着便得了个印象：牛仔驯的是马的精气神，说野马只有这么个降服法。要真是这么信的，那我这话就有的说了：要么这些人读的东西错得离谱，要么就是他们记岔了、把读的误会了。照牧场上的路数驯马，落在牲口身上，跟上学落在人家娃娃身上，差不多是一回事。野马的精气神，骑上多少年，跟头一回挨绳子之前一个样；天底下再没有谁，比牛仔更想把这份精气神在马身上守住——马的精神要是断了，那马就算不得马了，这个道理，比谁都清楚的就是他。

===Original===
To them that *only* sees a wild horse roped and rode and don’t know the insides of the game, horse breaking might seem a little rough; but I’m here to say that it’s not near as rough as it is necessary, and in the long run it’s the rider that gets treated the roughest. You let a wild horse get away with something once and he’ll try it again till there will come a time when even if there’s no meanness in him he’ll develop some. That’s what makes outlaws.

===Chinese===
有些人*只*瞧见野马挨套、挨骑，不懂这门行当的里子，便觉得驯马有点粗暴；可我把话放这儿：它的必要，远比它的粗暴来得大；到头来，挨收拾最狠的是骑马的那位。你放任一匹野马得逞一回，他就要来第二回，早晚有那么一天，哪怕他心里本没有坏，也养出坏来了。劣马就是这么造出来的。

===Original===
Outlaws are made mostly when a horse proves too much for the man that handles him. A wild horse will turn outlaw often if handled by any other than them that knows his kind, and there’ll be no way of breaking him only thru starvation and abuse. His spirit would be broken then too, and that proves that the cowboy, knowing his business, will see that the pony’s heart is kept intact.

===Chinese===
劣马之所以成劣，多半是马压过了摆布他的人。一匹野马，落到不懂他那一路的人手里，动不动就变成劣马，往后除了饿他、糟践他，再没法治他。到那时，他的精气神也就断了；这反倒证明：懂行的牛仔，自会看顾着，不让小马的那颗心受半点损伤。

===Original===
There’s a variety in horse minds as big as there is amongst human minds. Some need more persuading than others, and a few of ’em, no matter how firm they’re handled, will have to be showed again and again that they can’t get away with this or with that﻿—they’ll keep on a trying and if ever once they do put a bluff thru there’s most generally enough meanness in their system to make ’em plum worthless.

===Chinese===
马跟马的心思，花样同人跟人一样多。有的要多劝几分，有的不论把得多严，都得一遍又一遍教他认清：这一招行不通，那一招也行不通——他们偏要一遍遍试；哪天真让他们唬成了一回，他们那副根性里的坏，一般也就够把他们废个彻底了。

===Original===
And like I was saying with Smoky, “he remembered how that rope had upset him that first day he was picketed to that log outside the corral, and he wasn’t hankering to be ‘busted’ that way again.”﻿—That little horse had brains. If he was convinced a few times he had the sense to realize it, but at the same time, *he had* to be showed, and more because it was part of his necessary eddication than because of any meanness of his.

===Chinese===
就像我说烟儿的那句：「他记得头一天被拴在栏外木桩上时，那条绳子把他掀翻的那一下，他实在不想再那么挨一回『摔打』。」——那匹小马有脑子。说服他几回，他就有那份悟性认下这个理；可同时，*非得*做给他看不可——这多半是他必修的调教里的一课，倒不是他骨子里有多坏。

===Original===
He was willing to learn but the teaching had to come from one who *could* teach him. There was no meanness in Smoky, not an ounce of it, he was honest clear thru; but meanness would develop if a slip was made. He fought and bit and kicked, but Smoky was a wild horse, and he was going only according to his instinct and more to protect himself from the strange human.

===Chinese===
他肯学，可教他的，得是一位*教得了*他的。烟儿身上没有坏，一钱也没有，他里外透着实诚；可但凡有一步走岔，坏就会冒头。他斗过、咬过、踢过，可烟儿是匹野马，他不过是照本性行事，多半是为了防那个陌生的人。

===Original===
That’s the caliber of most range horses. Clint had handled many of ’em and always won out with their confidence and turned ’em over as broke with their spirit intact. He’d savvied Smoky the minute he dabbed his rope on him that first time: that pony was wild, wild as a horse or any animal can get, and he had the strength to go with it; but Clint seen where that little horse also had a mighty fine set of brains between them little pointed ears of his.

===Chinese===
牧场上的马，大半是这个成色。克林特经手过的多了，回回都赢得了他们的信任，交出去时马驯好了，精气神一分没伤。头一回把绳圈甩上烟儿的那一刻，他就门儿清了：这马野，野到马——或者说任何牲口——能野到的那个份上，而且有跟这份野相称的力气；可克林特也瞧出来了，那对小尖耳朵中间，还架着一副顶灵光的脑子。

===Original===
He treated him like a grown up would treat a kid, a kid of the kind that’d learn a lot if the chance showed up; and he missed no chance to show that pony all he should know and how good he wanted to be to him.

===Chinese===
他待他，就像大人待一个娃儿——待那种只要机会露头就能学下一大本的娃儿；凡能让这马明白的事，凡能让他知道自己是多想对他好的机会，他一处也没漏过。

===Original===
“Daggone it, Smoky” he’d say, “it’s too bad you can’t know without I have to use a lot of ropes, as it is sometimes. I bet you don’t think I’m a friend of yours, none at all.”

===Chinese===
「真要命，烟儿，」他常这么念叨，「可惜你没法自己个儿明白，害我得动用那么多绳子——有时候就是这样。我敢打赌，你压根儿不拿我当朋友，一点儿也不。」

===Original===
Clint was right. At first Smoky had took him as an enemy and fought him according; then had come a time when he was willing to trust him some, specially when Clint had come and untangled him out of that long picket rope, talked to him, and rubbed his ears. His heart had got over thumping so much when he’d see the cowboy coming of evenings, and even though the little horse didn’t realize it as yet, he’d got to expecting him.

===Chinese===
克林特没说错。起初烟儿拿他当敌人，照敌人的份儿跟他斗；后来到了一个时候，他愿意信他几分了——尤其是克林特来替他从那条长拴马绳里解围、跟他说话、揉他耳朵的那些时候。傍晚再望见牛仔来，他的心口也不那么咚咚乱撞了；小马自己还没察觉，可他已经盼着他来了。

===Original===
Then, and just about when his liking for the cowboy was coming to the top fast, something happened that’d make him wonder for a spell if that cowboy was a friend or still an enemy. The “sacking” he’d went thru in the corral had sort of jarred the confidence that’d begin to sprout for the bowlegged crethure, and then the way his head was jerked up out of his bucking spell with the empty saddle, all had left him puzzled as whether to start in and do some fighting or else be good and take his medicine.

===Chinese===
可就在他对牛仔的好感蹭蹭往头顶冒的当口，出了一档子事，害他一时又拿不准：那牛仔到底是朋友，还是照旧是敌人。栏里挨的那场「麻袋操练」，把他对那个罗圈腿怪家伙才冒芽的信任震得晃了晃；跟着空鞍尥蹶子时，脑袋又让人家那么一拽——这一切撂下他一个发懵：是接着斗一场呢，还是乖乖的，把这剂药喝下去。

===Original===
Smoky had no way of knowing as yet what was expected of him, and it was a ticklish time for him. It was right then that he’d have to be handled just right and when the turning point for the good or the bad would be decided on. But Clint knowed how the turn to the good layed, and it was right there that he proceeded to bring it out.

===Chinese===
眼下烟儿还无从知道人家指望他怎样，对他来说，这正是最要小心拿捏的火候。恰恰在这当口，得拿捏得恰到好处——向好向坏的那个转折点，就定在这上头。可克林特知道向好那道弯在哪儿，他偏就是在那儿下手，把它引了出来。

===Original===
There was only one way to it and that was for Clint to *show* Smoky he had to be good. The cowboy knowed Smoky had brains a plenty to realize once he was showed, that he had to do just what he wanted him to do, that of course would take a little time, the pony would fight some more and *want* to be showed, and to keep him from getting flustrated that horse would have to have his own way, some.


===Chinese===
路只有一条：克林特得*做给*烟儿看——他非乖不可。牛仔知道烟儿有的是脑子，教过一遍就明白：该照人家要的做。这自然要费些时日，小马还会再斗几场，还*非要*见真章不可；而要他不急不躁不上火，这马就得由着自己的性子——多少由着一些。

## VI / 六

===Original===
“The Squeak of Leather”

===Chinese===
「皮革的吱嘎声」

===Original===
Twenty feet of rope is laying between the cowboy’s hand and the pony’s head. The cowboy is standing there just watching and smiling some at the surprised look that’s in the pony’s face, that pony had just been stopped sudden in his bucking with an empty saddle;﻿—it was the first time a saddle had been on his slick back and it was no wonder he tried to get out from under that thing; nothing had ever clung there before.

===Chinese===
牛仔的手和小马的脑袋之间，摊着二十英尺长的一条绳。牛仔站在那儿，只是瞧着，冲小马脸上那副吃惊的模样笑了些——这小马方才驮着一只空鞍子尥蹶子，被猛地一下制住了——那油光水滑的背上，这是头一回压鞍子；也难怪他拼命想从那东西底下钻出去，打从前，可从来没有东西扒在那儿过。

===Original===
“Now, you just take it easy for a spell, and keep your head up,” says that cowboy as he started walking towards the pony.﻿—Legs wide apart, a wild look in his eyes, and a snorting his surprise Smoky watched him come; he didn’t know whether to stand his ground and start fighting or back away as the cowboy came.﻿—On he came, and as Smoky was seeing no sign of harm, he stood in his tracks, watched, and waited. A hand touched him on the forehead and moved on down his neck, the cowboy was a talking to him the while, and pretty soon Smoky’s heart wasn’t thumping so hard no more.﻿—

===Chinese===
「好了，你先消停一会儿，把头抬起来。」那牛仔一边朝小马走去，一边这么说。——烟儿四条腿叉得开开的，眼里透着野气，打着响鼻把惊诧报出来，望着那人过来；他拿不定主意：是钉在原地开打，还是趁牛仔过来先往后退。——那人偏偏径直来了；烟儿瞧着没有一丝要伤他的意思，便原地立定，看着，等着。一只手落到他额头上，又顺着脖颈往下抚；牛仔嘴里一路说着话，不多时，烟儿那颗心便不再咚咚擂得那么凶了。——

===Original===
He was then led a little ways, and as he heard the squeak of leather and felt the weight of the saddle with each step he took, an awful hankering came to him to put his head down and try to buck it off, but the cowboy was right there in front of him and he didn’t want to be stopped again and so sudden as he’d been stopped that first time.

===Chinese===
随后他被牵着走了几步；每迈一步，耳边听得皮革吱嘎作响，背上觉出鞍子的分量，一股凶急的念头便直往上冒——低下头去，试试把它尥下去；可牛仔就堵在正前方，他不想再被制住一回，更不想像头一回那样，被制得那么突然。

===Original===
The other side of the corral was reached and there Clint turned and rubbed Smoky on the ear. “Well, old boy, let’s see how you’re going to behave when I get up in the middle of you.”

===Chinese===
走到畜栏那一边，克林特转过身来，揉了揉烟儿的耳朵。「好啦，老伙计，等我骑上你脊梁正中间，且看你是个什么表现。」

===Original===
Smoky watched the man reach for the latigo and felt the cinch tighten up; a hump came in his back and which made the saddle set near on end﻿—it was the hump that carried the punch in the buck, and most likely Clint could of led the pony around some till the hump wore down and his back straightened up again, but that rider wasn’t for taking the buck out of a bronc too quick. He believed a good sensible horse should buck at the first few “settings” and he wasn’t the kind of rider that’d smother that natural feeling and have it come out later, when the horse is supposed to be broke gentle.

===Chinese===
烟儿看着那人伸手去够拉蒂戈（latigo）革条，随即觉出肚带勒紧了；他背上鼓起一个疙瘩，鞍子险些给顶得立了起来——尥蹶子那股冲劲，正打这疙瘩里带出来。克林特满可以牵着小马遛上一阵，等疙瘩消了、脊背重新放平了再作道理；可那位骑手不愿这么快就卸掉一匹野马身上的蹶子。他信一条：一匹有脑子的乖觉好马，头几回「骑坐」就该尥一尥；他也绝不是那种骑手——把这天性生生捂死，留到日后马儿本该驯得温驯听话的时节再迸出来。

===Original===
He let the hump be and never moved the pony out of his tracks;﻿—he knowed that just one move would be enough to start that pony to exploding, and Smoky was set and just a waiting for that signal to start. He watched the cowboy raise his chaps so the belt wouldn’t hinder his leg action, watched him pull his hat brim down solid, and then he couldn’t watch no more. Something had come between him and his vision, it was the cowboy’s thumb which had layed over his left eyelid and pulled it down over his eye﻿—In the next second he felt a weight added on to that of the saddle, and all of a sudden he could see again.

===Chinese===
他由着那个疙瘩去，没挪动小马半步——他明白，只消一个动静，就够把这匹小马点着炸了；烟儿也早攒足了劲，单等着那一声号令。他看着牛仔把皮套裤往上提了提，免得裤腰碍腿脚；看着他把帽檐结结实实往下按；然后，他就再什么也看不见了。有什么东西隔在了他和他的视线中间——是牛仔的大拇指，覆上他的左眼皮，把眼皮往下捋，盖住了眼睛——下一秒钟，他觉出鞍子之外又添了一份分量，跟着眼前猛地又亮了。

===Original===
But what he did see left him stary-yed and paralized. For half a minute he just stood like petrified. That cowboy had disappeared from the side of him, and instead, there he was right in the middle of his back and on that hunk of leather he’d been hankering to shed off ever since it was put on there.

===Chinese===
可这一看，看得他两眼发直、浑身僵住。足有半分钟，他就那么像化石一样钉着。那牛仔从他身边消失了；却原来，正端坐在他脊梁正中，坐在那块打一搭上背、他就一直惦着甩掉的皮子上。

===Original===
Instinct pointed out only one way for him to act﻿—it was telling him that neither the human nor the leather belonged up there in the middle of him that way, and that if he tried he could most likely get rid of ’em. There was nothing else to do that he could see, and right then he felt that he sure must do *something*.

===Chinese===
本能只给他指了一条道——它告诉他：人也好，皮子也好，都不该那样子待在他脊梁正中；只要肯下力气，多半能把这两个都甩掉。在他看来，旁的也没什么可干的了，而那一刻他只觉得：自己非干出*点什么*来不可。

===Original===
His head went down, and a beller came out of him that said much as “I want you”﻿—Up went Smoky’s withers followed by the hump that made the saddle twist like on a pivot, and last came steel muscles like shot out of the earth, and which carried the whole mixed up and crooked conglameration of man and horse up in mid air and seemed like to shake there for a spell before coming down. All seemed heads and tails and made a picture of the kind that was mighty hard to see, and still harder to figger out.

===Chinese===
他的头低了下去，嗓子里滚出一声吼，差不多等于说了句「我冲你来啦」——跟着，烟儿的耆甲腾地拔起，背上那个疙瘩又把鞍子拧得像上了轴一般；末了是钢铁般的肌肉，像从地底下崩出来似的，把人马搅作的那一大团歪七扭八的大家伙整个掀上半空，仿佛还在半空里晃了一阵，才落下来。满眼只见马头马尾搅作一处，那画面，瞧着已经够费劲，要想瞧明白，更是难上加难。

===Original===
Saddle strings was a popping like on a whiplash, leather was a squeaking, corrals shook as the hard hitting hoofs of the pony hit the earth, and a dust was stirred that looked like a young cloud. Smoky was scared, mad, and desperate. All the action, strength, and endurance that was in him was brought out to do its best. Not a hair on his hide was laying idle thru the performance﻿—every muscle tightened and loosened in a way to shake the weight on his back and make it pop.

===Chinese===
鞍穗像甩鞭梢似的噼啪炸响，皮革吱嘎尖叫，小马铁硬的蹄子砸在地上，畜栏都跟着直颤，扬起的尘土活像一朵初生的小云。烟儿又是怕，又是怒，又是豁出去了。他身上所有的身手、力气和耐性，全都被使唤出来，各尽其能。这一场从头到尾，他身上没有一根毛是闲着的——每一块肌肉紧了又松、松了又紧，全是那一个路数：把背上那分量抖下来，抖它个噼啪乱响。

===Original===
Clint felt the muscles work even thru the saddle, and every part of that pony which his legs touched seemed as hard as steel and full of fast working bumps which came and went, twisted his saddle under him, and made him wonder if it was going to stay. It seemed like sometimes that Smoky was headed one way and his saddle another﻿—he wasn’t always sure of the whereabouts of that pony’s head; and in all his riding that’s what he wanted to keep track of most, cause losing track of a horse’s head at them times is something like riding blindfolded﻿—a rider would prepare for one kind of a jolt and meet another, which would cause things to scatter considerable.

===Chinese===
隔着鞍子，克林特也觉出那些肌肉在底下干活；他那两条腿贴着的小马身上，处处硬得像钢，满是飞快滚动、来了又去的肉疙瘩，把他胯下的鞍子拧过来拧过去，拧得他直犯嘀咕：这鞍子还坐不坐得住。有时候，烟儿明明朝这一头去，鞍子却像朝着那一头——那颗马头到底在哪儿，他未必时时拿得准。而骑了一辈子马，他顶要盯住的就是这个，因为这种时候一跟丢马头，就跟蒙着眼骑马差不太多——骑手备好了要挨这一下颠，迎面撞上的偏偏是另一样，身上零零碎碎的物件，可就要撒出去一大片了。

===Original===
Clint was still straight up and on top when Smoky’s hard jumps finally dwindled down to crowhops and then a stop. That pony was needing wind mighty bad, and as his nostrils opened wide, was taking in the necessary air, he felt a hand a rubbing along his neck, and wild eyed, ears cocked back at the cowboy that was still there, he stood and heard him talk.

===Chinese===
等烟儿那狠蹦猛跳末了缩成几下原地蹦跳、又归于停当，克林特还端端正正骑在上头没动。这小马缺气缺得厉害，鼻孔张得老大，正一口一口把救命的空气往肺里收；这时他觉出一只手顺着脖颈抚过来。他眼睛还是野的，耳朵朝后别着，对准那个居然还在的牛仔，站在那儿听他说话。

===Original===
“You done a mighty fine job, little horse,” says Clint, “and I’d been disappointed a lot not to’ve found that kind of spirit in a horse like you.”

===Chinese===
「干得真漂亮，小马，」克林特说，「像你这样的马里头，要是寻不出这股子精气神，我才要大大地失望呢。」

---

===Original===
If Smoky had been raised amongst humans like a dog and been with ’em steady that way, he’d of had a hunch or felt what Clint said and meant. But Smoky was a wild horse of the flats and mountains, and even though the sound of Clint’s tone and the feel of his hand soothed him some, he would buck again and again. It was his instinct to fight the human, and he would fight till that human showed he could handle him and proved a friend.

===Chinese===
倘若烟儿是打小在人堆里、像狗那样日日厮守着长大的，那克林特这话、这意思，他多少能有个感应，咂摸出来。可烟儿是平地和大山里的野马，克林特的声口、那只手的抚触，虽说也让他安生了些许，他还是要一而再、再而三地尥下去。跟人斗，是他的天性；他要一直斗下去，直到那个人显出降得住他的本事、证明确实够朋友为止。

===Original===
That had to be done gradual, and Smoky had no way to know as yet that man could be a friend of his, not while the breaking was going on anyway, for thru that spell a horse is *made* to do things he sometimes don’t want to do, and which all keeps down the confidence that would come faster if that didn’t have to be done.

===Chinese===
这得慢慢来。烟儿眼下还没法知道人也能做他的朋友——至少，开驯的这段日子里还不能；因为在这段日子里，马是被*逼着*去干他有时不愿干的事的，而这一切都压着那份信任，叫它长不快；这些事若不是非干不可，那份信任本会长得快得多。

===Original===
Smoky was doing some tall figgering as he stood there trembling and wondering if there wasn’t anything that he could get by with. He’d been made to do things just as that cowboy pleased and he’d found no say in the goings on, none at all. If he could only’ve bucked him off that would of pleased him a lot, but the little horse didn’t know that he wouldn’t of won anything by that;﻿—he didn’t know he was on this earth for the purpose of the human and that if he did throw one man another would climb him till finally he’d have to give in and go thru a lot of grief the while.

===Chinese===
烟儿站在那儿直打颤，一面可着劲儿盘算：能不能寻出一条蒙混得过去的辙。事情全凭那牛仔高兴，他一件件照办了，却发现这一切里头自己没有半句发言权，半句也没有。方才能把那人尥下去的话，那才叫他痛快呢；只是这小马不知道，那样他也赢不来什么——他不知道自己活在这世上，原是给人为着用的；也不知道哪怕甩下去一个，还会有另一个爬上来，直到他终于服软认输，还得白白搭上许多苦恼。

===Original===
Smoky felt a light slap on his neck. “Come on, young feller,” says the cowboy. “Lets see you trot around the corral a while.”

===Chinese===
烟儿觉出脖颈上挨了轻轻一拍。「来吧，小家伙，」牛仔说，「咱绕着畜栏小跑一阵瞧瞧。」

===Original===
But Smoky bucked more than he trotted. The cowboy let him, and when his head would come up he’d keep him on the go till finally there seemed to be no buck in the horse at all.

===Chinese===
可烟儿尥得多，跑得少。牛仔由他去；他头一抬，就催他接着动，直到末了，这马身上仿佛再挤不出半点儿蹶子来了。

===Original===
“I reckon that’ll be enough for you today,” says Clint as he headed Smoky for the side of the corral and made him face the bars to a stop. He then reached for the pony’s left ear and twisted it some, just enough to keep that pony’s attention on the twist of that ear most while he got off.﻿—

===Chinese===
「我看你今儿个到这儿也就够了。」克林特说着，把烟儿朝畜栏边上带，让他正对着栏杆收住脚。随后他伸手攥住小马的左耳，拧了那么一下——火候恰好，够让小马的心思整个拴在那只耳朵的酸劲儿上，他好趁这个空当下马。——

===Original===
Clint touched the ground with his right foot, and keeping his left in the stirrup, at the same time keeping close to the horse’s shoulder and out of the reach of his hind feet, he held that position for a few seconds. Smoky was watching him, shaking like a leaf and ready to paw the daylight out of the cowboy at the first wrong move or sudden jab of a knee.

===Chinese===
克林特右脚点上地，左脚仍留在马镫里，同时贴紧马肩、避开后蹄够得着的范围，就那么撑了几秒钟。烟儿盯着他，浑身抖得像片风里的叶子，只等他一有失着、或膝盖猛地一磕，就照这牛仔狠狠刨上家伙，刨得他魂儿都出窍。

===Original===
Clint *wanted* him to watch. This was part of the eddication, and all that cowboy wanted to teach right then was for Smoky to stand and not to go to acting up. Slow and easy, at the same time having complete control of himself and his horse, Clint raised himself up in the saddle again. It was done in a way that only bronc busters know. Smoky never even felt the pull on the saddle as the cowboy climbed on, and if that saddle hadn’t even been cinched it wouldn’t of budged then, so neat it was done.

===Chinese===
克林特*偏要*他看着。这也是调教里的一课，而眼下这牛仔要教的只有一条：烟儿要站着立定，不许撒性子闹腾。克林特慢而稳，一面把自己和自己的马都拿捏得纹丝不乱，一面重新在鞍上坐直了身子。这一手，只有驯野马手才拿得出来。牛仔上马的时候，烟儿连鞍子被拽了一下都没觉出来；哪怕那鞍子压根儿没勒肚带，那时候也纹丝不会动——这一手上得就是这么利落。

===Original===
Clint climbed on and off a few times that way, Smoky stood and shivered, scared, but willing it seemed like to take his medicine. Maybe it’d come to his mind that there was no use fighting that cowboy, or else he was getting tired﻿—anyway that was the last of it; Smoky felt the cinch loosen and then slow and easy the saddle was pulled off. About that time he whirled and faced the rider who was holding the saddle, he took a sniff at the hunk of leather and snorted like to say, “Gee! I thought that thing was on me for good.”

===Chinese===
克林特就这么上了又下、下了又上好几回；烟儿站着，直打哆嗦，心里怕，倒像是也肯认下这剂苦药了。也许是心里转过念头了：跟这牛仔斗，没个赢头；也许是乏了——反正，到此为止了。烟儿觉出肚带松了，跟着，那鞍子也被慢慢稳稳地卸了下来。他一转身，正对着抱鞍子的骑手，凑上去嗅了嗅那块皮子，打了个响鼻，像是在说：「嚯！我还当这玩意儿要跟我一辈子呢。」

===Original===
The saddle was set to one side and the cowboy begin rubbing Smoky’s back with a gunny sack, and according to the way that pony acted that felt mighty good, his upper lip stuck out and twitched with every motion of the rubbing, and when Clint finally quit, the little horse’s action showed plain that he should do it some more; Clint rubbed again.

===Chinese===
鞍子被搁到一边，牛仔拿起一条麻袋（gunny sack），在烟儿背上搓擦起来；瞧那小马的样子，这一搓实在受用得很——上嘴唇翘着，随着搓擦一下一下直抽动。末了克林特住了手，小马那意思明明白白：该接着搓。克林特便又搓了起来。

===Original===
“I’m afraid,” he says as he grinned and rubbed, “that I’m naturally going to spoil you. Here we just got thru with the first saddling and you’re beginning to look for favors already.”

===Chinese===
「我看哪，」他一边咧嘴笑着搓，一边说，「我天生只怕就是要把你惯坏喽。头一回上鞍才刚了，你这便讨起好来了。」

---

===Original===
Smoky’s picket grounds was moved to a fresh one for that night and where the grass was tall, a plenty and green﻿—but somehow his appetite wasn’t at its best, and when the break of day come there was very little sign (as Clint noticed) that the pony had et at all. He’d just stood in one spot, looked like, and seemed to’ve done tall wondering and figgering instead of feeding. He was ganted up the same as if he’d been rode all that night, and still there was no show of any appetite for the feed that was under and all around him.

===Chinese===
当天夜里，烟儿的拴场挪到了一处新地界，草又高、又密、又青——可不知怎么，他的胃口却不怎么济；等到天放亮，（克林特看在眼里）几乎瞧不出小马吃过草的痕迹。看那样子，他就在一个点上钉着站了一宿，把吃草的工夫都拿去发愣、盘算了。他瘪着肚、塌着膘，就跟叫人骑着跑了一整夜似的；可脚底下、四下里明明全是草料，他却半点胃口也不露。

===Original===
As Clint worked in the corral busy with other broncs he’d look thru the bars for any show of interest in the little horse; he’d look often but most every time that pony’s position was about the same, and if he did catch him with his head down he noticed how Smoky was just nibbling at the feed, and not eating much.

===Chinese===
克林特在畜栏里忙着调教旁的野马，时不时隔着栏杆张一眼，看那小马可有什么动静；他望得勤，可十有八九，那马还站在老位置；偶尔赶巧逮着他垂着头，也不过衔着草尖细细啃两口，并不真吃多少。

===Original===
Smoky was taking the change, from the life he’d led to what he was now going thru, kinda hard, harder than the average wild horse ever does; and Clint layed it that the little horse had more brains than the average, more sensitive maybe, and more able to realize.

===Chinese===
从先前过的日子换成眼下这一遭，烟儿有点儿接不住——比寻常野马接得都苦。克林特把这归结成一条：这小马比寻常的脑子多几道褶，兴许也更娇嫩些，更能把事情往心里去。

===Original===
“I guess I’d better lay off of him today,” decides the cowboy, as he noticed very little change in him even late that afternoon, “he’s having a hard time trying to figger things out as it is.”

===Chinese===
「我看，今天还是饶了他吧，」牛仔拿定了主意——那天挨到后半晌，瞧他也没见什么起色——「他眼下光琢磨这些事儿，已经够他受的了。」

===Original===
It was bright and early the next morning when Clint looked out of the bunk house door and noticed Smoky out on the creek bottom. It appeared that the little horse, after figgering and figgering, had come to some sort of decision, and that done and settled had went to eating again, for that’s what he was doing when Clint looked out﻿—Smoky was eating like he was trying to make up for the time he’d lost, and he seemed all at peace with everything in general.

===Chinese===
第二天一大早，天刚放亮，克林特从工棚门口朝外一望，瞧见烟儿在溪滩上。看那光景，小马盘算了又盘算，到底是拿定了什么主意；主意一拿定、落停，他便重新吃起草来——克林特望出去的当口，他正吃得起劲呢——烟儿吃得像要把耽误下的工夫都补回来，那副模样，像是跟世上的一切都讲和了。

===Original===
The cowboy grinned, “I know what that son of a gun has decided on,” he remarked. “He’s going to fight, and I see where I’m sure due for a tossing from that pony today.”

===Chinese===
牛仔咧嘴一乐。「我知道这家伙打的是什么主意了，」他说，「他要接着干。照这么看，今儿个我八成得叫他颠下来一回。」

===Original===
Clint done his day’s work, and after riding and lining out nine head of rough and kinky broncs, went to where Smoky was picketed and led him into the corral where he’d been initiated a couple of days before. He was some kind of a different horse than what he’d been that day; his head was higher and more with just one purpose. He didn’t shy and snort at every little thing like he did that first time, and Clint noticed that he never seemed to see the saddle as it was eased on his back and cinched.

===Chinese===
克林特干完了一天里自个儿的活计，骑顺了、勒齐了九匹又粗又倔的野马之后，才到烟儿拴着的地方去，把他牵进那座两天前头一回开导过他的畜栏。这回他跟那天比，可是换了一副心肠的马：头抬得更高，心里只装着一桩事。他不再像头一回那样见个什么小物件就躲、就打响鼻；鞍子轻轻放上背、肚带勒紧的工夫，克林特留意到，他竟像压根没瞧见似的。

===Original===
“I don’t like the sound of them ‘rollers’ that’s making that noise in them nostrils of yours,” he remarked; “they sound to me like you meant business.”

===Chinese===
「你鼻孔里那阵『咕噜噜』的动静，我可不大爱听，」他说，「在我耳朵里，那就是要来真章的意思。」

===Original===
Smoky did mean business, and even though Clint was doing considerable kidding, he meant business too. He wasn’t going to let the little horse get away with anything, for he realized that if he did it’d be harder than ever to persuade him to be good; he’d have to be treated rough, and Clint didn’t want to treat him rough.

===Chinese===
烟儿果真是要来真章；克林特嘴上虽说笑个没完，他也是要来真章的。这小马想蒙混过去一件事，他都不容——他明白，这回容了，往后劝他学乖就难上加难；到那时就非动粗不可，而克林特不想对他动粗。

===Original===
The cowboy seen the light in Smoky’s eyes and understood it, he understood his every action, and they all meant fight.

===Chinese===
牛仔望见烟儿眼里那道光，读懂了；他的一举一动，牛仔也全读懂了——全是一个意思：斗。

===Original===
“I’m glad to see so much spirit in you, old boy,” he says as he pulled his hat down, “but if you want to fight I’ll have to fight too, and here’s hoping the best one of us wins;﻿—let’s go.”

===Chinese===
「有你这么股子劲头，我真高兴，老伙计，」他一边把帽子往下按一边说，「可你要是想斗，我也只好奉陪。就盼着咱俩哪个本事大哪个赢吧。——走着。」

===Original===
Smoky only shook his head a little as Clint put his hand on his left eye and mounted, he didn’t want to notice a little thing like that, which was just as much a warning from him for that cowboy to get set, set well and solid, for in this next performance things was a going to pop worse than ever.

===Chinese===
克林特把手掌罩上他的左眼、翻身上了马，烟儿只轻轻晃了晃脑袋——这等小事，他懒得计较。而这，恰恰也是他递过去的一个信儿，告诉那牛仔坐稳、坐扎实了：因为接下来这一场，炸的动静要比哪一回都凶。

===Original===
There’s a big difference between the bucking that comes with the first setting of a bronc and the bucking that comes with the settings that follows afterwards on that same bronc. The first time Smoky was rode he was just a plain scared pony, of course his intentions was all to the good towards throwing that cowboy, saddle and all, off, but he was too scared and desperate to try and figger out how that should be done. He’d learned from that first setting that plain bucking wouldn’t faze that rider, he’d have to use some science, and with a cool head, study out the weak points the rider might have, and work on them weak points till a shadow on the ground tells him the cowboy is *leaving*.

===Chinese===
一匹野马头一回被骑坐时尥出来的蹶子，和往后各回骑坐尥出来的蹶子，中间隔着老大一截不同。头一回被骑，烟儿只是一匹怕得没魂儿的小马：把那牛仔连鞍带人甩下去，他的心思固然十足十都在这上头，可他怕得太狠、慌得太凶，静不下心来琢磨这事该怎么办才好。头一回骑坐教给了他：光凭傻尥，撼不动那位骑手；他得使点儿学问，得端着个凉脑袋，把骑手身上的空当一条条琢磨出来，再照着那些空当下死劲——直到地上的影子告诉他，那牛仔*这就告辞*。

===Original===
Smoky had learned that it wouldn’t get him anything to stampede hot headed into bucking like he did that first time, maybe that’s what he’d been studying on the last day or so. Anyway, he was some cool horse, and when he “bowed his head” this time it was all done deliberate and easy. He lined out with a few easy jumps just to sort of feel out how that cowboy was a setting as a preliminary, and with an eye back on all the movements of the rider as he went, he layed his plans on just how to proceed and get his man.

===Chinese===
烟儿已经学明白了：像头一回那样火一上头就不管不顾地炸开尥，捞不着半点便宜——前两天他闷头琢磨的，多半就是这个。反正，这一回他是匹相当沉得住气的马了；这一回他「低头弓背」，从头一式起，就都是不慌不忙、稳稳当当做出来的。他先顺顺当当地蹦了几下，权当个试探，掂一掂那牛仔坐着是什么路数；一面走，一只眼还留着后头，把骑手的一举一动全收在眼里，一面就把章程排定了：接下来怎么下手，怎么拿下他这个人。

===Original===
It was just when Clint seemed to be riding his easiest when without warning Smoky “broke in two” and brought out some mighty wicked saddle-twisting, and cowboy-loosening jumps; crooked, high, and hard hitting was them jumps. It looked to the horse like his man was loosened at the sudden turning of events and had been shifted to one side a little﻿—and that’s just what Smoky was looking for to carry on the program he’d mapped out.

===Chinese===
恰恰在克林特骑得最松快的当口，烟儿冷不丁「拦腰断成了两截」，抖出一串又拧鞍、又松人的狠蹦——那几下蹦得又斜、又高、又砸得瓷实。在马眼里，自己那个人被这突如其来的变故颠松了，还朝旁边偏出去了一星半点——而这正是烟儿等着的一手，好照他排好的章程接着往下演。

===Original===
It was the first encouragement that pony’d got since he first felt a rope on him, maybe he could get it over that cowboy yet. He bucked all the harder from the new energy the signs of winning brought him. No chance did he give so that the cowboy would ever get back in the saddle and straight up, and every jump from then on was used as a kind of leverage against the rider﻿—he bucked in a circle and every time he’d hit the ground he was his whole length back from where he’d started up.

===Chinese===
这是从他头一遭挨上绳子以来，到手的头一份鼓励——说不定，还真能把这牛仔治下去呢。眼看要赢的兆头给他添了新力气，他尥得愈发狠了。他再不给牛仔半个机会回到鞍上坐正；打这一刻起，每一下蹦跳都拿来当了对付骑手的杠杆——他兜着圈子尥，每回砸到地上，都比起跳的点整整退后一个马身。

===Original===
The cowboy was well up on the fork of the saddle and still to one side. Smoky bucked on, and cool as a cucumber in a mountain stream, kept a watching and took care that he didn’t buck back under him. He was holding his own, and looked for signs of the rider loosening some more, but no sign of that showed. The cowboy was still to one side and well up in the saddle, but he sure hung there, and with his left hand on the “Mecate” (hackamore rope) he kept his right up in the air and fanned on the same as ever.

===Chinese===
牛仔整个人顶到了鞍叉（fork）上，人还侧在一边。烟儿接着尥，凉得活像山溪水里泡着的一根黄瓜，眼睛留定着神，小心别尥回到人家正底下去。他稳住了自己的阵脚，只等着骑手再松动的迹象——可那迹象偏偏不露头。牛仔仍旧侧着、深深挂在鞍上，他倒是真吊得住：左手攥定麦卡蒂（Mecate）——笼头上那根缰绳——右手照旧举在半空，一下一下扇乎个不停。

===Original===
As the fight kept on and no show of the cowboy ever loosening up any more was seen, Smoky begin to wonder. He’d tried different tactics and with all his figgering and variety of sidewinding he couldn’t tear away from that hanging hunk of humanity. He was getting tired, his lungs begin to call for air and pretty soon he wasn’t so cool no more.

===Chinese===
这场斗一直僵着，那牛仔却再没露出半点松动，烟儿心里犯开了嘀咕。各路招数他都试遍了；任他怎么盘算、怎么横拧竖甩地变着法儿来，就是撕掳不开那一挂吊在自己身上的人肉疙瘩。他乏了，肺开始一声声要气；不多时，那股凉劲儿也再撑不住了。

===Original===
All that was in him, science and everything, was brought out on a few more earth shaking jumps, and when a glance back showed Smoky the rider was still setting there, he got desperate again and begin to see red. He bellered and at the same time forgot all he’d studied on in the ways of getting his man.

===Chinese===
他肚里的全部家当——学问什么的一股脑儿——都倾在又几下震得地动山摇的蹦跳上；回头一瞥，那骑手居然还坐着没挪窝。他又慌了、急了，眼里见了红。他吼出声来，与此同时，为拿下他那个人琢磨下的种种路数，忘了个一干二净。

===Original===
The fight didn’t last long after that, it was too furious and unscientific. Smoky fought the air, the earth, and everything in general﻿—nothing in particular was his aim, and pretty soon he lined out in long easy crowhops and then a standstill.

===Chinese===
打那儿以后，这场斗没撑多久——它狂得太狠，也全没了章法。烟儿冲着空气、大地和世上一切乱斗一气——并没有哪个具体的对头；不多时，他拉成长长几记松松快快的原地蹦跳，随后一个立定，站住了。

===Original===
Clint climbed off as Smoky stood spraddle-legged and took in the air. The little horse never seemed to notice him and in a hazy way felt the rider’s hand rubbing around his ears and straightening out his mane.

===Chinese===
烟儿四条腿叉开着站在那儿，大口大口倒气，克林特从马背上爬了下来。小马像没瞧见他似的，只在一片朦胧里觉出骑手的手在耳朵边上揉搓，把他的鬃毛一绺绺捋顺。

===Original===
“I knowed you’d give me a tossing today,” says Clint.

===Chinese===
「我就知道，你今儿个非把我颠下来一回不可。」克林特说。

===Original===
And there was one thing Smoky didn’t know: it was that no time during the fight did the cowboy feel he was losing his saddle; a setting to one side the way he had been was just a long-staying holt of his, something like a half nelson with the wrastler.

===Chinese===
只是有一桩事，烟儿不知道：这场斗里，牛仔从头到尾没觉得鞍子要丢；像他那样侧坐到一边，本就是他一门吊得住、耗得起的长久把式，好比摔跤的使一记半纳尔逊（half nelson）。

===Original===
Poor Smoky had lost again, but in a way he’d won﻿—he’d won the heart of a cowboy, cause, thru that fight that cowboy’s feelings was for the little horse. He’d seen, understood, and admired the show of thinking qualities and the spirit which was Smoky’s.

===Chinese===
可怜的烟儿又输了一场；可换一头说，他也赢了——他赢下了一个牛仔的心；因为经过这一场斗，那牛仔的心已经向着这小马了。他看出了、读懂了、也佩服上了那属于烟儿的东西：那副会动脑筋的心思，和那股劲头。

---

===Original===
The idea might be got, on account of Smoky being the steady loser, that his spirit would get jarred and finally break, but if anybody thinking so could of seen that horse the next day that idea would of been scattered considerable. His time on the picket rope had been spent on *more* thinking and figgering, and the way he went after the tall grass showed he meant to be in shape to carry thru whatever the new scheme was.

===Chinese===
烟儿场场皆输，照这么一想，他那股劲头怕是要被磕碰得发松，末了折断；可谁要是这么想，但凡能瞧见那马第二天的事，这念头准得给打散不少。他在拴马绳上的光阴，又都花在*更进一步的*琢磨和盘算上了；瞧他扑向那片深草的吃相就知道，他打定了主意要养足精神，好把他那套新章程从头撑到尾。

===Original===
And some would of thought it queer to’ve seen how Smoky, the steady loser in the contest, seemed to hold no grudge or hate against the winning cowboy. As it was, that pony seemed to welcome that human a lot as he walked towards him the next morning, and the way he rubbed his head against the shoulder of that smiling rider showed that the fights in the corral had got to be some friendly. Both was mighty serious, and both meant to win in them fights, but soon as they was over and the dust cleared there was a feeling the likes of when two friends have an argument; when the argument comes to an end both the loser and winner are ready to grin, shake hands, and be friends again.

===Chinese===
再说，瞧见烟儿这个场场皆输的输家，对那赢家的牛仔竟像是不存半点嫌隙、不藏半点怨恨，有些人怕要觉得稀奇了。事实是，第二天早上那人朝他走去时，这小马倒像是大大地欢迎他；他把脑袋往那笑呵呵的骑手肩膀上蹭的那股劲儿，说明畜栏里那几场斗，早就斗出几分交情来了。两个都顶较真，两个在那些斗里都存心要赢；可斗一完、尘土一落，那股滋味，就跟两个朋友拌完了嘴一个样——嘴一拌完，输家赢家都乐得咧嘴、握手，重又做成朋友。

===Original===
Smoky had lost out twice in trying to dodge out from under his man, but he was nowheres near convinced as yet that it couldn’t be done. The third time Clint climbed him that pony bucked harder than ever and that cowboy just sat up there and let him. Clint had whipped *some* horses for bucking that way, but he’d whipped them because it was natural orneriness that made ’em buck. With Smoky it was different, there was no meanness in him so far﻿—that pony was confident that nothing could set him once he got onto the hang of knowing how to buck real well, and all he wanted was to be *showed* for sure that Clint could really set there and ride him thru his worst that way. After that was done he’d most likely quit.

===Chinese===
烟儿两回想从自己那个人底下溜出去，两回都输了；可要说这事压根办不到，他还远远没服这个理。第三回克林特上马，这小马尥得比哪一回都狠，那牛仔就端坐上头，由着他尥。这样的尥法，克林特抽过*某些*马的鞭子——可他抽他们，是因为叫他们尥蹶子的是天生的浑劲儿。烟儿不一样，他身上到现在没半点坏心眼——这小马笃信，只消叫他摸透了怎么把蹶子尥到十足地道，天底下就再没有谁坐得住他；他只等着有人*拿实据证明*：克林特果真能那样坐定了，扛过他最狠的去处。这一点一坐实，他多半就此罢手了。

---

===Original===
The first couple of times Smoky was rode and after he’d quit his bucking, there hadn’t been much more to it excepting that Clint would just run him around a bit and turn him a few times till the hump was well down on that pony’s back. Smoky had got to thinking that was all would ever come of being corralled and saddled, and so, he was some surprised, when after the bucking spell was over at that third setting, to see the corral gate opened wide, the cowboy on him again, and heading him for open country.

===Chinese===
头两回骑烟儿，等他尥完了，后头也就没多少戏了：无非是克林特骑着他小遛一圈、调几个头，直到他背上那个疙瘩彻底落平。烟儿渐渐当真了：被关栏、被上鞍，往后能有的，也就不过这些。于是，等第三回骑坐、蹶子也尥完了，他瞧见畜栏大门敞然大开、牛仔又骑上他、把他朝开阔的野地里一带，还真有些出乎意料。

===Original===
Smoky took to the high ridges like a duck takes to water, he trotted out like a good horse, and then was put into a long lope. Covering territory felt mighty good to the little horse for a change and he wasn’t caring much where the cowboy lined him out to. For a spell he’d forgot the weight on his back, his ears was straight ahead, and the hands he felt on his neck only reminded some that somebody was *with* him.

===Chinese===
上了高高的山梁，烟儿就像鸭子下了水。他像匹像样的好马那样踢踢踏踏小跑出去，随后被放进一个长长的驰步。换一换路数、走一走大地，这对小马实在受用，牛仔把他朝哪一带，他也顾不得多计较了。有那么一阵子，他忘了背上的分量；两只耳朵笔直朝前，脖颈上觉出的那双手，只淡淡提个醒儿：有谁*陪*着他呢。

===Original===
He was needing that change after being bested again like he’d been that third time. Clint had won once more and Smoky was a lot in favor of something, most anything, to drive off the feeling he’d got in losing. He was taking advantage of the run in that way and sashayed at a good clip. All went fine, till, of a sudden a jack rabbit scared out of his hiding place jumped up and right under Smoky’s nose﻿—he shied straight up and to one side, and at the same time he was scared more by the wing of Clint’s chap which had curled up and slapped along his shoulder. Away he went to bucking once again.

===Chinese===
第三回又输了之后，他正需要这么换一换气。克林特又赢了一场，烟儿巴不得有点什么——差不多什么都行——把输了的那股滋味赶跑。这一路奔驰，他正好拿来派这个用场，跑得又轻又快。一切都顺顺当当，直到冷不丁一只长耳兔（jack rabbit）从藏身处惊起，蹦出来正打烟儿鼻子底下过——他猛地竖起、朝旁一闪；同一瞬间，克林特那片皮套裤的裤翼卷了起来，啪地拍上他的肩膀，把他吓得更狠。他撒开蹄子，又尥上了。

===Original===
The first few jumps was mighty wicked but they didn’t last; he’d already had his buck out not long before and pretty soon he straightened into a lope once again. Clint let him lope a ways then turned him and headed him back to the corrals, stopped him there, turned him a few times and started him out a ways only to turn him and bring him back again. That went on for a few minutes, and then Smoky was unsaddled and put on the picket rope once more.

===Chinese===
头几下蹦得凶极了，只是没能持久；前不多时他才尥空了一回，不多会儿便又顺成一个驰步。克林特由他驰了一段，然后调转他，把他朝畜栏带回，在那里收住，又左右调了几个头，放他出去一段，只为再调转、再带回。这么来回折腾了几分钟，烟儿便被卸了鞍，重新拴上拴马绳。

===Original===
The run had tired Smoky a little and give him an appetite. He didn’t do so much figgering on how to get his man that night, and instead he grazed more, rested some, and even slept a little. When he was led to the corral the next day and the saddle put on he even neglected to watch the cowboy and begin to show interest in the broncs that was in another corral. His ambitions hadn’t allowed him to do that before, but somehow, things had changed.﻿—Figgering ways and means of throwing off that rider had got to be tiresome, specially when nothing but disappointment was ever got by it; and besides that saddle and man was getting so they wasn’t so bad to stand up under no more.

===Chinese===
这一跑，把烟儿跑乏了一点，也跑出了胃口。那天夜里，怎么拿下他那个人，他没再多盘算；倒是多吃了一阵草，歇了歇乏，还打了一小盹。第二天被牵进畜栏、上好鞍子之后，他竟连牛仔都懒得盯了，倒对隔壁栏里那几匹野马来了兴致。从前的雄心是不许他这样的；可不知怎么，世道变了——琢磨怎么把那骑手甩下去，已经琢磨得乏了心；何况琢磨来琢磨去，到手的净是失望；再说，那鞍子和那个人，也渐渐背得惯了，不像先前那么难顶了。

===Original===
But as neutral as Smoky showed and felt, that little son of a gun bucked again. Of course there was nothing in his bucking that was so wicked as it had been in them first three saddlings; it was more that he felt he should buck *some*; it made him feel better, and besides he was wanting exercise; but he raised the dust and pounded the earth in good shape even at that, and that play of his would of throwed many a man.

===Chinese===
可是，任凭烟儿表现得、也觉得多么与世无争，那小家伙又尥了一回。当然，这一场里，再没有头三回上鞍时那种凶法了；多半是他觉得总该尥上*这么*几下——尥了，心里痛快；再说，他也想活泛活泛筋骨；饶是如此，他也照样扬起了尘土、把地砸了个瓷实，他这一手把戏，能颠下去许许多多的人。

===Original===
Another run like the one of the day before, a few turnings and teachings on the feel of the rein, and Smoky was thru for another day. He was getting used to the lay of the program Clint had set, and the new game that was brought on right along as he was rode begin to draw the pony’s interest.

===Chinese===
又照前一天那样跑了一回，又就着缰绳的劲儿调教、领教了几番，烟儿这一天便又交了差。克林特排下的这套规程，他渐渐惯了；随着骑坐一天天带出新名堂，那门新戏法也渐渐勾住了小马的心思。

===Original===
Then one day, the cowboy begin dragging a rope on him; he let it drag quite a ways, and even though Smoky watched it mighty close so it wouldn’t circle around his legs and throw him like most ropes always did, it didn’t worry him much. Pretty soon Clint coiled the rope up and made a loop which he started whirling in the air﻿—the whirling was slow and easy at first and done with a small loop. Smoky looked back all interest and snorted a little; he wondered what the rope was doing up there and what Clint was up to.

===Chinese===
后来有一天，牛仔开始在他身上拖一条绳，由着它拖出去老远。烟儿盯它盯得死紧，生怕它绕上自己的腿、像多数绳子一贯干的那样把他绊倒——可他也并不怎么发慌。不多时，克林特把绳子盘拢，甩出一个套圈，在头顶上旋了起来——起先旋得又慢又从容，圈子也小。烟儿回过头，满心好奇地瞧着，轻轻打了个响鼻；他纳闷，那绳子在天上做的是什么文章，克林特又要的什么花招。

===Original===
But nothing happened only that the whirling kept up, the loop was gradually made bigger and then it was throwed on the ground a ways in front of him. Smoky shied and snorted and the coils shot out, straightened, and all of it pulled up again by the cowboy; but he didn’t try to run away from it, he hadn’t forgot the eddication he’d received from the long soft picket rope. He’d learned from it that it didn’t pay to stampede when a rope was around, on account that them ropes had a way of stopping him that couldn’t at all be argued with.

===Chinese===
可什么也没发生，只有那旋着的圈儿一直旋着；圈子渐渐放大，随后被甩到他前头不远的地上。烟儿一躲、打了个响鼻；绳圈蹿出去、拉直，又整个儿叫牛仔收了回去。可他没想逃——那条又长又软的拴马绳教给他的那一课，他没忘。他从那课里学得：周遭有绳的时候，撒腿狂奔划不来，因为那些绳子自有治他的法子，那法子全没有商量的余地。

===Original===
Loops was made, throwed out, and drug in again one right after another. They went one side one time, and another side the next, then in front and back, till Smoky begin to lose fear no matter which way the rope went or how it coiled up. It was at the point when he was beginning to lose interest in the game that Clint roped a small bush. The rope tightened on it and Smoky pulled﻿—he pulled more in wonder what was holding him than with the idea of what he should do, but anyway the bush came out and headed straight for Smoky as it did, he struck at it and would of left from there, but Clint held him and made him face it.

===Chinese===
绳圈一个接一个：甩出去，又拖回来。这一回在这边，下一回在那边，忽前忽后，直到绳子往哪边走、怎么盘卷，烟儿都不怕了。正赶上他要对这把戏失去兴致的当口，克林特套住了一丛小灌木。绳一勒紧，烟儿便往后拽——与其说他想好了该怎么办，不如说他奇怪是什么东西拽住了自己；可那灌木还是被连根拽了出来，而且一出来就直奔烟儿而去。他抬蹄便刨，恨不得当场离开那地方，克林特却把他定住，逼他正对着它。

===Original===
Smoky shook like a leaf as slow but sure the cowboy kept a pulling the bush towards him, he struck again and snorted as it touched his front feet, and he bucked a couple of jumps when he felt it up along his shoulder, but there was no getting away from it; the way that bush moved, it looked like something vicious to Smoky, and when Clint took the rope off of it, and held it out under the pony’s nose for him to see what it was the little horse near showed signs of shame for getting scared.

===Chinese===
牛仔不紧不慢、一寸不让，把那丛灌木一拽一拽往他跟前带；烟儿抖得像片叶子。灌木碰上他的前蹄，他又刨又打响鼻；等觉出它蹭上了肩膀，他还尥了两下——可躲，是躲不掉的。那灌木走动的样子，在烟儿眼里邪性得很；等克林特把绳从它身上解下来，把它举到小马鼻子底下让他看个明白，这小马差一点儿露出害臊的意思来——为着自己方才那场惊慌。

===Original===
Loose stumps, branches, pieces of old wagons, and everything that could be drug or moved was roped﻿—anything that was light enough was pulled up for Smoky to investigate, and each time he was showed that he’d been shying and fighting for no reason, till finally, nothing could be found that brought any more than a snort from him. An old coal oil can was then roped and brought up a rattling under Smoky’s nose, but he even stood his ground at that.

===Chinese===
活树桩、断树枝、破马车的碎块——凡是拖得动、挪得动的，都套了个遍——够轻的，都拽到烟儿跟前让他一一查究；每回都叫他看清：他那些躲闪和招架，纯属白忙。到后来，再找不出哪样东西，能换他一个响鼻以上的动静了。随后又套来一只旧煤油罐（coal oil can），哐啷哐啷提到烟儿鼻子底下晃——可连这个，他也钉在原地没动。

===Original===
He was learned to pull on the rope and made to drag things as heavy as a yearling critter. Then gradually Clint made him keep the rope tight and hold it that way till a couple of light jerks on it made him give slack. All that took time, and the cowboy learned him only one thing each day, sometimes very little of that one thing﻿—but as the days went by it all accumulated to a lot.

===Chinese===
他被教会了拽绳，也被逼着拖过重得像一岁口牲口那样的东西。随后，克林特又一步步教他把绳绷紧、一直绷住，直到绳上轻轻两抖，他才把绳放软。这些都要工夫；牛仔一天只教他一桩事，有时候那一桩也只教一点点——可日子一长，点点滴滴攒下来，就是一大宗。

===Original===
It done Clint’s heart good to watch the way Smoky was taking to things, his little ears worked back and forth, and with his eyes he never missed a move that went on; his nostrils quivered at all that was new, and the cowboy was noticing with a glad feeling that the pony was putting a lot of trust in him. A word from that cowboy, or a touch from his hand, was getting to mean a lot when that pony was dubious or at the point of scaring at some new happening.

===Chinese===
瞧烟儿接东西接的那副样子，克林特打心眼里熨帖：两只小耳朵前前后后地转，眼睛里一步一动都不肯漏；遇上新鲜的，鼻翼便颤颤地翕动。牛仔也正带着一股欢喜留意到：这小马把好大一份信任，搁到了他身上。每逢小马心里犯嘀咕、或是叫什么新动静惊得要炸毛的当口，那牛仔的一句话、一抬手，分量已经重得很了。

===Original===
Clint hunted up a bunch of cattle one day and acquainted Smoky with some pointers in the handling of the critters. He’d haze the horse in the bunch, cut out some fat kinky yearling, and make him put his interest on that yearling only. All was a puzzle to Smoky at first, and he had no idea of what he should do, but Clint give him his time, and coaching him along it wasn’t but a few days when the little horse understood some of what was wanted of him. In the meantime the teachings with the rope wasn’t left behind; that went along with working cattle, and once in a while Clint would snare some big calf and make Smoky keep his nose along that rope while the calf circled, bucked, and bellered.

===Chinese===
有一天，克林特寻着一群牛，让烟儿上了头一课，教他摆弄这些牲口的几路门道。他把马轰进牛群，从里头剔出一头膘肥倔拗的一岁口，让他把心思全搁在那一位身上。起初，这一切对烟儿全是谜，他全然不知自己该干什么；克林特不催他，一路点拨着，没几天，小马便明白了人家要他干的是哪几样。与此同时，绳上的功课也没撂下——跟牧牛的活计齐头并进；隔三差五，克林特还会套住哪条大个儿的牛犊，让烟儿把鼻子贴着那根绳跟定，任牛犊兜圈子、蹦跳、哞哞地叫。

===Original===
Smoky showed signs of liking all that went on. He took interest in it the same as a kid would to some new game﻿—he liked to chase the wild eyed cow, turn her when she didn’t want to be turned, and put her where she didn’t want to be put; he liked to hold the rope tight on one of the critters and feel that *he* was the one that was keeping ’er down. It all struck him as a kind of a game where every animal before him had to do as *he* and the cowboy wished.

===Chinese===
对这一切，烟儿都露出了喜欢的意思。他上心的那股劲儿，就跟小孩子得了样新游戏一样——追那头瞪圆了眼的母牛，她不想转头偏叫她转，她不想去哪儿偏把她送哪儿去，他都乐意；把绳绷紧在哪个牲口身上、觉出压着她的正是*自己*，他也乐意。这一切在他想来，成了一门游戏：凡是立在他跟前的牲口，都得照*他*和牛仔的意思办。

===Original===
He was all for catching on and not a nerve in him was idle as Clint would take him of evenings and ride him out for a spell, and chase, cut out, or rope at the critter. Them goings on had his mind occupied and the fact that he’d figger and think on the subject between times was proved by the way he’d go at things in a decided and knowing how way, when the day before the same thing had left him puzzled and wondering.

===Chinese===
他一门心思就要学个通透。克林特常在傍晚牵他出去骑上一阵，追一追、剔一剔，或是朝牲口甩两绳，那工夫，他浑身没有一根神经闲着。这些营生占满了他的脑子；而歇着的空当里，他还在这个题目上盘算、琢磨——证据是：头一天还叫他犯难发懵的活计，隔天再上手，他已经做得干脆利落、一副胸有成竹的架势。

===Original===
That little work he was getting and the all heart interest he was finding in it, had settled him to the big change from the free life he’d led with the old buckskin horse and the bunch of mares and colts﻿—his mammy was even forgotten, and instead there’d sprouted in him something that made him take a liking for the long lanky cowboy that came to see and *play* with him every day. He’d got to finding a lot of pleasure in doing just what that cowboy wanted him to do, and when that was done there was a hankering in him to do just a little bit more.

===Chinese===
手头这点小活计，和他一门心思扎进去的那股全副热乎劲儿，把他从先前跟着老兔褐马和那群骒马驹子过的自在日子，稳稳当当安顿进了这场大变故——连马妈妈都忘在了脑后；反倒有个什么在他心里发了芽，叫他一天天喜欢上那个瘦长条的牛仔——那个天天来看他、陪他*玩耍*的人。照那牛仔的意思把事办好，他已经从中得了好大的乐子；事办完了，心里还痒痒的，想再多办那么一点点。

===Original===
That’s the way Clint wanted to keep him; just a hankering to do more would get results, and he was careful to see that the little horse didn’t tire on the work. He wanted to make it play for him and keep it that way as long as he could, for he knowed that was the way to keep Smoky’s heart and spirit all in one hunk and intact.


===Chinese===
克林特要的，正是叫他这么保持着——心里那点「再多干些」的馋劲儿，自会出成效；他也留神盯着，别让小马在活计上乏了心。他要把这活计给马当成玩耍，并且能当多久是多久，因为他心里有数：唯有这样，烟儿的心和他的劲头，才能囫囵一块、完完整整地保得住。

## VII / 七

===Original===
Smoky Shows His Feelings

===Chinese===
烟儿露了真情

===Original===
Jeff Nicks, cow foreman of the Rocking R outfit, was riding along and headed for the horse camp where Clint was breaking horses. Spring works was over and Jeff thinking it was a good time for him to do a little lone riding and kinda visit the camps of the outfit, had left his straw boss in charge of the wagon, caught his best horse and strung out to cover some of the Rocking R territory.

===Chinese===
杰夫·尼克斯（Jeff Nicks）是摇椅R班底的牧牛工头，正一路骑行，奔着克林特驯马的那处马营去。春牧的活计已经收尾，杰夫寻思，这正是自个儿单溜一趟、顺道把班底各处营地转一转的好时候，便把大车撂给副工头（straw boss）照管，牵出自己顶好的马，动身去巡一巡摇椅R的地界。

===Original===
It was a hot day, not a breath stirred the air; and as the old cowman rode he lifted his hat often to kinda let a fresh supply of atmosphere come in underneath. His big brown horse was covering ground in a running walk, and Jeff keeping him down to that gate wasn’t passing a coulee nor a draw without a glance in it and then to the skyline above. It was his habit as a cowman to keep his eyes on the job while riding, and for the good of the company or his own, nary a thing had ever escaped his vision unless it was just too far for that vision to reach.

===Chinese===
那天热得很，一丝风也不搅动这空气；老牧牛人一路骑着，时不时抬一抬帽子，好让帽子底下透进一股新鲜气。他那匹高大的褐色马迈着跑走步赶路，杰夫把他按在这个步子上，逢着一条干沟（coulee）或一道洼地，必要朝里头瞟上一眼，再望一望上头的天际线。骑着马眼睛不离活计，这是他当牧牛人的老习惯；不论为着公司好还是为着自个儿好，还从没有一样事物逃过他这双眼睛——除非那事物实在是远，远得这双眼睛够不着。

===Original===
It was as he was riding along natural that way, that he noticed a thin streak of dust to the right of him quite a ways; that dust wasn’t made by anything traveling fast, and even though it reached up in the air good and high Jeff could see at a glance that the dust was stirred by something dragging.

===Chinese===
他正这么自然而然地骑着，忽然注意到右手边老远的地方腾起一线尘土；那尘土不是什么跑得飞快的东西扬出来的；它虽说蹿到半空里老高，杰夫却一眼看得出：这是叫什么东西拖拽着搅起来的。

===Original===
He stopped his horse so as to get a steadier view, and pretty soon he could make out the shape of a horse underneath that dust; something that looked like a turned pack was fastened or hanging on to him and dragging alongside.

===Chinese===
他勒住马，好瞧得稳当些；不多时，便认出那尘土底下有一匹马的身形；有个看上去像翻倒的驮包似的东西，拴在他身上还是挂在他身上，在旁边一路拖着走。

===Original===
Jeff had seen many happenings on the range between man and horse and from that figgered to always investigate anything that suspicioned of something gone wrong, and to investigate quick.﻿—He put his horse in a high lope. Down draws, over rolling hills, and acrost dog towns he went all at the same speed, and pretty soon he comes to where there’s only a small ridge between him and what he wanted to investigate.

===Chinese===
牧场上人与马之间的种种世事，杰夫见得多了，打这儿他得出一条道道儿：但凡有什么瞧着可疑、像是出了岔子的，就得去看个究竟，还得快着点看。——他把马催成大跑。下洼地，翻滚丘，穿过一座座犬鼠城（dog towns），他一路全是这个速度，不多时便到了一处地方，跟他要查探的东西中间只隔一道小山梁了。

===Original===
It was then that he figgered it best to take it slow till he’d seen just what was up; if some rider had got caught in his riggin’ some way as a horse fell, and that horse was wild and unbroke, riding in on a high lope would only make things worse and cause the horse to stampede.﻿—Nobody knowed that better than Jeff did.

===Chinese===
到这时他盘算着，还是放慢些好，先看明白到底怎么回事；万一是有哪个骑手在马摔倒时让鞍具缠住了，而那又是一匹没驯服的野马，你大跑着马冲过去，只会把事情弄得更糟，闹得那马撒腿狂奔。——这个道理，没人比杰夫更明白。

===Original===
He got off his horse, walked a ways, and peeking thru the tall grass seen the whole goings on at a glance. Fifty yards below him was a mouse colored horse﻿—looked like a half broke bronc to him on account of the way the hackamore was rigged﻿—but that horse didn’t act like half broke. He was going thru a performance that most gentle broke range horses wouldn’t put up with, and that was to half carry and half drag a man, *and on the wrong side*.

===Chinese===
他下了马，走了一截，从高草缝里窥过去，一眼瞧见了全部光景。下方五十码开外是一匹鼠灰色的马——瞧那笼头拴套的路数，他看像匹半驯的野马——可那马的做派又不像半驯的。他正经过的那一出，连大多数调教得服服帖帖的牧场马都消受不了：半驮半拖地带一个人，*而且还是在错的那一边*。

===Original===
Jeff recognized that man as his “bronc peeler” Clint, and he was all for rushing down to see what had happened and help, but he held back﻿—he wasn’t sure but what the mouse colored horse would scare and run away at the sight of him, and he couldn’t tell but what Clint’s hands was fastened to the saddle horn the way he was hanging on.

===Chinese===
杰夫认出那人正是他手下的「驯野马手（bronc peeler）」克林特，一心想冲下去看看出了什么事、搭上一把手，可他忍住了——他吃不准那匹鼠灰色马见了自个儿会不会受惊逃开；克林特那么吊挂着，他也说不准那两只手是不是卡死在了鞍角上。

===Original===
He could see there was still life in the rider, but if the man was conscious he wasn’t showing very good sense by hanging on the wrong side of a half broke horse that way. Still, as he watched, Jeff begin to wonder. He noticed for one thing that the horse was headed straight for camp, Clint’s camp, and then there was another thing he noticed and which made him wonder and watch more than ever﻿—The mouse colored gelding wasn’t dragging his man, he was more kinda helping him along, seemed like. Each step that horse took was with care and in favor of the man alongside; the pony watched every move that man made, and if the steps sorta lagged or hesitated he stopped or slowed down till the man braced up some and went again.

===Chinese===
他看得出骑手身上还有活气儿；可这人要还清醒着，那么吊在一匹半驯马的错边上，可实在算不得有见识。不过，瞧着瞧着，杰夫渐渐纳起闷来。头一样，他留意到那马直奔营地而去——克林特的营地；跟着又瞧出另一桩，叫他越发睁大眼睛盯住不放——那匹鼠灰色骟马并不是在拖拽他的人，倒像是一路照应着他往前走。那马每迈一步都带着小心，处处偏就着身旁的人；小马盯着那人的一举一动，那脚步要是有点儿拖沓、有点儿迟疑，他就站住或放缓，等人缓过一口劲、重新迈开步，再走。

===Original===
Jeff’s mouth was wide open with wonder as he watched the goings on, and when a little while later the gelding happened along a big rock, and seen him stop while the man tried to use the rock to get from it up in the saddle, Jeff wondered some more.

===Chinese===
杰夫瞧着这一幕，嘴张得老大；过了一小会儿，那骟马走到一块大石头跟前，杰夫眼见他在石头旁站定，让人家借那块石头往上爬鞍，就越发纳闷了。

===Original===
“By japers, I’ve seen and handled thousands of horses,” says Jeff, “but I never thought any horse ever had that much sense.”

===Chinese===
「老天爷，经我眼、过我手的马，成千上万，」杰夫说，「可我从来没想过，哪匹马能有那份心眼。」

===Original===
The old cowman watched for near a half an hour while Clint tried to get on his horse. He seen the horse stand there, all patience and a helping the best he could, and finally, with the help of the rock, the favoring of the horse, and the little strength the man had, and all put together, Clint was setting in the saddle at last. The hackamore reins was hanging loose: nothing was holding that pony from bucking, stampeding, or doing anything he pleased, but he stepped slow and easy, and ears cocked ahead, packed his man to camp with the same care any human would take.

===Chinese===
老牧牛人瞧了差不多半个钟头，克林特一直在试着上马。他瞧见那马立在那儿，一肚子的耐性，能帮的都尽力帮了；到末了，凭着那块石头，凭着马的成全，再加上人身上仅剩的那点儿力气，几样合在一处，克林特总算坐进了鞍子。笼头的缰绳松松垂着：没有什么拦着这小马不尥蹶子、不狂奔、不随心所欲，可他迈得又慢又稳，两只耳朵朝前支棱着，把他的那人驮向营地，那份仔细劲儿，跟随便哪个人都一般无二。

===Original===
Jeff got on his horse and keeping well behind followed. What he’d just seen had got him to the point where he begin a talking to himself, his horse, and the country around.

===Chinese===
杰夫上了马，远远缀在后头跟下去。方才见的那一幕，把他弄到了自言自语的地步——跟自己说，跟马说，跟四下的荒野说。

===Original===
“Yessir, by japers, and he let Clint get on him from the wrong side too, why this daggone old gentle horse I’m riding now wouldn’t let me do that﻿—But then, maybe I better not be too sure about that, I’m beginning to believe from what I’ve just seen that there’s things going on in horses’ think tanks that’s mighty surprising and which don’t come out till the right time shows up.”

===Chinese===
「可不是嘛，老天爷，他还让克林特从错边上了马呢——我胯下这匹该死的温驯老马，可不肯叫我这么来——不过话说回来，这话我也别咬得太死；照方才见到的，我算开始信了：马儿们那脑瓜里转悠着的事儿，才叫惊人呢，只是不到节骨眼上，轻易不露出来。」

===Original===
A couple of hours and the camp was reached. Jeff looked around the big corrals as he rode closer for signs of Clint and the mouse colored bronc, and sure enough, there the both of ’em are﻿—Clint is still in the saddle and to all appearance unconscious; the gelding is standing by the corral gate, still, and waiting.

===Chinese===
两个来钟头，营地到了。杰夫骑近时，绕着那几座大畜栏张望克林特和那匹鼠灰色野马的影踪；果然不差，两个都在——克林特还在鞍上，瞧那样子全然人事不省；骟马立在畜栏门边，一动不动，等着。

===Original===
The cowman rode on towards ’em, but he soon had to stop, for he noticed as the gelding sees him how by that pony’s action, he wasn’t for standing in one spot no longer at the sight of a strange rider coming on him that way. Jeff had to maneuver around considerable to keep that horse from hightailing it. The only way he could do it was to go back the way he came till out of sight, once there he circled around till he came up on the camp from the opposite side, the corrals and a long shed was between him and the half broke horse with his unconscious rider.

===Chinese===
牧牛人朝他们骑过去，可很快就不得不停住，因为他瞧出来，那骟马一看见他，凭那小马的做派，是见了生人骑手这么直通通过来，不肯再在一个点上待下去了。杰夫只得绕来绕去费好大的事，才没叫那马撒腿开溜。唯一可行的法子，是顺着来路退回去，退到瞧不见的地方，到了那儿再兜个圈子，从营地另一头摸上来，让畜栏和一座长棚子横在他和那匹驮着不省人事骑手的半驯马中间。

===Original===
Jeff left his horse out of sight, and hugging close to the shed made his way to where the mouse colored gelding had been; a peek thru a hole in the wall showed him the horse was still there, and Clint still in the saddle. How to proceed from then on was a sort of ticklish proposition. Jeff didn’t want the pony to get scared, run away and throw the hurt rider, and still, he couldn’t let the rider stay where he was.

===Chinese===
杰夫把自己的马撂在望不见的地方，贴着长棚子的墙根，摸到那匹鼠灰色骟马先前待的地方；从墙上的一个窟窿里窥了一眼：马还在那儿，克林特也还在鞍上。打那儿往后该怎么办，倒成了件棘手的事。杰夫既不想叫小马受惊、跑起来把伤着的骑手甩下去，可又不能叫骑手就这么待在他待的地方。

===Original===
He had to take a chance and do the best he could.
