Around the corner of the shed he came, and slow and easy, showed himself to the wild eyed gelding; he talked to him, and that seemed to help some, for the little horse stood his ground. *Stood his ground* is correct, but Jeff had hesitated somehow from coming any closer﻿—he noticed a light in that pony’s eyes which warned him plain to keep his distance, and even though Jeff was half peeved and half leery at the stand the pony had took, he couldn’t help but admire the show of liking that half broke gelding had for the rider that was still unconscious in the saddle, and laying with his head on the pony’s bowed neck.

===Chinese===
他只好冒个险，尽人事往前走。他从棚子墙角转出来，又慢又稳地，把自己的身子亮给那匹瞪圆了眼的骟马；他跟他说话，这倒像有些用处，小马守住了阵地。说*守住阵地*，是一点水分不掺的；可杰夫自己却不知怎么，不敢再往前凑——他瞧见小马眼里透着一道光，明明白白警告他保持距离。杰夫对这小马摆出的架势，一半着恼，一半发怵，可他不由得不佩服：这匹半驯的骟马，对还昏在鞍上、脑袋正歪枕在他弯下的脖颈上的那个骑手，竟有这样一份明明白白的情义。

===Original===
The horse’s actions had all been a puzzle to Jeff at first, and as he finally understood, it all left him mighty surprised and in a trance with wonder. He’d expected that horse to start running away at the sight of him, but instead, he was showing fight. The pony wasn’t wanting to go no further with the hurt rider, he wasn’t going to trust no strange human with that helpless pardner of his.

===Chinese===
这马的一举一动，起初在杰夫眼里整个是个谜；待他终于明白过来，只剩下一肚子的惊异，人跟失了魂似的怔在那里。他原打算着那马一见他就会撒腿跑掉，结果人家亮出来的，是应战的架势。小马不肯再驮着伤号往前走；他那个没了依着的伙伴，他也不肯托付给任何生人。

---

===Original===
Two months or more had passed since Clint and Smoky had met in the dust of the bare corral. In that time the man and horse’d had fights; some had been mighty wicked, and the wild horse would of killed the man too if the chance had come, but all thru them fights the man had won﻿—slow and easy, but he’d won. Then gradually Smoky begin to get confidence in the human, and then a liking; he’d got to looking for his company and would nicker with a glad feeling as he’d see that human come towards him of evenings, and he’d go the length of his picket rope to meet him.

===Chinese===
自打克林特和烟儿在那座光秃秃的畜栏的尘土里碰头，两个多月过去了。这中间，人和马交过手；有几场凶险得很，那野马但凡捞着机会，连人的命也要了去；可一场场斗下来，赢的都是人——赢得又慢又稳，可到底是赢了。渐渐地，烟儿对这个人先有了信任，跟着又有了情分；他盼起他来了，傍晚一望见那个人朝自己走来，便低嘶一声，声里带着欢喜，还要挣到拴马绳绷直的那一头去迎他。

===Original===
Steady good treatment from the rider, no matter what the horse done, had won that pony’s heart, till the little horse could near be seen smiling with the happy feeling that was his every time Clint came, saddled him, and rode him out for a little play with the rope and critter.

===Chinese===
骑手待他始终好——不拘马闹出什么来——就这么把小马的心赢到了手；到后来，每回克林特来、给他上鞍、骑他出去拿套索和牲口练上一小场，那小马那股欢喜劲儿，几乎都能从脸上看出笑来。

===Original===
That’s the way Smoky’s feelings had come to be for the bowlegged rider, and taking all as was, it’s no wonder the horse showed fight when a strange human appeared. In his life Smoky had seen no other but Clint; he knowed *him*, but he didn’t know the others, and he had no more love for them than he had when he was first run in from his free range. Them others was still enemies to him, and right then when that pony felt his pardner was depending on him most, he was sure ready to paw the daylight out of that stranger if he came any closer. He was his enemy, and according to his way of thinking, he was or should be Clint’s enemy too.

===Chinese===
烟儿对那个罗圈腿骑手的情分，就是这么来的；把前后一切算上，怪不得生人一露头，这马就摆出打的架势。烟儿这辈子见过的，只克林特一个；他认得*他*，旁人他一概不识，对他们的好感，也还跟头一回被人从自由牧场上赶进来时一样，一分没添。那些人于他还是敌人；而眼下，正当他觉得伙伴最指望他的时候，那生人胆敢再走近一步，他是真预备着照他刨过去，刨得他天昏地暗。那是他的敌人；照他的想法，那人也就是、也该是克林特的敌人。

===Original===
Jeff stood there figgering for quite a spell a trying to digest and believe what that pony showed. It couldn’t come to him to hurt or kill such a horse so as to get the man, and he’d just decided to get his rope, throw a loop over his head and snub him close to the corral, when the rider begin to show signs of life.

===Chinese===
杰夫站在那儿盘算了老半天，想消化、想相信小马亮出来的这一切。为了救那个人，去伤这么一匹马、结果他的性命，这他下不去手；他刚拿定主意，去取套索，甩个圈儿套上那马的头，把他死死拽靠到畜栏边上，鞍上的人却显出活气儿来了。

===Original===
“Come to, Clint,” hollered the cowman as he noticed the rider move, “and get off that horse.”

===Chinese===
「醒醒，克林特，」牧牛人一见骑手动弹，扯开嗓子喊，「醒过来，从马上下来。」

===Original===
Clint raised his head some at the sound of the voice, and as Jeff kept a speaking to him he made a big effort to understand and try to do as he was told. Pain showed in his face as he tried to straighten himself in the saddle, and as Jeff feared that the rider would lose consciousness again he hollered at him not to try to straighten up, but just slide off and hang on.

===Chinese===
听见这声音，克林特把头略略抬起了一些；杰夫不住口地跟他说着话，他便使出老大一股劲，想听明白、想照吩咐去做。他在鞍上试着坐直，脸上顿时显出痛来；杰夫生怕骑手再昏过去，大声喊他别急着坐直，只管滑下来，抓牢就是。

===Original===
With a lot of pain and time and coaching from Jeff, Clint finally managed to raise one leg over the cantle of the saddle and let himself slide to the ground. Smoky stood still as a statue and as solid, his eyes was on Jeff with a steady warning for him to keep his distance﻿—and Jeff did.

===Chinese===
忍着大痛，费了好些工夫，加上杰夫一路指点，克林特总算把一条腿抬过了鞍子的后鞍桥，任由自己滑到地上。烟儿站着，像一尊石像那样稳、那样实；一双眼睛钉在杰夫身上，一眨不眨地警告他保持距离——杰夫也真就保持了。

===Original===
“Hang on to the saddle,” coached Jeff, “try and get the horse thru the gate in the corral, and I’ll close the gate on him.”

===Chinese===
「抓着鞍子别撒手，」杰夫指点着，「想法子把马引进畜栏的门里去，门我来关。」

===Original===
That was done in time, and as the gate was closed Clint’s hands went limp and he fell to the ground. Lucky it was that Jeff could reach him thru the corral bars, but he had to do considerable maneuvering even then to get the cowboy thru and under so as not to stir Smoky. And it was a mighty good thing for Jeff as he picked Clint up and started towards the house that there was bars high and strong between him and that pony, for as high and strong as that corral was Jeff worried some and, looking back over his shoulder as he went, wondered if it would hold him.

===Chinese===
这事办得正及时，门一关上，克林特两手一软，瘫倒在地。幸亏杰夫隔着畜栏的栏杆够得着他；饶是那样，他也费了好一番腾挪，才把牛仔连拉带拽从栏杆底下弄出来，没惊动烟儿。而杰夫抱起克林特朝屋子走去的时候，横在他和那小马中间的是又高又结实的栏杆——这对他实在是桩天大的好事；因为那畜栏尽管又高又结实，杰夫心里还是犯着几分嘀咕，一边走一边回头张望，拿不准它到底拦不拦得住他。

---

===Original===
The sun had sunk away, and dark had come before Clint came to well enough so things was plain to him and he could talk. Jeff had made him as comfortable as was possible, boiled some jerky and made a strong broth which he was holding under Clint’s nose for him to sniff at.

===Chinese===
日头沉了下去，黑天上来，克林特这才缓醒到能把事情瞧明白、能开口说话的地步。杰夫把他安顿得能多妥帖就多妥帖，煮了些肉干，熬出一锅浓浓的汤，正端在克林特鼻子底下让他闻。

===Original===
That cowboy sniffed, looked around, and then said, “where’s Smoky?”

===Chinese===
牛仔嗅了嗅，四下里望了望，然后说：「烟儿呢？」

===Original===
“If you mean that mouse colored fighting son of a gun of a horse you was on,” says Jeff, “why he’s in the corral, and a fretting his head that I’m going to eat you up.”

===Chinese===
「你要说的是你骑的那匹鼠灰色的、好斗的浑家伙马，」杰夫说，「喏，在畜栏里呢，正焦心着呢，愁我要把你给吞吃下肚喽。」

===Original===
Clint couldn’t quite get the meaning of that just then, and he asked, “I wonder if you wouldn’t go take the saddle off of him and put him on the picket rope where he’d get something to eat. He’s gentle, and you can handle him easy.”

===Chinese===
克林特一时没大咂摸出这话的意思，便问：「麻烦你去把他身上的鞍子卸了，给他上拴马绳，好让他吃上口东西，行不行？他温驯，你摆弄他容易。」

===Original===
Jeff snorted and laughed, “gentle,﻿—﻿—? I wouldn’t try to handle him if you’d give me this whole outfit, I’m not enough of a bronc fighter no more, and that ain’t all. That pony is just a hankering for me to stick my beezer thru that corral.”

===Chinese===
杰夫哼了一声，笑了。「温驯，———？你就是把这个摊子整个儿白送我，我也不去招惹他；我如今早不是够斤两的斗马汉子了，这还没算完——那小马正巴望着我把鼻子伸进那畜栏缝里去呢。」

===Original===
Smoky circled around the corral not at all minding the saddle that was on him; he wasn’t caring for any grass either, he was too peeved and restless. If Clint had been right side up and able, things would of been different and Smoky would of hardly even noticed the stranger.﻿—There seems to be a heap of difference in the feelings of any thinking animal when a pardner is sick or dying﻿—the little horse knowed as well as any human that something had went wrong with his pardner, and the appearance of the stranger at such a time was worrying him.

===Chinese===
烟儿绕着畜栏转圈，身上还驮着鞍子，他全不理会；草也一口不吃，他又恼又躁，安不下来。要是克林特好好的、能走能动，那就是另一回事了，那生人烟儿怕是压根儿都不会留意。——但凡有点灵性的牲口，伙伴病了、要不行了的时候，那份心境同平日差着老大一截——小马跟随便哪个人一样明白：他的伙伴出了岔子；偏偏这么个当口冒出个生人来，叫他心里发慌。

===Original===
The next day was well along and the sun getting high, when Jeff helped Clint on his feet and half carried him towards the corral where Smoky had put in the night. Clint staggered on alone from the gate and the little horse nickering came to meet him﻿—his ears was all ahead and with his eyes a shining; he looked all interest and like he wanted to ask questions. He then spotted Jeff, and at the sight of him, his expression changed, his eyes showed fire, and his ears layed back on his neck.

===Chinese===
第二天，时候已经不早，日头也爬得高了，杰夫扶克林特起身，半抱半架地朝烟儿过了夜的畜栏走去。打从门口起，克林特独自摇摇晃晃往前挪，小马低嘶着迎上来——两只耳朵齐齐朝前竖着，眼睛亮亮的；一脸的关切，活像有一肚子话要问。跟着他瞧见了杰夫，一瞧见，神色就变了，眼里蹿出火来，两耳唰地伏倒，贴在了脖子上。

===Original===
“Well, I’ll be daggoned,” says Clint as he noticed the horse’s actions. He looked back at the old cowman and grinned, wondering﻿—but the old cowman wasn’t grinning any. Jeff figgered it best for him to vanish for a spell. Smoky was unsaddled, and put on good feed and water, which all seemed to take Clint a powerful lot of time; but he finally showed up and Jeff helped him back to the house.

===Chinese===
「嗬，我算是开了眼了，」克林特瞧见马的做派，说道。他回头望了望老牧牛人，咧嘴一笑，心里纳着闷——可老牧牛人一丝笑也没有。杰夫寻思，自己最好先避一阵。烟儿卸了鞍，吃上了好草好水；这些事克林特办起来像是费了老鼻子的工夫，末了他总算又露了面，杰夫扶他回屋去了。

===Original===
It was on the way over that Clint begin to speak, and on a subject that’d been on his mind for a long time. “You know, Jeff,” he says, “I think the time has come for me to quit riding broncs, I feel like I better quit, specially after this last that’s happened to me.”

===Chinese===
就在回去的路上，克林特开了腔，说的是在他心头搁了许久的一桩事。「你是知道的，杰夫，」他说，「我看哪，我到了该撂下野马不骑的时候了；我觉得还是撂下的好，尤其是刚经了这么一回。」

===Original===
“What *did* happen anyway?” asks Jeff.

===Chinese===
「到底*是*出了什么事？」杰夫问。

===Original===
“It was all on account of a fool cow,” starts Clint, “she’d showed signs of wanting to leave the country soon as she seen me riding up on her, and being she was good and fast, I figgered it’d be a good time to line Smoky out after her and let him turn ’er over a few times. I throwed my rope but the loop didn’t land good, it just sorta sailed in front of her, and she stepped in it. About that time I jerked up my slack and I jerked it too hard. Down went the critter all in a heap and sudden, so sudden that with the speed Smoky was going he couldn’t stop in time, and first thing we knowed we both was straddle the critter.

===Chinese===
「全是叫一头蠢牛闹的，」克林特起了头，「她一瞧见我骑上来，就摆明了要离开这片地界；偏她腿脚又快，我寻思正好趁这时候放烟儿追上去，叫她翻几个滚儿。绳我是甩出去了，可那圈没落利索，只飘飘悠悠荡在她前头，倒叫她一脚踩了进去。就当那阵儿，我把余绳一收，收得太狠。那牲口猛不丁一栽，囫囵个儿倒作一堆；倒得太突然，烟儿带着那股冲劲儿收不住脚，等我们俩回过神来，已经双双骑在那牲口身上了。

===Original===
“But she didn’t stay down long, she got up just at the wrong time and just right to yank Smoky’s front feet from under him, raise him up in the air with me on top, and just turned us a couple of somersets before we landed on the other side.

===Chinese===
「可她也没趴多久。她爬起来的那个时机，又偏又准，正好把烟儿的前蹄从底下猛地拽脱，带着背上的我把他掀上半空，就那么带着我们俩翻了两个跟头，才落到另一边去。

===Original===
“I didn’t know much more after that, till now, I just sorta felt a weight on my back, and that was all. Maybe I got under Smoky somehow as we fell, but I think it’s that fool cow that stepped on me and separated me from my thoughts.

===Chinese===
「打那以后，直到方才，我就知道得不多了，只迷迷糊糊觉出背上有分量压着，如此而已。兴许摔倒的时候，我不知怎么到了烟儿底下；可我琢磨着，是那头蠢牛踩了我一家伙，把我跟我的念头踩分了家。

===Original===
“I’ll most likely be all right in a few days, but I recognize this ailing. I got hurt a few years ago from an ornery black horse I was breaking for the Three C’s, and being that I don’t want this ailing to come back with me to stay, I figger I better quit riding rough ones. There’s other parts of me that’s hankering for me to quit too, and if you’ll let me join the boys at the wagon, I’m mighty willing that somebody else gets my job here.”

===Chinese===
「过不几天，我八成就好利索了；可这毛病我认得。几年前，我给三C（Three C's）驯一匹劣性的黑马，就落下过这么一档子伤；如今我不想叫这毛病回过头来跟我长住，我寻思还是别骑烈性的好。我身上旁的零件，也正撺掇着我撂挑子呢；你要是肯让我上车跟弟兄们一起干，这边这份差事换谁来接，我都十二分乐意。」

===Original===
Clint was quiet for a spell, and then pretty soon he goes on, “But there’s one favor I want to ask, Jeff, if you’ll let me stay with the outfit, I want to ask that you let me keep Smoky in my string and as long as I’m with the company.”

===Chinese===
克林特静了一阵，跟着不久又接着说：「不过杰夫，我还有一桩事求你。你要是肯让我留在班底里，我想求你让烟儿留在我名下的坐骑班（string）里——我跟公司一天，就留我一天。」

===Original===
What the cowboy had just said come from what he’d figgered, thought out, and worried on, ever since he’d first set eyes on Smoky. Clint liked all horses, maybe a little too much, but even at that he liked Smoky still more. The fear that somebody else would lay claim to the horse’d had him doing some tall thinking. He knowed that as long as he was breaking horses his work would come with raw broncs only and all half broke horses would be took away from him as fast as he’d turn ’em out. Smoky would had to go too.

===Chinese===
这牛仔方才那番话，是从他头一眼见着烟儿起，盘算出来、琢磨出来、也愁出来的。克林特喜欢所有的马，兴许喜欢得过了点头；饶是如此，他喜欢烟儿还要更多一层。生怕旁人把这马认领了去，这一层怕，逼得他好一番苦思。他心里清楚：只要他还干着驯马的营生，到他手上的就净是生坯子野马；凡是驯到半熟的，一出手立马就从他名下收走。烟儿也逃不脱。

===Original===
And that’s where the hitch came. He figgered he’d have to quit breaking horses and go to riding the range, and take the big chance that the horse might be took away from him even then. He’d noticed how Jeff had stood, watched, and admired Smoky; and if signs of a human wanting anything right bad ever showed, there was never no signs more visible than Clint had seen on Jeff’s features when the horse was in sight.

===Chinese===
症结也就卡在这儿。他盘算着，只得不干驯马，转去跑牧场巡骑——饶是那样，也得担老大一份风险：马兴许照样得让人收走。他早留意到杰夫是怎么站着、瞧着、赏鉴烟儿的；一个人要是当真把个东西稀罕到了家，脸上总是藏不住的——烟儿一露面时杰夫那副神情，克林特瞧得分明，再没有比那更藏不住的了。

===Original===
There’d been only one way out for the cowboy, and he’d took it.﻿—There was a worried look on his face as he glanced at the foreman and waited for him to answer, but Jeff didn’t seem to want to answer right then, and instead he asked:

===Chinese===
这牛仔本就只剩一条路可走，他走了。——他瞥向工头，等他答话，脸上挂着一层愁；可杰夫像是偏不急着答，反倒问道：

===Original===
“How long have you had that horse up, Clint?”

===Chinese===
「克林特，那匹马你上手多久了？」

===Original===
“Two months and maybe a little over,” says Clint, wondering some at the question.

===Chinese===
「两个月，兴许还出头一点儿。」克林特说，对这一问有些纳闷。

===Original===
“Wasn’t there a couple of boys here about a month ago to get *all* the broncs you’d started?”

===Chinese===
「一个月前，不是来了两个伙计，要收走你开了头的*全部*野马吗？」

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
「是。」

===Original===
“Well then, why didn’t you let ’em have that horse Smoky? He was as well broke then as any of the broncs the boys came after, wasn’t he?”

===Chinese===
「那么，你怎么没把烟儿那匹马交给他们？他那时候的驯熟劲儿，赶得上伙计们来收的随便哪一匹，是不是？」

===Original===
Clint begin to take interest in looking at the wall of the bunk house about that time. He grinned a little, and finally he answered:

===Chinese===
大约从这时起，克林特对瞧工棚的墙来了兴致。他咧了咧嘴，末了答道：

===Original===
“Well, Jeff, I guess you know why.”

===Chinese===
「这个嘛，杰夫，我想你是知道为什么的。」

===Original===
Jeff did know why, and knowed it a plenty. What he’d seen going on between Smoky and the cowboy the day before and that morning had already answered why Clint had hid the horse when the boys came to haze away the broncs he’d “started.” The foreman grinned back at the layed up rider and placed a hand on his shoulder, the same as to say that he understood.

===Chinese===
杰夫还真是知道为什么，而且知道得透透的。前一天和这天早晨，他瞧见的烟儿和这牛仔之间的一切，早把缘由答出来了：伙计们来轰走那些「开了头的」野马时，克林特为什么把马藏了起来。工头朝卧床的骑手咧嘴一笑，一只手按在他肩膀上，那意思等于说：他明白。

===Original===
“As long as I’m with this outfit,” he says, “and which from all indications will be a long time, you’re mighty welcome to join the wagon as one of my riders. You’ll be getting ‘top-hand’ wages too, Clint, the best string of ponies I can put together; and as for Smoky, why﻿—I sure would like that horse.”

===Chinese===
「只要我还管着这个班底，」他说，「——照种种情形看，这会是很长一段时间——你尽管来上车，做我手下的一名骑手。工钱也按『头把手』（top-hand）的价给你算，克林特，再配上我能张罗出来的顶好一串坐骑；至于烟儿嘛，这个——那匹马，我是真稀罕。」

===Original===
Clint’s heart fluttered up his throat and came near choking him﻿—“Yep! I’d sure like to have him” went on the foreman “but after thinking it all over, I figger that horse really belongs to you more than he does to the company or me. He’s a one man horse and you’re the one man, Clint, and even if the horse took a liking to me, which I know won’t happen, I’ll sure never want to take him away from you﻿—not after what I’ve seen.”

===Chinese===
克林特的心扑腾一下蹿上喉咙眼儿，险些把他噎住——「可不！我是真想要他，」工头接着往下说，「可前前后后想过之后，我算计着，这马真论起归属来，与其说是公司的、是我的，不如说是你的。他是匹一个人的马，而你，克林特，就是那一个人；退一步说，就算这马哪天看上了我——我知道没这回事——我也断断不想把他从你身边拿走——在我见过方才那些之后，断断不能。」

---

===Original===
Clint had underfiggered considerable when he’d said how he thought he’d be all right again in a few days. A week passed and very little strength had gathered from his hips up. His back felt as broke, and he had no power to straighten up again once he’d stoop. He couldn’t even pick up a spur.

===Chinese===
克林特说自己想过几天就没事了，这可是大大估摸不足了。一个星期过去，从胯骨往上，劲儿没攒下多少。背跟折了的一个样，一猫下腰，就再没力气直起来。连一只马刺，他都拾不起来。

===Original===
A new rider came one day and took up Clint’s work where he’d left it. From that time on Clint hung around the corrals a talking and watching the new “hand” ride, and when he wasn’t by the corrals, he could be seen in the shade of the big willows in the creek bottom where Smoky was picketed.

===Chinese===
一天，来了个新骑手，把克林特撂下的活计接过去干。打那时起，克林特就在畜栏边转悠，说说话，瞧那位新「伙计」骑马；不在畜栏边的时候，溪滩上那几棵大柳树的荫凉里也望得见他——烟儿就拴在那儿。

===Original===
Clint had looked at Smoky in a new way since Jeff had come and left. The visit of the old cowboy had brought out things in that little horse which Clint hadn’t dreamed of ever being in any horse. He’d been mighty surprised, and then sort of proud that he could raise such a feeling in the gelding. The horse was good as his too﻿—that put the cap on his worries of losing him, and all was well.

===Chinese===
自打杰夫来过又走，克林特看烟儿，换了一副新眼光。老牛仔这一趟，引出了小马身上的一些东西——那些东西，克林特做梦也不曾想过会出现在随便哪匹马身上。他先是大大吃了一惊，跟着又生出几分得意：能在骟马心里养出这样一份情义的，竟是他。这马也等于是他的了——这一下，把他怕失去马的那桩心事彻底封了顶，万事妥帖。

---

===Original===
A month went by, the roundup wagons was stringing out for the fall works, and the cow with the big “weaner” calf was hunting a hole. There was twenty-two riders with Jeff Nick’s wagon, and amongst ’em a grinning from ear to ear at some joke a cowboy had sprung was Clint, and riding Smoky.

===Chinese===
一个月过去，围拢车队一辆辆拉开，要赶秋牧的活计了；带着大「断奶」犊（weaner）的母牛，正寻摸着藏身的洞。杰夫·尼克斯的车队里有二十二名骑手，里头就有克林特——正为一个牛仔抖出的笑话咧着嘴乐呢——骑的是烟儿。

===Original===
Long days of rest had put that cowboy in shape to ride, but not to ride broncs, and when he at last felt that he could make a hand at riding “circle,” “herd,” and “night guard” Smoky had been saddled and rode to the home ranch, where the wagon was to start from.

===Chinese===
长日子的将养，把这牛仔的身子骨养得又能骑马了——只是骑不得野马。等他终于觉得，「跑圈」（circle）、「守群」（herd）、「值夜」（night guard）这些活计他都拿得起手了，烟儿也上了鞍，驮着他去了车队出发的本部牧场（home ranch）。

===Original===
Smoky’d had a long month’s rest before Clint saddled and rode him out that morning, and even though the rider looked OK again to the little horse, there was a feel from the hackamore rein that as much as asked him not to buck. He’d bucked that day when Clint had met too much cow, and far as that goes, he’d bucked some at every saddling, but as the cowboy started him out for open country and the home ranch that morning he was made to feel that he should keep his head up for once and line out without a kink.

===Chinese===
那阵子烟儿足足歇了一个整月，克林特才又给他上鞍，那天早上骑他出来。在小马眼里，骑手瞧着又对劲了；只是笼头缰上透出来的那股意思，跟开口求他别尥差不多。克林特那天碰上的牛太蛮，他尥过；往透里说，每回上鞍他都尥过几下；可这天早上，牛仔把他带向旷野、带向本部牧场时，让他觉出：这一回，该把头抬起来，撒开步子顺顺当当走一气，别再拧着劲儿闹悬。

===Original===
The home ranch had been reached a couple of days later, and there’s where Smoky’d got his first look at a busy cow outfit’s main camp. Cowboys was everywhere, and more of ’em than he could keep track of; big corrals full of horses, and more horses under the big sheds. Wagons and tents, and when the roundup cook rushed out of a log house to one side and pranced up to try and shake hands with Clint, Smoky let out a snort and shied out of reach.

===Chinese===
两天后，本部牧场到了；就在这儿，烟儿头一回见识了一家忙忙碌碌的牧牛班底的大本营。牛仔满世界都是，多得他数不过来；一座座大畜栏里挤满马，大棚子底下还是马。大车、帐篷，样样都有；围拢队的厨子从旁边一座木头屋子里冲出来，颠着步子凑上来要跟克林特握手，烟儿「噗」地打了个响鼻，一闪身躲到了够不着的地方。

===Original===
“Daggone it, Clint,” says that hombre, “I was told you’d quit riding broncs﻿—what in samhill do you call that spooky thing you’re setting on now?”

===Chinese===
「好家伙，克林特，」那一位开腔，「都听人说你撂下野马不骑了——你屁股底下这匹一惊一乍的家伙，又算个什么名堂？」

===Original===
“Some horse,” answered Clint, grinning.

===Chinese===
「算匹好马。」克林特咧着嘴答。

===Original===
Smoky felt some easier when he was finally unsaddled and turned in amongst the other saddle horses. He took a good roll, shook himself, and proceeded to get acquainted. It didn’t worry him none that very few of the ponies seemed to want his company and he was mighty busy going from one of the big corrals into another and giving ’em all the once over. He finally run acrost a bay gelding which seemed some familiar, and Smoky must of seemed the same to that gelding too for both of ’em started to show interest at once and came to meet one another.

===Chinese===
等鞍子终于卸下来、他被放进别的鞍马中间，烟儿才觉出松快了些。他结结实实打了个滚，抖了抖身子，便着手结识起大伙来。没几匹马像是乐意跟他做伴，这他一点儿不往心里去——他忙着呢，从这座大畜栏窜进那座，把大伙挨个儿相了一遍面。末了他撞见一匹骝色（bay）骟马，瞧着有几分面善；看那意思，烟儿在那骟马眼里想必也一样——两个立时都来了兴致，迎着对方走了过去。

===Original===
Necks bowed, they touched nostrils, some explaining and understanding must of went on, cause it wasn’t but a few minutes later when each was scratching the other’s neck like two brothers﻿—and that’s what they was, *brothers*. The bay horse was none other than the little colt, growed up, and which his mammy had brought in the bunch one day over three years before.

===Chinese===
脖颈弯着，鼻孔挨着鼻孔，中间想必有过一番诉说和会意；因为不出几分钟，两个就互相啃起对方的脖颈来，跟亲哥俩一样——他们本来就是，*亲哥俩*。那匹骝色马不是别个，正是当年那匹小马驹，如今长大成了马；三年多前的一天，是他的马妈妈把他随马群一并带了进来。

===Original===
Signs showed where the saddle had been on his back too. A cowboy had run him in a couple of weeks before and passed the remark as he piled his rope on him that, “This little bay horse sure showed the makings of a cowhorse.”

===Chinese===
他背上也有鞍子压过的痕迹。两三个星期前，一个牛仔把他赶了进来，往他身上码绳子的时候撂下一句话：「这匹小骝色马，一瞧就有一副牧牛马的坯子。」

===Original===
Jeff had agreed, and that’s how come Smoky found him in amongst the saddle horses that day. He’d showed some of how he’d took a *natural* liking for the bay, and if one didn’t recognize a brother in the other the way they went at scratching each other’s withers couldn’t of meant much.

===Chinese===
杰夫也这么看——所以那天烟儿才会在鞍马堆里找见他。他也多少亮了亮自己那份对骝色马*天生*的亲近；要是俩谁都没在对方身上认出个兄弟，他们那样互相啃起对方耆甲的热乎劲儿，也就算不得什么了。

===Original===
It was thru an intermission at wither scratching that Smoky seen Clint open the outside gate of the corral and walk in. Alongside of him was Jeff Nicks who’d come along to point out Clint’s string of ponies. Smoky watched them two for quite a spell; he watched Jeff the most, but pretty soon went to scratching his brother’s withers again. Clint was all right now and well able to take care of himself, he must of thought﻿—Anyway there wasn’t the feeling in him that Clint needed any protection.

===Chinese===
啃耆甲啃到中场歇气的当口，烟儿瞧见克林特推开畜栏的外门走了进来，身边是杰夫·尼克斯，他是专程来指认克林特名下那一串坐骑的。烟儿瞧了那两位好一阵；瞧杰夫瞧得最久，可没多会儿，又回头啃起他兄弟的耆甲来。克林特如今好好的，照管自个儿绰绰有余，他心里约莫是这么想的——反正，他心里没有那层感觉，觉着克林特还需要什么护持。

===Original===
Clint had come to see him that evening, and he’d noticed as his pardner came that some of the cowboys was watching him from the next corral. He looked over Clint’s shoulder at ’em and sent out a long whistling snort.

===Chinese===
那天傍晚克林特来看他，他觉出来了：伙伴一来，隔壁畜栏里有几个牛仔正打量着。他从克林特肩膀上头瞥了他们一眼，送出一声长悠悠、哨音似的响鼻。

===Original===
“I’m glad Clint didn’t break all the broncs like he did that one,” remarked one of the boys as he seen the fight in that pony’s eyes.

===Chinese===
「幸亏克林特驯野马，没都照那一匹的驯法来，」一个小伙子瞧见那小马眼里的战意，议论道。

===Original===
“Yep!” says another, “he sure made a *one man* horse out of him.”

===Chinese===
「可不！」另一个说，「他硬是把这马调教成了*一个人的*马。」

===Original===
Smoky was turned out in the big pasture that night with the other horses. Him and his brother paired off soon as they was out of the corrals and fed together till daybreak brought a rider on the skyline who corralled ’em all for the new day’s work.

===Chinese===
那天夜里，烟儿跟旁的马一道被放进大草场。哥俩一出畜栏就配成了对，肩挨肩吃到天亮；天一亮，天际线上来了个骑手，把他们统统收进栏去，好干新一天的活。

===Original===
That day’s work started early. Sun up found all the boys on their horses, the chuck wagon, bed wagon and wood wagon teams was all hooked on and ready to start at a wave of the hand from Jeff. Jeff waved, and away all went thru the big gates leading out of the home ranch, three wagons strung out, a “remuda” (saddle bunch) of two hundred saddle horses followed, and on the “swing” (sides) of the whole outfit twenty-two riders, riding good and bad horses, loped along﻿—The fall roundup had started.

===Chinese===
这一天的活计开得早。日头一冒头，弟兄们全都在了马背上；伙食车、铺盖车、柴车的骡马全挂妥了，只等杰夫一挥手就动身。杰夫一挥，一行人马便从本部牧场的大门口鱼贯而出：三辆大车拉开纵深，一支两百匹鞍马的「雷穆达」换乘马群跟在后头，全班底两翼的「侧翼」（swing）上，二十二名骑手骑着有好有歹的马，一路碎跑着——秋天的围拢，开张了。

## VIII / 八

===Original===
Smoky Starts Out

===Chinese===
烟儿初上征途

===Original===
The first day of the fall roundup was to Smoky a whole lot like the first day of school to the kid of the settlement, only, Smoky was full grown and his brain full developed. His eyes stayed wide open and worked with his ears so that nothing of interest would be missed.

===Chinese===
对烟儿来说，秋围拢的头一天，很有点像屯子里的孩子头一回上学，只不过烟儿已经长大成型，脑子也发育齐全了。他两眼睁得溜圆，跟两只耳朵搭伙干活，生怕漏掉半点新鲜事儿。

===Original===
There was so much that was strange and which kept his senses on the jump. The big wagons with the four and six horse teams done a lot of spooky rattling as they followed the pilot, sometimes on a high lope, across the rolling prairie, over benches and down draws. Then trailing along close behind the thumping of hoofs of many ponies, the remuda, made a sound which hinted everything to Smoky, everything from a stampede on up, and if it hadn’t been for the hand that once in a while was felt on his neck, and the voice which he heard and knowed so well, the little horse would of sure left a streak of dust and away from all that confusion of wagons and men.

===Chinese===
奇怪玩意儿多得是，搅得他五官一刻不得消停。几辆大车套着四匹、六匹骡马，跟在领路人（pilot）后头，有时还颠着大跑，一路哐啷哐啷响得瘆人，碾过起伏的草原，上台地，下洼地。紧跟在后面的是一大片马蹄的闷响——那支雷穆达，这声音对烟儿什么都暗示着，从炸群往上，样样都有；若不是脖子上时不时搭过来一只手，若不是那个他听得熟透了的嗓音，这匹小马准会拖出一道烟儿，从这一团大车加人群的乱麻里逃个没影。

===Original===
There was too many riders around him. They all kept too close, and once in a while as the outfit sashayed on towards the first camp grounds and some bronc would bust out a bucking and a trying to shed off a cowboy, Smoky felt a lot like doing the same. But always, and whenever he felt like “kettling” the most, Clint’s hand and voice was there to quiet him down. That hand and voice worked the same as to prove to Smoky, that as long as Clint was around close there was nothing for him to fear.

===Chinese===
他四围的骑手太多，全都贴得太近。班底晃晃悠悠往头一处营地去的路上，时不时就有哪匹野马蹦出一串尥蹶子，想把身上的牛仔甩下去，烟儿瞧着，心里怪想照办的。可每逢他最想「炸锅」的当口，克林特的手和嗓音总在跟前，把他安抚下来。那只手、那个嗓音回回都灵，等于向烟儿证明：只要克林特守在近旁，他就没什么可怕的。

===Original===
As the outfit rambled on, Clint gradually reined Smoky to one side till he was well away and where he would feel more at ease to watch without fear all what the layout had to show that was strange. Smoky’s ears then perked up at a different angle, and as Clint talked to him that spooky looking outfit lining out acrost the range got to look less spooky and more interesting.

===Chinese===
班底一路逶迤前行，克林特渐渐把烟儿带偏到一侧，带到离大队远远的地方，好让他定下心来、不带惧意地看全这一路人马要展给他瞧的新奇名堂。烟儿的耳朵换了个角度支棱起来；克林特一路跟他絮叨，那支在草原上摆开阵势、瞧着瘆人的班底，便越瞧越不瘆人，越瞧越有意思了。

===Original===
Smoky followed the outfit and watched it till the sun was well up in the middle of the sky, then the pilot raised his hand, made a circle and the wagons followed him to a standstill. A dry camp was made and the cook had the pots to working a few minutes after the outfit had come to a stop. The rope corrals was strung out in the wink of an eye and the remuda run in.

===Chinese===
烟儿跟着班底一路看，看到日头高高升到正当顶，领路人抬起手，画了个圈，几辆大车便跟着他停了下来。大家扎了个干营（dry camp，无水的营地），班底前脚刚停稳，厨子后脚就让锅灶忙活上了。绳畜栏眨眼工夫就张开摆好，雷穆达被赶了进去。

===Original===
Smoky had watched the whole proceedings with a lot of interest, the many horses, men, and all had him to using his eyes and ears to the limit, and the low snorts he’d let out every once in a while as he turned to watch all that went on, was as plain as talk, that for excitement this sure had everything he’d ever seen before beat to a frazzle.

===Chinese===
烟儿兴致勃勃地看完了全程：这么多的马、这么多的人，逼得他把眼睛耳朵都使唤到了极限；他时不时转过身来，冲这一切打出几声低低的响鼻，那意思明白得跟说话一样：论热闹，眼前这一场，准把他从前见识过的一切全比了个稀碎。

---

===Original===
“Come and get it, you Rannies!” It was the cook’s holler for the riders to come and eat. About then Smoky seen Clint headed towards him and where he’d been left picketed. A little rub back of the ear and Smoky was led to the rope corral, unsaddled, and turned in with the remuda.

===Chinese===
「开饭喽，你们这帮牧场的娃儿！」是厨子在吆喝骑手们来吃饭。约莫那时候，烟儿瞧见克林特朝他这边走来，朝他被拴着的地方走来。耳根子后头揉了两把，烟儿就被牵到绳畜栏，卸了鞍，放进雷穆达里去了。

===Original===
“Have a good roll, Smoky horse,” says Clint as he turned him loose, “and don’t let no ornery pony get the best of you.”

===Chinese===
「好好打个滚吧，烟儿马，」克林特放开他时说，「也别让哪匹赖皮马占了你的上风。」

===Original===
Smoky looked back at Clint for a spell the same as to ask him where *he* was going, and as the cowboy stood there watching the little horse moseyed on and disappeared amongst the saddle bunch.

===Chinese===
烟儿回头望了克林特一阵，像是在问他*自己*要上哪儿去；牛仔站在那儿望着，小马慢悠悠往前蹭，蹭着蹭着就没进了鞍马群里。

===Original===
The “roundup pan” was filling up with the tin cups and plates as the cowboys, thru eating, was making their way towards their saddles by the rope corrals. A hard-twist catch rope was unbuckled from them saddles, loops was shook out, and pretty soon them same loops begin a sailing and a reaching out like a mighty long arm for the horse each cowboy picked out for that afternoon’s ride.

===Chinese===
牛仔们吃罢饭，三三两两朝绳畜栏边自己的鞍子走去，「围拢大盆」（roundup pan，盛杯盘的大铁盆）里眼看着摞满了白铁杯子和盘子。硬捻的套马索（catch rope）从鞍子上解下来，索圈抖开，不多时，一个个索圈便又飞又探，像一条条长胳膊，直奔各人挑中那天下午要骑的马而去。

===Original===
Smoky seen and heard the hiss of them loops as they sailed on over past him to settle around some other pony’s neck, and even though all was done quiet so none of the horses would start running too much, Smoky had a mighty restless feeling whenever them snaky ropes appeared. Clint hadn’t roped him only once and that was when he was a raw bronc, but he hadn’t forgot the feeling that’d been his when that same rope had caught him, stretched him out, and left him plumb helpless.

===Chinese===
烟儿瞧着、听着那些索圈飕飕地从他头顶飞过去，落在别的马脖子上；虽说一切都做得悄没声，好叫马群别惊得乱跑，可每逢这些蛇一样的绳子露头，烟儿心里还是躁得不行。克林特拢共只套过他一回，那还是他做生坯子野马的时候，可那根绳子逮住他、把他抻直了、撂得他全然动弹不得的滋味，他没有忘。

===Original===
His brain was near stampeding with him at the sound of so many ropes, and once in a while when he’d spot some strange rider carrying one of them hated coils the sight made him hit for the middle of the herd﻿—but even there he wasn’t safe, for there was no telling how far them ropes could reach.

===Chinese===
这么多绳子一齐作响，闹得他的脑子也差点跟着炸了群。偶尔瞧见哪个生面孔骑手肩上挎着这样一圈可恨的家伙，那光景就逼得他一头扎进马群中央去——可就连那儿也不保险，谁知道那些绳子能探出多远呢。

===Original===
It was in winding around and thru the thick of the herd, that Smoky found himself on the edge and crowded against the big rope cable which was the corral. The sight that met his eye there had him wanting to hide back in the middle of the herd once again, but he had no chance, the herd had him wedged where he couldn’t move and as it was he had to stare wild eyed at all that was there for him to see.

===Chinese===
就在马群腹地里绕来绕去、穿来穿去的当儿，烟儿发现自己被挤到了边上，抵在那根当畜栏用的大绳缆上。那儿撞进他眼里的光景，害他又想缩回马群中央去，可他没了机会：马群把他楔得死死的动弹不得，只好瞪着一双惊惶的眼睛，硬着头皮看那满眼非看不可的景象。

===Original===
A few feet away was half a dozen riders saddling up, and that’s what kettled Smoky﻿—the few feet that was between him and them strangers was too close for comfort. He was just about to try another grand rush to get back into the middle of the herd when the sound of something familiar made him hesitate. It was the ring of a spur rowel, a ring he’d heard often, and pretty soon Smoky spots Clint only a few feet away from him and leading a strange horse to his saddle.

===Chinese===
几步开外有六七个骑手正在备鞍，正是这个让烟儿炸了锅——他和那些生人中间只隔着这么几步，近得让人浑身不自在。他正打算再发动一回大冲锋、杀回马群中央去，一个耳熟的声响让他迟疑了。那是马刺轮（spur rowel）的叮铃声，一串他常听见的铃音；不多时，烟儿就瞅见了克林特，离他不过几步远，正把一匹生马往自己的鞍子跟前牵。

===Original===
Smoky stuck his head and neck out far as he could and nickered at the sight of the cowboy, and that cowboy having his attention some other direction at the time was made to turn mighty quick as the well known nicker was heard.﻿—There was all in Smoky’s looks and nicker that seemed to say “Pardner, I need help.”

===Chinese===
烟儿把脑袋脖子伸得老长老长，一见那牛仔就低嘶起来。牛仔那会儿正把心思用在别处，听见这声再熟悉不过的低嘶，唬得赶紧回头。——烟儿这一副神色、这一声低嘶里满是话，仿佛在说：「伙计，我要人搭把手。」

===Original===
Clint laughed, but the laugh wasn’t of the kind that comes from a joke.

===Chinese===
克林特笑了，可这笑不是听笑话听出来的那种。

===Original===
“What’s the matter, little horse?”

===Chinese===
「怎么了，小马儿？」

===Original===
But Clint knowed what was the matter, he could hear the thump thump of Smoky’s heart as he came nearer, and feel the throb of it as he layed a hand on his neck. He rubbed on the slick hide a spell, and that cowboy experienced a mighty great feeling when he noticed as he stayed, that gradually the pony’s heart beats begin to slow down and soon was behaving normal again.

===Chinese===
可克林特知道是怎么回事。他走近时就能听见烟儿胸腔里咚咚的心跳，手往他脖子上一搭，就能觉出那悸动。他在那油光水滑的皮毛上揉搓了一阵；待着待着，他觉出来小马的心跳渐渐慢了下来，不多时又恢复如常——这份滋味，牛仔心里受用极了。

===Original===
Smoky watched the cowboy leave him to go to where his saddle was laying on the ground out a ways. He watched him put the saddle on the strange horse, and when Clint came back leading the horse and finished saddling by Smoky, that pony nipped at the cowboy’s chap’ leg the same as to say “Stick around a spell.”

===Chinese===
烟儿望着牛仔离开他，走到不远处鞍子撂在地上的地方。他望着他把鞍子放上那匹生马的背。等克林特牵着马回来、在烟儿旁边把鞍备齐整时，小马轻轻咬了咬牛仔的皮套裤腿，那意思像是说：「再陪俺待一阵嘛。」

===Original===
Clint did stick around for a spell. He wasted a lot of good company time fooling with the latigos and seeing that his rope was coiled up neat, and even though he knowed that as a good cowboy he should been helping tearing down camp, he stuck by the corral and Smoky till the last rider had caught, saddled, and rode his horse away. The remuda was let out then, the wrangler circled the bunch and started grazing ’em till the wagons started again for that night’s grounds.

===Chinese===
克林特还真多待了一阵。他摆弄摆弄拉蒂戈革条，再把绳圈盘得整整齐齐，把大好的相处的工夫全「浪费」在这些上头；他也明明知道，照一个好牛仔的本分，这会儿该去搭手拆营才对，可他就是在畜栏边、在烟儿身边待着不走，直到最后一名骑手抓完马、备好鞍、骑马走人。之后雷穆达被放了出来，马倌（wrangler）兜着马群转圈，领它们啃草，直等到大车重新上路，奔当晚的营地去。

===Original===
Smoky was hazed along and lost in the big horse herd, Clint watched him and when he couldn’t see him no more started coiling up the big cable, which was the rope corral used on open range, and with the help of another rider put it in one of the wagons where it’d be easy reached again.

===Chinese===
烟儿被裹在大马群里赶走了，淹没不见了。克林特望着他，望到再也望不见，才动手盘卷那根大绳缆——旷野上当作绳畜栏用的就是它——然后在一个骑手的搭手下，把它抬进一辆大车里，搁在往后好取的地方。

===Original===
It’d been less than an hour since the cook had stopped his team and jumped off the wagon to cook the cowboys’ noon bait, and now he was up on the wagon again and waiting there for the boys to finish hooking up his team and hand him the “ribbons.” Soon enough that was done, the pilot started and the cook warhooped his broncs into a running start, the bed wagon, loaded down with twenty some odd “Montana Rolls,” took up the swing, and the wood wagon tagged along behind. Then came the remuda of over two hundred saddle horses and hazing ’em was the “Wrangatang” (day wrangler).

===Chinese===
厨子停车、跳下车给牛仔们做晌午饭，到这会儿还不到一个钟头，他就又坐上了大车，等着伙计们把他的骡马套挂齐全，再把「缰绳」（ribbons）递到他手里。这事很快办妥，领路人起了行，厨子呜哇一嗓子，把他的野马催得撒腿就跑；装着二十来卷「蒙大拿卷」（Montana Rolls）的铺盖车接上了「侧翼」，柴车缀在最后。再往后就是两百多匹鞍马的雷穆达，赶拢它们的是「倌儿坦」（Wrangatang，白日马倌）。

===Original===
The first “circle” of the fall roundup was on that afternoon. The circle starts from wherever the roundup wagon might be. The roundup wagon of most countries is composed of three wagons, one for “chuck” and pots and the cook, another for the riders’ bedding which is rolled in big canvas tarpaulins. It takes quite some bedding for twenty or more men, specially in countries where it’s apt to snow in the middle of June. The third wagon is for wood and water and which is used in prairie countries where there’s neither wood or water to be found for a ways.

===Chinese===
秋天围拢的头一趟「跑圈」就在那天下午。跑圈从围拢车队停在哪里，就从哪里起头。多数地方的围拢车队都由三辆大车组成：一辆装伙食、锅灶和厨子；一辆装骑手们的铺盖，铺盖卷在大块帆布油布里。二十几号人的铺盖可不是小数目，尤其是在那种六月半还兴许落雪的地界。第三辆装柴和水，专为草原地带备着——在那儿走出老远，也寻不见一根柴、一滴水。

===Original===
The cook drives his chuck wagon, the “flunky” (cook’s helper) drives the bed wagon, and the “nighthawk” (rider who herds the remuda at night) drives the wood wagon. Them three wagons which is called “The Wagon” is the cowboys’ home while on the range. It carries his grub, his “war bag” (bag of clothes), his bedding, and strips of rawhide which he salts down and sometimes cuts into strings and braids things like “bosals” (nose bands) or such.

===Chinese===
厨子赶他的伙食车，「帮厨」（flunky，厨子的下手）赶铺盖车，「夜鹰」（nighthawk，夜里看守雷穆达的骑手）赶柴车。这三辆大车合称「车队」（The Wagon），是牛仔们跑牧场期间的家。家里驮着他的吃食、他的「杂物袋」（war bag，装衣裳的袋子）、他的铺盖，还有几条腌存着的生牛皮——兴许什么时候裁成皮条，编个「波萨尔」（bosals，鼻套）什么的。

===Original===
“The Wagon” moves camp most every day, and sometimes twice and three times a day; all depends on how quick the country is “worked.” The “circle” starts from “The Wagon.” The twenty or more riders and the cow foreman ride straight to some point for ten or fifteen miles. On top of some butte the bunch stops, then the cow foreman “scatters the riders.” He’ll send ’em in pairs to the right, left, and straight ahead and spread ’em fan shape to a certain point where they turn, or where there’s no more cattle to be seen, and they’ll head back towards the wagon again, bringing with ’em all the cattle that’s seen in the ride.

===Chinese===
「车队」差不多天天搬家，有时一天还搬两三回，全看这片地界「作业」得多快。「跑圈」从「车队」起头。二十几名骑手加上牧牛工头，照直奔某个目的地骑上十到十五英里。到了某座平顶孤山（butte）顶上，一行人停下，然后牧牛工头「撒开骑手」：他两人一组，把他们派往右翼、左翼和正前方，撒成一把扇面，撒到某个折返点，或撒到再也望不见牛的地方，再掉头往车队方向回拢，一路上把瞧见的牛统统带上。

===Original===
That’s what’s called a “circle.” It averages twenty-five miles and ends at the wagon where all the riders meet again each bringing with ’em whatever cattle was found. The wagon might of moved and a new camp set up while the boys was out on “circle,” but wherever the wagon is that’s where the “circle” ends. To one side of the camp a mile or so is the “cutting grounds” where the herd is “worked,” the spot where all the cattle is brought to from that one “circle” and held there for branding, and cutting out whatever is not wanted. Two “circles” are made a day.

===Chinese===
这就叫一趟「跑圈」。平均下来二十五英里，终点是大车队，全体骑手在那里重新会合，各人带上自己寻见的牛。伙计们在外面跑圈的时候，车队兴许已经挪了窝、另扎了新营，可不论车挪到哪儿，「跑圈」就收在哪儿。离营地约一英里的边上，是「分群场」（cutting grounds），牛群就在那儿「过手」：那一趟「跑圈」收来的牛全轰到那里圈住，等着打烙印，等着把不要的剔出去。一天跑两趟「圈」。

===Original===
Soon as Jeff, the cow foreman, seen the wagons lining out in good shape for that night’s camp, he put his horse in a high lope and looking back at the boys that was doing their best in putting up a ride on the sun-fishing ponies, he grinned as he seen that all stuck on and fanned, and felt mighty proud of being the cow boss of such a bunch of riders.

===Chinese===
牧牛工头杰夫一见大车队顺顺当当排开、奔着当晚的营地去，便把马催成大跑，回头瞧了瞧那帮伙计——他们正使出浑身解数，对付那些一路打着「晒鱼」蹦跶（sun-fishing）的坐骑；见他们一个个都坐得稳稳的、帽子挥得呼呼响，他咧嘴笑了：自己是这样一帮骑手的牛头儿，心里好不得意。

===Original===
Clint was riding a big “apelusa” called Chapo, and one of the best circle horses the outfit had, but he wasn’t appreciating him much just then. And as he rode along leaving the wagons and remuda to his left his eyes was a whole lot on the dust that remuda made, and a trying to get a glimpse of a mouse colored piece of horseflesh which he’d called Smoky.

===Chinese===
克林特骑的是一匹叫「查波」（Chapo）的大块头「阿佩卢萨」（apelusa，花斑马），也是班底里数得着的好跑圈马，只是那会儿他没多少心思稀罕它。他一路骑着，把大车和雷穆达撂在左手边，眼睛却大半黏在那片雷穆达扬起的尘土上，一心想从里头认出那一抹他唤作烟儿的鼠灰色身影。

===Original===
But Smoky was getting along fine as he trotted and loped along on the trail of the wagons. He’d no more than left Clint by the rope corral when he run acrost that brother of his again and after the two nickered “howdedos” at one another they trailed along side by side, plum contented with everything in general. The sound of the dozen or so bells that was strapped to the necks of the oldest and wisest ponies was new and mighty pleasant to Smoky’s ears, and it was good to be roaming again and with so much company.

===Chinese===
倒是烟儿自己，跟着大车的车辙一路小跑大跑，过得挺滋润。他在绳畜栏边刚离开克林特，就又撞上了他那位兄弟，俩人低嘶着互道了「久仰」，便肩挨肩一路同行，对眼前的一切都受用得很。十几只铃铛拴在那些顶老成、顶有主见的马脖子上，那声响对烟儿的耳朵又新鲜又熨帖；又能出来遛弯儿了，还有这么一大帮子作伴，真好。

===Original===
It was middle afternoon when the pilot came to a big creek bottom and circled by a grove of willows and cottonwoods. The second camp of that day was made, the wrangler let the remuda come to a walk and pretty soon left ’em to graze on towards the creek a half a mile below camp; and as he seen that all seemed contented to graze, drink, and roll, he left ’em to go and put up the rope corral, snake in wood for the cook, and whatever other things that’s all the responsibilities of the wrangatang.

===Chinese===
午后过半，领路人来到一条大溪的谷底，绕过一片柳树和三角叶杨的林子，当天第二处营地便扎下了。马倌由着雷穆达放缓成散步，不多时便撇下它们，任它们朝营地下方半英里处的溪边一边啃一边蹭；他见大伙儿吃得香甜、喝得痛快、滚得自在，便丢下它们去张罗绳畜栏，给厨子拖柴去，再办一应属于「倌儿坦」的分内事。

===Original===
He kept one eye on the ponies as he worked and if any restless bronc showed indications of wanting to start drifting, that boy jumped on his horse, turned him, and watched for a spell till that bronc seemed satisfied to stay. Many a wrangler has used the excuse of “hard-to-hold ponies” just so he could get away from too much work, and most always it was a mighty good excuse too.

===Chinese===
干活时他一只眼始终吊在马群上，哪匹不安分的野马露出想开溜的苗头，那小子立刻翻身上马，把它兜转回来，再盯上一阵，直到那马显出安营扎寨的诚心。多少马倌拿「马不好看」当借口，好躲开过重的活计，而且十有八九，这借口还真不赖。

===Original===
But Smoky and Pecos, which was his brother, had give no such a excuse to the wrangler. They both seemed mighty satisfied, and after they’d had a good drink in the cool stream, and a good roll afterwards, put their time in getting away with all the blue joint grass they could. Every once in a while Smoky would raise his head, and chewing on a mouthful of the tall feed, would look up at the ridges around him, then towards the camp and wonder at the noise the cook was making with his pots and pans. All had him interested, it was all new, and with the nicker he’d often hear from one side of the scattered remuda and then the other, the steady ring of the horse bells and all. The little horse wasn’t hankering for anything only just what he was in the thick of.

===Chinese===
倒是烟儿和佩科斯——就是他那位兄弟——没给马倌任何这样的口实。他俩看上去都知足得很：在清凉的溪水里灌了个饱，跟着又美美打了个滚，之后便把光阴全用在能骗到嘴多少蓝茎草（blue joint）上头。烟儿时不时抬起头，嚼着满满一口高草，望望四周的山梁，再望望营地方向，纳闷厨子把锅碗瓢盆敲出了什么名堂。样样都叫他上心，样样都新鲜：散开的雷穆达这一头、那一头不时传来低嘶，马铃铛的脆响连绵不绝，还有这一切的一切。这匹小马什么都不馋，只馋他正泡在里头的这份日子。

===Original===
He’d been grazing for a good long time, and the sun was hitting towards the ridges to the west, when to the south a ways he noticed a big dust a soaring up the sky and a mile high. There was a steady rumbling noise as the dust came closer and pretty soon he could make out the bellering of the critter. A big herd it was, the “combings” of the first “circle,” and a thousand head or more of white-faced, brockle-faced, speckled, red, black, and all colors and sizes of range cattle topped a ridge and on a high lope was swung towards the “cutting grounds.”

===Chinese===
他啃了老长老长时间的草，日头已经往西边的山梁坠去，这时南边老远处，他望见一大股尘土冲天而起，蹿得有一英里高。尘土越滚越近，底下带着一阵沉闷的轰隆，不多时他便听出了那是牲口的哞吼。好大一群牛——头一趟「跑圈」「梳拢」来的收获：一千多头白脸的、花脸的、带花斑的、红的、黑的，各色各样、大大小小的牧场牛，翻过一道山梁，甩开大跑，直奔「分群场」兜了过去。

===Original===
About that time the horse wrangler fogged in on the remuda, and in a short while Smoky and all the ponies found themselves in the rope corral once again; the cowboys was needing fresh horses and catch ropes begin a sailing once more as the twenty and more of ’em snared their “cut” horses, a few snaked out broncs and pretty soon all hands was mounted again, and working the herd they’d brought in.

===Chinese===
也就在那时候，马倌一阵烟似的卷进雷穆达，不多时，烟儿和众马就发现自己又进了绳畜栏：牛仔们要换新马了。二十几号人把套索再度抡飞，各人套住自己名下该骑的马，有几匹野马是被硬拖出来的；很快全体人手又都上了马，动手收拾他们赶回来的那群牛。

===Original===
Smoky was spooked up once more as he heard the ropes sing over his ears. He heard a familiar voice say “How’s she going Smoky?” but the little horse was busy hunting a hole about that time and he was too excited to nicker an answer. Then, after what seemed an awful long time to Smoky, the ponies was left out of the corral once more and when the wrangler checked ’em all to graze, him and Pecos was in the lead.

===Chinese===
套索贴着耳朵唱起来，烟儿又惊了一回。他听见一个熟悉的嗓音说：「过得咋样，烟儿？」可小马那会儿正忙着找地缝钻，激动得顾不上低嘶作答。后来——在烟儿感觉里过了老长老长时间——马们又被放出了畜栏，马倌把它们拢去吃草时，他和佩科斯走在头里。

===Original===
The ponies was grazing on a low bench and on the opposite side of the creek from where the cattle herd was being worked. Many was cut out and started back on the same range from where they come, and pretty soon Smoky’s sensitive nostrils smelled the smoke from the fire that kept the branding irons hot; then the smell of burnt hair followed, he heard the beller of the critters, and snorting sorta low and in wonder, the mouse colored pony watched.

===Chinese===
马群在一片低台地上啃草，跟牛群挨收拾的地方隔着一条溪。好多牛被剔出去，赶回它们来的那片草场；不多时，烟儿灵敏的鼻孔嗅到了让烙铁保持滚烫的那炉火的烟味；跟着是烧焦的毛气味儿，再就是牲口的哞吼声。鼠灰色的小马半低着响鼻、又惊又奇地望着这一切。

===Original===
He watched the riders at work, seen long ropes a swinging, and how them long ropes would stop the bunch-quitting steer; he was familiar with some of that and somehow there came in him a hunch that he’d like to be closer; there was something about the workings of that herd across the creek that had his blood racing above natural, and he felt a kind of a call for the whole of the goings on, a call of the kind he couldn’t as yet understand, but it was there sure enough.

===Chinese===
他望着骑手们干活，望见长长的套绳抡圆了飞，望见那些长绳怎么把想溜出群的阉牛（steer）拦停；这里头的门道他熟一些，不知怎么的，心里冒出个念头，想凑近些看。溪对岸那群牛的一举一动里，有点什么让他的血烧得过了头，他觉出一种召唤，冲着这整场热闹来的召唤；这种召唤他还参不透，可它千真万确就在那儿。

===Original===
Finally, the smell of singed hair wasn’t on the breeze no more, branding was over for that day, and the last rope was coiled up and fastened by the saddle horn. Smoky watched as all but a few riders left the herd and headed for camp, he went to grazing then, and neck and neck with Pecos he listened to the rattle of tin plates and the laugh of the cowboys as he nosed around for the tenderest stems of the blue joint.

===Chinese===
到末了，燎毛的气味儿从风里散尽了，那天的烙印活计收了场，最后一条绳子盘好，拴牢在鞍角上。烟儿望着骑手们只留下几个，其余的都离开牛群回营去，他便低头吃起草来，和佩科斯颈挨着颈，一面用鼻子挑拣蓝茎草最嫩的梗，一面听着白铁盘子的哐啷声和牛仔们的笑。

===Original===
Four riders on “cocktail” (hours between the last meal of the day and the first night guard) got on their horses and rode to “relieve” the riders holding the herd, and it wasn’t long after that when the quiet of the evening settled on the range. Even the critter seemed to want to stop bellering for a spell at that time, most of the bells of the remuda was quiet and the ponies was dozing.

===Chinese===
四名骑手趁着「鸡尾酒班」（cocktail，一天最后一顿饭和头一茬值夜之间的那几个钟头）上了马，去「换」看守牛群的骑手们的班；没过多久，黄昏的静谧便落在了牧场上。连牲口这会儿也像是乐意歇一阵不哞了，雷穆达的铃铛大多哑了声，马们打着盹。

===Original===
Smoky had been dozing too, but pretty soon his ears perked up at a sound the likes of which he’d never heard before, the sound came from the camp, and strange as it was there was something about it that wasn’t at all aggravating.

===Chinese===
烟儿本来也打着盹，不多时，一阵他从未听过的声响把他耳朵支棱了起来。声音是从营地那边来的；虽说是怪声，里头却有点什么，一点儿也不惹他烦。

===Original===
Around a good size fire was gathered the cowboys﻿—the cook, the flunky, the wrangler, Jeff the foreman and all was in the circle, all but the four riders on “cocktail” and the “nighthawk” who’d took the wrangler’s place for the night’s herding of the saddle horses. Most of the boys was setting on or leaning against a big roll of tarpaulin covered bedding, and one closest to the fire was a working away trying to get a tune on his mouth organ.

===Chinese===
一大堆旺火四围坐满了牛仔——厨子、帮厨、马倌、工头杰夫全在圈里，只有「鸡尾酒班」上那四名骑手，和顶了马倌夜班去看守鞍马的「夜鹰」不在。伙计们大多坐在一大卷油布裹的铺盖上，或者倚着它；离火最近的一个正忙活着，想在口琴上摸出一支调子来。

===Original===
That was the sound which’d come to Smoky’s ears; the older cowhorses all knowed that sound well, and if any of ’em could of packed a tune there’d been many in the remuda a humming.

===Chinese===
传到烟儿耳朵里的就是这动静；年老的牧牛马个个熟悉这声响，它们里头要是有谁能装得下一支曲子，雷穆达里哼曲的马可就多了去了。

===Original===
The song that was being worked at just then had been heard at all the cow camps and roundup wagons of the cow country for many years, and handed down from the injun fighting cowboy to the son that took up the trail where he left it and when the horns on the critter wasn’t so long no more. There was a lot of memories stirred up whenever them songs was heard and many a cowboy got sentimental at the sound of ’em, for most all cowboys can remember some quiet night when the time of such a song was spread around the herd;﻿—then of a sudden and for no reason a stampede is in full swing, a dead cowboy is found under his horse at the bottom of a fifty foot jump off, and leaves only the memory of the song he’d been singing that night.

===Chinese===
这会儿被折腾出来的那首歌，牧牛地带的各家牧营和围拢车队已经听了多少年，从跟印第安人打仗的老牛仔手里，传给接着他没走完的小道往下走的儿子——传到牲口头上的角都不再那么长的年代。这样的歌一响，总能搅起许多旧事，多少牛仔一听就动了感情：差不多每个牛仔都记得哪个安静的夜晚，这样的歌正围着牛群荡开；——跟着毫无来由地猛一下炸了群，一名死去的牛仔被发现压在自己马底下，躺在五十英尺跳崖的崖根，身后只留下他那晚唱着的这首歌。

===Original===
“Oh, I’m a Texas cowbo-o-oy, and far away from home,
					
					And if I ever get back again no more will I ever roam,
					
					Wyoming’s too cold for me-e-e, the winters are too long,
					
					And when roundup comes again, my money’s all go-o-o-*ne*.”

===Chinese===
「哦，我是个得克萨斯的牛仔哟，漂泊在外远离家门，

要是有朝一日回得去，我再也不四处游荡，

怀俄明对我太冷哟，冬天又太长，

等围拢再开始，我的钱哪，全花个精*光*喽。」

===Original===
Clint had got harmonious, and with the other cowboy a trying to keep up on the mouth organ was singing the song. He mixed in about ten verses and took in other songs as he went, the tunes changed some, but the “Texas whang” he carried with the tunes made ’em more or less alike and all appreciated the same.

===Chinese===
克林特来了雅兴，跟那个在口琴上勉强追赶的牛仔一道唱着这歌。他前后掺进去十来段词，又顺带带进了别的歌，调门变了一些，可他带在调子里的那股「得州腔」（Texas whang），把这些歌弄得大同小异，大伙儿也照样都受用。

===Original===
The last verse had died down, some of the boys looked up expecting more, and others, hat brim pulled down, was stargazing at the fire and letting the memories the songs had brought lead ’em back to times and happenings that’d been stirred the most.

===Chinese===
最后一段余音散了，有的伙计抬起头，巴望着再来几段；有的把帽檐压得低低的，眼睛落在火堆上出了神，任歌儿勾来的回忆，领他们回到那些被搅动得最深的年头和往事里去。

===Original===
All was quiet, excepting for the crackling of the fire, and one of the boys was just about to speak the name of some other old song when off from the direction where the remuda was held, a nicker was heard.

===Chinese===
四下里静悄悄的，只有柴火噼啪响。一个伙计正要开口点另一首老歌的名，忽然，从雷穆达拴放的方向，传来一声低嘶。

===Original===
Clint looked towards where the familiar nicker had come and smiled﻿—the cowboy’s voice had carried to where Smoky had been grazing, and the little horse had stopped grinding on his feed soon as the first verse had hit his ears; he’d listened on thru to the end, nickered, and watched the fire on the creek bottom from where the voice had come.

===Chinese===
克林特朝那声熟悉的低嘶传来的方向望去，笑了——牛仔的歌声传到了烟儿吃草的地方，头一段刚进耳朵，小马就停了嚼；他一路听到底，低嘶一声，然后望着溪谷谷底那团歌声来处的火光。

===Original===
He watched it long into the night till all was quiet and the fire had dwindled down to coals; time for first night guard to be relieved was near and Smoky was still watching. Pecos was dozing off a ways, and pretty soon Smoky begin to feel a little groggy too and he dozed with him.

===Chinese===
他望着那火光，望到深夜，直到万籁俱寂、火苗萎成了一堆红炭；头一茬值夜快到换班的时候了，烟儿还望着。佩科斯在不远处已经迷迷糊糊，不多时烟儿也犯起了困，便跟着他打起盹来。

---

===Original===
A new day was no more than hinted by the paling sky to the east when the “nighthawk” begin bunching the ponies and hazing ’em towards camp. It was still faint daylight when the catch ropes was a hissing over the ponies heads once again and loops settled around slick necks. Broncs was drug out, and a fighting against the saddle while the sun was still back of the ridges, but the day’s work had started at the roundup camp.

===Chinese===
东边的天刚泛出一线白、预示新的一天，「夜鹰」就开始把马拢成堆，往营地赶。天色还半明半暗，套马索又贴着马们的头顶飕飕作响，索圈落在油亮的脖颈上。野马被一匹匹拖出来，趁日头还窝在山梁背后就跟鞍子较劲——可围拢营地一天的活计，已经开了张。

===Original===
In a short while the remuda was let out again, and the day wrangler started grazing ’em while the outfit broke camp for other grounds. When all was loaded in the big wagons the pilot took the lead, and when the sun showed up to begin its circle up above, the cook had already moved his kitchen some ten miles and the pots was beginning to feel the heat of the fire underneath.

===Chinese===
不多时雷穆达又被放了出来，白日马倌领着它们啃草，班底这边则拔营搬家。等大车装停当，领路人打头先行；日头露脸、上天开始画它的圈儿那会儿，厨子已经把他的厨房挪出了十来英里，锅底也已经开始领教底下炉火的火力了。

===Original===
Smoky was in another new country that day, and as he grazed with the remuda he noticed the same workings of the day before, another big herd was brought in from that morning’s “circle,” then one more that afternoon, more cattle was cut out and then singed hair floated on the breeze once more.

===Chinese===
那天烟儿到了又一片生地界。他跟着雷穆达吃草的当儿，见识了跟头一天一样的路数：那天上午的「跑圈」又赶回一大群牛，下午又是一群，更多的牛被剔出去，跟着，燎毛的气味又随风飘了起来。

===Original===
Twice again he was corralled with the remuda for fresh horses the riders was needing, and the little horse was slow beginning to get used to the sound of the ropes and the sight of the strange cowboys. Clint was to see him at the last corralling of the day and when the nighthawk took the ponies out for the night Smoky nipped Pecos in the flanks. He felt playful.

===Chinese===
为着骑手们要换新马，他又跟着雷穆达进了两回畜栏；绳子的声响、生牛仔的模样，小马慢慢开始见惯了。当天最后一回进栏时，克林特来看了他；等「夜鹰」把马群带出去过夜，烟儿咬了佩科斯的侧腹一口。他来了玩兴。

===Original===
Outside of the time he spent in the rope corral the little horse was enjoying the following of the roundup mighty well﻿—there was always so many horses around, and all with the bellering of the big herds and the dust that was kept up sure tallied up with the beat of his heart. He hadn’t figgered on what to expect being one of the remuda that way, and being that he couldn’t make out all that went on he didn’t know just what could be expected, and that’s why maybe he wasn’t worried much.

===Chinese===
除了蹲绳畜栏的那点工夫，小马把跟着围拢队的日子过得有滋有味——四围总少不了那么多马，再加上大牛群的哞吼和扬个不停的尘土，桩桩件件都跟他的心跳对得上拍。作为雷穆达的一分子会摊上什么，他原先没盘算过；眼下的名堂他又瞧不透，不知道该等着什么，所以呢，也许正因如此，他也不大犯愁。

===Original===
“Going to make a very big circle this morning, Jeff?”

===Chinese===
「今儿上午要跑特大的一圈吗，杰夫？」

===Original===
It was the morning of the third day that Clint asked the foreman that question, and when Jeff answered he understood what was on Clint’s mind. He grinned at the cowboy as he spoke.

===Chinese===
那是第三天早上，克林特拿这话问工头；杰夫一答，也就明白了克林特心里打的什么主意。他一边说，一边冲着牛仔咧嘴笑。

===Original===
“You go ahead and ride your Smoky horse, Clint, I’ll put you on the inside circle so as it won’t be too hard on him.”

===Chinese===
「你只管骑你的烟儿马，克林特，我把你派在里圈，省得他吃不消。」

===Original===
And that’s how come when it was Smoky’s turn to be rode that the easiest was handed him. The horse spotted Clint coming towards him, a rope was in his hands but no loop was dragging and he met the cowboy halfways.

===Chinese===
所以轮到烟儿被骑的那一回，摊给他的就是顶轻省的差事。马瞅见克林特朝自己走来，手里虽拿着绳子，却没拖着索圈，便半道里迎了上去。

===Original===
Of the many ponies that makes up a “remuda” there’s seldom any that can be walked up to, even the gentlest has to be roped. They’re broke that way and it all saves time, for a cowboy can stand off thirty feet, rope his horse and start leading him out from there. It saves him many steps and when there’s so many riders and horses, them steps and the time it’d take to make ’em sure would accumulate. Then again there’s so many wild ponies that would *have* to be roped anyway. So making the whole thing simple, every horse is caught with a loop. No good roper ever whirls a loop in catching horses, and the only sound that’s heard is when the rope splits from the ground to the pony’s head.

===Chinese===
组成「雷穆达」的那许多马里，难得有一匹能让人径直走上前去——最温顺的也得套。它们就是照这个路数驯出来的，图的全是省工夫：牛仔站在三十英尺开外，一索套住自己的马，从那儿就牵上走。这给他省下多少步的路，而骑手和马都这么多的时候，那些步数、加上走那些步数的工夫，攒起来可不是小账。再说了，还有那么多野马，横竖*非*套不可。于是图个痛快，干脆每匹马都用索圈逮。真正的好套手套马从不抡圈，唯一的声响，是套索离开地面直取马头的那一声。

===Original===
Once in a while, and even with real cow outfits that’s well run, there’s exceptions in roping every horse that way. Smoky was the one exception on the Rocking R, and every cowboy was good natured jealous at the way that mouse colored son of a gun of a horse would stick his head out every time Clint came around and then left his hiding place from amongst the other horses to meet him.

===Chinese===
偶尔也有例外，连规规矩矩、运营得当的正牌牧牛班底里，也免不了有不必回回都套的马。烟儿就是摇椅R上独一份的例外：克林特人一到，那匹鼠灰色的、了不得的家伙就把脑袋探出来，撇下自己在别的马堆里的藏身处，迎上前去——牛仔们个个眼热这份光景，眼热得都带着善意。

===Original===
Smoky knowed that something was up soon as Clint came near him, but whatever it was he was anxious to be at it;﻿—him and that cowboy would get along.

===Chinese===
克林特一挨近，烟儿就知道有名堂，可不管是什么名堂，他都巴望马上就上手；——他和那个牛仔，合得来。

===Original===
The little horse humped up as he felt the cinch, and Clint grinned as he remarked:

===Chinese===
肚带一紧，小马就弓起了背，克林特咧嘴笑着一句话丢过来：

===Original===
“Going to make this old broke-down cowboy ride this morning, huh?”

===Chinese===
「今儿上午就要折腾这老掉渣的牛仔骑马喽，嗯哼？」

===Original===
And Smoky did. He bogged his head soon as Clint was well set, and bucked and bellered all over the flat like he was a man-eating outlaw. It was the right thing for a live horse to do them cold fall mornings, and Clint was enjoying fanning the dust off Smoky’s round rump the same as that pony enjoyed the idea that he sure was giving somebody a tossing.

===Chinese===
烟儿也真这么干了。克林特刚坐稳，他就把脑袋往下一扎，满平地尥蹶子、嘶吼，活脱一匹吃人的劣马。秋天清冷的早晨，一匹活蹦乱跳的马正该来这么一手；克林特挥帽扇着烟儿圆屁股上扬起的尘土，扇得那叫一个痛快，跟小马想着「这回可把谁谁谁颠了个结实」一样痛快。

===Original===
“Better save some of that,” says Clint as he finally pulled Smoky’s head up, “cause you might need all the energy you got before you get back.”

===Chinese===
「省着点用吧，」克林特终于把烟儿的脑袋拉起来时说，「说不定还没到家，你就得把浑身的力气全使出来。」

===Original===
About twelve miles or so from camp a knoll was reached; from there Jeff “scattered” his riders to circle and comb the country on the way back. Clint and another rider was the last to be let go, and on the “inside” brought with ’em all the cattle they found. Half ways back to camp, Smoky begin to notice big dusts on both sides of him; them dusts kept a getting closer and closer till pretty soon he begin to see that it was more cattle making them dusts. Herds kept a being drove in with the bunch Clint and the other rider had rounded up, and by the time camp was reached, all the dusts had throwed in and made one. Twenty or more riders and over a thousand head of cattle was turned to the cutting grounds and held there a milling.

===Chinese===
离营地十二英里上下，到了一座小圆丘跟前；杰夫从那儿「撒开」骑手，让他们一路「梳拢」着地界往回兜。克林特和另一名骑手最后放出去，走「里圈」，把沿路寻见的牛全带上。回程走到一半，烟儿开始注意到自己两边腾起大股大股的尘土；那些尘土越靠越近，不多时他就看明白了：扬尘的又是一群群牛。一群又一群的牛被赶拢来，并进克林特和那名骑手收拢的班里；等到了营地，所有的尘土已经汇成了一片。二十几名骑手、一千多头牛，被转向分群场，圈在那儿打转转。

===Original===
Smoky was tired, he’d been breathing dust and turning bleary-eyed critters till it seemed like there’d be no end. Besides it felt awful hot on his back where the saddle was, and even though Clint often got off, uncinched the saddle and raised it so the cool air could circulate thru, it wasn’t long when his back, not used to long saddling, would feel as hot as ever again.

===Chinese===
烟儿累了。他吃了半天灰，撵着转那些眼神发直的牲口，转得像永远没有个头。再说鞍子底下那块脊背也热得难受；克林特尽管时常下马，解开肚带，把鞍子抬起来给凉风过一过，可不消多大工夫，那片还不惯久驮鞍具的脊背，就又热得跟先前一个样了。

===Original===
It was a great relief to the little horse when the saddle was pulled off as they reached camp and the rope corral. Clint then led him to the creek and washed the dry sweat off his back with the cool water, and as that was done Smoky right away forgot the work of that first circle. He felt a lot at ease with everything in general as Clint turned him loose in the corral, and a while later when fresh horses was caught and ropes begin sailing again, Smoky wasn’t for hunting a hole like the times before; he felt that he’d done his.﻿—Pecos was snared while standing a few feet from him, and then the ponies was turned loose. But there Smoky lagged behind a little; he’d spotted Clint, who was saddling another horse, and he stood in his tracks, watching, and maybe wondering. Then the wrangler came, and Smoky followed the remuda up the draw.

===Chinese===
到了营地、到了绳畜栏跟前，鞍子被掀下来，小马浑身一轻，如释重负。克林特牵他到溪边，用清凉的溪水把他背上干结的汗渍洗掉；这一洗，头一趟跑圈的辛苦当下就被烟儿忘到了脑后。克林特把他放进畜栏时，他对样样事情都舒坦得很；过了一阵，新马被抓、绳圈重新飞起来的时候，烟儿不再像前几回那样钻空子找地方躲了——他觉得自己尽过了力。——佩科斯就站在离他几步远的地方被套了去，随后马群被放开。可烟儿在这儿稍稍落在了后头：他瞅见了克林特，克林特正在备另一匹马；他站在原地望着，也许还琢磨着什么。后来马倌来了，烟儿便跟着雷穆达上了洼地。

===Original===
Plenty of grass, under, and all around him, and a chance to stand still was for the first time appreciated by the mouse colored gelding. He’d had a taste of real work, the first taste, and with it had come the feeling that he wasn’t no half broke bronc no more. He was even beginning to look at the critter with a knowing eye and something was sprouting up in him which left no doubt but what *he* was the boy that could handle ’er.

===Chinese===
身底下、四周围全是满满的草，外加一个站定了歇气的机会——鼠灰色的骟马头一回懂得领这个情。正经活计的滋味他尝过了，头一遭的滋味，随滋味还来了这么个感觉：他再不是什么半吊子野马了。他甚至开始拿内行的眼光打量牲口，心里冒出点什么东西，让他笃定：*他*就是那个能拿捏这活计的角色。

===Original===
He never figgered on how much there was to learn in the ways of handling that split-hoofed range animal﻿—he’d had no way to know as yet, and as it was he grazed feeling sure that he knowed a lot about ’em. He felt equal to the old saddle marked cowhorses that was in the same remuda and he wouldn’t have nothing to do with the raw broncs that was mixed in. But there that high opinion of himself was stopped, for the old cowhorses wouldn’t let him associate with ’em and as they’d chase him away, he failed to notice that they felt the same about him as he did about the uneddicated raw broncs.

===Chinese===
摆弄这种劈蹄子的牧场牲口里头还有多少学问要学，他压根没盘算过——他也没法知道；就这么着，他一边啃草，一边笃信自己已经懂了它们个七七八八。他觉得自己不输同一支雷穆达里那些带着鞍印的老牧牛马，对掺在里头的生坯子野马，则是不屑与之为伍的。可他这份自视甚高，到这儿就碰了壁：老牧牛马们不待见他，撵他走；他也没瞧出来，那些老马看他的眼光，跟他看那些没调教过的生坯子，是同一副眼光。

===Original===
But then, credit had to be handed to the little horse on account that even though he still had a powerful lot to learn, he sure was all for learning, and the pride he’d naturally took in the game, along with the coaching of such a cowboy as Clint, all promised that he’d sure get there.

===Chinese===
不过话得说回来，功劳簿上也得给小马记一笔：要学的门道他还有一大堆，可他那份肯学的心是实打实的；他在这行当里自然生出的那股傲气，再加上克林特这样一位牛仔的调教，桩桩件件都担保：他准能成。

===Original===
Smoky watched every herd that came in, followed the wagon on its everyday move, and was even getting used to them ropes that sung over his head three or four times a day. Of course Clint was always on hand at each corralling to kind of help him get used to all the commotion, and came a time when the little horse knowed exactly where and which side of the corral that cowboy would be. His saddle was always on the ground a few feet on the outside, and every horse he caught to ride would always be led or “snaked” to that same spot, and Smoky got so that whenever he was corralled he’d make a rush for that one spot where he could easy reach Clint’s shirt whenever the attention of that cowboy was needed.

===Chinese===
每群牛进场，烟儿都看；车队天天搬家，他都跟着；连一天要在头顶上唱三四回的那些绳子，他都渐渐听惯了。当然，每回进栏，克林特总在场，帮着他一点点适应这一片乱哄哄。到后来，小马摸得清清楚楚：那牛仔会待在畜栏的哪个位置、哪一侧。他的鞍子总撂在外面几步远的地上，他抓来要骑的每匹马，总要牵到——或者说「拖」到——老地方去备；于是烟儿落下了这么个习惯：一进栏，就一头冲向那个地点，哪天要用得着那位牛仔的注意了，抬嘴就能够着他的衣襟。

===Original===
Each rider on the outfit was furnished on the average of ten horses; there was anyway three changes of horses every day which made it that every horse was rode from four to six hours every third day, and that’s how Smoky’s turn came. Clint rode him out on “circle” three times, and till the little horse got pretty well onto the hazing of the critter, and then that pony was of a sudden promoted to the “day-herd” class. Of course Smoky was somewhat of a privileged character or he wouldn’t made that so soon, but the way he took holt of the bit and went to work he sure didn’t disappoint Clint any.

===Chinese===
班底里每名骑手平均配十匹马；每天至少换三班马，这样摊下来，每匹马每隔三天被骑上四到六个钟头，烟儿的班就是这么轮到的。克林特带他出「跑圈」出了三回，一直出到小马把驱赶牲口的路数摸得差不离，跟着，这匹小马就一下子提拔进了「日间牛群」（day-herd）这一级。当然了，烟儿多少算个有特权的角色，不然也升不了这么快；可看他咬住嚼口、一头扎进活计的那股劲儿，他是真没让克林特失望。

===Original===
The promotion started when that cowboy thought of trying him out one day as a big herd was brought in to work. He’d changed his tired “circle” horse to Smoky, and after that pony had his buck out he lined him out to a standstill close to the milling critters. It was Clint’s and Smoky’s job to see that none broke away outside of what was cut out to be held for the “main herd.” A dozen other riders was on the same job and most all riding well reined cowhorses, and as Smoky noticed the kind of company he was keeping, a ticklish feeling came between his ears and a spark showed in his eyes.

===Chinese===
提拔是打一天开始的：那天一大群牛被赶进来过手，克林特动了念头，想拿烟儿试一试。他把跑累了的「跑圈」马换成烟儿，等小马把那一通蹶子尥完，便把他排成直线，刹停在打转转的牛群近旁。克林特和烟儿的差事，是看着别让一头牛冲出圈去——剔出来留作「主群」（main herd）的除外。另外十来名骑手干着同一份差事，骑的多半是缰口利索的牧牛马；烟儿留意到自己身边混的是这样一班同侪，两只耳朵之间起了一阵痒酥酥的感觉，眼睛里迸出了火花。

===Original===
He was about at the height of his glory and hardly able to stay on earth, when, quick as the eye could see, a big rawboned steer broke out, and wild eyed dodged past the riders and hightailed it out for the open country. In the trance Smoky was in he hardly seen anything of the critter but a flash, but as the earth had no strings on him either just then it only took a feel of the rein for him to be up and a flying. That flash that went past him a second before was recognized as an earthly critter soon as Smoky set eyes on ’er, and soon as he got the hunch that that critter needed turning, the distance between was et up the same as though that horse had been starving for such.

===Chinese===
他正风光到顶点，飘飘然几乎挂不住地面，就在这当口，说时迟那时快，一头瘦骨嶙峋的大阉牛冲了出来，瞪着狂眼从骑手们身边连躲带闪地穿过去，翘着尾巴直奔开阔地窜了。烟儿那会儿正出着神，那牲口打他眼前过，他只瞧见一道影子；可那会儿大地也没拿绳子拴着他，缰绳刚一沉，他就弹起来飞了出去。前一秒擦身过去的那道影子，烟儿眼睛一搭上就认出是头尘世间的牲口；而他一得了「这头得兜转回来」的暗示，两个之间的那段距离，就跟这匹马饿疯了似的，一口吞了个精光。

===Original===
There was a mighty satisfied smile on Clint’s face as the steer was shot back in the herd the same way he’d come out, and as for Smoky, there sure was nothing about him that suggested “the end of the trail.” He was brought to a mighty proud standstill by the herd again, and no critter broke out that he wasn’t right on the tail of from the start, unless it was in some other rider’s territory.

===Chinese===
那头阉牛照原路被射回牛群里，克林特脸上挂着一个舒坦到家的笑；至于烟儿，他浑身上下可找不出半点「路到头了」的意思。他又被刹停在牛群旁，姿态骄傲得很；此后哪头牲口再敢冲出来，除非是落在别家骑手的地界，头一起步他就咬在它尾巴上了。

===Original===
Working the herds that was drove to the cutting grounds, and holding the day-herd, was from then on Smoky’s work. He liked working the herds best on account there was more to do, but then day-herding wasn’t so bad either, Clint always seen that his rope was kept well stretched, and soon as he knowed the foreman was gone on circle with the other riders he could easy find some critter he had a grudge against and pile his rope onto, and Smoky sure enjoyed turning ’em over.

===Chinese===
从那以后，收拾赶进分群场的牛群、看守日间牛群，就成了烟儿的活计。他顶喜欢收拾牛群，因为名堂多；不过守日间牛群也不赖：克林特总惦记着让他的绳圈保持抻展，而且一瞅准工头跟着别的骑手跑圈去了，他就不愁找出一头积过怨的牲口，把绳甩上去——烟儿可太爱把它们掀个底朝白了。

===Original===
All the boys, excepting the “reps” from other outfits, had one half a day of day-herding every three days. Smoky’s time to be rode came on the dot of that time, but Clint didn’t always take him out on that, and often he’d switch so that the little horse would get plenty of work cutting out or bringing big calves and “slicks” to the branding fire, and that pony was sure beginning to shine there.

===Chinese===
除了各家牧场派来的「代表」（reps），伙计们每三天轮上半天守日间牛群。烟儿该被骑的钟点准准地落在这档口，可克林特并不回回都带他去干这个；他时常调个班，好让小马多多的剔牛、多多的把大牛犊和「光板牛」（slicks，没打烙印的）送到烙印火堆跟前——在这份活计上，这匹小马是真开始放光了。

===Original===
Once in a while though Clint would get sort of selfish and want Smoky’s company on that long half a day’s herding, and it was during them spells that the two got to be more understanding, if that’s possible, to one another. Neither was so rushed for work then, and there was times when the big herd of beef steers and cows and weaners would want to graze and not try to drift away or scatter. At them times Clint would rein Smoky up a knoll, and where both could see the whole of the herd. He’d get out of his saddle and stretch out in the shade Smoky made, and take it easy, and there, with one eye on the cowboy, the other on the herd, and swishing flies, Smoky would stand.

===Chinese===
不过克林特偶尔也会犯点私心，想在那长长的半天守群里带上烟儿做伴。正是趁那些时辰，要是还能更亲厚的话——他们俩把彼此又摸得更透了。那时候谁也不赶活，那一大群肉用阉牛、母牛和「断奶」犊也有安分吃草、不思量乱跑乱散的时候。逢上那样的时辰，克林特就把烟儿勒上一座小圆丘，挑个俩人都能望见全群的位置。他跳下鞍，往烟儿投下的荫凉里四仰八叉一躺，自在去了；烟儿就站在那儿，一只眼看着牛仔，一只眼看着牛群，尾巴甩着蝇子。

## IX / 九

===Original===
Fights for Rights

===Chinese===
为权利而战

===Original===
The fine, cool, and sunshiny days of fall was making a last stand﻿—rains begin to come, and as time was a crawling towards early winter, them rains got colder and then turned to a wet snow. Mud was where dust had been, the hard-twist throw ropes had turned stiff as steel cables, saddles and saddle blankets was wet, heavy, and cold, and the shivering ponies met the feel of them with a hump and a buck.

===Chinese===
秋天那些又晴又亮、凉爽可人的日子，正做最后的抵抗——雨下起来了；时日朝着初冬慢慢爬，那些雨一场冷过一场，末了变成了湿雪。起土的地方换成了泥，硬捻的投绳变得跟钢缆一样硬，鞍子和鞍垫湿了、沉了、凉了，打着战的马儿们一沾着这些滋味，便弓起背、尥起蹶子。

===Original===
The cowboys, all a packing long, yellow slickers, was beginning to tally up on how much wages would be due ’em. As the end of the fall roundup drawed near, and as they waded thru slush and mud from the chuck wagon to the rope corral, not many was caring. Wet socks, damp beds, two hours of shivering on night guard, saddling ornery ponies in daytime and when a feller can’t even get a footing, and then riding ’em a wondering if them ponies will stand up as they beller and buck on the slick and muddy ground, all left a hankering only for a warm dugout somewheres, where there’s a stove, a bunk to set on, and a few magazines to read as mother nature does her best to make the outside miserable.

===Chinese===
牛仔们一个个背着长身的黄雨披，开始盘算该结给自己多少工钱了。秋围拢临近收尾，他们从伙食车到绳畜栏一路蹚着雪泥，谁也懒得计较了。湿袜子、潮铺盖、值夜冻得抖上两个钟头、白天在连个下脚地都没有的当口给赖皮马备鞍、骑上去还得提着心，不知这些马在又滑又泥的地上一边嘶吼一边尥蹶子时撑不撑得住——这一切攒下来，只叫人一门心思惦记个哪儿都行的暖和地窨子（dugout）：有炉子，有条能坐的铺，还有几本杂志好翻，随老天爷在外头使足劲儿去折腾。

===Original===
The last of the beef herd had been turned over to another “wagon” of the Rocking R and shipped, and Jeff’s main herd was from then on made up of cows with big weaner calves, and all stock that’d need feeding thru the winter.

===Chinese===
最后一批肉牛已经移交给摇椅R的另一支「车队」装车运走，打那以后，杰夫的主群就换成了带着大个儿「断奶」犊的母牛，还有所有那些过冬得靠喂养的牲口。

===Original===
“A couple of weeks more now and we’ll be seeing the gates of the home ranch,” says Jeff one day, but it was a long three weeks before the stock was tended to and when camp was made for the last time. The wet snow had got flaky and dry by then and six inches of it was covering the ground.

===Chinese===
「再等一两个礼拜，咱们就能望见本部牧场的大门了，」杰夫有一天说。可把牲口料理完、最后一回扎营，足足又拖了长长的三个礼拜。到那时，湿雪已经变得又干又酥，地上积了六英寸厚。

===Original===
“Now hold on a minute, Smoky, and give a feller a chance, won’t you?”

===Chinese===
「先稳住一会儿，烟儿，给人个机会，行不行？」

===Original===
It was Clint a talking, and trying to hold Smoky down till he got his foot in the stirrup. The cowboy being all bundled up couldn’t handle himself as he’d like to; the little horse was cold; crusted snow had to be rubbed off his back before the saddle could be put on and he was aching to put his head down and go to bucking so he could warm up.

===Chinese===
说话的是克林特，正使劲按住烟儿，好腾出工夫把脚踩进镫。牛仔裹得严严实实，手脚放不开；小马也冷；背上结了壳的雪得先搓净才能上鞍，他早就憋着要低头弓背尥上一通，好把身子暖起来。

===Original===
Clint was only halfways in the saddle when that pony lit into it, but the cowboy didn’t mind that. His blood was also a long ways from the boiling point and any excuse to get circulating good was welcome.

===Chinese===
克林特才半边身子进了鞍，那马就开了张，可牛仔不在乎。他自己的血也离滚开远得很，但凡有个由头能让血好好循环循环，都欢迎。

===Original===
Around and around him Smoky went and all in one spot. All the fancy twists of a bucking pony was gone over and the rider met him all the way, and as Clint rode and fanned and laughed, he’d get fast glimpses of other riders and other horses a tearing up the white landscape and getting down to the earth underneath.

===Chinese===
烟儿围着他转着圈尥，全在一块地皮上。尥蹶子的马会耍的花招，他一招不落地过了一遍，骑手一路全都接得住；克林特又骑、又扇、又笑，眼角还飞快掠过别的骑手、别的马，把那片白茫茫的景致撕腾得稀碎，直撕腾到底下的黑土。

===Original===
It was the last day of the roundup, all the work was done, the cook climbed on his seat, grabbed the lines the boys handed him, and letting out a war whoop, scared his already spooky team into a long lope towards the home ranch.

===Chinese===
这是围拢的最后一天，活计全完了。厨子爬上驾车座，接过伙计们递来的缰绳，吼出一声战吼，把他那本就一惊一乍的骡马队吓得撒开一路大跑，直奔本部牧场去了。

---

===Original===
The sight of the big gates was a mighty fine one to all as the outfit clattered in, specially with the sky a threatening the way it was; the old cowhorses had their ears pointed towards the big pole corrals. They knowed what the sight of them meant at that time of the year and none tried to break away as the wrangler run ’em in. They was turned out in a big pasture that night, and the next days a couple of riders came, bunched ’em up, and took ’em thru another gate leading out of the ranch.

===Chinese===
班底哐啷哐啷进了大门，那两扇大门的气派在人人眼里都美得很，尤其老天还阴着一张吓唬人的脸；老牧牛马们把耳朵齐齐支向那几座大木杆畜栏。它们知道每年这个时节见了这玩意儿意味着什么，马倌往里赶时，没有一匹想溜。当晚它们被放进一片大草场，过一两天来了两个骑手，把它们拢成一群，赶着出了另一道通向牧场外头的大门。

===Original===
Clint had took it onto hisself to be one of them riders﻿—he wanted to get another look at Smoky before letting him go to the winter range and find out for sure just what conditions that range would be in. The outskirts of it was reached that noon and as Clint rode along back of the remuda he was more than satisfied to notice the tall feed that the six inches of snow couldn’t hide. He noticed the breaks and the shelter they would give, then the thick growth of willows along the creek bottom and which meant more shelter.

===Chinese===
克林特自荐当了那两个骑手之一——他想在放烟儿去冬季牧场（winter range）之前再看他一眼，也顺便弄个确凿，看看那片牧场到底是什么光景。晌午到了牧场的边沿，克林特骑马缀在雷穆达后头，一眼瞧见六英寸厚的雪也遮不住的高草，心里别提多满意。他看了看那些沟壑地（breaks）和它们能给的遮蔽，又看了溪谷底上密密丛丛的柳树——那意味着又多一处藏身的地方。

===Original===
Clint stopped his horse and the two hundred ponies was left to scatter. His eyes run over the well known backs for a last time, he wouldn’t be seeing them again till spring roundup started and he watched ’em slowly graze away. Many was in that bunch that he’d broke and named, and starting from the meanest fighting bronc of the rough string, and taking all the ponies on up to the best cowhorse of the foreman’s string there wasn’t one that Clint didn’t know and know mighty well as to tricks and good or bad points.

===Chinese===
克林特勒住坐骑，两百匹马撒开任他们散去。他把那些熟识的脊背最后巡了一遍——开春围拢开张之前是再见不着它们了——望着它们慢慢啃着草走远。那群里有许多是他驯过、起过名的，从烈性坐骑班（rough string）里最凶最好斗的野马数起，一路数到工头坐骑班里顶好的牧牛马，没有一匹克林特不认识，而且连它们的把戏、长处短处，全都门儿清。

===Original===
A big old sorrel with a kinked neck and by the name of Boar Hound caught his eye, and Clint remembered how that pony tried to commit suicide rather than be rode and how he’d now changed to wanting to commit murder instead and kill a few cowboys. Then a smile spread over his face as he spotted a tall Roman-nosed gruller who’d never made a jump till a rope got under his tail. He’d took a sudden liking to bucking from then on and made hisself a reputation at that which scattered over four counties.

===Chinese===
一匹脖子上拧着劲儿的老大栗色马入了他的眼，名字叫「野猪猎犬」（Boar Hound）。克林特记起这马当年怎么宁可寻死也不肯让人骑，如今又怎么改了主意要行凶，想弄死几个牛仔。跟着他瞅见一匹高大的罗马鼻（Roman-nosed，凸梁鼻）灰兔褐马（gruller），脸上便漾开笑意——这马从前一蹦也不蹦，直到有条套索钻到了他尾巴底下。打那以后他忽然迷上了尥蹶子，还靠这个闯出了名声，散遍了四个县。

===Original===
Every horse Clint looked at brought to memory some kind of a story, and there was a variety of expressions which changed with every horse that came under his eye. A big shaggy black looked his way and snorted and with the sight of him Clint remembered how that horse had reached ahead one time and kicked to pieces a cowboy that’d been unsaddling him.

===Chinese===
克林特看的每一匹马都勾出一段故事，随着一匹匹马进入视线，他的神情也变来变去。一匹毛长蓬乱的大黑马朝他这边望了望，打了个响鼻；一见这马，克林特就想起它有一回怎么朝前探身，把一个正给它卸鞍的牛仔踹了个稀碎。

===Original===
His expression was mighty solemn at the thought of that, but it didn’t last long. Like a ray of sunshine, something shot out and scattered that dark cloud of memories four ways﻿—Smoky had showed himself from behind other horses and not over fifty feet from where Clint was setting on his horse.

===Chinese===
想到这事，他的神情凝重得很，可惜没撑多久。像一道阳光，有个什么东西蹿出来，把那团记忆的乌云四下里冲散——烟儿从别的马身后露了面，离克林特立马的地方不到五十英尺。

===Original===
The cowboy’s face lit up with a smile at the sight of the pony, and getting down off his saddle he made tracks his way, but he didn’t have to go all the way, for soon as Smoky spotted him he left Pecos, his running pardner, behind, and nickering came to meet Clint.

===Chinese===
一见这匹小马，牛仔的脸顿时亮起笑容。他翻身下鞍，迈步朝那边去，其实根本不必走全程——烟儿一眼认出他，撂下他的跑伴儿（running pardner）佩科斯，低嘶着迎克林特来了。

===Original===
“A feller would think to see you act that you’re a sure enough sugar eater,” Clint remarked as the little horse came up to him and stopped. He rubbed a hand on the pony’s head and went on.

===Chinese===
「瞧你这副做派，人家还当你真是顿顿离不得糖的呢，」克林特在小马走上前来站定时说。他伸手在马头上揉了揉，接着道：

===Original===
“Well, anyway, Smoky, I’m glad to see that you’ve got a mighty fine winter range to run on; with all the feed I see here and the shelter that’s with it you hadn’t ought to lose an ounce of fat,” Clint felt for the pony’s ribs and grinning resumed, “and if you ever get any fatter than you are now you’ll be plum worthless.”

===Chinese===
「喏，不管怎么说，烟儿，看到你有这么一片好得不得了的冬季牧场可跑，我挺高兴；就我在这儿见的草，加上跟着草一道来的藏风处，你一指膘都不该掉，」克林特摸了摸小马的肋条，笑嘻嘻地接着说，「你要是比眼下再肥下去，可就压根儿不值钱喽。」

===Original===
Smoky followed Clint as he turned and went to where he’d left his horse, “I wonder,” says that cowboy, “if you’ve got the hunch that you won’t be seeing me no more till next spring?﻿—That’s a long time ain’t it? But never mind old horse, I’m the first cowboy you’re going to see when spring does break up.”

===Chinese===
克林特转身朝拴马的地方走，烟儿跟在后头。「我纳闷，」那牛仔说，「你是不是心里有数，到来年开春之前再见不着我了？——这可是段不短的时日，是不？不过别往心里去，老马，开春雪一化，你头一个见着的牛仔就是我。」

===Original===
Clint was about to get on his horse and ride away, but he stopped, and felt of Smoky’s hide once more.

===Chinese===
克林特正要上马走人，却又停住，把烟儿的皮毛又摸了一遍。

===Original===
“Well, so long, Smoky, take care of yourself and don’t let anything drag you down.”

===Chinese===
「那么，回见了，烟儿。照顾好自己，别让任何事情把你拖垮。」

===Original===
Smoky watched him ride away and nickered once as the cowboy went over the point of a ridge and disappeared. He watched a long time even after that and till he was sure Clint was gone, and finally turning went to grazing back till he was by the side of Pecos again.

===Chinese===
烟儿望着他骑远，等那牛仔翻过一道山梁的梁尖没了影，低低嘶了一声。那之后他还望了好久，直到拿准克林特真走了，才转过身，边啃草边往回磨蹭，一直蹭回佩科斯身边。

---

===Original===
The winter came and hit the range with the average amount of snow, freeze ups, and cold winds. The cayotes howled the hunger they felt, for there was no weak stock to speak of for them to feed off of, and outside of small varmints they could get once in a while, pickings was mighty poor. Horses and cattle was and stayed in fine shape; and the stockman could hit his bed after the long day’s ride knowing that he could go to sleep right off, and not lay awake a wondering what he could do to pull his stock thru.

===Chinese===
冬天来了，照着常年的份量朝牧场上撒雪、上冻、灌冷风。郊狼嚎着肚子里的饥——简直没有弱牲口可供它们下嘴，除了偶尔到手的几只小野东西，能捞的油水少得可怜。马和牛一直膘肥体壮；牧人跑完一天长路倒上铺，知道自己脑袋一挨枕头就能睡死过去，用不着睁着眼干琢磨怎么把牲口拖过这个冬。

===Original===
Smoky met all what the weather had to hand him, with a good layer of fat, a thick skin, and a long coat of hair. He lost a few ounces but he could of spared many pounds and felt as good. Feed was a plenty and the little pawing that had to be done to reach it was like so much exercise and only kept his blood in good circulating order.

===Chinese===
老天爷发下来什么，烟儿都拿一层好膘、一张厚皮、一身长毛接着。掉是掉了那么几盎司，可就算匀掉许多磅他也顶得住，感觉照样好。草料管够，刨开雪够着草的那点小活儿权当锻炼，反倒让他一身血循环得顺顺当当。

===Original===
The winter months wore on, the ponies drifted from ridge to ridge, from shelter to shelter, and nothing much came to disturb the quiet of the land, nothing much excepting when a big shaggy black tried to throw in with Pecos, the same black that’d kicked the cowboy over the Great Divide. But his interfering and butting in was welcome though in a way. Smoky and Pecos had so much good energy going to waste that they’d been just aching for some excuse to use some of it for some good.

===Chinese===
