冬天的月份一天天捱过去，马群从这道山梁挪到那道山梁，从一处避风地挪到另一处避风地，没什么来搅扰这片土地的清净——除了那匹想凑到佩科斯身边搭伙的毛长蓬乱的大黑马，就是把一个牛仔踢过大分水岭（Great Divide，送了性命）的那一匹。不过他这么横插一杠子，倒还真有点儿受欢迎。烟儿和佩科斯一身好力气闲得白白糟蹋，正馋着找个由头，把力气派上点正经用场。

===Original===
It came about that the big black had took a liking to Pecos, and at the same time a dislike for Smoky. Pecos was neutral for a while and wondered what the black was up to when he tried to chase Smoky away from him. Smoky wouldn’t chase worth a nickel but he was getting skinned up considerable a trying to hold his ground. Things went on that way for a day or so and every once in a while the black made a dive for Smoky like he was going to tear him to pieces.﻿—His intentions was good, but Smoky sure was no invalid. When the snow settled again where he’d held his ground the little horse hadn’t give away one inch.

===Chinese===
原来那匹大黑马相中了佩科斯，同时也看烟儿不顺眼。佩科斯起先两不相帮，只是纳闷这黑马到底想干什么，居然要把烟儿从他身边撵走。论逃，烟儿半个子儿都不肯——他只认死守地盘，皮肉为此着实挂了些彩。这么着闹了一天上下，那黑马时不时朝烟儿猛扑一通，像要把他撕成碎片似的。——他心意是好的，可惜烟儿也断不是个病秧子。等雪面重新落定，小马守的那块地，一寸也没让。

===Original===
But the black was twice as old as Smoky, more up to the game of fighting, and heavier by a hundred pounds. All that begin to tell on the mouse colored hide, and there might of come a time when Smoky would of had to hightail it, only, as the scraps was repeated off and on, Pecos begin to notice and realize that that black was taking too much territory, and he didn’t like him nohow.

===Chinese===
可那黑马比烟儿年长一倍，打架的道行深得多，还重上一百磅。这一切渐渐在那身鼠灰色的皮毛上见了账，弄不好早晚有一刻，烟儿真得夹起尾巴开溜——幸亏架一回断一回续地打着，佩科斯慢慢看出、也咂摸明白了：这黑马占的地盘太宽，他怎么瞧他怎么不顺眼。

===Original===
So, that’s how come, that when the black put down his ears and made another grand tearing rush for Smoky, that something struck him from the off side and upset him and his plans of attack all to pieces. He found hisself jerked off his feet and rolled plum over the top of Smoky and he lit head first on the other side. When he picked himself up out of the snow his spirits was dampened some in wonder, and more so when he shook his head and was able to see and noticed that there was two mighty vicious looking ponies a waiting for him to come again. He shook his head once more at that, and as Smoky and Pecos bowed their necks and came his way the black turned tail and started a looking for other company and which would appreciate him more.

===Chinese===
所以呀，等到那黑马抿下耳朵、又朝烟儿发起一轮气势汹汹的猛冲时，有个东西从背侧撞上了他，把他连同他的进攻计划一古脑儿掀了个粉碎。他只觉四脚离地，被扯得整个从烟儿头顶上滚了过去，脑袋朝下砸在另一边。等他从雪里爬起来，那股气焰先懵了三分；再一摇头，眼睛能瞧清楚了——好家伙，两匹满脸凶相的马正候着他再来一回呢，气焰便又灭了七分。他为此又摇了摇头，而烟儿和佩科斯弓着脖子朝他通过来时，这黑马调转屁股就溜，另找更能赏识他的伴儿去了。

===Original===
But whether it was orneriness or just plain thick headedness, the black tried to butt in again the next day; maybe he just wasn’t convinced. Anyway, Pecos noticed him first and before the black could even get to Smoky. War was started right there, but Pecos was no match for the black and even though he wasn’t for quitting, the worst of the battle was on his side. It was about when the crusted snow was flying the thickest that Smoky, who’d been off a ways, noticed the commotion. He seen his pardner down on his knees and the black a chewing away on him, and right about then the standing Smoky was transformed into a eleven hundred pound bombshell. The explosion came as he connected with the black and then black fur begin to fly and soar up above.﻿—Somehow or other the black managed to gather enough of his scattered senses to know what had happened; them senses told him to act, and act quick, and he did. He tore himself away from the pressing, tearing mixture of flying hoofs and sharp teeth and split the breeze making far apart tracks to where horseflesh wasn’t so thick.

===Chinese===
可不知是赖皮劲儿没散，还是纯粹的死脑筋，第二天那黑马又来硬挤；兴许他就是不服。反正佩科斯头一个发现了他，赶在他挨到烟儿之前。战端当场就开，可佩科斯不是那黑马的对手，他虽说不肯认输，败势却全在他那头。差不多在雪壳子飞得最密的时候，在一旁待着的烟儿瞧见了这场乱子。他看见他的伙计跪倒在地，那黑马正咬着他不撒口——就在那一瞬，站着的烟儿变身成了一枚一千一百磅重的炸弹。他一撞上那黑马，爆炸就来了，黑色的马毛跟着四处横飞、直上云天。——那黑马好歹收拢起四散的魂儿，明白了出了什么事；那些魂儿催他快行动，马上行动，他也照办了。他从飞蹄利齿、又压又撕的一团乱麻里挣脱出来，撕开风一溜而去，蹄印隔得老开，直奔马肉不那么扎堆的地方去了。

===Original===
The next day he was seen with Boar Hound, the kinked-necked sorrel, the Roman-nosed gruller, and a few more ornery ponies of the “rough string.” A company bunch more fitting to his kind.

===Chinese===
第二天，有人瞧见他和野猪猎犬——那匹拧脖子栗色马、罗马鼻灰兔褐马，还有「烈性坐骑班」里另外几匹赖皮马——待在了一处。总算是伙更对他脾气的伴儿。

---

===Original===
The days was getting longer and warmer, the snow begin packing and melting some, and pretty soon bare patches of ground showed in plain sight. Smoky and Pecos’ hides begin a itching and the two was often busy a scratching one another and starting from the neck went to the withers along the backbone to the rump and back again. Big bunches of long winter hair begin a slipping and falling to the ground as they scratched, and came a time when as they rolled, more of that hair was left till finally patches of short slick satin-like hair begin to show.

===Chinese===
白天一天天变长变暖，雪开始板结、化冻，没多久，一块块光地明晃晃露了出来。烟儿和佩科斯的皮发起痒来，两个常常忙着互相蹭：从脖子蹭起，顺着脊梁骨过耆甲、到屁股，再蹭回来。一撮撮长长的冬毛随着蹭蹭洒洒落地，到了后来，他们打个滚都能多留下一层毛，末了，一小片一小片又短又滑、缎子似的毛开始露头。

===Original===
Then green and tender grass begin to loom up and plentiful, and that finished the work of ridding the ponies hides of all the long hair that was left. Creeks was swelling from the waters of the fast melting snows, spring had come and the sunshine and warm winds that came with it was doing its work.

===Chinese===
跟着，又嫩又青的草大片大片冒了头，长得密密实实，这一来把马身上剩下的长毛彻底送了终。溪水叫急化的雪水灌得涨起来，春天来了，随之而来的阳光和暖风也各自忙起了各自的活计。

===Original===
The roundup cook was once again scrubbing on the chuck box that was on the end of the long wagon, and the cowboys, one by one begin a drifting in from parts near and far anxious to be starting on the spring works again. Some came from the different cow camps of the Rocking R range, a few of the riders that’d been let go when work was done the fall before never showed up, but others rode in and after a few words with Jeff took the places of them that was missing.

===Chinese===
围拢的厨子又在长车尾上的伙食箱上刷洗开了，牛仔们也从远近各处一个接一个往回流，急着想再开春天的活计。有的来自摇椅R牧场各处的牧牛营子（cow camps）；头年秋天活儿完了被辞掉的几个骑手再没露面，另一些人骑马来了，跟杰夫说上几句话，便补了缺。

===Original===
Clint had wintered at one of the outfit’s camps and drawed his wages regular, and when the range land begin to get bare of snow and the watching out for weak stock was no more necessary he put his bed on one horse, his saddle on another, and headed for the home ranch. He was one of the first riders to reach that place, and when the horse roundup started he was one of the first to have his horse saddled, topped off, and lined out to sashay in all of the ponies that could be found on the horse range.

===Chinese===
克林特在班底的一处营子里过的冬，工钱照领。等牧场土地上见了黑、不必再照看弱畜，他就把铺盖驮上一匹马、鞍子搭上另一匹，奔本部牧场去了。他是最早赶到那儿的骑手之一；套马围拢一开张，他又头一批备好马、骑服（top off）妥当，列队出发去把马场上能找见的马统统收拢进来。

===Original===
Smoky had been feeding on the sunny side of a butte, and for no reason other than to be looking around he raised his head; only his ears and eyes showed as he looked over the top of that butte; but that was enough for him to see a rider coming his direction, and see him before that rider ever had a hunch any horses was around anywheres near.

===Chinese===
烟儿正在一座平顶孤山向阳的那面啃草，不为别的，只想四下望望，便抬起了头——从山头上探出去的只有耳朵和眼睛；可这就够了，他望见一个骑手正朝他这方向来，而且那骑手还压根儿没觉出附近哪儿有马呢，他就先望见了。

===Original===
Smoky snorted and hightailed it down the side of the butte to where Pecos and a few other ponies had also been feeding, and the way he acted left no doubt in their minds but what they should be on the move. They all was at full speed the minute he landed amongst ’em, and when the rider topped the butte where they’d been a few minutes before, they had the lead on him by near a mile.

===Chinese===
烟儿打了个响鼻，顺着孤山这面一溜烟冲下去，冲到佩科斯和另外几匹也在啃草的马那儿；他这副架势让他们全都拿准了：得挪窝。他一落进群里，大伙儿立时撒开全速；等那骑手登上他们几分钟前还在的山头，他们已领先了他将近一英里。

===Original===
But the ponies wasn’t wanting to get away near as much as might of been thought. It was only that Smoky had got spooked up at the sudden sight of the rider, and him and all the others feeling good as they did wasn’t needing much excuse. The cowboy fogged down on ’em and a little to one side so as to turn ’em. They turned easy enough even though the rider was a long ways behind, and making a big circle that rider finally had ’em headed towards the big corrals of the home ranch.

===Chinese===
不过这些马想逃的心思，远没有想的那样重。不过是烟儿乍见那骑人受了惊，加上他和大伙儿身上正得劲儿，不需要多大的由头。牛仔一阵烟似的朝他们压下来，稍稍偏到一边好拐他们的头。他们拐得挺痛快，虽然骑手远远落在后头；兜了个大圈之后，那骑手总算把他们朝着本部牧场的大畜栏赶去了。

===Original===
A big grin spread over the cowboy’s features as the sun shined on the slick back of the mouse colored horse at the lead of the bunch, and even though there was a half a mile between him and that horse, *that cowboy* knowed daggone well it was *him*, for the sun never reflected on no other horse’s hide as well as it did on Smoky’s, and besides, there was no mistaking the good feeling action of that pony’s.

===Chinese===
跑在群头的那匹鼠灰色马，油光的脊背映着太阳，牛仔的脸上顿时咧开一个大大的笑。他和那马之间虽说还隔着半英里，*那位牛仔*心里可是明明白白得很，那就是*他*——太阳照在谁的皮毛上，都没有照在烟儿身上那么亮堂；再说，那匹马这副浑身舒坦的步态，也错认不了。

===Original===
“Told you I’d be the first to see you when spring broke up,” says the cowboy as he held his horse down to a lope.

===Chinese===
「说过开春雪化，头一个见你的准是我吧，」牛仔勒着马只许大跑，嘴里说道。

===Original===
The twenty-five mile run from the time Smoky had been spotted kinda filled the bill far as running was concerned, and when the long wings of the pole corrals at the home ranch was reached the rider was right on the ponies’ tails and on the job to keep ’em going straight ahead into the corral;﻿—then the big gate closed in on ’em.

===Chinese===
打瞧见烟儿那会儿起，这一趟二十五英里的奔跑，论跑是跑了个够本；等赶到本部牧场木杆畜栏那两道长长的翼墙，骑手已经咬在马群尾巴梢上，一门心思只管让他们照直往畜栏里冲——随后，大门在他们身后合上了。

===Original===
“Guess you don’t know me no more,” says Clint to Smoky as he stood afoot in the corral and watched the pony tear around;﻿—then to hisself:

===Chinese===
「看这样子，你是认不得我喽，」克林特站在畜栏里，望着那匹横冲直撞的小马说——跟着又对自己道：

===Original===
“Maybe he don’t know it’s *me* that’s watching him.”

===Chinese===
「兴许他不知道，盯着他看的这个是*我*呢。」

===Original===
Clint was right, the long winter months of freedom without seeing one human had kind of let him get back to his natural wild instinct, and the first sight of Clint had been of just a human, and it’d spooked him up till he’d have to calm down some before it’d come to him just *who* that human was.

===Chinese===
克林特没说错：一冬自由自在，连一个人影也没见过，多少让他退回了天生的野性里；头一眼看见克林特，看见的只是「一个人」，这就吓着了他，得先定一定神，才能慢慢想起来那个人*究竟是谁*。

===Original===
The cowboy spoke to him as Smoky, wild eyed, snorted and hunted for a hole, but Clint kept a speaking, and as the pony tore around and heard the voice, something gradually came to him that seemed far away and near forgot. He stopped a couple of times to look at the cowboy, and each time his getting away was less rushing, till, as the voice kept a being heard, things got clearer and clearer in that pony’s brain.

===Chinese===
牛仔一个劲儿跟他说话，烟儿眼里全是野光，打着响鼻满处找缝儿钻，克林特只管说；那马横冲直撞地兜着圈，听着那嗓音，有个遥远得几乎忘干净的东西，一点一点回到他心里来。他停了两回，站下看看那牛仔，一回比一回逃得不急，到后来，那嗓音不住地响着，这马脑子里的东西就越发清亮了。

===Original===
Smoky had stopped once more, and neck bowed, ears straight ahead, and eyes a sparkling, faced acrost the corral to where the cowboy, still and standing, was talking to him.

===Chinese===
烟儿又停了下来，弓着脖子，耳朵直直朝前，眼睛亮闪闪的，隔着畜栏望向那个站定不动、正跟他说着话的牛仔。

===Original===
“Daggone your little hide,” says Clint, “are we going to have to get acquainted all over again?﻿—come on over here and let me run my hand over that knowledge bump of yours, and maybe I can get your think tank to functioning right again.”

===Chinese===
「真该死，你这张小皮，」克林特说，「咱们还得从头再认识一遍是怎么着？——过来，让我把手搁你那『学问包』（knowledge bump）上摸摸，兴许能把你的『思想罐』（think tank）重新拨弄转了。」

===Original===
Smoky didn’t come, but he held his ground and listened to the talk. Clint talked on and watched him till the horse lost some of his wild look and then slow and easy started walking his way. Something and away in the past seemed to hold Smoky as the cowboy slowly came nearer and nearer. His instinct was all for him a leaving the spot he was holding, but that something which stuck in his memory was the stronger and sort of kept him there.

===Chinese===
烟儿没过来，可他守着原地，听着那些话。克林特一边说一边留意他，直到马眼里那股野气淡了些，他才慢而轻地朝这边迈步。牛仔一步步挨近的当口，好像有个远远搁在过去里的什么东西拴住了烟儿。他的本能一个劲儿催他离开这块守着的地，可粘在他记忆里的那个东西更硬气些，愣是把他钉在了那儿。

===Original===
Clint came on a few steps at the time, and then stopped, and talking the while, took his time till he was within a few feet of Smoky. A little flaw of any kind right then in that human’s actions could of spoilt things easy and sent the pony a skeedaddling away from there in a hurry; but Clint knowed horses and specially Smoky too well to do anything of the kind. He knowed just what was going on between that pony’s ears, and how to agree with all that mixed in there.

===Chinese===
克林特每回只挪几步，随即停住，嘴上不停地说，不慌不忙，直到离烟儿只几英尺远。这时候那个人举动上但凡出一星半点的岔子，都容易坏事，管叫这马撒腿就逃、头也不回；可克林特太懂马，尤其太懂烟儿，绝不会干那种事。那马两只耳朵之间在转什么念头，他一清二楚，也懂得怎么跟那脑子里搅和着的一切投契。

===Original===
Finally, Clint got to where by reaching out he could near of touched Smoky. Slow and easy the cowboy raised a hand and held it to within a few inches of the pony’s nose, Smoky looked at it and snorted, but pretty soon he stretched his neck and mighty careful took a sniff of the human paw. He snorted again and jerked his head away from it, but it wasn’t long when he took another sniff, then another and another, and each time the snort growed less to be heard, till at last, Smoky even allowed that paw to touch his nostrils, the fingers rubbed there easy for a spell and gradually went on a rubbing along his nose along on up to between his eyes and pretty soon between his ears to that knowledge bump. Five minutes afterwards Smoky was following the grinning cowboy all around the corral.

===Chinese===
末了，克林特挪到了一伸手就差不多能碰着烟儿的地方。牛仔慢而轻地抬起一只手，悬在离马鼻几英寸的地方。烟儿瞅了瞅，打了个响鼻，可没多会儿就伸出脖子，加着十二分小心，对那只人爪子闻了一闻。他又打个响鼻，猛地把头闪开；可没过多久又闻了一回，接着一闻再闻，响鼻一声比一声轻，到后来，烟儿竟容许那只爪子碰上他的鼻孔了。指头在那儿轻轻揉了一阵，又顺着他鼻子往上蹭，蹭到两眼中间，没多会儿又蹭到两耳中间那个学问包上。五分钟之后，烟儿已经跟着那个咧嘴笑的牛仔满畜栏转悠了。

---

===Original===
The roundup wagons, all cleaned and loaded, was ready to pull out, the remuda was all accounted for and each string pointed out to each rider, and Jeff giving the whole outfit another look over waved a hand, the pilot reined his horse into a bucking start, all took up his lead and thru the big gates of the home ranch, wagons, riders, remuda, and all lined out. The spring roundup had started.

===Chinese===
围拢车队全部洗刷停当、装载齐备，随时可以拔营；雷穆达一匹不落清点完毕，各人的坐骑班也一一指到了各人名下。杰夫把全班底又扫了一眼，挥了挥手；领路人催马尥着蹶子起了步，大家接上他领的头，伙食车、骑手、雷穆达，鱼贯出了本部牧场的大门。春围拢开张了。

===Original===
Smoky broke the record for learning that year, and when the fall roundup was over and the saddle was pulled off him for the last time before being turned out on the winter range, there was two little white spots of white hair showed on each side of his withers and about the size of a dollar﻿—saddle marks they was, and like medals for the good work he’d done. There was a knowing spark in his eyes for the critter too, for the little horse had got to savvy the cow near as well as the old cowhorses that’d been in the same remuda that year.

===Chinese===
那一年，烟儿把学东西的纪录都给破了。秋围拢收尾、放他去冬季牧场之前最后一回卸鞍时，他耆甲两侧各露出一小块白毛，差不多一块银元大小——那是鞍痕，也像给他出色活计颁的勋章。他眼里对牛也亮起了内行的火花，因为这匹小马摸牛的道行，已经快赶上那年同在雷穆达里的老牧牛马了。

===Original===
There was only one thing that could of been held against the good record of that pony, and that was his bucking;﻿—he just had to have his little buck out every morning, and sometimes he bucked harder than other times﻿—that all depended on how cold the weather was﻿—but Clint didn’t seem to mind that at all. If anything he tried to preserve that bucking streak in the pony, and he was often heard to remark:

===Chinese===
这马的良好纪录里，只有一样能挑出来数落，就是他爱尥蹶子——每天早上，他那几下定要尥出来才罢，有时尥得比别的时候凶——这全看天有多冷——可克林特好像全不介意。非但不介意，他还存心护着马身上这股「尥」性，常听他这么说：

===Original===
“A horse ain’t worth much unless that shows up some.”

===Chinese===
「一匹马要是没这点冒头的东西，值不了几个钱。」

===Original===
But Clint had other reasons for keeping the “buck” in Smoky’s backbone.﻿—Old Tom Jarvis, superintendent and part owner of the Rocking R, had joined the wagon for a few days that summer and wanted to see his cowboys work his cattle for a spell. Him being an old cowman, and from away back before cattle wore short horns, made all the working of a herd all the more interesting and to be criticized one way or the other. He was present steady on the cutting grounds, and so was Smoky one day.

===Chinese===
不过克林特护着烟儿脊梁里的这股「尥」性，另有缘故。——那年夏天，摇椅R的总管（superintendent）兼股东老汤姆·贾维斯（Old Tom Jarvis）到车队来了几天，想看看他的牛仔们怎么收拾他的牛。他是位老牧牛人，打老早以前、牛还没改短角那会儿就干这行，因此群里每一路活计在他眼里都格外有看头，也免不了要褒贬几句。他在分群场上天天坐镇，有一天，烟儿也在场。

===Original===
Clint felt that the eyes of Old Tom was on Smoky the minute he rode him to the edge of the herd, and an uneasy feeling crawled up his backbone as he noticed that that Old Grizzly seemed to’ve lost his eyesight for anything else but his Smoky horse. Clint knowed Old Tom’s failing for a good horse, and he’d heard of how many a time that same failing had come near putting the cowman in jail for appropriating some horse he couldn’t buy;﻿—of course them times was past, but the failing was still in the old man’s chest, and *Smoky belonged to him*.

===Chinese===
克林特骑着烟儿刚到牛群边上，就觉得老汤姆的眼睛落在了烟儿身上；再一看，那头「老灰熊」（Old Grizzly）除了他的烟儿马，简直对旁的一切都失了明，一股不安便顺着克林特自己的脊梁骨往上爬。他知道老汤姆见了好马迈不动腿的老毛病，也听说过这毛病有多少回险些害这牧牛人因为强占哪匹买不来的马而蹲班房——那样的年头当然过去了，可这毛病还窝在老头儿胸口，而烟儿*可是他克林特的*。

===Original===
The cowboy had started Smoky to cutting out, a work where all the good points of a cowhorse have a chance to show up, and Smoky sure wasn’t hiding any. Old Tom’s eyes was near popping out of his head as he watched the mouse colored gelding work, and finally, as Clint noticed all the interest, he figgered it a good idea to get out of the herd and hide Smoky somewheres before the old cowman came to him and suggested swapping horses. The cowboy was afraid he’d already showed too much of that horse, and as he come out of the herd he made a circle and took his stand away on the opposite side from where Old Tom was holding.

===Chinese===
牛仔让烟儿干起了往外剔牛的活计——一桩牧牛马浑身好本事都有机会亮相的活儿——烟儿也确实一点没藏着掖着。老汤姆看着那匹鼠灰色骟马干活，眼珠子都快瞪出眼眶。克林特瞧出这份兴头，末了寻思：趁这老牧牛人还没凑上来提议换马，还是先撤出牛群、把烟儿藏到哪儿去为妙。牛仔怕这马已经露得太多，于是出了牛群，兜了个圈，远远站到老汤姆把守那一头的对面去了。

===Original===
But Old Tom was controlling owner of that outfit and he could be any place he wanted to on that range any time. A steer broke out, Old Tom took after him, circled him around the herd, and when he put him back in and brought his horse to a standstill, there was only a short distance between him and the horse he’d had his eye on.

===Chinese===
可老汤姆是那班底的大股东，牧场上任何地方、任何时辰，他想在就能在。一头阉牛窜了出来，老汤姆追上去，绕着牛群把他兜回来；等他把牛塞回群里、勒马站定，他和他惦记的那匹马之间，就只剩一小截距离了。

===Original===
Clint was scared and he cussed a little. He tried to keep Smoky down whenever a critter broke out that needed turning, and even tried to let a couple of ’em get away, but he couldn’t do it without making it too plain to see, and besides, Smoky had ideas of his own about handling them critters.

===Chinese===
克林特慌了，小声骂了两句。每逢有牲口窜出来该去拦截，他都死命压着烟儿的火气，甚至存心想放走一两头算了，可这么干未免太露形迹，再说，对怎么收拾那些牲口，烟儿自己也有自己的主意。

===Original===
The cowboy was worried all the rest of the day and lost some sleep that night a wondering how he was going to dodge Old Tom. He knowed the old cowman would be around with some proposition to swap him out of Smoky, and that was one of the last things the cowboy would do. There wasn’t a horse in the outfit or anywheres else he’d trade Smoky for.

===Chinese===
牛仔这一整天都提心吊胆，夜里又少睡了些觉，翻来覆去琢磨怎么躲开老汤姆。他知道这老牧牛人早晚要拿着哪套换马的说辞找上门，而换马这事儿，是牛仔打死也不肯干的最后几桩事之一。班底里、天底下，没有一匹马是他肯拿烟儿换的。

===Original===
It’s took for granted on any real cow outfit that whenever a horse is swapped or borrowed out of a cowboy’s string and handed to somebody else, that that cowboy is requested to quit or be fired, in other words it’s an insult that makes any real cowboy want to scrap and then ask for his wages.

===Chinese===
在任何一个真正的牧牛班底里，有条不成文的规矩：凡是有人从牛仔的坐骑班里换走或借走一匹马、转手给了别人，那牛仔就算被客客气气请走了，或者干脆被开革了；说白了，这是桩侮辱，任哪个真牛仔受了，都先想干一架，再讨工钱走人。

===Original===
Clint was a valuable man to the outfit, but with Old Tom one cowboy more or less didn’t matter, that is if that cowboy stood between him and a horse he wanted. He walked up to Clint the next day and not hesitating any he says:

===Chinese===
克林特是班底的要紧人手，可对老汤姆来说，多一个牛仔少一个牛仔不打紧——倘若那牛仔挡在他和一匹他想要的马中间，那就是另一说了。第二天，他走到克林特跟前，半点不带犹豫地说：

===Original===
“I’m going to try that mouse colored horse you was riding yesterday”; and thinking it’d please Clint to hear, he went on, “and if I like him I’ll trade you my brown horse Chico for him; he’s the best horse I got at the home ranch.”

===Chinese===
「我要试试你昨天骑的那匹鼠灰马」；想着这话克林特听来必是受用，他又接着说，「我要是相中了，就拿我那匹棕马奇科（Chico）跟你换——那是我本部牧场里最好的一匹马。」

===Original===
But all that only made Clint get red in the face, and fire showed in his eyes as he spoke.

===Chinese===
可这一通话只说得克林特涨红了脸，开口时眼里蹿着火苗。

===Original===
“Huh! you can’t ride Smoky.”

===Chinese===
「哼！烟儿你骑不了。」

===Original===
“Why in samhill can’t I?” asks Old Tom, also getting red in the face.

===Chinese===
「活见鬼，我为啥就骑不了？」老汤姆问，脸也红了。

===Original===
“Cause you can’t,” answers Clint, “why you couldn’t even put a saddle on him.”

===Chinese===
「骑不了就是骑不了，」克林特答，「你连鞍子都给他上不去。」

===Original===
Clint was for quitting the outfit right there and hit for some other country, but the thought of leaving Smoky behind kinda put him to figgering another way out;﻿—if he could get Old Tom sort of peeved and let him handle Smoky while he was feeling that way, most likely that pony would do the rest.

===Chinese===
克林特当场就想撂挑子不干、奔别的地界去，可一想到要把烟儿撂下，又不得不另想出路——他要是能把老汤姆撩拨得火起，趁他那个劲儿上让他去摆弄烟儿，余下的事，多半那匹马自己就办妥了。

===Original===
“I’ll show you whether I can saddle that horse or not,” says Old Tom, frothing at the mouth; “why I’ve handled and rode broncs that you couldn’t get in the same corral with, and before you even was born.”

===Chinese===
「我能不能上鞍，这就做给你看，」老汤姆说，嘴角冒着白沫，「嘿，我驯过骑过的野马，有的你连跟它们同圈都不敢站——那会儿你还没出娘胎呢。」

===Original===
“Yep,” says Clint, grinning sarcastic, “that was too long ago, and you’re too daggoned old now for that kind of a horse.”

===Chinese===
「可不，」克林特带着讥诮一笑，「那都是老皇历喽，如今你老得太很喽，那种马骑不动喽。」

===Original===
Old Tom glared at Clint for a second, and not finding no ready come back done the next best thing and got busy. He went to his saddle, jerked his rope off it, and spitting fire, shook out a loop that could be heard a whistling plum to the other side of the corral.

===Chinese===
老汤姆瞪了克林特一秒，一时找不出还嘴的话，便退而求其次，忙活起来。他走到自己的鞍子跟前，一把扯下套索，喷着火似的甩开一个绳圈，那圈风带着哨音，隔着整个畜栏都听得真真的。

===Original===
Smoky was surprised into a dozen catfits as that same wicked loop settled over his head and drawed tight and sudden around his neck. He bellered and bucked thru the remuda a dragging Old Tom with him. The old cowman made a motion and two grinning cowboys went and helped him.

===Chinese===
那同一个狠巴巴的绳圈扣上烟儿的头、猛地在他脖子上勒紧时，烟儿惊得连着犯了十二阵「猫疯」（catfits）。他嘶吼着、尥着，从雷穆达里一路穿过去，身后的老汤姆叫他拖得踉踉跄跄。老牧牛人招了招手，两个笑嘻嘻的牛仔便过去搭了把手。

===Original===
Clint stood on the outside and watched the performance. He rolled cigarette after cigarette and tore ’em up fast as they was made, not a one was lit. He seen Smoky brought to a choking standstill and that cowboy felt like committing murder as he noticed the fear in that pony’s eyes as he faced the strangers; but there Clint noticed something else and which he gradually recognized as *fight*﻿—there was more fight than fear, and at the sight of that the cowboy took hope.

===Chinese===
克林特站在外头看这场戏。他卷了一支又一支纸烟，卷好一支撕一支，一支也没点。他看着烟儿被勒得喘不上气、被迫站定；看着那马面对生人时眼里的惧意，牛仔心里直想杀人。可也就在那儿，克林特看出了另一样东西，并且慢慢认出来那是*斗志*——斗志多过惧意，一瞧见这个，牛仔心里又有了指望。

===Original===
“Since when does a cowboy get help to rope and saddle his horse,” he hollered as Old Tom was sizing up Smoky. “Pretty soon you’ll be wanting one of us to top him off for you.”

===Chinese===
「打什么时候起，牛仔套马备鞍还得人搭手了？」老汤姆正打量烟儿，他那边嚷道，「回头你该要我们哪个替你把它骑服了吧。」

===Original===
It worked just right, and Old Tom’s answer was only a jerk on the rope that held Smoky. The old cowman knowed better than to handle a horse that way and as a rule was always easy with ’em, but he was mad, mad clear thru, and rather than shoot a cowboy he was taking it out on the horse.

===Chinese===
这激将法用得恰到好处，老汤姆的回应只是把拴着烟儿的绳子狠狠一拽。老牧牛人比谁都明白不该那么摆弄马，平日待马一向和气，可他这回是气了，从里到外气了个透——与其崩了一个牛仔，他就冲着马撒气。

===Original===
And Smoky by that time was fast catching up with the spirit of all that went on. He was like a raw bronc that’d never seen a human or a saddle, and when he was finally brought up alongside the saddle, there was all about him to show he wasn’t safe for anybody coming near. But Old Tom, even though it was a long time ago, had handled many mean horses;﻿—he knowed he was past handling ’em any more, but this time was different and he’d do his best to carry it thru.

===Chinese===
到这会儿，烟儿也把眼前这阵势的火气全接了过来。他活像一匹从没见过人、没见过鞍子的生野马，终于被拽到鞍子跟前时，浑身上下都写着：谁靠近谁危险。可老汤姆当年——虽说那是老早以前了——也收拾过不少歹马；他知道自己这把年纪早干不动那个了，可这一回不一样，他要使出浑身解数撑到底。

===Original===
The two riders that’d been helping him was waved away; he’d show Clint and the rest of the young fellers that he could still do it. He then spread a loop and caught both of Smoky’s threatening front feet; Smoky knowed better than to fight a rope and he stood still knowing he’d soon have another chance. Rawhide hobbles was fastened on his front legs, a bridle put on his head, and then the saddle was reached for and put on his back and cinched to stay.

===Chinese===
那两个搭手的骑手被他挥走了；他要让克林特和那帮小子瞧瞧，他老家伙还成。说罢他撒开一个绳圈，把烟儿那两只不住示威的前蹄一并套住；烟儿深知跟绳子较劲没好处，便站定不动，心里有数：一会儿还有的是机会。生皮绊子绑上了他的前腿，笼头套上了他的头，跟着鞍子被够过来搁上他的背，肚带勒得死死的，再不容挣。

===Original===
“Better say your prayers before you climb up,” says Clint, still prodding Old Tom, at the same time hoping that he would stop before he went too far. But there was no stopping him, he pulled up his chap’ belt, set his hat down tight, and still mad enough to bite a nail in two, loosened the hobbles, grabbed a short holt on the reins and climbed on.

===Chinese===
「上马之前，最好先祷告祷告，」克林特说，嘴上还在撩老汤姆，心里却盼着他趁早收手，别走得太远。可谁也拦不住他：他勒紧皮套裤的腰带，把帽子往下一按，气得能把一根钉子咬成两截，解了绊子，攥住短短一把缰绳，翻身上去了。

===Original===
Smoky looked back at the stranger that was a setting on him, and soon as a touch of the rein on his neck told him that all was set, things started a happening from there. He bowed his head, made two jumps, and was just getting started good when he felt the saddle was empty;﻿—he made a few more jumps just for good measure, and then stopped.

===Chinese===
烟儿回头看了一眼骑在身上的生人；缰绳往脖子上轻轻一触，告诉他一切就绪，打那儿起，好戏就接连开了张。他一低头，蹦了两下，刚要进入状态，就觉得鞍上空了——他又添头似的补了几下，然后收住了。

===Original===
Clint was grinning from ear to ear as he walked up to Smoky and put his hand on his neck.

===Chinese===
克林特咧着大嘴走到烟儿跟前，把手搭上他的脖子。

===Original===
“Good work, old boy,” he says﻿—and then turning to Old Tom, who was picking himself up: “Want to try again?”

===Chinese===
「干得漂亮，老伙计，」他说——跟着转向正从地上爬起来的老汤姆：「还要再试试吗？」

===Original===
“You bet your doggone life I do,” says that old cowboy.

===Chinese===
「拿你的小命打赌，我准要试，」那老牛仔说。

===Original===
“All right,” answers Clint, getting peeved some more. “Go ahead and break your fool neck, there’s plenty of buffalo wallows around here we can bury you in.”

===Chinese===
「行啊，」克林特答道，火气又添了几分，「去摔断你那蠢脖子吧，这一带野牛打滚坑（buffalo wallows）多的是，埋你随便挑。」

===Original===
Old Tom walked over and jerked the reins out of Clint’s hands and started to get in the saddle again, but he didn’t even get well in it that time﻿—Smoky bowed his head and went out from under him, leaving Old Tom come down on the other side.

===Chinese===
老汤姆走过去，从克林特手里一把抢过缰绳，又往鞍上爬，可这回他连鞍都没坐实——烟儿头一低，从他底下抽身溜了，把老汤姆撂在另一边着了地。

===Original===
It was as the old man was about to try Smoky once more when Jeff Nicks interfered, and told his boss how he’d rather not have him try that horse any more.

===Chinese===
正赶上老头儿还要再试一回烟儿的时候，杰夫·尼克斯拦了进来，跟自家老板说，他宁可他别再试那匹马了。

===Original===
“That horse bucks every time he’s rode,” says Jeff.

===Chinese===
「那马每回有人骑都要尥，」杰夫说。

===Original===
Old Tom knowed he’d come to the end of his string but that didn’t ease his feelings any, and he was looking for some way of letting some of them feelings out before they choked him. When he spots Clint a standing to one side and by Smoky.

===Chinese===
老汤姆知道自己这回是把招数使尽了，可这并不让他心里松快半分；他正找着个口子，好趁那口气把他噎死之前放出去点儿。恰在这时，他一眼瞅见克林特站在一边、站在烟儿旁边。

===Original===
“You’re fired,” he hollered, pointing a finger at him, “I’ll get somebody to take the buck out of that horse, and the sooner you’re off this range the better I’ll like it.”

===Chinese===
「你被开革了，」他伸手指着他嚷道，「我会另找人把这马尥蹶子的毛病治掉，你有多早滚出这片牧场，我就有多痛快。」

===Original===
Clint just grinned at Old Tom, which made him all the madder, and about then Jeff spoke:

===Chinese===
克林特只冲老汤姆咧嘴一笑，这更叫他火冒三丈；恰在这时，杰夫开了口：

===Original===
“I’m doing the hiring and firing on this outfit, Tom, and as long as I’m working for you I’ll keep on a doing it.”

===Chinese===
「汤姆，这个班底里雇人开人归我管，只要我还在你手底下干一天，就一天归我管。」

===Original===
Old Tom turned on him like a wild cat. “Fine!” he hollered, “you can go too.”

===Chinese===
老汤姆像头野猫似的猛冲他转过身。「好哇！」他嚷道，「那你也一块儿走。」

===Original===
The old cowman had went as far as he could, and as he walked away to catch himself another saddle horse, he had a hunch that he’d also went further than he should; that hunch got stronger as he went on saddling, and as he gave the latigo a last yank, it all developed into plain common sense that he’d sure enough went too far.

===Chinese===
老牧牛人把能撒的邪火都撒完了；走开去给自己另逮一匹鞍马时，他心里隐隐觉出，这回自己也走得过了界。备着备着鞍，这感觉越来越沉；等他最后一把拽紧拉蒂戈革条，那感觉干脆长成了明摆着的常识：他真是走得太远了。

===Original===
But Old Tom wasn’t for giving in, not right then anyway. He got on his horse and riding close enough so Jeff could hear, says:

===Chinese===
可老汤姆还没打算低头——至少那会儿没有。他上了马，骑到杰夫听得见的地方，说：

===Original===
“You and Clint can come to the ranch and I’ll have your time ready for you,” and then to another rider﻿—“you handle the outfit till I send out another foreman.”

===Chinese===
「你和克林特到牧场来一趟，我把你们的工钱结清」——又冲另一个骑手——「在我另派工头来之前，班底归你带。」

===Original===
A lot of orneriness was scattered to the winds as Old Tom covered the long fifty miles back to the ranch, and as he opened the big gate leading in, a brand new feeling had come over him﻿—he was for catching a fresh horse the next morning early and hightailing it back to the wagon to sort of smooth things over best as he could.

===Chinese===
老汤姆往回赶那长长的五十英里，一路把好些邪火撒给了风。等他推开进牧场的大门，心里已经换了个崭新的念头——明儿一早，他要牵一匹新马，一路紧赶回车队去，尽力把这一场圆一圆。

===Original===
He unsaddled and turned his horse loose, and was mighty surprised as he came up to the big ranch house to find both Jeff and Clint already there and waiting for him. Not a hint of the good resolutions he’d made showed as he walked up to ’em, and after some kind of a “howdy,” Old Tom heard Jeff say:

===Chinese===
他卸了鞍，把马放了；走到大牧场房跟前却大吃一惊：杰夫和克林特俩居然已经到了，正等着他。走上前去时，他路上打的那些好主意一点没挂在脸上，不咸不淡地道了句「好啊」，老汤姆就听见杰夫说：

===Original===
“All the boys sent word in by me, that as long as you’re making out my check you’d just as well make theirs out too. I’m sorry for that,” went on Jeff, “and I tried to talk ’em out of it, but it’s no use, they’re all for quitting if I go.”

===Chinese===
「弟兄们全托我捎了话进来：反正你要给我结账，索性把他们的也一并结了。这事对不住您，」杰夫接着说，「我劝过他们，可劝不动——我一走，他们全都要散伙。」

===Original===
The old cowman never said a word as he led Jeff and Clint in the big house. He walked to a big table in the center of the living room and there he turned on his two riders. A smile was on his face and he says:

===Chinese===
老牧牛人一声没吭，领着杰夫和克林特进了大房。他走到堂屋正中一张大桌子跟前，在那儿转过身来，面对他这两个骑手。脸上挂着笑，他说：

===Original===
“Daggone it, Jeff, I’m glad to hear that.” Then Old Tom, still pleasant, but serious, went on, “for no man does his best work unless he’s doing it with somebody he likes and has confidence in. Yes,” he repeated, “I’m glad to hear that, but the question is now, you’re fired and free to go, ain’t you?” he asks.

===Chinese===
「该死的，杰夫，这话我爱听。」跟着老汤姆仍是和颜悦色、却郑重地往下说：「因为人只有跟着自己喜欢、信得过的人，才干得出最出色的活儿。是啊，」他又说了一遍，「听你这么说我高兴。可如今问题来了——你这会儿是被开革了、来去自由的了，对不对？」他问。

===Original===
“Yes,” says Jeff, “soon as I get paid off.”

===Chinese===
「是啊，」杰夫说，「工钱一结清就是。」

===Original===
“Well, how’s chances of hiring you over again? I can’t afford to let a foreman like you go, Jeff.”

===Chinese===
「那么，把你重新雇回来，还有门儿吗？像你这样的工头，杰夫，我可舍不得放——也放不起。」

===Original===
Jeff seemed to figger a while and then looked at Clint, and Old Tom guessing what was on his foreman’s mind, went on “and of course, being that I have no say in the hiring and firing of your riders, Clint wasn’t fired at all, and he can keep on riding for you.”

===Chinese===
杰夫寻思了一阵，又看了看克林特；老汤姆猜到了他工头心里想什么，便接着说：「当然喽，骑手的雇与开，我本来就插不上嘴，所以说克林特压根儿没被开革，他可以接着给你当差。」

===Original===
Finally hands was shook all around, and as Jeff and Clint started back for the wagon the next morning Old Tom was on hand to see ’em go.

===Chinese===
末了，大家彼此握了握手；第二天早上杰夫和克林特动身回车队时，老汤姆还亲自到场送行。

===Original===
“And don’t worry about that doggone mouse colored horse of yours, Clint,” he says as him and Jeff rode away, “I’ll never want him.”

===Chinese===
「还有，别为你那匹该死的鼠灰马操心，克林特，」两人骑马走时他说，「我永远不会再惦记他了。」

===Original===
The riders reached the big gate leading out the ranch, and there Jeff remarked as he got off his horse to open it:

===Chinese===
两个骑手到了出牧场的那扇大门，杰夫下马开门时说：

===Original===
“I guess Old Tom didn’t have to say that he was sorry.”

===Chinese===
「我看哪，老汤姆那句『对不住』，是用不着说出口的了。」

===Original===
And Clint more than agreed.

===Chinese===
克林特岂止是同意而已。

## X / 十

===Original===
“Amongst the Missing”

===Chinese===
失踪者之列

===Original===
The remuda was in the big corrals of the home ranch once more, and after a few “winter” horses was cut out, the rest was hazed towards the winter range, and let go.﻿—Four long winter months went by.﻿—Then one day the roundup cook begin to get busy cleaning the chuck box, meadow larks was a tuning up on the high corral posts, and along with the bare patches of ground that could be seen, no better signs was needed that spring had come.

===Chinese===
雷穆达又圈进了本部牧场的大畜栏，几匹「过冬马」挑出去以后，其余的便被轰向冬季牧场，撒开了。——长长的四个月冬天过去了。——然后有一天，围拢厨子张罗起清理伙食箱来，草地百灵在高高的畜栏桩上调试嗓子，再加上地里望得见的一块块化雪秃斑——春天来了，再不需要比这更好的迹象。

===Original===
Clint was again the first to spot Smoky that spring and notice the amount of tallow that pony was packing. He was in fine shape for whatever work that’d be his to do that summer, and soon as him and the cowboy got thru with their first “howdys” they both went to work like they never had before.

===Chinese===
那年春天，又是克林特头一个望见烟儿、头一个留意到那匹小马身上驮着多少膘。那年夏天无论派他干什么活计，他这副身板都是好样的；他和牛仔刚寒暄过头一阵「你好哇」，两个便拿出了从来没有过的劲头干起活来。

===Original===
Smoky took up to where he’d left off the fall before and kept on accumulating science in ways of handling the critter till that critter would just roll up an eye at the sight of the mouse colored pony and never argue as to where he wanted to put ’er;﻿—she’d just go there.

===Chinese===
烟儿从上年秋天撂下的地方接着来，在摆弄牲口的门道上一路攒学问，攒到后来，牲口一瞅见那匹鼠灰色小马就只剩翻白眼的份儿；他要把「她」撵到哪儿去，再不争一句嘴；——她自会乖乖地到那儿去。

===Original===
Spring work went on, middle summer came, and sometime after, the fall roundup was in full swing again. Thousands of cattle was handled, cut out, and culled. Big herds of fat steers was trailed in to the shipping point and loaded in the cars, and when the weaning was done and the old stock was all brought in close to the cow camps, Jeff headed his wagon towards the home ranch once more. The work was over, the remuda was turned out and the riders that was kept on the payroll saddled their winter horses and scattered out for the outfit’s different camps.

===Chinese===
春季活计一路往前走，仲夏来了，又过了一些时候，秋围拢重新轰轰烈烈地全面铺开。成千上万头牛过了手、剔了群、汰了劣。大群大群的肥阉牛被赶到装运点装上车皮；断奶的活计收了尾，老弱牛只全都拢到牧牛营子近旁，杰夫便又把车队赶向本部牧场。活计完了，雷穆达放了出去，留在工籍上的骑手们各自备上过冬马，散往公司各处的营子。

===Original===
Winter came on and set in, then spring bloomed out green once again, and with it the cowboys spread out on the range once more. Season after season followed one another without a ruffle that way, the same territory was covered at the same time of the year, the wagon rolled in at the same grounds, and the rope corral stretched at the same spot, old riders disappeared and new ones took their place, like with the ponies; the old cowhorses was pensioned, replaced by younger ones and the work went on, season after season, year after year, the same outfit rambled out of the home ranch and combed the range like as if no changes was taking place.

===Chinese===
冬天来了又坐实，跟着春天又绽出满眼青绿，牛仔们随之重新撒向牧场。一季又一季，就这么纹丝不乱地轮转下去：一年里同样的时节走同样的地界，车队滚进原来的场子，绳畜栏在老地方张起来；老骑手不见了，新的顶上来，跟那些小马一个样；老牧牛马退了役，由年轻的接班，活计照旧往前走。一季又一季，一年又一年，还是那一班人马晃出本部牧场，把牧场梳得沟沟坎坎都不放过，仿佛什么变化都不曾发生。

===Original===
Jeff, the cow boss, the roundup cook, Clint, and a couple more riders was all that was left of the old hands as the wagon pulled out one spring;﻿—the others’d had cravings for new countries and went and throwed their soogans on some other outfit’s wagons.

===Chinese===
有一年春天车队拔营时，杰夫、牧牛头儿、围拢厨子、克林特，再加两三个骑手，便是老伙计里剩下的全部了；——旁的都眼馋起别处的新地界来，把铺盖卷（soogan）一扔，扔到了别家车队的车上。

===Original===
Five years had went by since that day when Clint, riding Smoky, had joined the wagon. Five summers was put in when every time Smoky was saddled and rode Clint was the cowboy that done it. Not another hand had touched Smoky’s hide in that time, excepting when Old Tom had *tried* to appropriate the horse for his own string, and since that day there hadn’t been any excuse for Clint to worry about anybody taking Smoky away from him. There wasn’t a cowboy in the outfit who didn’t more than want the horse, and if Clint ever failed to show up when the spring works started there’d most likely been some argument as to who should get him; but he’d always been the first to ride in at the home ranch at them times and none had the chance to lay claim on the horse.

===Chinese===
打克林特骑着烟儿入伙车队那天起，五年过去了。五个夏天干下来，烟儿每回上鞍受骑，骑他的都是克林特。这五年里，再没有第二只手碰过烟儿的皮毛——只除了老汤姆*企图*把马划拉进自己坐骑班的那一回；打那天起，克林特就再没有理由担心有谁会把烟儿从他手里弄走。班里没有一个牛仔不想这马想得不得了；倘若春季活计开张时克林特有一回没露面，多半少不了一场这马归谁的争执；可每赶到这种时候，他回回都是头一个骑进本部牧场的人，谁也没机会对这马下手认领。

===Original===
In them long summers, and as Smoky was rode off and on, the little horse had got to know Clint as well as that cowboy knowed hisself; he knowed just when Clint was a little under the weather and not feeling good﻿—at them times he’d go kinda easy with his bucking as the cowboy topped him off. The feel of Clint’s hand was plain reading to him, and he could tell by a light touch of it whether it meant “go get ’er,” “easy now,” “good work,” and so on. The tone of his voice was also mighty easy to understand. He could tell a lot of things by it, specially when he was being got after for doing something he shouldn’t of done. His eyes was wide open at them times, his neck bowed, and he’d snort sorta low, but when Clint would tell him what a fine horse he was, Smoky was some different﻿—he’d just take it all in the same as he would warm sunshine in a cold fall day, and near close his eyes for the peace he was feeling at the sound of the cowboy’s voice.

===Chinese===
在那些长长的夏天里，烟儿就这么断断续续地被骑着，把这小马对克林特的了解，养到了跟那牛仔了解自己一样深；克林特哪会儿有点儿不舒坦、身上没劲儿，他一清二楚——那种时候，牛仔骑服他，他尥起蹶子来也会透着几分客气。克林特的手搭上来是什么意思，在他读来一目了然，轻轻一触，他便分得出那是「上」，是「稳着点儿」，是「干得好」，等等。人的嗓音也是一等一的好懂。从那里面他能听出许多名堂，尤其是他干了什么不该干的事、正挨数落的时候。那种时候他两眼睁得溜圆，脖子弓着，鼻子里低低喷一声；可克林特要是夸他是匹好马，烟儿就另是一副样子——他照单全收，就像秋寒天里收下一身暖洋洋的日头，眼睛几乎阖上，为着牛仔嗓音里那一片安然。

===Original===
The way Smoky could understand the man who rode him thru and around the big herds had a lot to do in making him the cowhorse he’d turned out to be. His strong liking for the rider had made him take interest and for learning all about whatever he was rode out to do. There’d come a time when Smoky knowed the second Clint had a critter spotted to be cut out, and the pony’s instinct near told him which one it was, till nary a feel of the rein was needed and the dodging critter was stepped on and headed for the “cut.”

===Chinese===
烟儿能这么懂得骑着他穿行大牛群、绕着大牛群转悠的那个人，跟他后来长成这样一匹牧牛马，大有干系。他对骑手的这份稀罕，叫他上了心，凡是骑他出去干的活计，他都要学个通透。到后来，克林特哪一秒钟相中了要剔出群的牲口，烟儿当场就知道，小马的本能几乎直接告诉他那是哪一只，缰绳一下都不用带，那东躲西闪的牲口就被他截在前头，掉头赶向「剔群」里去。

===Original===
The same with roping and where Smoky could do near everything but throw the rope that caught the critter. There he shined as he did anywhere else under the saddle. He’d keep one ear back, watch out and follow the loop leave Clint’s hand and sail out to settle around a steer’s horns, and the slack was no more than pulled when that pony would turn and go the other way﻿—he knowed how to “lay” the critter, and none of the big ones ever got up, not while Smoky was at one end of the rope.

===Chinese===
套索上也一样——除了甩出那把牲口套住的绳，烟儿几乎样样都拿得起来。在这上头他照样出彩，跟在鞍子底下别的任何地方一样。他一只耳朵朝后别着，瞧着、跟着那绳圈离开克林特的手，飞出去，落定在一头阉牛的犄角上；松绳刚一带上劲，小马便转身朝相反的方向走——他懂得怎么把牲口「放倒」，而且只要烟儿在绳子的一头，再大的牲口也没一头起来过。

===Original===
Of the many happenings that all went to show of Smoky’s knowing how in handling the critter, there’s one Clint and the boys liked to tell of. It was only an average of the others that happened, but there was something about that one which made the telling easier as to the wonders of that horse. It was the detail that counted there.

===Chinese===
在那一桩桩一件件都印证着烟儿摆弄牲口真本事的事情里，有一件是克林特和伙计们最爱讲的。事情本身不过是平常水平，跟旁的发生过的差不多，可它自有一股什么，叫人讲起这马的种种神奇来格外顺口。顶要紧的，恰恰是那份细节。

===Original===
There was a big steer in the herd with a crooked horn which had curved and threatened to grow some more and right thru his eye. Clint and Jeff spotted the steer at the same time, and while one of the boys went to the wagon to get a saw to cut the horn off with, both Clint and Jeff took their ropes and proceeded to catch the critter.

===Chinese===
牛群里有一头大阉牛，长着一只歪犄角，弯了回来还憋着劲儿要接着长，直冲着他的眼珠子去。克林特和杰夫同时相中了这头牛；一个小伙子去车上取锯犄角的锯，克林特和杰夫两个便各自抄起绳索，动手去逮这牲口。

===Original===
The steer was wild, big and husky, and wise, and soon as he seen the two riders coming thru the herd headed his way, he broke out of it and tail up in the air begin to leave the flat. About then is when Smoky appeared on the scene.

===Chinese===
那阉牛又野又大又魁实，还贼精；一见两个骑手穿过牛群直奔他来，他登时冲出群，尾巴翘上天，撒腿就要离开那片平地。也就约莫在这当口，烟儿出场了。

===Original===
That little horse et up the distance between him and that steer in no time and soon carried Clint to within reach. On account of the crooked horn Clint had to rope the steer around the neck, and that he did neat and quick. Everything went on as it should﻿—Smoky run on past the steer and Clint throwed the slack of his rope over that same steer’s rump and in another second that critter would of been laying with toes up to the sky and ready to tie.

===Chinese===
那小马三口两口把同阉牛之间的距离吃了个精光，转眼便把克林特驮到了够得着的地方。碍着那只歪犄角，克林特只能套牛脖子，这一套他套得又干净又麻利。接下去一切照章办事——烟儿从阉牛身边冲过去，克林特把松绳往那头阉牛的屁股上一搭，再有一秒钟，这牲口就该四脚朝天躺倒在地、听凭捆蹄了。

===Original===
The unexpected happened about that time, and when the rope tightened the steer didn’t lay at all. Instead there was a sound of something ripping. Clint went up in the air about three feet, turned a somerset and hit the ground, the saddle stood up on end on Smoky’s back and only the flank cinch was holding it there. The stub latigo of the front cinch had been ripped right thru by the tongue of the cinch buckle like it’d been paper.

===Chinese===
偏赶这时候，意外来了：绳子一绷紧，阉牛压根儿没躺。倒是响起一声什么被撕豁了的动静。克林特蹿起三英尺高，翻了个跟头砸在地上；鞍子在烟儿背上竖了起来，全靠后肚带吊在那儿。前肚带上那截短拉蒂戈革条，叫肚带扣的扣舌整个豁穿，跟豁一张纸似的。

===Original===
Every rider around the herd seen the thing happen, and had already figgered how it wouldn’t take long for Smoky to get himself out from under the remains of that saddle. For near every horse would go to bucking and raising the dust when being pinched around the flanks that way, and Smoky had seemed so inclined to want to buck that it was thought he’d never overlook that chance.

===Chinese===
牛群四近的骑手个个瞧见了这一幕，心里都已经盘算好：用不了多大工夫，烟儿就能从那鞍子的残骸底下把自己择出来。因为差不多随便哪匹马，两肋叫这么夹住，都得尥起蹶子、扬起尘土；而烟儿那副爱尥蹶子的性子又是人人皆知，谁都当他绝不会放过这个机会。

===Original===
The boys was already grinning at such a good promise of seeing a little excitement, but the grins soon faded to looks of wonder, for Smoky, instead of trying to get shed of the saddle, showed he was using his brain to the best way of *keeping it there*. He was a cowhorse and working, and it was no time for foolishness, so, when the rigging reared upon his hind quarters that way he reared up with it, and turned while in the air. When his front feet touched the ground again the saddle was where it belonged and he was facing the steer.

===Chinese===
伙计们已经咧开了嘴，等着瞧这么现成的一场热闹；可笑容很快换成了满脸惊奇——烟儿非但不设法甩脱鞍子，反倒摆明了在动脑筋，琢磨怎么把它*留在背上*最稳妥。他是一匹牧牛马，正在干活的当口，不是撒野的时候；于是，鞍具那么朝他后躯竖起来的时候，他也跟着扬起了前半身，并在半空里转了个向。前脚重新落地时，鞍子各归各位，他也正对着那头阉牛了。

===Original===
When that story was told to the country around there was many hard-to-be-convinced riders, who laughed and shook their heads and remarked how it was pure luck that the pony acted that way, but if they’d knowed Smoky, if they’d seen how he juggled that saddle and worked to keep his holt on the steer, there’d been a different tune.

===Chinese===
这故事传到四邻八乡，多的是不肯轻易信服的骑手，笑着摇头，说那小马当时那副做派纯属走运；可他们要是认识烟儿，要是见过他怎么把那只鞍子耍弄得服服帖帖、怎么死死咬住那头阉牛不撒劲，唱的可就是另一支曲子了。

===Original===
The steer had stayed up and with his ten hundred pounds of wild weight had fought at the rope and hit the end mighty hard. Then Smoky done another thing which kept the boys a staring and doing nothing. The steer was making another wild dash for open country, and Smoky, instead of holding his ground and waiting for the steer to hit the end of the rope broke out in a sudden run and right after the critter. When the speed of both of ’em was up good and high, Smoky of a sudden planted himself till his hocks touched the ground, and when Mr. Steer hit the end of the rope that time it was just as though that rope had been fastened to a four foot stump. His head was jerked under him, he turned in the air, and when he came down *he layed*.

===Chinese===
那阉牛一直没倒，凭着一千磅的野分量拽着绳子死顶，一次次狠狠撞上绳头。跟着烟儿又干了一件事，看得伙计们只会干瞪眼。阉牛又一次朝旷野狂冲，烟儿却不钉在原地等他撞上绳头，而是猛地撒开蹄子，直追着那牲口去了。两边速度都飙得又足又高的时候，烟儿突然钉子般站定，坐得飞节都挨了地；这回阉牛先生撞上绳头，那绳子就像拴在了一根四英尺粗的树桩上。他的脑袋被狠狠别到身子底下，在半空里转了个个儿，落下来的时候，*他躺下了*。

===Original===
“There was only one thing that horse didn’t do,” Jeff had remarked afterwards﻿—“he didn’t give the rope a flip before he set down on it.”

===Chinese===
「那马就差一样儿没干，」杰夫事后这么评说——「坐上绳子之前，没先把绳子那么一挑。」

===Original===
Smoky had kept the rope tight and Clint tied the steer down to stay till the crooked horn was sawed off. When that was done Clint put up a hand and spoke, and Smoky gave slack so the rope could be pulled off the steer’s head.

===Chinese===
烟儿把绳子一直绷得紧紧的，克林特把阉牛捆牢按住，直到那只歪犄角锯了下来。完事之后，克林特抬抬手说了一句，烟儿便送出松绳，好让绳子从牛头上褪下来。

===Original===
Big herds of Mexico long-horned steers had been bought by the Rocking R and shipped into that northern country. They got fat on that range and wilder than ever, and there’s where Smoky showed he had something else besides the knowing how. Them long-horned critters are too fast for the average cowhorse to catch up with in a short distance, but not with Smoky;﻿—he had the speed to go with what he knowed, and Clint would have time to whirl his rope only a few times when the little horse would climb up on the long legged steer and pack the cowboy within roping distance.

===Chinese===
摇椅R买进过大群大群的墨西哥长角阉牛，运到这片北方地界。他们在那片牧场上上了膘，也野得胜过以往；就在这上头，烟儿显出他那身门道之外，还另有一样本钱。那种长角牲口腿脚太快，寻常牧牛马短距离里追不上——烟儿不在此列；——他的脚力配得上他的学问：绳子在克林特手里才抡得几圈，小马已经逼到那长腿阉牛身上，把牛仔驮进了甩绳够得着的距离。

===Original===
Many a cowboy had remarked that it was worth the price of a good show to watch Smoky work, whether it was around, in or out of a herd; and many a rider had let a cow sneak past him just so he could see how neat that pony could outdodge a critter. And when after the last meal of the day and the cowboys stretched out to rest some, talk, or sing, none ever had any argument to put up and no betting was ever done against whatever Clint said Smoky could do or had done. They all knowed and admired the horse, and came a time as these cowboys came and went that Smoky begin to be talked about in the cow camps of other cow outfits. One whole northern State got to hear of him, and one cowboy wasn’t at all surprised when hitting South one fall and close to the Mexican border to hear another cowboy talk of “Smoky of the Rocking R.”

===Chinese===
多少牛仔说过，看烟儿干活，值一张好戏票的价钱——不管是在牛群四近、群里还是群外；多少骑手甘愿放一头牛从身边溜过去，就为瞧瞧那小马跟牲口斗躲闪能躲得多么利索。一天最后一顿饭下肚，牛仔们躺倒歇乏、说话或者唱歌的当口，凡是克林特说烟儿能干、干过的事，从来没人抬杠，也从来没人押注跟他对着赌。他们都识得这马、服气这马；这些牛仔来了又去，到后来，烟儿开始在别家牧牛公司的牧牛营子里被人念叨起来。北方整整一个州都听说了他；有一年秋天，一个牛仔南下到了贴近墨西哥边界的地方，听见另一个牛仔说起「摇椅R的烟儿」，他一点儿也不觉得稀奇。

===Original===
The owner of a neighbor outfit sent word by one of the “reps”1 one day that he’d give a hundred dollars for that horse; Smoky had been broke only two years then. Old Tom laughed at the offer, and Clint got peeved. The next year that offer was raised by the same party to two hundred, and Old Tom laughed again, but Clint didn’t know whether to get mad or scared this time. Anyway, things went on as usual for a couple of years more, and then a big outfit from acrost the state line sent in an offer of a cool four hundred dollars for the mouse colored cowhorse.

===Chinese===
有一天，邻家牧场的老板托一位「代表」1捎话来，说愿出一百块买那匹马；那时烟儿受驯才满两年。老汤姆对这出价一笑置之，克林特却着了恼。第二年，还是这位买家，把价抬到了两百，老汤姆又一笑置之，克林特这回却不知道该动怒还是该心慌了。总之，日子照旧往前过了两年，接着，州界那头的一家大牧场递来出价：整整四百块，买这匹鼠灰色牧牛马。

===Original===
Good saddle horses could be bought by the carload for fifty dollars a head about that time, but there never was no set price on a good cowhorse, and as a rule that kind can’t be bought unless an outfit is selling out. The biggest price that was ever heard offered in that country for any cowhorse had never went over two hundred, and when rumors spread around that four hundred had been offered for Smoky many figgered that whoever offered it had a lot of money to spend;﻿—but them who figgered that way had never seen Smoky work.

===Chinese===
那年月，好鞍马论车皮买，五十块一匹；可好牧牛马从来没有个定死的价，照规矩，不等到哪家牧场清场变卖，这类马是有钱也买不着的。那片乡里听说过的牧牛马最高出价，从没超过两百块；四百块买烟儿的传闻一散开，许多人都寻思，出这价的人准是钱多得没处花；——不过这么寻思的人，都没见过烟儿干活。

===Original===
Old Tom came up to Clint that fall after the wagon had pulled in, and showed him the letter offering the four hundred. Clint had heard about the offer and he just stargazed at the letter, not reading;﻿—instead he was doing some tall wondering at what Old Tom was going to do about it. He was still stargazing and sort of waiting for the blow to fall, when he felt the old cowman’s hand on his shoulder, and then heard him say:

===Chinese===
那年秋天车队收庄之后，老汤姆走到克林特跟前，把那封出四百块的信拿给他看。出价的事克林特已有耳闻，他只是对着那封信望得出神，并没有读；——他正一门心思往深里琢磨，老汤姆打算拿这事怎么办。他还在那儿望天出神，多少等着那只靴子落地，忽觉老牧牛人一只手搭上肩头，接着听见他开了口：

===Original===
“Well, Clint, I’ll tell you”﻿—then Old Tom waited a while, maybe just to sort of aggravate the cowboy, but finally he went on﻿—“if my cattle was starving, and I needed the money to buy feed to pull ’em thru with, I might *sacrifice* Smoky for four hundred, but as things are now there’s no money can buy that horse.”

===Chinese===
「咳，克林特，跟你说吧」——老汤姆顿了半晌，兴许就为着逗一逗这牛仔，末了才接着往下说——「要是我的牛正饿着，我等着这笔钱买草料拉他们熬过冬，兴许我还会为这四百块把烟儿*忍痛舍*了；可照眼下这光景，天底下没有哪笔钱买得来那匹马。」

===Original===
The cowboy smiled, took a long breath, and grabbed the paw the old man was holding for him to shake.

===Chinese===
牛仔笑了，深深吸了一口气，一把攥住老人伸过来等他握的那只巴掌。

===Original===
“But I’m hoping,” resumed Old Tom, “that some day soon you’ll get to hankering to drift to some other country and quit this outfit, so I can get Smoky for myself; I’d fired you long ago, only I’d have to fire Jeff too, and somehow I’d rather get along without the horse till one of you highbinders quit.”

===Chinese===
「不过我盼着，」老汤姆又接着说，「盼着不用多久你就起了心思，要漂到别的地界去、不在这班里干了，好叫我把烟儿归到自己名下；要按我的心意，早把你辞了——只是辞你就得连杰夫一块儿辞，没法子，我宁可先没着这马，等你们这两个刺头里头的哪一个先撂挑子。」

===Original===
Clint had kept a smiling all the while the old man was speaking, then he gave his hand another shake and walked away. He knowed Old Tom had said that last just to hear how his voice sounded.

===Chinese===
老人说这话的当口，克林特自始至终笑着，随后又握了一回那只手，走开了。他知道，老汤姆末了那几句，说来说去不过是想听听自己的嗓音是个什么动静。

---

			

===Original===
As usual, Smoky was turned out on the range along with the remuda for that winter. Clint had helped haze ’em to the breaks as he’d always done, and noticed as he stopped and let the ponies graze and scatter that the feed was mighty short and scarcer than he’d ever seen it. The whole summer had been mighty dry and the range short on grass, but this little scope of country that was the saddle horse range had always been good, and the ponies had always wintered there better than if they’d been in a warm stable and fed grain.

===Chinese===
照老规矩，那年冬天烟儿跟着雷穆达一道放上了牧场。克林特照往年一样帮着把他们轰到沟壑地；停下放马吃草散开的时候，他留意到草料短得厉害，比他见过的哪一年都稀。整个夏天旱得够呛，牧场上草本就薄，可这一小片做鞍马牧场的地方年景向来不坏，小马们在这儿过冬，向来比圈在暖和马厩里喂粮食还强。

===Original===
Clint thought some of taking Smoky back with him and keeping him up for a winter horse, but then he’d have to turn him out when spring works came on, and the cowboy didn’t want to think of going out on spring roundup without his “top horse.”

===Chinese===
克林特琢磨过把烟儿带回庄去，留作过冬马使唤；可春季活计一到，又得把他撒出去；没了他那匹「头号好马」（top horse）就出春围拢的日子，牛仔连想都不愿去想。

===Original===
“No,” he decided, “I’m going to let you run out this winter, but I’ll be out to see how you’re making it and don’t lose too much tallow. You’re getting to be too valuable a horse to take any chances of losing,” he says to him as he scratched him back of the ear﻿—“but,” he went on, “you’re not half as valuable to the outfit as you are to me, old pony, even though Old Tom won’t consider no price on you.”

===Chinese===
「不成，」他拿定了主意，「今年冬天还是放你在外头跑；可我会出来瞧你怎么熬的，别掉了太多膘。你这马如今金贵得经不起半点闪失了。」他一边给他挠着耳根子，一边跟他说——「不过，」他又接着说，「老伙计，你对牧场的用处，抵不上对我的一半金贵——哪怕老汤姆压根儿不肯在你身上论价。」

===Original===
Clint was on his way back and had no more than got sight of the buildings of the ranch when Old White Winter hit him from behind and made him clap his gloved hands over his ears.

===Chinese===
克林特往回走，才刚望见牧场的房舍，「老白冬」（Old White Winter）就从背后扑了上来，冻得他赶紧把戴手套的双手捂上耳朵。

===Original===
“Holy smoke,” he whistled thru his chattering teeth, “she’s sure starting ferocious.”

===Chinese===
「乖乖，」他从打战的牙缝里吹出一声口哨，「这一上来可真够凶的。」

===Original===
And she had﻿—the first initiating blizzard of the season was more than just a snowstorm with a wind, it was a full grown blizzard drifting over the country, covering up the feed with packed snow, and freezing things up. It kept up for two days and nights, and as it cleared up, the thermometer went down. The next day Clint was busy bringing in old stock closer to the ranch and where they could be watched, and as another blizzard hit the country again a few days later that cowboy was *kept* on the jump bringing under the sheds and next to the haystacks all the stock he’d hunted up.

===Chinese===
她也当真凶——这个季节头一场来开场的暴风雪，不只是带着风的一场降雪，而是一场长足了个头的暴风雪，整个儿漂过乡野，用压瓷实的雪埋住草料，把万物冻了个透。它足足闹了两天两夜；天一放晴，水银柱又往下掉。第二天，克林特忙着把老弱牛只往牧场近旁收、收到照看得住的地方；没几天又一场暴风雪扑过来，那牛仔就*没个消停*，把寻摸到的牲口一股脑儿往棚舍底下、草垛旁边赶。

===Original===
Clint was in the saddle all day every day, and sometimes away into the night. A month went by and in that time two feet of snow had accumulated on the range;﻿—more was threatening to come, and all the cowboys that was kept on the Rocking R payroll more than had their hands full. The ranch hands would roll up their eyes at every bunch of stock the riders brought in to be fed, for as they figgered they already had all they could handle, and if this kept up, Old Tom would have to hire more hay shovelers and buy more hay.

===Chinese===
克林特天天整天整天地泡在鞍上，有时一忙忙到深夜。一个月过去，牧场上积起了两英尺厚的雪；——更大的雪还压在头顶上；摇椅R工籍上留下的那些牛仔，手头的活儿早满得往外溢。骑手们每赶回一群等着喂的牲口，庄上的伙计们就把眼珠子往上一翻——照他们盘算，手头这点儿早就撑到头了；再这么下去，老汤姆就得多雇铲草的伙计、多买草料了。

===Original===
Clint had worried some about Smoky and figgered to hunt him up sometime, but as on account of the deep snow he couldn’t get his horse out of a walk, he never could make it. Besides there was always a bunch of cattle somewheres on the way, and amongst ’em there’d be a few that needed bringing in.

===Chinese===
克林特对烟儿多少悬着心，打算抽个空当去找他；可雪太深，马连快步都迈不开，他怎么也赶不到那儿。再说，路上总有一群一群的牛，里头总有那么几头等着收回去。

===Original===
But with all them drawbacks, Clint finally reached Smoky’s range late one day. The gray sky was getting darker, and night was coming on as the cowboy topped a ridge and spotted a bunch of ponies. Amongst the bunch was a long-haired, shaggy-looking, and lean mouse colored horse, and Clint could hardly believe his eyes or keep from choking as he rode closer and recognized his Smoky horse.

===Chinese===
可顶着这一切难处，一天傍晚，克林特终于到了烟儿那片牧场。天灰得越来越沉，夜正压上来，牛仔登上一道山梁，望见一小群马。群里有一匹长毛邋遢、精瘦精瘦的鼠灰色马；骑近了认出那正是他的烟儿时，克林特简直不敢相信自己的眼睛，嗓子眼儿也一阵发堵。

===Original===
The cowboy was for catching the horse right there and bring him in to the ranch. He wondered if Smoky could travel that far, but as the horse raised his head out of the hole in the snow where he’d been pawing for feed, and spotted the rider coming towards him, Clint was surprised to see so much strength and action. Smoky hadn’t recognized the cowboy, and before he’d took a second look, he’d hightailed it from there in a hurry.

===Chinese===
牛仔本想当场就把马逮住，带回牧场去。他还拿不准烟儿撑不撑得到那么远的路；可那马从自己刨草的雪坑里一抬头，望见骑马人朝自己过来，克林特瞧着那股子精气神，倒吃了一惊。烟儿没认出这牛仔来，第二眼还没来得及瞧，早就撒开蹄子一溜烟跑了。

===Original===
Clint watched him and smiled as he seen that the horse wasn’t in near as bad a shape as he’d first thought.

===Chinese===
克林特望着他，笑了——瞧得出来，这马的境况远不像他起初想的那么糟。

===Original===
“But I’m going to take you in just the same, you little son of a gun, for God knows what you’ll be like in a few weeks from now if this weather keeps up.”

===Chinese===
「可我还是要把你带回去，你个小兔崽子——天知道这天气再这么闹下去，几个礼拜以后你会成个什么模样。」

===Original===
He started on the trail Smoky and the other ponies had left, it was good and dark by then, but the trail in the deep snow was easy enough to follow. He wondered as he rode if he could get Smoky to stand long enough so as the horse would recognize him under all the disguise of his winter clothes, for at night specially he looked more like a bear than anything; then again, horses are spookier and harder to get near at that time. Clint had his doubts if he could catch him, and he figgered he’d most likely have to take the whole bunch along in order to get him to the ranch.

===Chinese===
他顺着烟儿和那群马留下的蹄迹走。这时天已经黑透，好在深雪里的蹄印跟起来不难。他一边走一边犯嘀咕：能不能叫烟儿站得够久，久到从这一身冬装的伪装底下认出他来——夜里他这副模样，活脱脱更像头熊；再说了，马在夜里本就更容易受惊，也更难凑近。克林特没多大把握逮得住他，寻思多半得把整群马一道圈着走，才能把他弄回牧场。

===Original===
He was riding along on the trail and trying to get sight of the ponies, when to his left just a little ways, and out of the snow, came a faint beller. It sounded like a critter in trouble, and Clint stopped his horse. The beller came again, and he rode towards the sound. All curled up, shivering, and near covered with snow, a little bitty calf was found﻿—couldn’t been over two days old, figgered the cowboy, and he wondered how the poor little cuss could still be alive.

===Chinese===
他正顺着蹄迹往前走，眼睛找着那群马，忽听得左边不远的地方，雪窝里传来一声细细的哞吼。听着像一头遭了难的牲口，克林特勒住了马。哞吼又响了一声，他朝声音那边骑过去。只见一头小小的牛犊蜷成一团，打着战，几乎叫雪埋严了——牛仔估摸着，落地顶多两天；他都纳闷，这可怜的小家伙怎么还能有口气。

===Original===
“Where’s your mammy, Johnny?” says Clint as he got off his horse and came near the calf.

===Chinese===
「你妈妈哪儿去了，小家伙？」克林特下了马，凑近那牛犊，问道。

===Original===
But the words was no more out of his mouth when a dark shadow appeared, and bellering, tried to get to the cowboy with her horns before he could get on his horse. In making his getaway, Clint noticed tracks of more cattle, and following ’em a ways, come acrost another cow and with another calf, only this second calf was older and more able to navigate.

===Chinese===
话音未落，一道黑影蹿了出来，一边哞吼一边抡着犄角，赶在他上马前往他身上招呼。脱身的当口，克林特注意到还有旁的牛的蹄印；跟着走了一截，又撞上一头母牛，也带着一头牛犊——只是这第二头岁数大些，腿脚也利索些。

===Original===
“These two walleyed heifers must of been missed during last fall’s roundup,” Clint figgered, “and just as luck would have it they both have winter calves. Well, Smoky,” he says as he looked the direction the ponies had went, “I guess that leaves you out, *this time*.”

===Chinese===
「这两只白眼珠儿的小母牛，准是去年秋围拢时漏下的，」克林特寻思，「偏赶这时气不作美，俩都揣着冬犊。得，烟儿，」他朝马群去的方向望了一眼，说，「看来到不了你这儿了，*这一趟*。」

===Original===
It was near noon the next day when Clint showed up at the ranch packing a little calf on the front of his saddle. He found Jeff by the big sheds where the cattle was sheltered and fed, and told him:

===Chinese===
第二天晌午前后，克林特才回到牧场，鞍前驮着一头小牛犊。他在遮风喂牛的大棚舍旁边找着杰夫，对他说：

===Original===
“I had to leave this little feller’s mammy out about ten miles. There’s another cow and young calf with ’er, and maybe you better send a man out after ’em before this storm that’s coming catches up with ’em. Me, I’m going to eat the whole hind leg off a beef and roll in between my soogans.”

===Chinese===
「这小家伙的妈妈，我只好把她撂在十来英里外。她那儿还有一头母牛带一头小犊，你顶好派个人出去接接，赶在这场要来的暴风雪撵上他们之前。我嘛，这就去啃掉一整条牛后腿，然后钻进铺盖卷里睡他个昏天黑地。」

===Original===
The storm Clint had spoke of came sure enough, and seemed like to want to clean the earth of all that drawed a breath, the snow piled up and up till, as the cowboy remarked, “the fence posts around the ranch are only sticking up about an inch, and soon won’t be visible no more.”

===Chinese===
克林特念叨的那场暴风雪果真来了，那架势，像是存心要把世上一切喘气儿的都从地面上清扫干净。雪一层压一层地堆上去，堆到——照牛仔的说法——「牧场四周的栅栏桩只剩一英寸露在外头，眼瞅着就要全没影儿了」。

===Original===
That storm would of meant the death of all the cattle that was on the range, and most of the horses too, but as the tail of it came, a high wind sprung up, the snow drifted and piled high in the coulees, and at the same time took the depth of it down considerable wherever that wind hit. When it all finally quit raging, there was many patches where the grass was buried only by a few inches, and them patches the wind had cleared was what saved the lives of the range stock that winter.

===Chinese===
那场风暴，本来是要了牧场上全部牛只、连带大半马匹的命的；偏偏赶到尾巴梢上，刮起了大风，雪在干沟里又漂又堆，可风刮到哪儿，哪儿雪的厚度又见天似的往下削。等这一切终于消停下来，好些地块上的草只剩几英寸的雪埋着——正是风扫出来的那些秃斑，救下了那年冬天牧场牲口的性命。

===Original===
Clint had worried about Smoky as the stormy weather came on; he’d tried time and time again to get to him, but always some helpless critter made him branch off and finally turn back. “Tomorrow,” Clint kept a saying, but the “Tomorrow” came and went and the cowboy, always a fretting, hadn’t got near Smoky’s range.

===Chinese===
风暴天气一起头，克林特就为烟儿悬着心；他一回一回地想赶到他那儿去，可回回总有哪只走投无路的牲口把他的道岔开，末了只能折回来。「明天，」克林特总这么念叨；可「明天」来了又去，牛仔干着急干了一趟又一趟，愣是没沾着烟儿那片牧场的边儿。

===Original===
The great liking Clint had for the mouse colored horse made him fret and worry more than was necessary. That liking made him imagine a lot that was nowheres near true, and many a time that cowboy rolled in his bunk, tired, and wore out, and dreamed of seeing Smoky caught in a snow bank, weak, starving, and wolves drawing near.

===Chinese===
克林特对那匹鼠灰色马的深挚稀罕，叫他操了许多本不必操的心。这份稀罕叫他凭空想出一堆八竿子打不着的情形；多少回，这牛仔累得散了架、乏得脱了形，滚进铺里，梦里却看见烟儿陷在雪窝里，虚弱，挨饿，狼群正围拢上来。

===Original===
Smoky had sure enough lost considerable fat, and his strength was reduced some too, but he was nowheres weak;﻿—that is, not so weak that he couldn’t get up easy once he layed down, or be able to travel and rustle for his feed. The last big storm had took him down some more, but he was still able to plow thru the snow banks that’d gathered on the sides of the ridges, and get on the other side where the feed was easier reached.

===Chinese===
烟儿的确掉了不少膘，气力也减了些成色，可他离「虚弱」远着呢；——也就是说，还没虚到卧下之后不能利利索索站起来，也没虚到走不动路、刨不上食。最后那场大风暴又把他削下去一层，可他照样拱得开山梁两侧积起的大雪堆，翻到草料容易够着的那一边去。

===Original===
If it didn’t snow too much more there was no danger for Smoky and the bunch he was with. Him and Pecos had got to know that range so well, they knowed where the best of shelter could be found when the winds was cold or the blizzard howled, and then again, they knowed of many ridges and where the snow was always the thinnest. They had a spot to fit in with or against whatever the weather had to hand out, and whether the next on the program was to be sunshine or more snow, they was still well able to enjoy or compete with either.

===Chinese===
只要雪不再下得太大，烟儿和他那一群就没有什么凶险。他和佩科斯把那片牧场摸得烂熟：寒风乍起、暴风雪嚎叫的时候，哪儿寻得着最好的遮蔽，他们知道；再说，哪几道山梁上、哪几处地方的雪向来最薄，他们心里也有数。老天爷要发什么牌，他们都有一张能接能顶的牌；节目单上接下来排的是日头还是再一场雪，他们都有的是本事消受，或者对付。

---

			

===Original===
Weeks had passed since Smoky had raised his head out of the hollow in the snow and spotted the rider, who’d been Clint, coming onto him; and then one day, here comes another rider. Smoky had been the first to spot that other rider, and as was natural him and the rest of the bunch made tracks away from there and till the rider couldn’t be seen no more.

===Chinese===
打烟儿从雪窝里抬头、望见那个骑马人——也就是克林特——朝自己走来的那天起，几个礼拜过去了；然后有一天，又来了一个骑马人。这回又是烟儿头一个望见他；出于天性，他领着群里其余的马撒蹄走开，直走得那骑马人再也看不见影儿。

===Original===
A mile or so on, the bunch went to pawing snow and grazing again. Night was coming on, a wind was raising, and pretty soon light flakes of snow begin to come. Then, when night was well on, and as the wind got stronger and the snow heavier, the rider showed up again, right in the middle of the bunch this time and before Smoky or any of the others could see him. The ponies scattered like a bunch of quail at the sight of him and so close, but they soon got together again, and on a high lope went along with the storm.

===Chinese===
走出一英里上下，马群又刨起雪、啃起草来。夜色压上来，风起了，不多时，细碎的雪花飘了下来。随后，夜色沉沉，风越刮越紧、雪越下越重，那骑马人又冒了出来——这一回正在马群当间儿，烟儿他们谁都没来得及瞧见他。一帮小马像一群鹌鹑似的，一见他挨得这么近，轰一下炸散了；可他们很快又聚拢，踏着大跑，顺着暴风雪去了。

===Original===
The rider followed on after ’em, and as mile after mile of snow covered country was left behind, the ponies realized there was no dodging *him*. Heavy drifts was lunged into and hit on a high run as they tried to leave him behind, and then as they’d cross creek bottoms a mile or so wide, and where the snow was from two to three feet deep, the run begin to tell on ’em. They finally slowed down to a trot, and as the rider wasn’t pressing ’em any, there came a time when going at a walk seemed plenty fast. They was getting tired.

===Chinese===
骑马人在后头缀着他们；一英里又一英里的雪封乡野甩在身后，小马们明白了：*他*是躲不掉的。想把他甩在后面的时候，大雪堆他们一头扎进去，又拔出来高跑着冲过去；等到蹚那些一英里来宽、雪深两三英尺的谷底，这一路狂奔开始在他们身上讨还代价了。他们终于落到快步上；骑马人既不逼他们，走着走着，便觉得迈开步子走都已嫌快。他们乏了。

===Original===
The night wore on with ’em a traveling that way. The heavy wind pushed ’em on and their long hair was matted with snow, but tired, and hard as the deep snow was to buck thru, it all seemed better to drift on that way than stand still in such as the storm had turned out to be. They drifted on, not minding the rider much no more. Then after a while it begin to get light, slow and gradual; the new day come, and the rider, finding a thick patch of willows, let the ponies drift in the shelter. He tried to look on the back trail as he let ’em drift, and he grinned as the thick stinging snow blurred his view.

===Chinese===
夜就这样磨着，他们就这样走着。狂风在后头推，一身长毛叫雪粘成一绺一绺；他们乏透了，深雪又难拱——可左比右比，这么漂着走，也强似在这样一场暴风雪里干站着。他们一路漂着走，对那骑马人也不再怎么放在心上。过了一阵，天色慢慢透亮，一点一点地亮；新的一天来了，骑马人寻着一片密匝匝的柳丛，放马群漂进避风处。放他们漂着走的时候，他想回头看看来路，扎脸的密雪糊住了视线，他咧嘴笑了。

===Original===
“That old blizzard will sure do the work of covering up my trail,” he remarked as he looked for a sheltered spot amongst the willows.

===Chinese===
「这场老暴风雪，准会替我把踪迹盖个严实。」他一边在柳丛里寻摸避风的地方，一边自言自语。

===Original===
He soon found the sheltered spot and where the wind was more heard than felt; and getting off his tired horse begin tamping himself a place where he could move around a little and not have the snow up to his waist. He tied his horse up where he’d be within easy reach, and soon had a fire started out of dead willow twigs. Rice and jerky was cooked in a small lard bucket, and et out of the same. When that was gone, a few handfuls of snow was melted in the same bucket and coffee was made. Then a cigarette was rolled, a few puffs drawed out of it, and the man, curled up by the fire, was soon asleep.

===Chinese===
他很快寻着了避风的地儿——在那儿，风只闻其声、不觉其形；他下了那匹乏马，开始踩出一块转得开身的站脚地方，免得雪没到腰。他把马拴在伸手够得着的地方，不多时就用干柳枝生起了火。米饭和肉干在小猪油桶里煮了，也就着这只桶吃了。吃完，又用这桶化了几捧雪，沏了咖啡。然后卷上一支烟，吸了几口，这人往火边一蜷，很快睡着了。

===Original===
All of him, from the toe to his gunny sack covered boots to the dark face which showed under the wore out black hat, pointed out as the man being a halfbreed of Mexican and other blood that’s darker; and noticing the cheap, wore out saddle, the ragged saddle blanket on a horse that should of had some chance to feed instead of being tied up, showed that he was a halfbreed from the *bad* side, not caring, and with no pride.

===Chinese===
这人浑身上下，从麻袋片裹着的靴子尖，到那顶磨旧了的黑帽子底下露出的一张黑脸，处处都点明：他是个混血儿，墨西哥血统里掺着别的更深的血统；再看看那副便宜货的破鞍子、那条搭在一匹本该撒开吃草、却给拴死了的马背上的破烂鞍垫，更点明他是混血儿里*不学好*的那一路——什么都不上心，也没有半点儿自尊。

===Original===
He slept, feeling sure that no rider would be on his trail in this kind of weather; for the trail he’d made was wiped out and covered over near as soon as he made it, and as for the horses he’d stole, he knowed they wouldn’t be facing this storm and trying to go back; they’d be more for staying in shelter instead and try to find something to eat.

===Chinese===
他睡得踏实，笃定这种天气里不会有骑手追他的踪；因为他留下的踪迹，前脚刚踩出来，后脚就近乎盖了个严实；至于他偷来的这些马，他知道他们绝不会顶着这场暴风雪往回跑；他们只会更乐意待在避风的地方，想法子找点什么吃的。

===Original===
Seventeen head of Rocking R saddle stock, counting Smoky, was half a mile or so further down the creek bottom from where the halfbreed was sleeping. They hugged the thick willows for the shelter they’d give, and feed off the small green branches, the rye grass, and everything they could reach which they could chew on. Smoky and Pecos, side by side, rustled on thru the deep snow, sometimes ahead and sometimes behind the other horses, all a nosing around or pawing for whatever little feed could be found. But many cattle had been there ahead of ’em, and when darkness came on they showed near as drawed as they’d been that morning.

===Chinese===
连烟儿在内，摇椅R的鞍马一共十七匹，就待在混血儿睡觉的地方往下半英里来地的谷底上。他们紧贴着密柳丛，借它遮风，啃它的小绿枝、黑麦草，凡够得着、嚼得动的一概不放过。烟儿和佩科斯并肩在深雪里刨食前行，一会儿走在别的马前头，一会儿落在后头，个个东嗅西刨，寻摸那一口一口的草。可早在他们前头，大批的牛来过这儿了；夜幕落下来时，他们瞧着还跟早上一样瘪着肚皮。

===Original===
The snowing had let up some during the day, but as night drawed near the wind got stronger. The snow was drifting, and there’d be another night of travel when no trail would be left to show.

===Chinese===
白天里雪小了些，可夜色一近，风又紧起来。雪在漂，今夜又会是一个赶路的夜——一个留不下半点踪迹的夜。

===Original===
The breed woke up, looked around and grinned, then got up and shook himself. The fire was started again, another bait was cooked and consumed, and after all was gathered, he mounted his horse and went to looking for the ponies he’d left to graze down the creek bottom. He run onto ’em a couple of miles further an’ where he’d figgered they’d be; and as dark settled over the snow covered range, he fell in behind ’em and started ’em on the way.

===Chinese===
混血儿醒了，四下里望了望，咧嘴一笑，起身抖了抖身子。火重新生起来，又煮了一顿吃下肚；东西收拾齐了，他翻身上马，去找他撂在谷底下游吃草的那群马。往下又走了两英里，正落在他估摸的地方撞见了他们；雪封的牧场上夜色四合，他跟到他们屁股后头，赶着上了路。

===Original===
An hour or so of traveling, and then Smoky, who was in the lead, found himself between the wings of a corral, a corral that was made of willows and well hid. The breed had built it for *his purpose*, and signs showed that it’d been used many a time before. Long willow poles made the gate, and after he run the ponies in, and put up the poles, he went after his rope on his saddle.

===Chinese===
走了一个钟头上下，打头阵的烟儿发现自己已经置身于一座畜栏的两翼之间——一座柳条扎成、藏得巧妙的畜栏。混血儿为*他自己的营生*修的它；种种痕迹显示，这畜栏先前已经用过许多回了。长柳杆权当栏门；他把马群赶进去，架上柳杆，便回身去鞍上取绳。

===Original===
Many a brand had been changed in that corral, and on both horse and cow. Other times he’d used it just to change horses, and that’s what he wanted just now, a fresh horse. He wasn’t changing for the sake of the tired horse he’d been riding, it was just that he didn’t want to take chances of having a tired horse under him in case somebody jumped him.

===Chinese===
那座畜栏里改过的烙印多了，马的、牛的都有。旁的时候，他只用它换乘马匹，眼下要的也正是这个：一匹新换乘的马。他倒不是心疼胯下那匹乏马才换的，只是不想赌那个运气——万一有人扑上来，底下却压着一匹跑不动的马。

===Original===
His loop was made, and thru the dark he was trying to see just what horse to put his rope on. The white background helped him considerable in making out the shapes of the ponies, and there was one shape he was looking out for before he let his loop sail, the shape of a mouse colored blazed faced horse which he’d noticed and watched all along. Pretty soon, and furthest away from him, he got a glimpse of Smoky’s head﻿—he recognized the white streak on his forehead and his rope sailed.

===Chinese===
绳圈挽好了，他在黑地里辨认，该把绳甩上哪匹马。雪白的底子帮了他大忙，小马们的轮廓认得出；放绳出去之前，他单盯着一副轮廓——那匹他一路留意在心的白章脸的鼠灰色马。不多时，在离他最远的犄角旮旯里，他瞥见了烟儿的头——认出了前额上那道白纹，他的绳随之甩了出去。

===Original===
Smoky snorted and ducked, the rope just grazed his ears, and went on to settle over another horse’s head. In the dark, the breed couldn’t follow his rope, and he didn’t know but what he’d caught Smoky till he pulled on the rope and brought the horse to him. He cussed considerable as he seen he’d caught another horse than the one he wanted, but as he noticed that this horse was good size and strong looking, he let it go at that, and didn’t take time to make another try for Smoky.

===Chinese===
烟儿喷了个响鼻，脑袋一低；绳贴着他的耳朵扫过，往前落在了另一匹马的头上。黑地里，混血儿瞅不见自己的绳，还只当套住了烟儿，直到拽着绳把马拉到跟前才看清。见套着的不是他要的那匹，他骂骂咧咧了好一阵；可瞅见这马个头不小、瞧着也结实，便将就着算了，没再费工夫冲烟儿补一绳。

===Original===
“I’ll get you next time, you﻿—” he says as he looked Smoky’s way and saddled the horse he’d caught.

===Chinese===
「下回再收拾你，你——」他一边朝烟儿那边望着，一边给他套住的这匹马备鞍。

===Original===
Letting the poles down, the breed mounted the fresh horse, let the ponies out, and turned ’em out of the creek bottom onto a long bench. The strong winds had blowed most all the snow off there, and excepting for a few low places where it had piled deep, traveling was made easy. He kept the ponies on a trot most of the night, and sometimes where the snow wasn’t too deep he’d crowd ’em into a lope.

===Chinese===
柳杆放下来，混血儿骑上新换乘的马，放出马群，把他们从谷底赶上一道长长的台地（bench）。大风早把那上头的雪刮了个七七八八，除了几处洼坑里积得深，赶起路来不算费劲。大半夜他都让马群走着快步，遇上雪不太深的地方，就催他们跑成大跑。

---

			

===Original===
Steady, the gait was kept up, and finally, after the breed seen that the ponies was too tired and weak to travel much more, he begin to look for a place where he could hide ’em and where they could rustle feed during the day that was soon to come. On the other end of the ridge he was following, he knowed of a place; and taking down his rope, he snapped it at the tired ponies and kept ’em on the move till that place was reached. There, another stop was made.

===Chinese===
步子就这么不紧不慢地维持着；到后来，混血儿见马群乏得弱得走不动几步了，便开始寻地方——一个能把他们藏起来、又能让他们在眼看就到的白天里刨上草的地方。他跟着的那道山梁的另一头，他知道有这么一处；于是解下绳来，朝乏透的马群甩得啪啪响，逼着他们不停脚，直到到了地方。在那儿，又歇了一站。

===Original===
The storm had dwindled down and wore out till nothing was left but the high wind. It kept the snow drifting, which would keep on covering tracks and make traveling easier. But the breed didn’t need the storm to help him no more, for, as he figgered, the country ahead and where he was headed was all open. He expected no riders would be found on the way at that time of the year, and as he’d been on that route many a time before with stolen stock, he knowed just how far it was between each good hiding and stopping place, both for himself and stock.

===Chinese===
暴风雪渐渐消磨殆尽，只剩下大风还刮着。风把雪刮得四处漂，正好接着盖踪迹，路也好走些。可混血儿已经用不着暴风雪帮忙了——照他盘算，前头的地界、他去的那一路，全空旷无人。他料定一年里这个时节，路上碰不上一个骑手；再说，带着偷来的牲口走这条道，他走过不知多少回，哪儿到哪儿有个好的藏身处、歇脚点，人一份、牲口一份，他门儿清。

===Original===
There was corrals on the way, some built by him, and others built by more of his kind. Sometimes he would change the iron on the ponies he’d just stole, but as the hair was too long for anybody to be able to read the brand that was on ’em, that could wait a while till he got further away and he could travel in daytime more.

===Chinese===
沿路上还有畜栏，有的是他亲手修的，有的是他的同类们修的。有时他会给刚偷到手的马改火印；可马毛太长，谁也认不出他们身上原有的烙印，这事儿不急，可以等一等——等他走得再远些、白天也能多赶些路了再说。

===Original===
He was pleased with everything in general as he left the ponies and started hunting a shelter for himself. He grinned, satisfied, as he melted snow for his coffee and figgered on the price the ponies would bring. He knowed good horses, and even though they was in poor shape now he knowed what they’d turn out to be after a month’s time on green grass.

===Chinese===
他撂下马群，动身给自己寻过夜的地方，心里对眼下的一切都挺受用。他化着雪沏咖啡，盘算这批马能换回多大一笔钱，满意地咧嘴笑了。他识得好马；他们眼下虽掉膘掉得可怜，可他知道，青草上一放一个月，他们能成个什么模样。

===Original===
And then there was “Smoky,” that mouse colored horse;﻿—he’d heard how four hundred dollars had been offered for that pony, and allowed that some other cowman to the south would be glad to give at least half that price for him, once it was showed what a cowhorse he really was.

===Chinese===
再说还有「烟儿」呢，那匹鼠灰色马；——四百块的出价他都有所耳闻；他认定，南边随便哪位牧场主，只要亲眼见着这是一匹什么样的牧牛马，出到那个数的一半也乐意。

===Original===
A few hundred miles to the south was the breed’s hangout, a place in a low country and where the snow hardly stayed. Once there he could take it easy, let the ponies fatten up, and after the brand was well “blotched” so nobody would recognize the original, he’d sell ’em one at a time for a good price or ship ’em out to some horse dealer. In the meantime he had nothing to worry about, the storm had took his trail off the face of the earth, there was a good seventy miles between him and where he’d started with the horses, and near a hundred miles to the Rocking R home ranch.

===Chinese===
往南几百英里，是这混血儿的老巢——一片洼地，雪存不住脚。一到那儿，他就能歇口气，让马儿们上膘；等烙印「糊」得严严实实、谁也认不出原印了，再一匹一匹卖个好价，或者整车发给哪个马贩子。眼下他没什么可操心的：暴风雪把他的踪迹从地皮上抹了个干净；离他带着马起程的地方，足有七十英里；离摇椅R的本部牧场，也近一百英里。

## XI / 十一

===Original===
“The Feel of a Strange Hand”

===Chinese===
生人手掌的滋味

===Original===
A long month had passed since Clint had rode out to get Smoky and came back with a calf instead. Every day since, that cowboy had been for going after Smoky again, but the deep snow and storms had more than kept him breaking trails for snowbound cattle that was weak and needed bringing in. He couldn’t find no time and hadn’t been able to frame no excuse so as he could hit out for Smoky’s range. Then one morning he got up with a hunch. He tried to keep it down, but every morning it got stronger till finally Clint just had to saddle up the best horse he had, and hit out for where Smoky had been wintering.

===Chinese===
打克林特骑马出去寻烟儿、结果牵回来一头牛犊那天起，整整一个月过去了。打那以后，这牛仔天天都惦着再去寻烟儿，可雪深风暴急，他光忙着给被雪困住的弱牛开道、把该收的收回去，就够他脚打后脑勺了。他挤不出一点工夫，也编不出一个由头，好动身奔烟儿那片牧场去。后来一天早上，他起了床，心里就搁上一个念头。他试着把它压下去，可这念头一天比一天壮，到末了，克林特只好备上手头最好的一匹马，直奔烟儿过冬的地方去了。

===Original===
The last big storm had let up a few days before, and many fresh tracks covered the horse range. Clint trailed and trailed; he found and went thru many bunches of ponies, but no Smoky. Even the bunch that pony was running with when last seen had seemed to evaporate into thin air, and there Clint wondered. He wondered if somebody’d stole him and the bunch, but he put that off, figgering that no horse thief would steal horses packing as well known a brand as the Rocking R, unless he was a daggone fool, or a daggone good one. Anyway, as worried as Clint was, he felt some relieved in not finding the bunch Smoky had been with; for if he’d found them and no Smoky, that’d been proof enough that the pony had went and died somewheres. The other ponies he’d seen that day still looked good and strong, and that was proof enough that Smoky must be the same.

===Chinese===
最后那场大风暴几天前歇了劲，马场上盖满了新鲜的蹄印。克林特跟了一道又一道，寻见又穿行了一群又一群小马，就是没有烟儿。连那匹小马上回叫人瞧见时跟着跑的那群马，也像是化进空气里没了影儿，克林特心里犯起了嘀咕。他寻思是不是有人把他连同那群马一块儿偷了，可又把这念头撂下了——他琢磨着，哪个偷马贼也不会去偷驮着摇椅R这么一块响当当烙印的马，除非他是个天字第一号的傻瓜，或者是个天字第一号的好手。不管怎么说，克林特心里再怎么悬着，没寻见烟儿跟的那群马，他反倒松了口气；因为那群马要是寻见了、里头却没有烟儿，那就足证这小马是上哪儿死了。那天见着的旁的小马，匹匹瞧着还精神结实，这也就足证烟儿必然也一样。

===Original===
“Most likely him and his bunch just drifted with that last storm and went back to their home range,” Clint thought, as he headed his horse back for the ranch; but the hunch that was still with him didn’t seem to agree with that thought none at all.

===Chinese===
「十有八九，他是带着他那群马、随那场暴风雪漂回自家牧场去了。」克林特拨转马头往牧场走，心里这么想；可揣在心里的那个念头，却一点儿也不肯附和这个想法。

===Original===
Two weeks later found the cowboy on the horse range once more, and making a bigger circle; but Smoky and his bunch still kept being amongst the missing. He told Old Tom about it as he got back to the ranch that night, but the old man didn’t seem worried; he waved a hand as Clint said how he’d finally got to believe that the whole bunch had been stole.

===Chinese===
两个礼拜后，这牛仔又出现在马场上，兜的圈子比上回更大；可烟儿和他那群马，还是稳稳待在失踪者之列。当晚他回到牧场，把这事说给老汤姆听，老头听着并不怎么着急；等克林特说到他到头来认准整群马是叫人偷了，老头摆了摆手。

===Original===
“Don’t worry,” he says, “we’ll find him and all the rest during horse roundup.”

===Chinese===
「别急，」他说，「马匹围拢的时候，保管能找见他和其余那些。」

===Original===
Finally, spring broke up, the deep drifts started to melting and the creeks begin to raise. Then after a while, and when the “hospital stuff”2 had been turned out on the range a couple of weeks, riders begin stringing out towards the horse range and gathering the remuda. Clint lined out by himself and hit for the country where Smoky had been raised. He reached the camp where he’d started breaking him, and from there he rode, every morning with a fresh horse and running down every bunch of stock horses a hoping to get sight of the mouse colored gelding.

===Chinese===
终于，春天解了冻，深深的雪堆开始消融，溪水开始上涨。过了一阵，「病号班」2撒出去在牧场上放了两三个礼拜，骑手们便开始一串一串地往马场进发，收拢雷穆达。克林特自个儿单走一路，直奔烟儿出生长大的那片地界。他到了当初开手调教烟儿的那个营子，从那儿起，每天早上换一匹新马，见着一群役马就追上去过目，巴望能瞅见那匹鼠灰色骟马。
