===Original===
He rode for a week and seen every horse that was on that range, strays and all; and finally after he’d combed the whole country where Smoky had run as a colt, he rode back to the ranch, feeling disappointed but a hoping that the other riders had found him.

===Chinese===
他跑了一个礼拜，那片牧场上的马，连走散的在内，一匹不落全见了；末了，把烟儿当年当小马驹撒欢跑遍的整片地界梳了个遍，他拨马回牧场，心里失望，却又巴望着旁的骑手把他寻着了。

===Original===
The remuda was in the big corrals, when he got there﻿—all of it, excepting for the seventeen head which couldn’t be found nowheres. Smoky was one of the seventeen.

===Chinese===
他赶到的时候，雷穆达已经圈进了大畜栏——全数都在，只除了哪儿也寻不见的那十七匹。烟儿就是那十七匹里的一匹。

===Original===
There was a few more days riding, and then of a sudden Old Tom decided Clint had been right, the horses was sure enough stolen. His big car hit only the high spots as the old man headed for town. Jack rabbits was passed by and left behind the same as if they’d been tied, and when he hit the main street he was doing seventy. He put on his brakes and passed the sheriff’s office by half a block, but he left his car there, and hoofed on a high run all the way back.

===Chinese===
又骑了几天，忽然间，老汤姆认了克林特的账——马，千真万确是叫人偷了。老头开着他的大汽车直奔镇子，一路只拣高处落。长耳兔一只只被他甩在身后，就跟拿绳子拴死在原地似的；冲上大街时，码速已经到了七十。他一脚刹车，车滑过了治安官办公室半条街才停住；他把车撂在那儿，迈开两条腿一路飞奔了回来。

===Original===
That official was notified of the theft, and notified to notify other officials of the State and other States around; and Old Tom stuck close to see that that was done and mighty quick. A thousand dollars reward was offered for the thief, and the same reward for the return of the horses, naming one mouse colored saddle horse as special.

===Chinese===
失窃案报到了那位官老爷跟前，又责令他去知会本州和左近各州的官老爷们；老汤姆寸步不离地盯在一旁，就为着这事儿件件办妥，还得快。一千块悬赏捉偷马贼，马匹归来，另赏同样数目，还特别点名一匹鼠灰色鞍马，从优计酬。

===Original===
The spring roundup went by, summer, and then the fall roundup and the close of the season’s work. Nothing of Smoky, nor any of the ponies he’d run with or the horse thief was heard of. It seemed like one and all had left the earth for good, and if what all Old Tom often wished on the thief could of come true, the hombre would of sure found himself in a mighty hot place.

===Chinese===
春围拢过去了，夏天过去了，跟着秋围拢也过去了，一季的活计收了尾。烟儿没有音信，他一起跑过的小马没有音信，偷马贼也没有音信。看那光景，是一个跟着一个，全都彻底离开了这块地皮；老汤姆平常冲那贼许下的那些愿，倘若真能应验一条，那家伙这会儿保准待在一个热得够呛的地方。

===Original===
Clint rode on for the Rocking R thru that summer and fall, and always as he rode, he kept an eye on the country around and hoping that sometimes he’d run acrost his *one* horse, Smoky. He didn’t want to think that the horse had been stolen, and he kept a saying to himself as he rode: “He’s just strayed away somewheres.” There wasn’t a draw, coulee, or creek bottom passed by without the whole of it was looked into; and never before was the Rocking R country looked into so well. Every rider, on down to the wrangler, kept his eyes peeled for the mouse colored horse; and even though cattle is what the wagons was out for, there was more eyes out for Smoky, and cattle was only brought in as second best.

===Chinese===
那年夏天和秋天，克林特照旧替摇椅R骑着马当差，一路骑一路留意着四下的地界，巴望有朝一日撞上他*独一份*的马——烟儿。他不愿往马是被偷了那头想，骑在马上总跟自己念叨：「他不过是上哪儿逛荡迷了路。」一道洼地、一条干沟、一处谷底，凡打跟前过的，没有一处不翻个底朝天；摇椅R的地界，从来没有叫人这么细细搜过。上上下下的骑手，一路数到马倌，个个把眼睛睁得溜圆，留神那匹鼠灰色马；车队虽说本是出来为牛的，掏出来找烟儿的眼睛倒更多，牛反倒成了退而求其次。

===Original===
It wasn’t till fall roundup was near over that Clint begin losing all hope of ever seeing Smoky again in *that* country, and as them hopes left him, there came a hankering for him to move. Maybe it was just to be moving and riding on some other range for a change, but back of it all, and if Clint had stopped to figger some, he’d found that his hankering to move wasn’t only for seeing new territory﻿—there was a faint hope away deep, that some day, somewheres, he’d find Smoky.

===Chinese===
直到秋围拢临近收尾，克林特才渐渐断了在*那片*地界再见烟儿的念想；念想一去，他心里生出一股挪窝的痒。也许只为动弹动弹、换个牧场骑骑，可这一切底下——克林特只要肯停下细想想就会发现——他想挪窝，并不光是图看新地界；在极深极深的地方，还埋着一星微弱的指望：有朝一日，在什么地方，他会找到烟儿。

===Original===
For that pony had got tangled up in the cowboy’s heartstrings a heap more than that cowboy wanted to let on, even to himself. He couldn’t get away from *how* he missed him. He’d thought of him when on day-herd and how the horse had seemed to understand every word he’d said. On the cutting grounds, he’d kept a comparing whatever horse he’d be riding with Smoky, and find that pony (no matter how good he was) a mighty poor excuse of a cowhorse alongside of the mouse colored pony that was missing.

===Chinese===
原来那匹小马在这牛仔心弦上缠的结，比这牛仔肯露的——哪怕只对自己露的——要多得多。他是怎么个想他的，这他躲不开。守日间牛群的时候他想起过他，想起那马好像连他说的每一个字都听得懂。在分群场上，他总把胯下随便哪匹马拿来同烟儿比，比来比去，只觉得那马——不管有多好——搁在那匹不见了踪影的鼠灰色小马旁边，都只算一匹顶不像样的牧牛马。

===Original===
But all them good points of Smoky’s was nothing as compared to the rest of what that horse really had been *as a horse*, and there’s where Smoky had got under Clint’s hide, as a horse, one in a thousand.

===Chinese===
可烟儿这些好处，比起这马*作为一匹马*骨子里真正的那一切，又都不算什么了；正是在这上头，烟儿钻到了克林特的皮底下——作为一匹马，千里挑一。

===Original===
The last of the wagons had trailed into the home ranch, and the next day, the remuda was hazed out to the winter range. Clint wasn’t along that fall to see the ponies turned loose. Instead he was in the big bunk house at the home ranch, and busy stuffing his saddle into a gunny sack. A railroad map was spread on the floor and which the cowboy had been studying.

===Chinese===
最后一拨车队进了本部牧场，第二天，雷穆达被轰出去撒到冬季牧场。那年秋天，克林特没跟着去看放马。他正待在本部牧场的大工棚里，忙着把鞍子往麻袋里塞。地板上摊着一张铁路地图，牛仔对着它琢磨了好一阵了。

===Original===
Jeff opened the door of the bunk house and took in at a glance what all Clint was up to. He noticed the railroad map laying by his foot and smiled.

===Chinese===
杰夫推开工棚门，一眼扫明白了克林特这是在忙活什么。他瞧见摊在克林特脚边的铁路地图，笑了。

===Original===
“I figgered you would,” he says, “now that Smoky is not with the outfit no more.”

===Chinese===
「我就寻思你会这么干，」他说，「如今烟儿不在这班人马里了嘛。」

===Original===
The first of winter had come and hit the high mountains of the southern country. Big, dark clouds had drifted in, drenched the ranges down to bedrock with a cold rain, and hung on for days. Then the rain had gradually turned to a wet snow, kept a falling steady, and without a break, till it seemed like the country itself was shivering under the spell.

===Chinese===
初冬来到了南方地带的高山上。大团大团的黑云漂了进来，一场冷雨把一座座牧场从头顶浇到根底，一连几天赖着不走。后来雨渐渐变成了湿雪，不紧不慢、不歇气儿地下，下到后来，仿佛这片乡野自己也在那道魔咒底下打起了哆嗦。

===Original===
Finally, and after many long days, the dark clouds begin to get lighter and lighter and started lifting and drifting on. Then one evening, the sun got a chance to peek thru and smile at the country again. It went down a smiling that way, and after it disappeared over the blue ridge, a new moon took its place for a spell, and like as to promise that the sun would smile again the next day.

===Chinese===
末了，熬过许多漫长的日子，黑云开始越来越薄，慢慢抬起来、漂走了。后来一天傍晚，太阳得了个空儿，从云缝里探出头来，重新朝这片乡野笑了笑。他就这么笑着落了下去；他沉到青色的山脊后面之后，一弯新月顶了他的班待了一阵，像是在应承：明天，太阳还会再笑的。

===Original===
And it did; it came up bright and real fitting to that Arizona country. The air was clear as spring water in a granite pool, and as still. The whole world seemed dozing and just contented to take on all the warmth and life the sun was giving. A mountain lion was stretched out on a boulder, warm and comfortable, where the day before he’d been in his den all curled up and shivering. Then a few deer come out of their shelter, hair on end and still wet thru, but as they reached the sunny side of the mountain it wasn’t long when it dried again, and layed smooth.

===Chinese===
太阳果然说话算话；他升上来，亮堂堂的，跟亚利桑那这片乡野正相配。空气清得像花岗岩水潭里的泉水，也一样静。整个世界像在打盹，一心安享太阳撒下来的全部暖意和生气。一头美洲狮在一块大圆石上伸展了身子，又暖和又舒坦——头一天，他还蜷在窝里筛糠来着。随后几头鹿从藏身处走了出来，毛炸着，还湿了个透；可等他们走到山的向阳面，没多大工夫，毛又干了，服服帖帖地顺了下来。

===Original===
Further down the mountain and more on the foothills, a little chipmunk stuck his head out of his winter quarters and blinked at the sun. He blinked at it for quite a spell like not believing, and pretty soon came out to make sure. He stood up, rolled in the warm dirt, and in more ways than one made up for the long days he’d holed away. Other chipmunks came out, and then he went visiting. More seeds was gathered as he went from bush to bush and even though he already had a mighty big supply already stored away, he worked on as though he was afraid of running short long before spring come.

===Chinese===
再往山下、更靠近山脚的地方，一只小花栗鼠（chipmunk）从他的冬季住所里探出脑袋，冲太阳直眨眼。他冲太阳眨了好一阵子，像是不敢信，不多时便钻出来验看个明白。他站起来，在暖烘烘的土里打了几个滚，把猫冬的那些漫长日子怎么着都加倍找了回来。旁的花栗鼠也出来了，接着他就走门串户去了。他从这丛灌木逛到那丛灌木，一路又捡了些草籽——虽说囤下的存粮早已经满满当当，他还是忙个不停，倒像生怕挨不到开春就断了顿。

===Original===
He was at his busiest, and tearing a pine cone apart for the nuts he’d find inside, when he hears something a tearing thru the brush and coming his way. Away he went and hightailed it towards his hole, and he’d no more than got there when he gets a glimpse of what looked like a mountain of a horse and running for all he was worth. A long rope was dragging from his neck.

===Chinese===
他正忙到兴头上，撕着一颗松果掏里头的松子，忽听得什么东西哗啦啦撕开灌木丛，直奔他这边来了。他撒腿就跑，一溜烟奔向自己的洞口；才刚到地方，就瞥见一样东西——瞧着像一匹山一样大的马，正没命地跑。一条长绳从他的脖子上拖了下来。

===Original===
The chipmunk went down as far in his hole as he could, stood still and listened a minute, and then storing away the nuts he’d gathered, stuck his head out once more. He chirped considerable as he looked around to see if any more out of the ordinary or dangerous looking was in sight, and he’d just had time to blink at the scenery a couple of times, when he gets a glimpse of another horse﻿—this one was packing a man, and at the same speed went right on the trail the other had left.

===Chinese===
花栗鼠往洞里钻到不能再深，站定了听了一分钟，把捡来的松子归置好，又把脑袋探了出来。他叽叽喳喳叫了好一通，四下张望，看还有没有什么出格的、瞧着危险的东西在视野里；他统共才来得及冲这片风景眨两下眼，就又瞥见一匹马——这一匹驮着个人，顺着前头那匹留下的蹄迹，原速跟了下去。

===Original===
The chipmunk never wondered what this running was all about, he just chirped and ducked out of sight; but it wasn’t long when he stuck his head out again and gradually showed all of himself. He stood up on a rock close to his hole, and looking around from there, he could see two objects out towards the flat, moving fast, and seeming like one trying to catch up with the other. He watched ’em, till a raise finally took ’em out of sight; then he watched some more and in other directions, and seeing nothing that’d need watching, he went to visiting again and to gathering more nuts.

===Chinese===
花栗鼠才不管这一通狂奔是为了什么，他只叽喳两声，一缩脖没了影儿；可没多大工夫，他又把脑袋探了出来，又一点一点把整个身子都亮了出来。他站上洞口近旁一块石头，从那儿四下望去，望见平地那头有两个东西，跑得飞快，瞧着像一个正要追上另一个。他望着他们，望到一道高坡终于把他们挡出了视野；接着他又朝旁的方向望了一阵，见再没有什么值得望的，便又走门串户、捡他的松子去了。

===Original===
Out on the flat, and on the other side of the raise, the two objects went on. How glad that one object in the lead would of been to’ve changed places with the chipmunk and like him been able to crawl down a hole and hide for a spell. For hours and hours thru the night he’d been trailed. His hoofs had sunk deep into the mud every step he’d took, but acrost foothills and dobe flats he’d went on, always the human close behind.

===Chinese===
平地上，高坡的那一头，那两个东西还在跑。打头的那一个，若是能同花栗鼠换个个儿，像他那样钻进洞里躲上一阵，该有多好。一整夜，好几个钟头好几个钟头，他都被人缀着。他每落一步，蹄子都深深陷进泥里；可他仍旧翻过一重重山脚，蹚过一片片干泥滩（dobe flats），一个劲儿往前去，那个人总是紧咬在身后。

===Original===
Twice that human’d disappeared and he’d took hope, but soon he’d show up again, and mounted on a fresh horse would chase him some more. A rope had settled around his neck once﻿—he’d fought till it broke, and run on a dragging it.

===Chinese===
有两回那人不见了，他升起过指望，可那人很快就又冒出来，骑上一匹新换乘的马接着追。有一条绳曾经套上过他的脖子——他挣到绳断为止，又拖着那条断绳接着跑。

===Original===
He was getting tired, mighty tired, and beginning to feel with each step he took that the country was in a cahoots with the man and trying to hold him back. His feet went ankle deep in the soft, rain-soaked ground, and pulling out and placing ’em ahead steady, on and on, was getting to be more and more of an effort.

===Chinese===
他乏了，乏得厉害，一步一步开始觉出：这片乡野是跟那人串通好了的，存心拖住他。四条腿踩进被雨水泡软的地里，一踩半截深；把蹄子拔出来、再稳稳当当迈到前头去，这么一步又一步，越来越吃力了。

===Original===
Once again the man disappeared, only to show up mounted on another fresh horse. The man’s relay string had been well placed and as the horse he’d been chasing was getting tired, and easier right along to turn the way he wanted him, he could near see how the end of the chase was going to be.

===Chinese===
那人又一次不见了，转眼又骑上另一匹新换乘的马冒了出来。那人的换乘班（relay string）布得周到；眼看他追的这匹马越乏，一路上越容易照他心里想的方向掰，这场追逐的收场会是个什么样，他差不多都看得见了。

===Original===
The sun was getting well up in the sky when skirting along the foothills and going thru a thick bunch of cedars, the tired horse noticed dead cedars piled up in a way that made a fence. Any other time he’d whirled at the sight and went some other way, but his vision wasn’t very clear no more, nor was his brain working very good. He’d went on his nerves and kept on long after his muscles had hollered “quit,” and he’d got to the point where he was running because something away back in his mind kept a telling him that he should, really not knowing why. He was past caring where he went, and even if the rider behind had stopped and quit, he’d kept on running just the same and till he’d dropped.

===Chinese===
太阳已经高高升上半空，乏马贴着山脚绕行，正穿过一片密密的雪松（cedar），望见枯死的雪松一棵棵垛起来，垛成了一道栅栏。搁在旁的时候，他一见这个就得打个旋儿改道走，可这会儿他的眼神早已不清亮，脑子也不大好使了。他一直是凭着一股神经撑着，肌肉早喊过「不成了」，他还照样往前；到后来他还在跑，只因为脑子深处有个什么老在说「该跑」，他自己也真不知道为什么。往哪儿跑，他已经无所谓了；就算后头那骑手停下不追了，他也照样会跑下去，一直跑到栽倒为止。

===Original===
He followed the cedar fence hardly realizing it was there. Then from the other side of him appeared another fence. It gradually pinched in on him as he went, till finally both fences led up to a gate and into a corral hid in the thick trees. There he stopped, realizing somehow that he couldn’t go no further, and legs wide apart, breathing hard, sweat a dripping from every part of him, he stood still.

===Chinese===
他顺着雪松栅栏走，几乎意识不到它的存在。跟着，另一侧又冒出一道栅栏。他往前走，两道栅栏一步步往里夹他，末了，两道栅栏并到一扇栏门前，通进一座藏在密林深处的畜栏。到了那儿他站住了，不知怎么就明白过来：再也走不动了。他四腿岔开，喘得粗重，浑身每一处都在淌汗，就那么定定地站着。

===Original===
The halfbreed closed the pole gate, and turned looking at the horse.

===Chinese===
混血儿关上木杆栏门，回身打量着这匹马。

===Original===
“Now, you ornery mouse colored hunk of meanness, I guess I got you.”

===Chinese===
「哼，你这块讨人嫌的鼠灰色坏料，这回我看你是落在我手里了。」

===Original===
But Smoky, eyes half closed and not seeing, head near touching the ground, and the rest of him trying hard to stay up, never seemed to hear.

===Chinese===
可烟儿两眼半阖着，什么也看不见，脑袋几乎贴到地上，剩下的那副身子还在拼命撑着不倒——他像是压根儿没听见。

---

			

===Original===
Many months had passed and many things happened since Smoky had been hazed away from his home range on the Rocking R. There’d been long nights of traveling when many miles was covered and very little feed was got on the way. Then long, weary miles of travel had accumulated till near a thousand of ’em separated him from the country where he’d been born and raised.

===Chinese===
打烟儿被人从摇椅R的自家牧场上轰走那天起，许多个月过去了，许多事情发生了。有过一个又一个赶路的长夜，一夜一夜走出老远，路上却没寻上几口草。这漫长累人的路程一程一程攒下来，到后来，差不多一千英里，把他和生他养他的那片乡野隔开了。

===Original===
Many strange looking hills and flats he’d crossed as he was kept on the go with Pecos and the rest of the bunch, and when he’d come to the desert it’d been a great relief. The deep snow had gradually been left behind by then and the bare sagebrush flats had took the place of the snow covered prairie. Many bunches of wild ponies had been seen on the way, and once in a while a little bunch of cattle was passed by. The country had kept a changing; from rolling prairie it went to low hills, low hills to mountains, and on the other side more low hills and then sagebrush. The sagebrush had stayed in the landscape from then on and only added some yuccas as the southern country was reached, then Spanish dagger, barrel cactus, and catclaw.

===Chinese===
他跟着佩科斯和群里其余的马被撵着一路走，翻过一座座生模怪样的山，蹚过一片片生模怪样的滩；等到进了沙漠地带，倒大大松了口气。到那时节，深雪已经渐渐甩在了身后，光秃秃的山艾（sagebrush）滩接替了雪封的草原。一路见着了一群群野马，偶尔也路过一小股一小股的牛。乡野一路变个不停：起伏的草原变成低矮的丘陵，丘陵又变成大山，翻过山去又是丘陵，然后是山艾。打那以后，山艾就没离开过这片地界，只是越往南走，又添了些丝兰（yucca），再添上西班牙匕首刺（Spanish dagger）、桶形仙人掌（barrel cactus）和猫爪刺（catclaw）。

===Original===
Finally a wide river in a deep canyon of many colors had been reached and swimmed acrost. A few days more travel, and then it seemed like Smoky and the bunch had got *there*﻿—anyway there’d been no more traveling. The next day, the halfbreed had corralled all the ponies, and with a running iron blotched the Rocking R  brand over with what looked like a wagon wheel.  The original brand was disfigured complete, and then the horses was shoved up on a high knoll while the new brand healed. The knoll was a high flat mesa, with rimrocks all around and where it could be got up on only in one place. That place had then been closed with a rope and a blanket stretched over it. There was good feed up there, and enough snow and rain water in a natural reservoir to last many days.

===Chinese===
末了，一条宽阔的大河到了——河卧在一道五颜六色的深峡谷里——他们游了过去。又走了几天，然后就到了烟儿他们要到的*那个地方*似的——反正，再没有赶路了。第二天，混血儿把小马全都圈了拢来，拿一支走印烙铁（running iron），在摇椅R的烙印上覆「糊」了一个车轮模样的新印。原印毁了个干干净净；随后马被赶上一座高高的小圆丘，好让新烙印养伤。那座丘是一方又高又平的平顶台地（mesa），四边围着崖岩（rimrocks），只有一处上得去。那一处随即拿绳子拦死，上头还蒙了一条毯子。台地上头草料不坏，一处天然水窖里蓄的雪水雨水，也够对付许多天。

===Original===
All would of been well for Smoky, and the long trip with the bucking of snow, hard traveling, and all with the changes of the country would of been took in as it comes; but along with that trip, there’d growed something between that pony’s ears which had got to chafe. It was a hate, a hate with poison and all for the breed that’d kept him and the others on the move.

===Chinese===
这一切，烟儿本来都能受着——这一趟长途，拱雪开路、苦赶苦行，外加乡野的种种变换，来什么受什么就是了；可伴随着这趟行程，那对小马耳朵中间的东西也长了劲儿，磨得他难受。那是恨，一种带毒的恨，全冲着那个撵着他和伙伴们不停脚的混血儿。

===Original===
Smoky was born with a natural fear and hate of the human. He’d carried it always, excepting when Clint, *that one man*, was around; but hating humans had never bothered him, not till the dark face of the breed had showed itself over the skyline.

===Chinese===
烟儿生来就带着对人的天生的怕和恨。这怕和恨他一直揣着，只除了克林特、*那一个人*在跟前的时候；不过恨人这件事，从来没有让他烦心过——直到混血儿那张黑脸在地平线上冒了出来。

===Original===
With him in sight, that hate had got to grow till murder showed in his eye, and the little fear that was still with him was all that’d kept him from doing damage to the dark complected human that’d trailed along behind all the way. He’d boiled over to himself, stayed in the lead, and far away from the breed as he could.

===Chinese===
那人在望，这恨便一长再长，长到他眼睛里都露出了杀气；全靠心里残存的那一点点怕压着，他才没有回头去收拾那个黑脸膛的人——那人一路缀在队伍后头。他自己个儿在心里翻滚着，只管走在头里，离那混血儿多远是多远。

===Original===
The breed had throwed a rope at him one day, and missed. Smoky had never been missed that way before, and from that once he’d learned that by ducking at the right time there was such a thing as dodging a rope. The next day the breed had throwed his rope at him again, and Smoky, watching, had ducked at the right time and once more the loop had missed. The breed begin cussing as he spread another loop and tried to place it around Smoky’s neck, but his cussing didn’t do him any good, and the loop had fell short a foot from the dodging pony’s head.

===Chinese===
有一天，混血儿朝他甩了一绳，落了空。烟儿还从来没叫人这么甩空过；就打那一回他学会了：只要低头低得是时候，绳套是躲得开的。第二天，混血儿又朝他甩绳，烟儿盯着看着，瞅准时机把头一低，绳套又落了空。混血儿一边挽起又一个绳圈、想把它套上烟儿的脖子，一边骂开了；可骂也白骂，绳圈落在那躲闪的小马头前一英尺的地方，短了一截。

===Original===
Smoky would of enjoyed all that, if he hadn’t meant it so much; and what’s more the breed had got ferocious, which all made things more serious for the horse. He’d hated the sound of that breed’s voice as that hombre, fighting his head, and cussing for all he was worth, had coiled up his rope once more and made ready for another try.

===Chinese===
这一切烟儿本来乐得受用，若不是对方来真的；再说那混血儿已经动开了真火，这对马来说就更不是闹着玩的了。那家伙勒着马头破口大骂、把吃奶的劲儿都骂出来，又把绳子挽成一圈一圈备着再甩一回——他嗓音的那股动静，烟儿恨透了。

===Original===
And in that third throw the breed had fooled Smoky. He’d, swung his rope like as to throw it, but the loop had never left his hands. Smoky had dodged and dodged thinking sure that the rope had came, but it never had, and finally when he’d quit dodging, it did come, and with the speed of a “blue racer” had circled around his neck.

===Chinese===
到第三甩，混血儿把烟儿耍了一道。他把绳抡起来，做出要撒手的架势，绳圈却压根儿没离手。烟儿一躲再躲，认定绳已经飞过来了，其实一回也没有；末了等他不躲了，绳真来了，带着「蓝奔蛇」（blue racer）般的速度，绕上了他的脖子。

===Original===
Smoky had fought like a trapped grizzly as the rope had drawed up, and the breed had to take a few turns around a corral post to hold him.

===Chinese===
绳一勒紧，烟儿就像一头落了陷阱的灰熊那样死命挣扎，混血儿不得不把绳在畜栏桩上多缠几道，才拽得住他。

===Original===
“I’ll fix you now, you﻿—”

===Chinese===
「这会儿看我怎么收拾你，你——」

===Original===
Cussing a blue streak, the breed had broke a limb off the willows that hung over the corral, and coming towards Smoky had been for showing that horse who was boss. He’d went to work, and tried to break the limb over the fighting pony’s head. Orneriness had stuck up in the breed’s gizzard, and anything would be done, even killing the horse right there would of been hunkydory so long as he could ease his feelings some.

===Chinese===
混血儿一串脏话骂得跟连珠炮似的，从畜栏上方垂下来的柳树上撅下一根枝子，朝烟儿走过去，要叫这马瞧瞧谁才是爷。他动了手，抡着那根枝子往死挣的小马头上砸，一心要把枝子砸断。一股子横劲儿顶上了那混血儿的胸口，什么事都干得出来——哪怕当场把马弄死，只要能稍稍出口气，他都算痛快。

===Original===
He’d pounded and pounded till the limb begin to break, and as he’d noticed it give that way he was going to keep on till it did break, but there again, luck had been against him. The rope that’d held Smoky went and separated at the honda and set the horse free.

===Chinese===
他一砸再砸，砸到枝子开始发裂；他瞅出枝子吃不住劲了，正要接着砸、砸到断为止——偏偏在这儿，运气又没站他那边。拴着烟儿的那条绳，在绳扣（honda）那儿说断就断，把马放了。

===Original===
The breed had raved on some more at seeing his victim getting away, and throwed the club after him as the pony staggered back amongst the other ponies; and then somehow realizing that then was no time to fool with ornery horses, the breed had caught another horse.

===Chinese===
眼瞅着到手的猎物脱了身，混血儿又嚎了一通，朝小马背后抡出了那根棍——小马正踉踉跄跄退回马群里去；不知怎么他回过味儿来，明白这会儿不是跟淘气马纠缠的时候，便另逮了一匹马。

===Original===
“I’ll tend to you some more,” he hollered at Smoky, and getting on the other horse he’d let the bunch out and started ’em on the trail.

===Chinese===
「回头再慢慢收拾你。」他冲烟儿吼了一嗓子，骑上另一匹马，放出马群，赶着上了道。

===Original===
Two hundred miles of that trail was covered, and in the time it took to cover that distance, Smoky had fed on hate for the breed till that hate growed to a disease. Killing the breed would be all that could cure it. Every blow that human had pounded on his head that day, a couple of weeks past, had left a scar, a scar that healed on the surface, but which went to his heart instead, spread there, and stayed raw.

===Chinese===
那条道走掉了两百英里；走完这段路的工夫里，烟儿拿对那混血儿的恨当料喂自己，喂到这恨长成了病。治这病的法子只有一个：宰了那混血儿。几个礼拜前那天，那人砸在他头上的每一棍，都留下一道疤——疤在皮上愈合了，内里却径直进了他的心，在那儿漫开，烂着，不肯结痂。

===Original===
Then one day, on the edge of a big desert flat and amongst the junipers, the breed spotted a high, strong, corral. A log cabin with smoke coming out of the chimney was off to one side a ways, and standing in the door was a man, the first man the breed had seen since starting out with the stolen horses. But he felt safe, five hundred miles had been covered, the brands on the horses had all been “picked”3 and besides, as he figgered, it’d be a good place to stop a while and recuperate; and as he seen the place was a cow camp, he thought maybe he could get the cowboy to help him some with that mouse colored horse he was still wanting to “tend” to and packing a grudge against.

===Chinese===
后来有一天，在一片大沙漠滩的边上、杜松（juniper）林子中间，混血儿望见一座又高又结实的畜栏。一旁不远有座圆木屋，烟囱里冒着烟，门口站着个人——这是混血儿带着偷来的马上路以来见着的头一个人。可他心里踏实：五百英里都走下来了，马身上的烙印也都已经「剪」3过；再说，照他盘算，在这儿歇一阵子、养养精神倒不错；他又瞧出这地方是个牧牛营子，心说没准儿还能拉那牛仔搭把手，帮着对付那匹鼠灰色马——那匹他一直惦着要「收拾」、憋着仇没报的马。

===Original===
The cowboy wasn’t much for the breed the minute that hombre rode up, but as company was scarce, he kinda stood him, and even agreed to help him with the horse.

===Chinese===
那家伙刚骑到跟前，牛仔就对他没多大好感；可这荒天野地伙伴难寻，他多少容得下他，居然还答应了帮他弄那匹马。

===Original===
Smoky watched the two walk in the corral the next day, and knowed something was up. His ears layed back at the sight of the breed and hate showed from every part of him;﻿—he was ready to fight, and if anything he was glad of the chance.

===Chinese===
第二天，烟儿瞧着那两个人走进畜栏，就知道要出事。一看见那混血儿，他的耳朵唰地贴了回去，浑身上下处处冒出恨来；——他早预备好斗一场了，不但预备好，简直巴不得有这么个机会。

===Original===
But Smoky had no chance, too many ropes settled on him at once, and the first thing he knowed, he was flat on his side and tied down before he could use either hoof or teeth.

===Chinese===
可烟儿没捞着机会，一下子太多条绳落到了他身上，他还没明白过来，已经侧躺在地、捆了个结实，蹄子和牙齿一样都没来得及使上。

===Original===
The horse was no more than down and helpless, when the breed, seeing his victim within reach and where he couldn’t get away, begin to get rid of what’d been on his chest for so long, and when Smoky even though tied down, reached over and near pulled the shirt off of him with his teeth, was when the breed figgered he had an excuse to beat that horse to a pulp even though the horse had no chance.

===Chinese===
马刚一倒地动弹不得，那混血儿眼见仇家够得着、又跑不掉，便开始清倒他在心里窝了这么久的东西；烟儿虽然叫人捆着，还侧过头去，一口差点儿把他衬衫撕了下来——就冲这个，混血儿认定自己有了由头，能把这马捣成肉泥，哪怕这马半点还手的份儿都没有。

===Original===
The cowboy, not understanding the breed’s tactics for a spell, stood off a ways, and watched. There was all about the horse to show that he’d been right in his first dislike for the dark faced hombre. At first he was for interfering and shove the club the breed was using right down his throat. Then as he noticed how the pony would like to do the damaging instead, he thought of a better way and walked up.

===Chinese===
牛仔起先没瞧懂混血儿的路数，站在一边瞅了一阵。这马的种种光景，处处证明他头一眼讨厌这张黑脸的家伙没讨厌错。起先他打算出手管管，把混血儿手里那根棍子顺着嗓子眼儿给他捅下去；后来瞅出这小马自己更想亲手出这口气，他想起一个更好的法子，便走上前去。

===Original===
“Listen, feller,” he says to the breed, “what’s the use of beating a horse up that way. Why don’t you give him a chance and try to do it *while you’re setting on him*?”

===Chinese===
「听着，伙计，」他对混血儿说，「那么往死里揍一匹马，顶什么用？你干吗不给他个机会，*骑在他背上*再试试？」

===Original===
“Maybe you think I can’t do it,” says that hombre, bleary-eyed and mad clear thru.

===Chinese===
「怎么，你当我弄不服他？」那家伙说，一双眼熬得通红，从里到外都是火。

===Original===
The scheme had worked fine﻿—the cowboy grinned to himself as he helped the breed put the saddle on Smoky. Once he’d got a little too close to that pony’s head while helping that way, and that horse come within an inch of getting his arm, the cowboy overlooked it, and to himself remarked: “the poor devil had sure got a reason to be mean, and I guess he’s at the point where he figgers no human is his friend any more.”

===Chinese===
这条计策灵得很——牛仔帮着混血儿往烟儿背上放鞍子，心里暗自好笑。搭手的时候，他有一回凑得离那小马的脑袋近了点儿，那马一口下来，离他胳膊就差一英寸；牛仔没计较，心里只念叨：「这可怜鬼也真有凶的道理，瞧这架势，他八成认定人里头再也没有他的朋友了。」

===Original===
The cowboy was right, anything on two legs, whether it was the breed or any other human, had sure enough got to be Smoky’s enemy﻿—a crethure to scatter into dust and put out of the way whenever a chance showed up.

===Chinese===
牛仔说得对：凡是两条腿走道的，管他是那混血儿还是别的什么人，如今都实实在在成了烟儿的仇敌——一桩该碾成齑粉、一有机会就该除掉的怪家伙。

===Original===
The saddle was cinched on, and while the breed was getting as much of the seat under him as he could, the cowboy took off the foot ropes, and soon as the last coil was pulled away, he made long steps for the highest part of the corral and where he could watch everything to *his heart’s content*.

===Chinese===
肚带勒紧了，混血儿往鞍座上尽量多安顿他的坐处，牛仔解开了捆腿的绳；最后一圈绳一抽走，他就迈开大步直奔畜栏最高的那头——在那儿，他可以*看个心满意足*。

===Original===
The cowboy had no more than reached the top pole of the corral when a sudden commotion, which sounded like a landslide, made him turn. Smoky had come up, and at last given a chance had more than started to make use of it. It was his turn to do some pounding, and he done it with the saddle that was on his back and which went with every crooked and hard hitting jump he made.

===Chinese===
牛仔才刚扒上畜栏顶上的横杆，身后一声大乱，动静听着像塌方，叫他赶紧回头。烟儿起来了，盼了这么久的一个机会到手，他岂止是抓住不放。这回轮到他动手砸了，砸的家什就是背上那副鞍子——他每尥一个又歪又狠的蹶子，那副鞍子就跟着砸一下。

===Original===
The breed had rode many hard horses and he was a good rider, but he soon found that Smoky was a harder horse to set than any he’d ever rode before; and as good a rider as he was there was many a twist brought in that he couldn’t keep track of. They kept a coming too fast, and it wasn’t long when he begin to feel that setting in that saddle on such a horse was no place for him. The saddle horn and cantle was taking turns and hitting him from all sides, till he didn’t know which way he was setting. Pretty soon he lost both stirrups, and once as he was a hanging over to one side, one of them stirrups came up and hit him between the eyes. That finished him; he hit the ground like a ton of lead.

===Chinese===
混血儿骑过多少烈马，骑术也确实有两下子，可他很快发现，烟儿比他骑过的哪一匹都难坐；他骑术再好，这马使出来的弯弯绕也多得他数不过来。而且一招赶着一招，快得叫人喘不过气；没多久他就觉出来了——这匹马背上的这副鞍子里，不是他待的地方。鞍角和后鞍桥轮着班儿、换着方向地撞他，撞得他都闹不清自己坐的是哪个方向。不多时，两只马镫全丢了个干净；有一回他正悬在一边，一只马镫荡上来，正磕在他两眼中间。这下他算完了；他像一吨铅似的砸在了地上。

===Original===
The cowboy up on top of the corral had laughed and enjoyed the performance all the way thru; and when the breed dug his nose in the dust of the corral he laughed all the more. He’d never been more agreeable to seeing a man get “busted” in his life.

===Chinese===
畜栏顶上的牛仔从头到尾笑着、受用着这场表演；等混血儿一头扎进畜栏的浮土里，他笑得更欢了。活了这辈子，看人「摔趴下」，就没有比这回更叫他痛快的。

===Original===
The breed layed in a heap, never moving; and then the cowboy, finally getting serious, was for getting him out of there before the horse spotted him, and reduced him into thin air. Somehow, he wasn’t caring to see a human get tore apart and right before his eyes that way even if that human did deserve killing; but Smoky’s interest was all for shedding the saddle right then and all that carried the breed’s smell; finally it begin to slip;﻿—higher and higher on his withers it went till the high point was reached, and then it started going down. When it reached the ground the hackamore had come off with it, and before Smoky, slick and clean, straightened up again, the breed had picked himself up, and without the help of the cowboy, sneaked out of the corral.

===Chinese===
混血儿堆作一团，一动不动；牛仔这才正经起来，琢磨着趁马还没瞧见他，先把他弄出去，免得他被撕扯成一阵青烟。说不好为什么，人叫活活撕开、还当着自己的面这么撕——这景象他就是不想看，哪怕那人真该杀；可烟儿那会儿的心思全在甩掉鞍子上，甩掉一切沾着混血儿气味的东西；鞍子终于开始滑了；——在耆甲上越蹭越高，蹭过了高点，又往下走。等鞍子落到地上，笼头也跟着一齐脱落；还没等浑身精光、利利索索的烟儿重新站直，混血儿已经自己爬了起来，不用牛仔搭手，溜出了畜栏。

===Original===
The next few minutes was used by that cowboy in telling the breed to get another horse saddled and hit the trail while the hitting was good, and helping him getting his horses together, boosted him out of camp.﻿—But the breed wasn’t thru with Smoky, he was going to “tend to him” again some other time.

===Chinese===
接下来那几分钟，牛仔全用来打发混血儿：叫他赶紧另备一匹马，趁走得脱赶紧上路；又帮他把马拢齐，把他轰出了营子。——可混血儿跟烟儿这笔账没完，他还要挑个日子再「收拾」他一回。

===Original===
Months had went by before that other time come, and it’d been away late in the next fall before that hombre ever put his hands on Smoky again. In that time the other ponies, which all had seemed inclined to behave, had been sold. Smoky had been kept in the corral, treated with a club regular, and fed “post hay,” till, as the breed figgered, he’d break that pony’s spirit, or break his neck; but he was going to *make* him behave some way, so as he’d get the price he’d be asking for him.

===Chinese===
那个日子又隔了好几个月才来；等那家伙重新把手搭上烟儿，已经是第二年深秋了。这中间，群里旁的小马——匹匹瞧着都服帖——都陆续卖掉了。烟儿被单独圈在畜栏里，棍子按时按晌地伺候，吃的是「木桩草」（post hay）；照混血儿的盘算，这么熬下去，要么熬断这小马的烈性，要么熬断他的脖子；反正他横下心要*叫*他服帖，服帖到他开的价码能拿到手为止。

===Original===
Then one night a high March wind had sprung up, rattled the corral gate, and finally worked it open. Smoky hadn’t been long in seeing the opening, and when a few days later the breed, hunting for the horse, spotted him, the mouse colored gelding had took up with the wild bunch, and only a glimpse of him did he get.

===Chinese===
后来一天夜里，一场三月的大风起了，摇得畜栏门哐啷哐啷响，末了硬给摇开了。烟儿没多大工夫就瞧见了那个口子；几天以后，混血儿出来寻马，远远望见他时，那匹鼠灰色骟马已经入伙了野马群，他也只捞着瞧了个影儿。

===Original===
Every once in a while that whole summer the breed had tried cutting Smoky out of the wild bunch and run him in, but that pony had been harder to get near than any of the wild ones he was with. He knowed what was on the program for him if that breed ever caught him again. The steady beatings he’d got from him had made his hate grow for the human till a striking rattlesnake looked like a friend in comparing.

===Chinese===
那一夏天，混血儿隔三差五就想把烟儿从野马群里剔出来往回赶，可这小马比群里哪匹野马都难近身。那混血儿要是再逮住他，节目单上给他排的是什么，他心里有数。那一顿接一顿的毒打，把他对人的恨喂得越长越大，比起来，一条昂着头要咬人的响尾蛇都算得上朋友了。

===Original===
But the breed hadn’t been for quitting﻿—he couldn’t stand to have anything get the best of him, not even an ornery pony; and as Smoky enjoyed his wild freedom them summer months, the breed had kept a studying which circle Smoky and the wild ones would take whenever they was being chased, and getting a good lay of the land he finally figgered a plan.

===Chinese===
可混血儿不肯撒手——他受不了让任何东西占他的上风，连一匹淘气马也不行；那几个月里，烟儿自在地撒着野，混血儿却一直在琢磨：每回被追，烟儿和野马群会兜哪个圈子？他把这一带的地势摸了个透，末了，盘算出一条计来。

===Original===
And, that’s how come, when he started out after Smoky again in the fall he knowed just where to place a relay string of ponies. At the other end was a trap corral and well hid. Then the breed spotted the horse late one afternoon, and fell in behind him and the other wild ones he was with. It had been a long chase; the wild ones had dropped out of the run one by one and branched to one side, but Smoky and the rest of the strongest had kept on right along on the trail where the breed had stationed his fresh relay horses. Finally, and as the breed kept a coming in on ’em with fresh horses, the strongest of the mustangs kept a branching out; but Smoky had kept on straight ahead, till, leg weary and staggering, he’d found himself in the wings of the trap corral, and then inside, past being able to see the grinning halfbreed who’d closed the gate on him.

===Chinese===
于是，等到那年秋天他再出动去追烟儿，换乘班的马该摆在哪儿，他早已门儿清。另一头，是一座藏得严严实实的陷阱畜栏（trap corral）。一天后半晌，混血儿远远盯上了那匹马，缀上了他和他一起的野马。那是一场长追；野马一匹接一匹退出了狂奔，拐向一旁，可烟儿和几匹最壮实的，一直顺着那条道跑下去——道上正是混血儿摆好的新换乘马。到后来，混血儿骑着新换乘的马步步逼近，最强健的那几匹野马也一匹匹散了拐开；唯独烟儿照直往前，跑到最后，两腿又乏又晃，一抬头已经站在陷阱畜栏的两翼里，跟着就进了栏——人已经木然，连那关上栏门、咧嘴而笑的混血儿都顾不上看了。

---

			

===Original===
A few days went by when Smoky seemed in a trance. He remembered some of being led and jerked all the way back to the breed’s hangout, of being saddled the next day and jerked around some more, and then rode out, and with spur and quirt, made to trot around. He didn’t realize the breed had set on him or he didn’t seem to care. The little hay that was throwed out to him wasn’t noticed, and hardly did he drink﻿—only if by chance he happened to mope around the corral and find himself standing in the stream that was running in one side of it.

===Chinese===
有好几天，烟儿像失了魂。他还记得一点儿：被牵着、拽着，一路弄回混血儿的老巢；第二天被备上鞍，又拽来拽去，跟着被骑出去，马刺加短马鞭（quirt），逼着他转着圈小跑。混血儿骑没骑在他背上，他没觉出来，或者根本不在乎。扔给他的那点儿草，他看也不看，水也几乎不喝——只有偶尔闷着头在畜栏里遛弯，恰巧站进从栏边流过的那条小溪里的时候。

===Original===
There was everything about the horse to indicate that in a few more days he’d be laying down, never to get up no more; his trail seemed fast coming to an end, and the heart that was left in him had shrunk till nary a beat of it could be felt. The breed kept a riding him out, thinking he at last and for sure had the horse right where he wanted him.

===Chinese===
这浑身上下的光景都表明：再过几天，他就要躺下去，再也起不来了；他的路眼看到了头，他里头剩下的那颗心缩啊缩啊，缩到一星跳动都摸不出来。混血儿还是天天把他骑出去，自以为这一回总算、笃定，把马攥进了他想要的手心里。

===Original===
“I’ll make a good horse out of you, you scrub,” he’d say as he’d beat him over the head with his quirt and at the same time cut him with the spur. Smoky had seemed to feel neither the quirt nor the spur. He didn’t flinch nor even bat an eye as both would come down on him and leave the marks. There seemed to be no sign of hopes or life left in the horse, and the abuse went on till, finally and one day, the breed happened to cut the horse a little deeper and in a more sensitive place.

===Chinese===
「我能把你调教成一匹好马，你这匹下等货。」他一边拿短马鞭抽马头，一边拿马刺划马身，嘴里这么念。鞭子和马刺落在烟儿身上，他像是哪样都觉不着。两样家伙一齐往下落、落出一道道印子，他既不缩、也不眨一下眼。这马身上，指望和生气像是都没了影儿；糟践就这么一天天下去，直到末了一天，混血儿失手划深了些，正划在一处更金贵的皮肉上。

===Original===
That cut had stirred the pony’s shrunk up heart, and a faint spark had showed in his eyes for a second. The next day Smoky even snorted a little as the breed walked into the corral, and he tried to buck some as he climbed into the saddle. The breed was surprised at the new show of spirit, and remarked as he took down his quirt:

===Chinese===
那一刺，把小马缩成一团的心搅动了，他眼里闪过一星微弱的火星。第二天，混血儿走进畜栏时，烟儿居然冲他打了个响鼻；他爬上鞍的时候，烟儿还试着尥了两下。这股新冒出来的烈性叫混血儿吃了一惊，他摘下短马鞭，撂下一句：

===Original===
“I’ll take *that* out of you.”

===Chinese===
「*这股劲儿*，我非从你身上打出去不可。」

===Original===
From that day on Smoky’s heart begin to expand towards natural size once more. But it wasn’t the same kind of heart that had once been his﻿—that first one had died, and this one had took root from abuse, growed from rough treatment to full size, and with hankerings in it only for finding and destroying all that wasn’t to his liking. And there was nothing to his liking no more.

===Chinese===
打那天起，烟儿的心重新往原本的大小长。可这已不是从前那颗了——原先那颗死了；这一颗是从糟践里扎的根，靠虐待喂到了足个头，里头装的念想只剩一个：找出一切不入他眼的，毁掉。而如今，再没有什么入他的眼了。

===Original===
The breed he hated more than anything in the world, but Smoky, with that new heart of his, wasn’t for showing them feelings much. He’d got wise in ways of how and when to do his fighting, and where it’d do most good;﻿—he’d wait for a chance. In the meantime he’d got to eating every stem of what little hay the breed would hand him; he’d have to live to carry out them new ambitions of his.

===Chinese===
天底下他顶恨的就是那混血儿；可烟儿揣着他那颗新心，不再肯把这些心思挂在明面上。怎么斗、什么时候斗、在哪儿斗最见效，他都学精了；——他等机会。这期间，混血儿给的那点儿草，他一梗一梗全吃了个净；他得活着，活着才办得成他那些新的心愿。

===Original===
But somehow, a hint of Smoky’s new ambitions must of leaked out; anyway the breed had a hunch that it wouldn’t be well for him to come too close to that pony’s teeth and hoofs. He’d often watch him thru the corral poles and wonder. He’d sometimes wonder if it wouldn’t be best to just place a forty-five slug between that pony’s ears instead of fooling with him, but the hopes of still being able to sell the horse for a good price would always keep him from drawing his gun.

===Chinese===
可不知怎么，烟儿那些新心愿的风声，多半还是漏了出去；反正混血儿起了疑，觉着离那小马的牙口和蹄子太近了不是好事。他常隔着畜栏的木杆端详他，直犯嘀咕。他有时候也嘀咕：与其这么费劲纠缠，倒不如干脆往那对小马耳朵中间送一颗四五口径的枪子儿（forty-five slug）；可指望着这马还能卖个好价，回回都把他的枪摁在了套里。

===Original===
“A good long ride’ll fix you,” says the breed one morning as he drug his saddle near the corral chute. “And I’ve got a hell of a long one ahead for you today.”

===Chinese===
「好好跑一趟长路，就治好了你这毛病。」一天早上，混血儿把鞍子拖到畜栏的保定栏（chute）跟前，说，「今儿个给你备下的这一趟，长得要命。」

===Original===
Smoky was prodded into the chute with a long pole, and saddled where he couldn’t move. Then the breed climbed in the saddle, opened the chute gate and started the horse out on a long run.

===Chinese===
一根长杆把烟儿戳进保定栏，在那儿他动弹不得，由人备好了鞍。随后混血儿跨上鞍座，拉开栏门，催着马撒开一个长程的飞奔。

===Original===
Ten miles of country was covered which Smoky didn’t see; his instinct made him dodge badger holes and jump washouts, and his eyes and ears was steady back and on the human he was packing, if he could only reach with his teeth and get him down.

===Chinese===
十英里的乡野跑过去了，烟儿一眼都没瞧见；本能替他躲开獾洞、跳过冲垮的沟坎，他自己的眼睛和耳朵却死死朝后别着，钉在背上驮的那个人身上——哪怕够得着一口、把那人拽下马来也好。

===Original===
The breed’s spurs kept a gouging him, and along with the quirt a pounding, Smoky was kept into a high lope. With that kind of tattoo being played on him the pony gradually begin to warm up and getting peeved. It wouldn’t be long, if that gait was kept up, when he’d be reaching the boiling point, and then get desperate.

===Chinese===
混血儿的马刺不停地剜他，短马鞭跟着抽，烟儿被钉死在大跑上。这么一套敲敲打打落在身上，小马一点点烧热起来、上了火。照这个步子跑下去，用不了多久就该烧到滚点，到那时候，可就要不管不顾了。

===Original===
A steep bank was reached by the edge of a creek, and there Smoky sorta hesitated a second. His ears and eyes was pointed ahead for that second and looking for a place where the going down wouldn’t be so sudden; when the breed, always looking for some reason to deal the horse misery, put the steel and layed the quirt to him at once. That took Smoky by surprise, and the flame that’d been smoldering in his heart loomed up into a active volcano all at once.

===Chinese===
到了溪边一道陡坎前，烟儿微微迟疑了一秒。那一秒里，他耳朵和眼睛都朝前指着，找一处下坡不这么陡的地方；偏偏那混血儿从不放过任何叫这马遭罪的机会，当即马刺一催、鞭子一抡。烟儿猝不及防，心里闷烧了这许久的那团火，轰地一下，蹿成了一座活火山。

===Original===
Down over the bank he went, and when he landed he had his head between his front legs and went to bucking from there. By some miracle the breed stuck him for half a dozen jumps, then he made a circle in the air and landed on all fours at the foot of the bank.

===Chinese===
他顺坡冲了下去，四蹄一落地，脑袋已经收进两条前腿当中，就地尥起了蹶子。混血儿鬼使神差扛了六七个回合，跟着在空中划了半个圈，四脚着地，摔在了坡根底下。

===Original===
A shadow on the ground and right by him made the breed reach for his gun near as quick as he landed; it was the shadow of the horse and *too close*; his gun was out of the holster and he turned to use it; but he was just the splinter of a second too late, and the six-shooter was buried in the ground as Smoky, like a big cougar, pounced on him.

===Chinese===
身旁地上一道影子，落地的混血儿几乎是本能地就去摸枪；那是马的影子，而且*贴得太近*了；枪出了套，他拧身要打；可就差那么一丝丝，来不及了——烟儿像一头大美洲狮扑了上去，那支六响枪（six-shooter）深深插进了土里。

## XII / 十二

===Original===
“When the Good Leaves”

===Chinese===
当好日子离去时

===Original===
Big posters was tacked on the telegraph poles all around the little town of Gramah. Them posters could be seen in many windows of the town’s stores, and advertised the coming rodeo and cowboy’s reunion. Amongst the prizes that was wrote down on the poster was prints from photographs of bucking horses and steers, and taking most of the room in the centre of it was the picture of a bucking horse which outdone all the others. It showed that horse throwing his riders in a way few riders ever get throwed. Then in big letters underneath was the words: **The Cougar Challenges the World’s Best.**

===Chinese===
大张的海报，钉满了格拉玛（Gramah）小镇四下的电线杆，镇上许多店铺的橱窗里也贴得见着，为即将开办的牛仔竞技会（rodeo）和牛仔联谊会张目。海报上开列的各色奖品里，有尥蹶子马和阉牛的照片印片，而正当中最占地方的一幅，是一匹盖过其余所有的尥蹶子马：画上那马正把骑手往出摔，那摔法，少有骑手这么被摔过。底下一行大字：**美洲狮（The Cougar）向天下好手叫阵。**

===Original===
The Cougar was the name of a bucking horse, the main attraction, and challenger to all the good riders of the country. No line was drawed as to where them riders came from or how far, and the purse that was offered for the one who could ride that horse and scratch him was enough to make any good rider want to come a long way and try.

===Chinese===
「美洲狮」是这匹尥蹶子马的名字——头牌台柱，向全国好骑手叫板的挑战者。骑手打哪儿来、来多远，一概不划界；谁若能骑住那马、降服他，赏金开得足足，足以叫任何好手心甘情愿跋涉远路来试一回。

===Original===
Many had come and tried him at other rodeos and where The Cougar had performed, and found that that pony was no ordinary bucking horse; and as all that tried him could tell, afterwards, there was more than his bucking to contend with. He was mean, there was murder in his eye, and if it wasn’t for the “pickup” men who hazed him, many a cowboy would of been pawed to pieces even before he could of hit the ground.

===Chinese===
许多人在别的竞技会上、在「美洲狮」登过场的各处试过他，发现那小马绝非寻常的尥蹶子马；凡试过他的，事后都能作证：要对付的还不止他那手跳。他凶，眼里带着杀气，若不是有「接应骑手（pickup men）」在一旁轰赶，多少牛仔不等那人落地，就该叫他拿蹄子刨成碎片了。

===Original===
That pony seemed to have a grudge against humans in general; his ambition was for exterminating ’em all off the face of the earth. But there was one thing which the riders noticed in him as most queer, and that was in the way he seemed to hate some humans worse than others﻿—his hate was plainest for the face that showed dark.

===Chinese===
那小马像是跟整个人类结着仇，抱负就是把人类从地面上斩尽杀绝。可有一样顶古怪，骑手们都瞧出来了：他恨某些人，好像恨得比恨旁人更狠——对晒得黑黑的脸膛，他的恨最藏不住。

===Original===
A story followed the horse, and which kept a being repeated as rider met rider at different rodeos and frontier day celebrations. It was that the horse had been found on the desert, amongst a bunch of wild horses and packing an empty saddle. There’d been dried blood sticking to the hair along his jaw, and some more on his knees; the horse had been roped and tied down and the riders had looked for signs of wounds or cuts on his hide but nary a scratch had been found.

===Chinese===
一桩传闻跟着这马走，骑手遇上骑手，在各地竞技会和边疆节上翻来覆去地讲。说是这马是在沙漠上寻见的，混在一群野马里，背上驮着一副空鞍。他下颔的毛上粘着干血，膝盖上还有几处；马被套住按倒之后，骑手们在他皮毛上寻伤口割痕，却连一道擦痕也没寻见。

===Original===
The horse was then advertised in the county and State papers and described as “A mouse colored, blaze-faced, stocking legged gelding, and packing a brand that looked like a blotched wagon wheel.”  The advertisement was kept running for two weeks and nobody showed to claim the horse. He was kept in the pasture for a few days more, and then one day one of the riders run him in the corral.

===Chinese===
跟着，县报和州报上登了招领，形容他「鼠灰色、白章脸、四蹄穿白袜的骟马，驮着一块像糊过的车轮烙印」。启事一连登了两个礼拜，没人来认领。他又在草场上多圈了几天，后来一天，一个骑手把他赶进了畜栏。

===Original===
The cowboy had liked the looks of the pony from the day he’d set eyes on him; he’d figgered him as an ordinary horse that’d been spoiled a little, and shaking out a loop, there’d been no doubt in his mind but what that could be took out of him easy enough. But he hadn’t got very far when he found that the pony would have to be throwed before a saddle could ever be put on his back. There was a look in the horse’s eye which he didn’t like, and that cowboy having handled all kinds of horses knowed mighty well what that look meant.

===Chinese===
那牛仔打头一眼瞧上这小马，就喜欢他的品相；把他估成匹寻常马，不过惯坏了一星半点，心里笃定，甩开索套收拾收拾，这点毛病轻轻松松就能给他去掉。可他没走出多远就明白：这小马得先摔翻在地，鞍子才谈得上往他背上搁。马眼睛里有种神色，他不喜欢；而这牛仔什么马没调理过，那神色意味着什么，他知道得太清楚。

===Original===
He kept his distance, and from there worked his ropes till the horse went down to his knees and then flat to the ground. The saddle was cinched on tight, and seeing that the hackamore was on the pony’s head to stay, the cowboy took his seat while the horse was down, and reaching over took the foot ropes off.

===Chinese===
他守住距离，从那儿一趟趟做他的绳活，直把马折腾得跪下前膝、放平在地。鞍子勒紧了肚带，眼见笼头稳稳当当待在小马头上，牛仔趁马卧地，跨坐上去，探身解了捆腿的绳。

===Original===
What went on in the next few minutes was past ever being described with talk, and as that cowboy felt, telling about it would be a disgrace as compared with what really happened﻿—something like trying to paint the Grand Canyon of Arizona on black canvas with black paint.

===Chinese===
接下来那几分钟里的事，远不是嘴能描画的；照那牛仔的感受，讲出来反倒糟蹋了实情——就好比想拿黑颜料在黑画布上画亚利桑那大峡谷。

===Original===
Anyway, that cowboy had reached for the top pole of the corral and got on the other side of it before the pony had really got started to whatever he was up to; and there on the safe side he done a mental roundup, and it all came to him. He remembered the empty saddle that was on the pony’s back when found that day two weeks past﻿—then the dried blood that’d been on his jaw and more of it on his knees.

===Chinese===
反正，不等小马真个施展开他要干的勾当，那牛仔已经够着畜栏顶上的横杆，翻到栏杆另一头去了；在安全的那一侧，他心里默默打了一回「围拢」，把一切拢到了一块儿。他想起两个礼拜前寻见这马那天，马背上驮的那副空鞍——还有下颔上粘的干血、膝盖上更多的血。

===Original===
The cowboy had remarked as thru the corral poles he’d watched the man killer:

===Chinese===
那牛仔隔着畜栏横杆，瞅着那匹杀人马，撂下过这么一句：

===Original===
“A twelve hundred pound mountain lion is what that horse is.”

===Chinese===
「这马啊，就是一头一千二百磅重的山狮。」

===Original===
That’s where his name Cougar had come in, and no horse never lived up to a name like the mouse colored gelding did to his.

===Chinese===
「美洲狮」这名字就是这么落下的；而天底下，再没哪匹马把一个名字活得像这匹鼠灰色骟马这般名副其实。

===Original===
Then had come rumors of a Fourth of July celebration which was going to be pulled off in some big town to the south; there was to be bronc riding and everything that went with it. A prize of a hundred dollars had been offered for the best bucking horse, and that’s how come one day that The Cougar made his first appearance before a grandstand. A warning was given to the “pickup” man and “hazers” to be on hand and watch out nobody got hurt, and them few words of warning that way had proved to sound mighty right before that day was over.

===Chinese===
跟着又起了风声，说南边哪座大镇要办「七月四日」庆祝会，有骑烈马，还有连带的各色名堂。最优尥蹶子马悬了一百美元的奖，于是有一天，「美洲狮」头一回在看台（grandstand）前亮了相。「接应骑手」和「驱赶手（hazers）」都得了叮嘱：随时候着，留神别叫人受了伤；那天还没过完，那几句警语就应验得不能再准了。

===Original===
The Cougar had been *tried* out, and then a hundred dollars was handed to the rider who’d brought him in. He’d won the prize. There was no doubt in anybody’s mind but what that pony was by a long ways the meanest and hardest horse to ride there, and not only there, but anywhere else and wherever hard bucking horses was rode. Fifty dollars additional was offered for the right to keep the horse for rodeo purposes. That was refused, and when the last day of the doings come, and the riders came up for the “finals,” another fifty was added to the first offer, and accepted. A bill of sale was made out, and The Cougar from that day on was drove from stockyard to stock car and from arena to arena.

===Chinese===
「美洲狮」被*试用*过一回，随后一百美元便交到了把他牵来的骑手手里。他夺得了那份奖。人人心里都毫不怀疑：这小马不光是当场头一号凶、头一号难骑，放眼别处、凡是骑烈性尥蹶子马的地方，也再找不出第二匹。又有人添了五十美元，要买下这马供竞技会使唤的权利。人家没答应；待到活动末日、骑手们上场争「决赛」，头一笔出价上又加五十，这才点头。买卖文书写定，打那天起，「美洲狮」便从牲畜围场被赶进运畜车厢，从一个竞技场转场到又一个竞技场。

===Original===
In front of the crowded grandstand is where his fame as a fighting, man-hating, bucking outlaw begin to spread, and from State to State, town and range folks alike was on hand and whenever he was to be rode and handled; for watching that horse perform was alone worth more than the price that was asked for the ticket at the gate of the rodeo grounds.

===Chinese===
就在人山人海的看台前，他作为一匹好斗、恨人、尥蹶子的劣马的名声传开了；一州又一州，城里人、牧场上人，凡轮到他被人骑、被人摆布，总要赶去到场；因为单瞧这马一场表演，就抵得上竞技场门口那点票钱还有富余。

===Original===
It wasn’t long when the folks thru the whole of the southwestern states begin to talk of The Cougar as they did of their favorite movie actor, actress, or the Prince of Wales. Tourists from Europe and from all parts of the U.S. came and went, and carried stories with ’em about the wonders of the wickedness of that horse. Then rodeo committees begin to perk up their ears, and at the same time started bidding for him. The Cougar’s presence got to be valuable, and came a time when five hundred dollars was offered by a rival who also made a business of furnishing rodeos with strings of bucking stock. The offer wasn’t considered, none at all, and the riders around had their doubts if even a thousand would change the ownership of that horse.

===Chinese===
没多久，整个西南各州的百姓提起「美洲狮」，就像提他们心爱的电影男角女角，或者威尔士亲王。欧洲来的、美国各地来的游客来了又走，随身带走这马那份恶名的种种奇谈。竞技会的主事们竖起了耳朵，跟着便竞相抬价。「美洲狮」的到场身价看涨，到后来，一位同行开出五百美元——那人也做着一门营生，专给竞技会供应成批的尥蹶子牲口。这价压根儿没被考虑，周遭的骑手们心里都犯嘀咕：只怕一千块，也换不走这匹马的主人。

===Original===
Every summer thru, the mouse colored outlaw was shipped along with the others more or less of his kind and unloaded at some different rodeo grounds; every few weeks and for three or four days he was *rode at*. Twice or three times a day during the doings, some strange rider would climb him, the chute gate would fly open, and out would come a tearing, bellering hunk of steel coils to land out a ways, and like a ton of lava from up above, jar the earth even up to the grandstand.

===Chinese===
一年年整个夏天，这匹鼠灰色劣马都跟旁那些多多少少同类的马一道装运出去，在不同的竞技场卸下；每隔几个礼拜，他就有一连三四天*被人当靶子骑*。会期里一天两三回，总有生面孔骑手爬上他背，出场栏（chute）栏门砰地弹开，冲出来一团嘶吼着、拧成弹簧的钢铁疙瘩，窜出老远才落地，像从天顶上砸下一吨熔岩，震得大地连看台一齐发颤。

===Original===
The judges, pickup men, and others around would find themselves short about ten pairs of eyes as all tried to catch every crooked move that pony put into his work. All breaths seemed to be held up during that time; but never no time was them breaths held up for very long, cause, very soon, there’d be a scattering of a tall cowboy who, from the chute, had started on top, took a lot of wicked jars while setting there and so high; and good rider as he’d have to be, soon come to conclude that it sure was no disgrace to be separated from his saddle and flung out a ways﻿—not on that horse.

===Chinese===
裁判、接应骑手和周围一干人，总恨不能多长十双眼，好把那小马活计里每一手阴招都盯全。那时节，人人像是都屏住了气；可这气从来没屏多久，因为转眼间，就有个高个子牛仔四下里飞散出去——他从出场栏出来时人还端在马背上，坐在那高处挨了一顿狠颠；凭他非好手不可的身份，也很快悟了：跟鞍子分家、被甩出老远，实在算不得丢人——在那匹马背上，不丢人。

===Original===
Very seldom would the rider have to walk back very far, and sometimes only a few feet was between the rider who was picking himself up and the chute where he’d rode out from so fast and furious.

===Chinese===
骑手很少要往回走多远；有时候，正从地上爬起来的骑手，跟他方才风驰电掣冲出来的那道出场栏之间，不过几步远。

===Original===
As an all around outlaw and bucking horse The Cougar had no rival; there wasn’t a horse in the State or any State neighboring that could compete with him in either fighting or bucking, and folks seeing or studying the horse often wondered; for anybody who knowed horses could see that that horse hadn’t been born a natural outlaw like most of the rodeo’s bucking horses generally are. That pony had brains, a big supply of ’em and which showed in the way he’d go about throwing his man. He wasn’t like the average bucking horse, who’d often buck back under the man that was already loosened; and instead, when The Cougar felt a man lose an inch, that inch was never got back. The saddle kept a getting away from him from then on.

===Chinese===
论全能的劣马和尥蹶子马，「美洲狮」没有对手；本州的、邻州的，没有一匹马能在斗或者跳上跟他较量。看马的、琢磨马的人常常犯疑；因为懂马的都看得出，这马并不像竞技会上多数尥蹶子马那样，生来就是天生的劣马。那小马有脑子，存量还大，就显在他收拾背上人那一套路数上。他不像寻常的尥蹶子马——那种马往往在骑手已经松了跨时，偏又往人底下跳回来；「美洲狮」不然，他觉出一个人丢了一寸，那一寸就再也讨不回来。打那以后，鞍子便一路朝离开他脊背的方向滑去，再也没回来。

===Original===
But there was more and which was all proof as to the amount of brains that pony carried. There was his hate for the man, and which showed the same as the hate one human would have for another, only it was more dangerous. And then again, and as the cowboy who took care of him often remarked:﻿—

===Chinese===
可还不止这些，桩桩件件，都是那小马脑力的明证。还有他对人的恨；那恨跟人对人的恨一个样，只是更危险。再说一层，照管他的那个牛仔就常念叨——

===Original===
“The way that horse packs a grudge, somebody sure must of dealt him a dirty deal sometime or other. I know there’s sure something on his mind besides that too, and like he’s pining for something that’s gone and hopeless; at them times he acts like he wants my company the same as though he was craving for somebody; but them spells don’t last long, and soon he seems to come back to earth and realizing things. Then’s when I’m not within reaching distance no more﻿—but by golly, I sure wish sometimes that horse would like me as well as he hates.”

===Chinese===
「瞧那马揣仇的那份架势，准是谁在什么时候狠狠坑过他一回。我还知道，他心里头除了仇，准还压着别的，像是在苦想一件没了指望、回不来的事；那样的时候，他待我倒像盼着有人做伴，眼巴巴馋着谁来似的；可这阵儿长不了，很快他就像回了魂，认清了眼前。打那时起，我就再不敢站进他够得着的地界——不过老天爷，有时候我真巴望，那马恨我有多深，喜欢我就有多深。」

===Original===
The first two years he put in as The Cougar and bad horse was the most ferocious two years any horse went thru. It was wicked times, not only for the horse, but for all who handled and tried to ride him. There was so much poison in that pony’s heart that the only way he could live was by hating and being hated; he fed on it, and the bars or poles that was between him and whoever he wanted to get at in his fits of wickedness showed signs a plenty of his hankering to murder﻿—the destroying ability of that pony’s teeth and hoofs sure was visible, and convincing.

===Chinese===
他当「美洲狮」、当坏马的头两年，是天下任何马都没经过的最凶狂的两年。那是邪火的时日，不光马受着，凡调理他、想骑他的也一样受着。那小马心里的毒，浓到只有靠恨人、被人恨才能活命；他拿这个当饭吃，凡他邪火发作时要够着谁，隔在中间的栏杆木杆，斑斑痕迹把他那股杀瘾供述了个够——那小马牙口和蹄子的毁伤力，看得见，也叫人信得下。

===Original===
He wasn’t at all the same horse that’d faced a cowboy some eight years or so past. He hadn’t wanted to fight then, he’d just wanted to get away and be left alone, and he’d only fought the rope that held him; and even though his suspicions and hate of the human had been natural he hadn’t seen anything about that cowboy he wanted to disfigure.

===Chinese===
他早不是八年前那匹面对一个牛仔的马了。那时候他不想斗，只想脱身、图个清净，斗的也只是拴住他的那条套索；就算对人的疑心和憎恶出白天性，那位牛仔身上，他也还没瞧出什么叫他一心要毁了的东西。

===Original===
He’d done a mighty neat job of bucking in the Rocking R corrals and made Clint pay attention to his riding pretty well; but his bucking then, even though it was hard, didn’t compare much with the bucking of The Cougar. He’d just been bucking thru instinct. It was the natural thing for a brainy horse to do, and when he bucked it wasn’t for meanness but just to see if he couldn’t get out from under that rig and man. He’d felt like it didn’t belong up there in the middle of him, and he’d only wanted to make sure that it all could stick.

===Chinese===
他在摇椅R的畜栏里那通跳，干得干净漂亮，逼得克林特把自己的骑功好生顾了一路；可他那时的跳，纵然够硬，跟「美洲狮」的跳比，就不算回事了。他那时不过是凭本能跳。一匹有脑子的马，天经地义就这么办；他跳不为使坏，只为试试能不能把那套鞍具连人从背上抖出去。他觉着那玩意儿蹲在他脊梁正中本就不该，他只想弄清楚，它们到底站不站得住。

===Original===
He’d given it all a mighty good test of course, but as compared with the way Smoky had acted with how he was now acting as The Cougar, it would match well with a man playing a peaceful game of solitary and a gambler dealing for his life with some hated enemy.

===Chinese===
当然，他把这一切都考较了个透；可拿烟儿当年的做派，跟他如今当「美洲狮」的做派一比，就好比一个人悠悠闲闲打单人纸牌，同一个赌徒拿性命跟死对头发牌对赌。

===Original===
The Cougar would of killed himself to get his man; he was past caring for his own hide and only lived to hate. But even as strong as that hate was, it was queer to see that he wasn’t interested to do damage only to the men that handled or tried to ride him. Maybe that was because there was always so many around﻿—the grandstands was full of people and it was the same around the chutes and corrals of the rodeo grounds. Them crowds might of confused him to a standstill and sort of made him keep neutral till only one or two come near.

===Chinese===
「美洲狮」为了够着他的人，赔上自己的命也干；他早不心疼自身皮肉，活着就为了恨。可那恨再烈也有一怪：他一门心思要伤的，偏偏只是那些上手调理他、想骑他的人。许是因为四下人总太多——看台上满是人，竞技场的出场栏、畜栏四周也一样。那一圈圈人海，兴许把他搅得怔在当场，多少逼他守了中立，直到只剩一两个人挨近前来。

===Original===
Another thing that might of fooled a few was the way The Cougar carried his ears. Most every town person has noticed how some horses in the city’s streets have some kind of leather muzzles to keep ’em from biting passing folks. Them horses have their ears back most of the time and whenever somebody comes near, they have a mighty cranky look too; but as a rule they’re not as wicked as they look. It’s just that they’re tired of having everybody that goes by stop and try to feed ’em peanuts or apples and such, or being petted and sometimes rubbed the wrong way. Some horses’ disposition can’t stand it, and them few seem to get so that they can’t keep their ears forward and look pleasant any time;﻿—they’re always laying ’em back and looking like they would do some damage; but the most they would do if they had no muzzle would be to maybe just nip a little hunk of hard-twist serge or a little silk off different folks’ arms.

===Chinese===
还有一桩，容易瞒过几双眼的，是「美洲狮」摆弄耳朵的路数。城里人差不多都留意过：街面上有些马戴着各式皮口笼，免得咬着过路人。那些马多半时候耳朵向后背着，谁一凑近，还一脸乖戾相；可照例，他们并不像瞧上去那么歹毒。他们只是烦透了：个个过路的都要停下来喂把花生、苹果什么的，或者伸手摸摸，有时还把毛捋反了。有的马脾性受不住这个，那几匹就渐渐落成这么个样子——耳朵再竖不起来，脸色再受看不起来；成天背着一对耳朵，一副要伤人的架势；可真摘了口笼，他们至多也不过从这条那条胳膊上，叼走一小口硬捻哔叽，或一星半点丝绸罢了。

===Original===
Like a feller says to me one time, “It’s just that they’re bored.”

===Chinese===
就像有位老兄有回跟我说的：「他们呀，就是闲得慌。」

===Original===
The horse out on the range, no matter how mean he might be, hardly ever puts his ears back at a human; when he does, it’s only once in a coon’s age and only for the split of a second. In the next split of that second *something has happened*.

===Chinese===
牧场上的马，任凭多凶，几乎从不朝人背耳朵；真背了，也是八百年一遇，且只那一瞬。下一瞬里，*事情已经出了*。

===Original===
The Cougar, being a sure enough range horse and with real mustang4 blood to boot, carried his ears in the ways of that kind. He’d look at a man thru the chute timbers and with his ears *straight ahead*, but in them eyes under the shadow of them ears was a fair picture of what would happen if that man ever stepped in that chute with him. It didn’t need no imagination to see it either.

===Chinese===
「美洲狮」呢，是匹不掺假的大牧场马，还带着真真的野马4血统，耳朵守的也是那一脉规矩。他会隔着出场栏的木条望一个人，耳朵*端端朝前*；可那对耳朵荫翳下的那双眼睛里，明明白白映着：这人若当真跨进栏来同他站到一处，下场会是什么样。这也用不着什么想象力，一望便知。

===Original===
Never did The Cougar lay his ears back unless he was sure of his victim. When he did there’d be an ambulance wagon racing thru the arena and remarks in queer low tones passed by white-faced folks up in the grandstand; which all kept accumulating and piled up in The Cougar’s reputation as a bad horse.

===Chinese===
「美洲狮」从不背耳朵，除非猎物十拿九稳。他一背，竞技场里便该有救护车飞驰，看台上白着脸的人们便压着嗓子、用古怪的调门交换言语；这一桩桩攒下来、堆上去，全垫在了「美洲狮」坏马名声的底下。

---

			

===Original===
A little bit of a freckle faced hombre who’d made the “grand finals” was along the chute one day and “up” to ride The Cougar. He’d come from acrost the border, and thru the first three days of the rodeo had proved himself to be a “ranahan”5 in bronc riding as well as in steer roping.

===Chinese===
一天，一个矮墩墩、满脸雀斑的汉子（hombre）顺着出场栏站着——他已闯进「大决赛」，轮到「上场」骑「美洲狮」了。他是打边境那头过来的；竞技会头三天里，骑烈马、套阉牛两门，他都证明了自个儿是个「兰纳汉（ranahan）」5。

===Original===
“By golly,” he was heard to say as The Cougar was hazed into the saddling chute, “I’ve come a long ways to get a setting at that pony.” He felt of his taped spurs to make sure they was there to stay, “and if you watch close,” he went on, grinning, “I’ll give you all a few lessons on how to play a tune with a spur rowel at the tip of a pony’s ears.”

===Chinese===
「美洲狮」被轰进备鞍栏那会儿，有人听见他说：「老天爷作证，我可是跑了老远的路，才讨来这么一骑。」他摸了摸缠布的马刺，确信它们牢靠不掉，「你们睁大眼睛瞧好了，」他咧着嘴又道，「我给大伙上几课，教教怎么拿马刺轮，在小马耳朵尖上弹一支曲子。」

===Original===
The little “vaquero”6 was feeling good; he hadn’t been to town for a year or more, and a chance to ride a mean horse where there was folks around was a big change to him. Barrel cactus and Spanish dagger had been the only witnesses to his riding ability, and riding a sidewinding pony on dobe flats or high mesas wasn’t so apt to bring out the best in a rider as when in a nice arena, where there’s a band playing and folks a cheering.

===Chinese===
这位小个子「瓦凯罗（vaquero）」6正自得意；他一年多没进过城了，有机会当着一圈人骑匹凶马，对他是天大的新鲜事。先前给他骑艺做过见证的，只有桶形仙人掌和西班牙匕首（Spanish dagger）；在泥灰碱滩上、高台原上骑一匹斜着窜的小马，总不像在体面的竞技场里——有乐队奏着、有人声喝着——那样，能把一个骑手压箱底的本事逼出来。

===Original===
“There’s a horse to my liking,” he says as he took a look at The Cougar. The way that pony was acting while being saddled didn’t faze the rider none at all, the grin on his face kept a spreading all the wider as he made ready to climb the chute; he’d handled many a fighting horse, and to him they all could do only one thing, and that was their worst.

===Chinese===
「这才是一匹对我脾气的马。」他打量了一眼「美洲狮」，说道。备鞍时那小马的一举一动，一点儿没吓着这位骑手；待到攀上出场栏，他脸上的笑反倒越咧越宽；会斗的马他经手过不知多少，在他看来，这一路的马全会的一样本事，就是往死里使坏。

===Original===
As a true rider of the range he welcomed anything that’d test his skill and ability, and if The Cougar had come straight up from hell, wore horns, a forked tail, and cloven hoof, he’d of grinned all the more and bet his year’s earnings that he could send him back to where he came with his tail between his legs and hollering “enough.”

===Chinese===
身为牧场上真正的骑手，凡能考较他身手本事的，他一概欢迎；就算「美洲狮」是打地狱里径直上来的，长着角、拖着叉尾、蹄子分瓣，他也只会笑得更欢，拿一年的工钱打赌：他能打发他夹着尾巴、一路嚎着「服了」，滚回他来的地方去。

===Original===
“Rider up,” hollered the hazer, but the judges was already watching, for it was The Cougar “coming out.”

===Chinese===
「骑手预备。」驱赶手喊了一嗓子；裁判们却早已盯死了，因为这是「美洲狮」「出场」。

===Original===
The cowboy let out a war whoop and grinned as the chute gate flew open and The Cougar came “uncorked.” He packed that grin past the judges and at the same time “reefed” (spurred) the earth-jarring outlaw with *taped* rowels from back of the ear to the back of the saddle skirts.

===Chinese===
栏门砰地弹开，「美洲狮」「崩开了塞」，牛仔放声打了个战嚎，咧嘴迎了上去。他把那副笑一路带到裁判跟前，同时拿*缠了布的*马刺轮在这匹震得地皮发颤的劣马身上「猛踢」（reefed）——从耳根一路踢到鞍裙后沿。

===Original===
“Yee-e-e-ep!” he howled, as the bellering Cougar left the earth once more. A cloud of dust went up which kept the judges from seeing what went on, but even if there’d been no dust they couldn’t of followed what all had happened, it had happened too fast. In the next particle of time a twisting hunk of mouse colored horseflesh was tearing up the arena towards the chutes and the fence along it. The cowboy was still war whooping and fanning but he was to one side and being snapped around like a whip lash. The Cougar had found his stride and, as usual, was getting his man.

===Chinese===
「咿——咿——咿——！」嘶吼着的「美洲狮」再次腾了空，他仰天嚎了一声。一股尘头冲天而起，罩得裁判什么也瞧不见；可就算没尘土他们也跟不上趟——一切来得太快。下一霎眼，一团拧着劲儿的鼠灰色马肉已经撕开竞技场，直奔出场栏和沿场围栏而去。牛仔战嚎未歇，挥帽抽打（fanning）个不停，人却已偏到一侧，像条鞭梢似的被甩得团团打转。「美洲狮」找着了自己的步点，一如既往，正去收他的人。

===Original===
The “pickup men” rode up to grab holt of the horse’s head and before the man was throwed; but they was just too late and in another second something happened that made everybody in the grandstand turn pale and hang on to each other. For the cowboy, still a fanning, was, by a wicked jolt, loosened from his saddle and headed for the ground. The Cougar reared up while the rider was still in the air, then turned, and with ears back, teeth a flashing, hoofs a striking with lightning speed, went to carry out his heart’s cravings.

===Chinese===
「接应骑手」们打马抢上，要赶在人被摔下来之前揪住马头；可就迟了那么一步，转眼间的下一幕，叫看台上人人变色、彼此攥得死紧。那牛仔还在挥帽抽打，却被一记毒颠颠离了鞍，直奔地面。「美洲狮」趁骑手还悬在半空，前躯猛地拔起，随即转身，耳朵背死，牙闪着光，蹄子挟着闪电连击，去兑现他心尖上那份渴念。

===Original===
The man was juggled up there for a second and then came down, the horse, like the cougar he was, right after him and to finish what he’d started.

===Chinese===
人在半空被抛闪了一瞬，跟着砸了下来；那马，真像他名字里那头美洲狮，紧随其后扑下，来收他开的那笔账。

===Original===
It was then that Providence or something seemed to interfere; for as the rider came down and reached the earth he was on the other side of the fence, which kept him from being totally reduced to dust. But even with the fence separating, The Cougar wasn’t thru. There was a noise of splintering timbers as he tried to reach the cowboy, and it wasn’t till two ropes settled around his neck and pulled him away that it was what you’d call ended.

===Chinese===
就在那时，老天爷还是什么插了把手；骑手落地挨土时，人已在围栏另一边，这才没被整个碾成齑粉。可纵有围栏隔着，「美洲狮」也没算完。他朝那牛仔够去，木栏噼啪碎裂之声大作；直到两条绳索落上他脖子、把他拖开，这事才算得上叫结束。

===Original===
A few riders rushed up to find the cowboy setting up and shaking his head like a trying to get back amongst the living. Pretty soon he looked up at the men around him and a sort of vacant grin spread over his features; then he looked at his clothes, noticed his shirt was most tore off of him. He wrinkled his face as he moved his body and felt kinks along his ribs and back, and looked at his handmade rawhide chaps which showed marks where hard hoofs had connected. The sight of them made him grin again, and after a while he says:

===Chinese===
几个骑手抢上前，只见那牛仔坐起身，晃着脑袋，像要把自个儿晃回活人堆里。没多久，他抬头望望围着的人，脸上摊开一抹茫然的笑；接着打量衣裳，发觉衬衫几乎叫撕了下来。他一动身子，肋骨上、后背上处处摸着筋结，疼得直皱脸；又看看那双手工生牛皮皮套裤，上面落着硬蹄子「问候」过的印子。一见这些他又咧了嘴，过了一会儿，说道：

===Original===
“Daggone good thing I had these chaps on or I’d be setting here and going Adam one better.”

===Chinese===
「真是托了这皮套裤的福，不然我这会儿坐在这儿，光得就要比亚当还胜一筹喽。」

===Original===
From that day on the freckle faced cowboy was, or tried to be, at every rodeo and near whatever chute The Cougar honored by his presence. He’d run up against a horse he couldn’t ride; it was hard to take and he couldn’t get it into his head how it was done. He’d never seen a horse he couldn’t ride before, but there was more and which all kept the cowboy to following the outlaw. The unnatural meanness of that pony had him guessing, and he sort of wanted to figger it out while a setting on top. *There* was a horse that not only called for skill and nerve, but the thinking ability of the pony was sure worth a trying to match.

===Chinese===
打那天起，凡「美洲狮」赏脸驾到的竞技会，这满脸雀斑的牛仔场场必到，或想法子必到。他撞上了一匹骑不下来的马；这口气难咽，他也想不通这究竟是怎么个干法。他从没见过骑不下来的马；可还不止此——桩桩件件都拽着这牛仔，叫他跟定了这匹劣马。那小马那份不合常理的歹毒叫他猜不透，他倒想跨在马背上，把这事参透。*这*才是一匹不光讨手艺和胆量的马；那小马的脑力，也真值得人来过一过招。

===Original===
Winters and springs and falls found him on the range and doing his work there, he was getting all kinds of good practice with his every day work, and when summers come he was always on the trail of The Cougar and with new hopes that he could go back to the range and tell his “majordomo” that he “rode him, slick and clean and to a standstill.”

===Chinese===
冬、春、秋三季，他都在牧场上干他的活，日常活路里练出各路好功夫；一到夏天，他就总在追「美洲狮」的脚踪，揣着新的指望：这回能回牧场告诉他的「大总管（majordomo）」，说自己「把他骑住了，利利索索，骑到他钉在原地站住不动」。

===Original===
For two summers he followed him, in that time, competing with other good riders; he’d had three chances at him and each time them chances wound up with him hitting the ground, and running as he hit.

===Chinese===
两个夏天他追着他，其间与别的好手同场比过；他得了三回机会，每回的收场，都是他人砸在地上，落地撒腿就跑。

===Original===
“That horse sure means what he does,” he was heard to say to one of the riders one time, “and by golly that’s just what makes me keep after him.”

===Chinese===
「那马干什么，可都是来真的。」有一回，有人听见他对一个骑手这么说，「老天爷作证，就冲这个，我才咬住他不放。」

---

			

===Original===
Three more long summers of rodeo work went by, and The Cougar kept on a challenging the world’s best riders. Another spring came, more rodeos was followed and where it was advertised that “The Cougar Will be Present.” The posters went on a telling how in five years’ time no rider had been able to set the horse till the gun was fired, and as the cowboys remarked, “That was one truthful statement.”

===Chinese===
又三个漫漫的竞技之夏过去，「美洲狮」照旧一场场向天下好骑手叫阵。又一年春天来了，他跟着一场场竞技会走，海报上照例宣称「『美洲狮』将到场」。海报还接着讲：五年里头，没有一个骑手能在这马背上坐到鸣枪为止；用牛仔们的话说，「这可是一句实话」。

===Original===
Smoky kept on a throwing men right and left that spring and on thru the summer. He kept his record and back clean that way till away along towards the fall, and then one day at the start of another rodeo, a cowboy from the Wyoming country, and who’d come south for the winter, happened to hear of the doings. A couple of days later that bronc fighter showed himself at the rodeo headquarters, and remarking how he’d heard of The Cougar, signed his name and entered on bronc riding.

===Chinese===
那年春天，烟儿照旧左一个、右一个往地上摔人，一路摔进夏天。这纪录他保持得干干净净，直到深秋将近；这时又一场竞技会开了场，一天，一个打怀俄明地界来的牛仔——他是南下过冬的——碰巧听说了这档子事。几天后，那位斗烈马的行家在竞技会大本营露了面，说久闻「美洲狮」大名，签下名字，报了骑烈马一项。

===Original===
He qualified and went thru the “tryouts” and “semifinals” like it all was so much play. The Cougar was a horse kept for the finals only, and that’s the pony the cowboy had been trying to reach; the others he’d had to ride had only been a means for him to get to The Cougar.

===Chinese===
他顺顺当当过关，「预选赛」「半决赛」一路穿行，全当玩耍。「美洲狮」是单留给决赛的马，而那正是这牛仔一路要够着的马；他先前不得不骑的那些，不过是通往「美洲狮」的台阶。

===Original===
He’d easy won the right to ride that horse, and also the chance to win the thousand dollars that was up for any rider that could. He hung around the chute and mighty close the next afternoon. Soon the time would come for him to really try his ability; and while waiting he was using that time to seeing that the latigos and cinch had no weak spots, and would be able to stand the strain of staying around the middle of that Cougar horse.

===Chinese===
他轻轻松松赢下骑那匹马的资格，也赢下赢那一千美元的机会——谁骑得住，钱就归谁。第二天下午，他在出场栏跟前寸步不离地守着。真刀真枪试身手的时刻眼看就到；等待的当儿，他把工夫用来查验拉蒂戈革条和肚带有没有软处，经不经得起贴在那匹「美洲狮」腰当中的一番熬磨。

===Original===
Then the judges hollered out his name as the next rider out, and about that time the mouse colored outlaw peeked thru the bars of the chute at him and snorted. The rider whistled at the sight of the mean looking head, and, grinning a little, remarked:

===Chinese===
这时裁判喊出他的名字，下一个出场；恰在此时，那匹鼠灰色劣马从出场栏的木条缝里瞅了他一眼，打了个响鼻。骑手瞧见那颗恶相毕露的头，打了个呼哨，微微一笑，说道：

===Original===
“I got a hunch that this pony is going to be tetotally different than any horse I ever rode, but here goes, and I got to wish myself luck.”

===Chinese===
「我有个预感，这小马保准跟我骑过的任何马都彻头彻尾两样；不过说上就上——我得先祝自己走运了。」

===Original===
“You’ll need lots of that,” says one of the cowboys.

===Chinese===
「那玩意儿你得多备点儿。」一个牛仔说。

===Original===
The saddle was on, the cinch reached for and drawed up to stay, and then the rider climbed over the poles of the chute and took his seat on a back that’d throwed the country’s best riders. He pulled the rope rein up just tight enough, worked his feet ahead a little, and setting back some to sort of meet the first jolt. He took off his hat, layed all the balance he could in it, and then hollered:

===Chinese===
鞍子备好，肚带一把一把勒到死，骑手随后翻过出场栏的木杆，坐上了那条摔过全国顶尖好手的马背。他把鞍绳收勒到刚刚够紧，两脚稍稍往前送，身子微微后坐，预备先接住头一记毒颠。他摘下帽子，把浑身能攒下的平衡都存了进去，然后喊了一嗓子：

===Original===
“We’re coming out.”

===Chinese===
「要出来了。」

===Original===
“Coming out” was right, but “shot out” would of been more fitting in that case; anyway, the judges hardly seen either the horse or the man till both was *out there*, and both a fighting to win.﻿—There was a mighty big surprise showing on all the faces around when as the first big cloud of dust cleared, it was noticed the rider was still *up there*, and what’s more, all indicated that he was going to stay there.

===Chinese===
「出来了」不假，可这情形下，「射出来了」才更贴切；反正裁判还没看清人影马影，人马已经*到了外头*，双双都在为赢而搏——头一大团尘土散开时，四下的脸上齐齐写满天大的惊愕：那骑手居然*还在上头*；还不止，种种迹象都表明，他还要一直待在上头。

===Original===
The judges was a setting on their horses, and, pop-eyed with the miracle of the performance, looked on petrified. Such a rider on such a horse was seldom seen, and they was so all took up with the goings on, they didn’t notice that the rider had rode past the limit, and forgot to fire the gun marking the end of the ride. Then somebody hollered and jarred ’em out of the trance they was in.

===Chinese===
裁判们骑坐在马上，叫眼前这桩奇迹惊得眼珠子凸出，看得呆了。这样的骑手骑这样的马，百年难遇；他们全叫这场面勾了魂，竟没留意骑手早骑过了时限，忘了鸣枪记下骑程的终点。还是有人大喊一声，才把他们从怔忡里震醒。

===Original===
The shot was fired, and the report had no more than died down when the rider seemed to quit from there and fell off the horse﻿—the punishment he’d took in that ride had been enough to do him for many a day to come. He’d felt like his backbone was going to be pushed thru his throat from the first jump, and that feeling had kept a repeating right along with each fast coming jolt till he was near unconscious. Being the rider he was, he stuck there and tried to fight away the dizzy feeling and keep track of the horse at the same time; then after what seemed an hour, he heard a faint echo of the shot, and realized in a way that he’d qualified for first money. *He’d been the first man to ride that horse past the judges*, and that was enough﻿—he wasn’t caring right then if it would be said that he didn’t ride the horse to the finish.

===Chinese===
枪响了；余音未散，骑手就像从那儿断了电，一头栽下马背——那一骑里领受的刑罚，够他往后许多天消受的。打头一跳起，他就觉着脊梁骨要从嗓子眼里顶出来，这感觉随着一记记紧追的毒颠反复袭来，直到他神志将失。凭他骑手的本色，他死死钉在马上，一面强压晕眩，一面盯死马的去向；之后，仿佛过了一个钟头，他听见枪声一记遥远的回响，才隐约明白：自己够格拿头奖了。*他是头一个骑着那马熬过裁判的人*，这就够了——至于人家会不会说他没把那马骑到底，他这会儿不在乎。

===Original===
One of the riders who knowed The Cougar *mighty well* had watched the horse “come out” with the same thrill that’d always been his at that time. He’d seen the pony come out many a time before, and as that last performance came to an end, he leaned over to one of the boys near him, and says:

===Chinese===
一个把「美洲狮」摸得*透熟*的骑手，看那马「出场」时，心头照旧涌起每逢此际必有的那阵激颤。这小马出场，他见得多了；那最后一场收尾时，他凑到身旁一个小伙子跟前，说道：

===Original===
“Do you know, it strikes me like The Cougar is beginning to fade out as a bucking horse. I don’t think that pony’s been keeping up his standard the last few times he’s been rode, and specially this last time.﻿—If that cowboy who’s just left him had straddled him last summer, I’m sure and certain that he wouldn’t of stuck as long as he did.”

===Chinese===
「你说，我怎么瞧着，『美洲狮』当尥蹶子马，是渐渐要谢幕了。我看这小马近来几场都没保住水准，尤其是这最后一场——方才下马那牛仔，若是去年夏天就跨上他，我敢打包票，他绝撑不了那么久。」

===Original===
“Well, I’ve been sort of noticing that too, and figgered the horse had slowed down some,” agrees the other rider, “but that’s got to be expected, considering that The Cougar’s been in the arenas for going on six years now. I don’t see, myself, how them legs of his has been able to stand the strain *that* long.”

===Chinese===
「嗐，这我也多少瞧出来了，琢磨着这马是慢了些。」另一个骑手附和，「不过这也难怪——想当年，『美洲狮』在竞技场里都泡了快六个年头了。要叫我说，他那四条腿是怎么撑住*这么*久的，我才想不明白呢。」

===Original===
Them remarks was true﻿—nothing was meant against the cowboy who’d been the first to stick him past the judges; and as them words was said they meant just that, with no hint that *they* could of done the same; and what’s more, other cowboys had noticed the same what these two had spoke of. The Cougar was beginning to slow down﻿—but that last would maybe give some idea of what a bucking horse The Cougar really was, *or had been*.

===Chinese===
这些话句句是实——丝毫没有贬损那位头一个骑着他熬过裁判的牛仔的意思；话怎么说，就怎么是，绝没有半丝「换*我们*也照样」的弦外之音；况且，这两人瞧出的，旁的牛仔也早瞧出了。「美洲狮」确是在走下坡了——可那最后一场，倒许能让人掂一掂，「美洲狮」这匹尥蹶子马到底有多硬，*或者说，曾经有多硬*。

===Original===
That pony slowing down that way begin to be noticed more and more every time he was rode. The little vaquero from acrost the border went back satisfied that fall: he’d been the second man to *ride* The Cougar, and when the last rodeo of the year had been pulled off The Cougar had been rode twice more, *and to a finish*. The folks in the grandstands was surprised, and come to the conclusion that he wasn’t so much of a bucking horse after all, but they didn’t realize. Anyway, the thousand dollar purse that’d been offered for anyone who could ride him had dwindled down to five hundred, and The Cougar was fast losing the reputation he’d made as a man-hating bucking horse.

===Chinese===
这小马这么一路慢下来，每被人骑一回，就多叫人瞧出几分。那年秋天，边境那头来的小个子瓦凯罗心满意足地回去了：他是第二个*骑住*「美洲狮」的人；到全年末一场竞技会收场，「美洲狮」又被人骑了两回，*还骑到了底*。看台上的看客惊了，便断定这马到底也没多了不起；可他们不识就里。反正，那笔悬给任何骑得住他之人的一千美元，已缩水成了五百；「美洲狮」那匹恨人劣马挣下的名声，正在飞快地散去。

===Original===
Even his hate for the human had seemed to die down. He’d throwed a rider one day who’d landed right in front of him; the crowd had held their breath, expecting to see that cowboy mangled to pieces right before their eyes. All that would of happened, and mighty quick a year or so before; but this time the outlaw didn’t seem to notice the man. He’d bucked on right over him and seeming like careful how he placed his hoofs as he’d went so as to miss him.﻿—There was murmurs in the grandstand afterwards that The Cougar was no outlaw at all, maybe just a pet and trained to buck, and like his man killing reputation, which was most likely only a sort of a draw card and advertising for the rodeo.

===Chinese===
连他对人的恨，像也熄了。一天，他把一个骑手摔将下来，那人正正落在他跟前；全场屏住气，等着亲眼看那牛仔在他们眼前被撕成碎片。这一幕真要发生，一年多前不过是眨眼的事；可这一回，这劣马像压根没瞧见那人。他就那么一路跳了过去，落蹄时仿佛还留了神，专拣空当下脚，绕开他——事后看台上一片窃窃：说什么「美洲狮」根本不是什么劣马，兴许不过是匹宝贝宠物马，跳是调教出来的；他那杀人的名声，八成也只是竞技会招揽看客的幌子和广告罢了。

===Original===
But whatever the folks in the grandstand thought, Smoky had reasons of his own for gradually getting away from being The Cougar. It wasn’t that his legs was getting stove up or giving away on him so much as the way things had come to him as year after year he met up with the strange riders that’d come to try him; and even though none of ’em seemed to want a *close acquaintance* with him, there was nothing about them boys for the hate he was packing to feed on.

===Chinese===
可不管看台上的人怎么想，烟儿自有烟儿的道理，才一步步脱下「美洲狮」这身行头。倒不是他的腿垮了、顶不住了，而是这些年落到他身上的种种：一年又一年，来试他的尽是些生面孔骑手；这些小伙子虽说没一个想跟他*深交*，可他们身上，也再没有什么，可供他揣着的恨去啃食。

===Original===
Not once, since that day he’d bogged his head in front of the first grandstand, had a club, nor even a twig, ever been layed on him. For the first couple of years, Smoky had let the heart the halfbreed had transplanted in him, control his actions. The poison of hate in that heart had kept him from noticing or go according to the good treatment he’d been getting; and it was close on to the fifth year before his ears begin to perk up to the show of admiration and respect that was handed him from all around.

===Chinese===
打他头一回在看台前垂头发蔫那天起，一根棍子、连一截树枝，都不曾落到过他身上。头两年，烟儿由着混血儿移植进他胸口的那颗心，摆布自己的一举一动。那颗心里恨的毒，蒙得他既瞧不见、也不肯顺着自己一直受着的好待遇走；直到将近第五个年头，他的耳朵才渐渐竖起来，听进四面八方递来的那一份看重与敬意。

===Original===
The name of The Cougar lived on for a spell, but the horse that had been packing that name was fast getting away from having the right to such.﻿—Then the next spring came and with it rodeos begin to be pulled off here and there. Good riders begin following The Cougar again as before, and with the hopes that some day, sometime or other, they’d be able to pull their riggins off that pony’s back and be able to say:

===Chinese===
「美洲狮」这个名字还红火了一阵，可驮着这名号的那匹马，正在飞快地失掉用它的一应资格——又一年春天来了，各地又办起了竞技会。好骑手们照旧追起「美洲狮」来，指望着哪天有那么一遭，能把鞍具从那小马背上卸下来，然后说上一句：

===Original===
“I rode him.”

===Chinese===
「我骑住过他。」

===Original===
But long before middle summer come, them hopes had died down in many of the boys, for The Cougar wasn’t The Cougar no more. Them fast, crooked, and hard hitting jumps of his, and which had jarred the thoughts and balance out of so many a good rider, had died down, and put the horse as an average with the other bucking horses. Rider after rider forked him, and sorta disappointed, had rode and fanned him easy enough; where a year or so before no fanning had been required to qualify.

===Chinese===
可盛夏未到，许多小伙子那点指望就凉透了，因为「美洲狮」不再是「美洲狮」了。他那一手手又急、又刁、又砸得狠的蹦跳——曾把多少好骑手的神思和平衡颠出窍的那些蹦跳——都熄了火，把这马放平到寻常尥蹶子马一档。骑手一个接一个跨上他，多少有些扫兴，轻轻松松骑着、挥着帽子抽打着就过了关；而一年多前，过关是用不着挥什么帽子的。

===Original===
The Cougar kept a bucking on and on every time he was saddled, and he was rode thru to the finish oftener and oftener till finally, no rider was ever throwed no more, not from that pony’s back.

===Chinese===
每逢备鞍，「美洲狮」还是一跳再跳；被人整骑到终场的回数也一回多似一回，到末了，再没有骑手被摔下来过——从那小马背上，再没有过。

===Original===
The heart of The Cougar was shriveling up and leaving space for the heart that was Smoky’s, and that heart, even though older and weaker, was making a mighty strong stand, and steady coming back.

===Chinese===
「美洲狮」的心在枯萎，腾出地方来，给那颗本是烟儿的心；那颗心虽说老了、弱了，却站定了阵脚，一步一步往回走。

===Original===
