Soon, there came a time when the mouse colored outlaw didn’t have to be handled from a distance no more; no high corral was needed for protection against his teeth and hoofs, and like most of the other buckers he could be led from the stock car to the rodeo grounds without any other ropes holding him back, and away from the man that was leading him.

===Chinese===
不久，时候到了：这匹鼠灰色劣马再不必远远隔空摆布；也用不着高畜栏来提防他的牙和蹄；跟旁的尥蹶子马一样，从运畜车厢往竞技场，一根牵绳引着就走，再不用别的绳子拽着拦着，防他扑向牵马的人。

===Original===
Then one day, a rider brought in a big rawboned grey, remarking that “*here* was an outlaw,” and an outlaw he was sure enough. From his Roman nose on up to his sunk, dead looking eyes, and taking in his lantern jaws on to his thick neck and along with the rest of him, all indicated the *natural* outlaw; but what made him as a most valuable horse for the rodeos, was in the how he could buck; that’s all he knowed, and like all natural outlaws that way, that’s all he wanted to know.

===Chinese===
后来一天，一个骑手牵进来一匹骨架粗大的灰马，撂话说「*这*才叫劣马」，而他也确确实实是匹劣马。从他的罗马鼻往上，到那双深陷、死气沉沉的大眼，再算上瘦长的马灯下巴、粗壮的脖子，连同他其余的一身，处处都标着*天生*的劣马；不过，他之所以成了竞技会上一匹顶值钱的马，全在他会跳；他就懂这一样，而且像所有天生的劣马一样，也就只想懂这一样。

===Original===
Right away, he was called “The Grey Cougar,” the same as to try and bring back the real Cougar. But there was no comparing the grey outlaw with The Cougar, not when that last one had meant business. To begin with, the grey horse was mean only because it was his natural instinct to be that way; he didn’t have the special ambition nor the brains that The Cougar had. With the grey it was just jug headed orneriness, and in no way could he compete with the mouse colored man killer; but he made a fine outlaw just the same, a second best that’d do.

===Chinese===
他当即得了个名号，「灰美洲狮（The Grey Cougar）」，为的是想借他把真「美洲狮」请回来。可这灰劣马跟「美洲狮」没得比——在那位动真格的年月里更没得比。头一条，灰马凶，只因天生本能如此；他没有「美洲狮」那份特别的雄心，也没有那份脑子。灰马有的，不过是笨脑壳的死倔；他怎么也比不了那匹鼠灰色的杀人马；不过他照样是匹像样的劣马，一个顶得使的二把手。

===Original===
He managed to buck a few men off from the start, and right then is when the Old Cougar begin sliding into the background, for it’d been quite a spell since that pony had made a man ride for his money.﻿—The appearance of the grey outlaw had kinda marked the downhill start for Smoky’s career as a bucking horse, and then one day the end came sure enough, and in a few minutes.

===Chinese===
开手他就把几个骑手尥了下去；就在那时，老「美洲狮」开始退到幕布后头去，因为那小马已有好一阵子，没逼哪个骑手掏过真本事换他的赏钱了——灰劣马这一露面，多少算给烟儿的尥蹶子生涯标出了下坡的起点；后来一天，终点当真来了，前后不过几分钟。

===Original===
As usual, The Cougar was announced to the crowd, and them in the grandstand who’d often heard but never seen that wicked pony in action was naturally mighty interested as that notorious horse made his appearance in the saddling chute. Many in the crowd had seen him buck before, and some of them stopped breathing for a spell; and while the gate was opened, most anything was expected from that horse; and all of them that looked on felt sure of seeing something that’d come up to their expectations, and then some.

===Chinese===
照例，场子里向众人报出「美洲狮」的名号；看台上那些常听说、却没见过这恶马发威的，见这匹声名狼藉的马在备鞍栏里现身，自然个个来了劲。人群里有不少从前看过他跳的，有几个当场屏住了一阵气；栏门拉开的当儿，这马什么样的事干不出来？看的人个个笃定，这回管保能瞧上不负所望的好戏，还得饶上一份。

===Original===
The gate was opened, and out came a streak of a mouse colored horse with a cowboy on top, and The Cougar, that famous outlaw, lined out acrost the ground *on a long lope*.

===Chinese===
栏门开了，一道驮着牛仔的鼠灰色影子蹿将出来；「美洲狮」，那大名鼎鼎的劣马，*迈着悠悠的长步大跑*，径直排开场地去了。

---

			

===Original===
Anywheres, and in any line, very little respect is ever showed for a “has been.” If The Cougar had fought and tore things up as he’d once had, all would of been hunkydory, and the crowd would all been satisfied; but the horse had come to the end of his fighting streak. Not a jump was left in him, for the Smoky heart had growed over and smothered the heart that’d been The Cougar’s. He was a “has been” and only willing to be the plain behaving Smoky again.

===Chinese===
无论何地，无论哪一行，对「过气货」都难得有什么敬意。倘若「美洲狮」还像当年那样又斗又毁，一切自然顺顺当当，看客个个满足；可这马的斗性已经走到了头。他身上一丝跳劲也不剩了，因为烟儿的心已经长严长实，把当年那颗「美洲狮」的心捂死了。他成了「过气货」，一心只想再做回那匹规规矩矩的烟儿。

===Original===
The crowd was disappointed, they felt they wasn’t getting their money’s worth, and there was hollers of “Take him away and hook him up on a milk wagon,” or “Sell him for a lady’s saddle horse,” and so on. It was queer, but only natural, to notice that them loud mouth remarks was passed only by the most useless, and of the kind that’s plum helpless whenever away from their home grounds. Others hollered more to kind of show off, but the looks they’d get from the sensible folks around only went to prove that the show off was of just plain *ignorance*.

===Chinese===
看客们失望了，觉得这钱花得不值；于是有人喊「牵走，套到送奶车上去」，有人喊「卖给他做太太小姐的鞍马吧」，如此等等。说来也怪，却也不奇怪：那些大嗓门，恰恰出自最没用的一路人——那种人一离了自家地界，就彻底没辙。旁的人喊，多半是为了逞能；可四周明白人递过去的目光恰恰证明：这能逞的，是地道的*无知*。

===Original===
The cowboy rode The Cougar till the other side of the grounds was reached. There he stopped him and climbed off, and hearing the hurrahs from the grandstand, he touched the horse on the neck and says:

===Chinese===
牛仔骑着「美洲狮」，一直骑到场地那一头。他在那儿收住马，翻身下来；听着看台上那阵哄闹，他把手搭上马脖子，说道：

===Original===
“Never mind, old horse, you’ve done yours﻿—and I’d liked mighty well if I could of turned you loose amongst that bunch that’s making all that noise up there, and watch ’em scatter﻿—but you’re not fighting any more.”

===Chinese===
「别往心里去，老伙计，你的份内事办完了——我倒真巴不得，把你放进上头那帮嚷嚷得最凶的人堆里，瞧他们炸窝四散——可你呀，再也不打架了。」

===Original===
The rodeo was on its last day, the prizes was handed out that night, and the next morning the bucking horses was loaded in the stock cars on the way for some other town where another rodeo was going to be pulled off. In them box cars there was one place where The Cougar had stood while on the road, but this time, and in that same place, was a grey horse who snorted as the train begin to move. The Cougar had been left behind, and from the inside of the stockyards watched the train pull out of sight.

===Chinese===
竞技会到了末日，奖品当晚发讫；第二天一早，尥蹶子马们装进运畜车厢，运往另一座镇子，那边还有一场竞技会等着开。那些车皮里，原本有一处是「美洲狮」路上站的位子；可这一回，站在老位子上的，是一匹灰马，火车启动时正打着响鼻。「美洲狮」被留下了；他站在牲畜围场里头，望着火车开出视线，没了影儿。

## XIII / 十三

===Original===
“A Many-Men Horse”

===Chinese===
一匹众人之马

===Original===
The Cougar being he was useless for rodeo purposes, had been sold to the livery stable man for twenty-five dollars.

===Chinese===
「美洲狮」既然对竞技会再没了用处，就以二十五美元的价钱，卖给了出租马房（livery stable）的老板。

===Original===
It was figgered that at least twenty-five dollars worth of use would be got out of him there. The horse was fat and strong looking, could be broke to harness, and made to do his share with any of the six and eight horse teams which was kept on the road acrost the deserts as freight teams.

===Chinese===
人们盘算着，在他身上少说也能榨出二十五美元的用处来。这马看着膘肥体壮，可以套上挽具（harness）调教出来，去跟那些六套、八套、常年跑在沙漠路上的货运马队搭伙出力。

===Original===
But one day, and before the harness ever disgraced The Cougar’s hide, a bunch of tourists had flocked into town to stay for a spell, and one of the crowd suggested a little horseback riding. The livery stable man was at once swamped with orders for saddle horses, and before he got thru tallying up how many he could furnish, he found he was short of about three. By scouting around, he dug up two more, but he was still short one, and then his eye fell on the mouse colored horse.

===Chinese===
可没等挽具哪天来辱没「美洲狮」的皮毛，一天，一群游客先涌进了镇子，要小住一阵，其中一位提议出去骑骑马。出租马房老板顿时被租鞍马的订单淹了，还没数完自个儿能供出几匹，就发现短了约莫三匹。四下张罗，又刨出两匹，还差一匹——这时他的眼光落在了那匹鼠灰色的马身上。

===Original===
At first, he was for overlooking that horse entirely, but as he needed one more to finish up the party, he couldn’t very well afford to overlook any horse that might do. He caught the horse and saddled him, and scared but game, he got in the saddle. If that pony still had one jump left in him, it was up to that old boy to find out; and one jump from *that horse* would be that much too many. He’d never do for no tourist then.

===Chinese===
起先，他压根儿想把这马撂开不管；可还差一匹才凑得齐这一拨人，但凡可能顶用的马，他一匹也挑剔不起。他把马抓来备上鞍，心里发毛，却又不肯服输，跨进了鞍子。这小马要是身子里还剩着一跳，那就该由这位老兄来把它探出来；而*那匹马*哪怕只跳一下，一下也是多的。真跳了，他往后就再没法派给哪个游客了。

===Original===
But The Cougar never even humped up as he was rode around the stable corrals. The man’s legs begin to quit shaking, and as he sat there, his face gradually turned from blank white to natural color again; and then he begin to grin and show pleasant surprise as he noticed how well the horse reined whichever way was wanted.

===Chinese===
可「美洲狮」绕着马房的畜栏被人骑着转，连背都没弓一下。那人的两条腿渐渐不抖了；他坐在鞍上，一张脸从煞白慢慢缓回本色；待瞧出这马缰绳要哪边给哪边、听话得很，便咧开嘴笑了，满脸又惊又喜。

===Original===
“By japers,” he remarked to the stable door, “this feller is a real saddle horse.”

===Chinese===
「乖乖，」他冲马房的门念叨，「这家伙可是匹不掺假的鞍马。」

===Original===
So, when the tourists, all togged up in their shiny riding habits, appeared sometime later, the stable man was all ready and waiting for ’em. He sized ’em all up as to which would get along with each horse best, and being he was still dubious as to what The Cougar might do, he looked ’em all over careful once more till the strongest and most able looking young man in the bunch was spotted.

===Chinese===
于是，过些时辰，游客们一身锃亮的骑装、打扮得齐齐整整露了面，马房老板早已备办停当，专候着他们。他把人挨个儿掂量，看谁跟哪匹马最合得来；因还拿不准「美洲狮」会闹出什么名堂，他把众人又仔仔细细筛了一遍，直到瞄上那一伙里最壮实、最像有两下子的年轻人。

===Original===
The Cougar’s reins was handed to him, and sort of cautious, he asked:

===Chinese===
「美洲狮」的缰绳递到了他手上，他带着几分小心，问道：

===Original===
“I suppose you know how to ride well?”

===Chinese===
「想必您骑术很在行吧？」

===Original===
That young man turned on him, surprised at such a question, and answered sarcastic:

===Chinese===
那年轻人转过身来，叫这么个问题问得一愣，带着讥诮答道：

===Original===
“Why certainly.”

===Chinese===
「那还用说。」

===Original===
The stable man grinned as he watched him and all ride up the street. “Why certainly,” he says to himself, and grinned some more. “I hope he’s just as *certain* on his riding when he gets back.”

===Chinese===
马房老板咧着嘴，望他领着一行人沿街骑远了。「那还用说，」他自言自语，又多咧了几分。「但愿他回来那会儿，骑术上头也照样这么*十拿九稳*。」

===Original===
It was evening before the party, slouching all over their horses, returned to the stable. The stable man smiled, satisfied, as he noticed that the young feller, not at all mussed up, was still riding The Cougar. He’d been worried about letting that young feller have the horse, but everything was OK now and the folks seemed to’ve all enjoyed their ride considerable, and so well that they wanted the horses again for the next day.

===Chinese===
直到傍晚，那队人才东倒西歪、瘫趴在各自马背上，回到了马房。马房老板瞧见那年轻人一身齐整、还稳稳骑在「美洲狮」背上，满意地笑了。把马交给那小伙子，他原本还提心吊胆；这下万事妥帖，这帮人像是都骑得相当尽兴，尽兴到第二天还想接着要马。

===Original===
“This is a very fine horse,” says the young feller as he got off The Cougar. There was all about him that as much as went on to say, “Why certainly I can ride.”

===Chinese===
「这马真够好的，」年轻人跨下「美洲狮」时说。他浑身上下都透着那么一股气，差不多就等于在说：「那还用说，我当然会骑。」

===Original===
The stable man had seen many like him, and knowed exactly *how well* he could ride; but he was relieved in learning that The Cougar had behaved so well.

===Chinese===
这样的年轻人，马房老板见得多了，他*骑术到底如何*，老板一清二楚；但听说「美洲狮」表现这么乖顺，他心里一块石头落了地。

===Original===
“And what’s the horse’s name?” asks the young feller.

===Chinese===
「这马叫什么名字？」年轻人问。

===Original===
For a minute the stable man done some tall thinking; if the horse’s real name was given out, the young feller would sure swell up and bust in learning that he’d rode the famous outlaw nobody else had been able to ride for so long; and even though the horse hadn’t made a single jump with him, his “*certainly*” would get more conceited than ever. And then again, he maybe wouldn’t want the horse any more. So after hesitating a while he finally came onto a new name for the horse.

===Chinese===
马房老板动开了脑筋：要是把马的真名实姓报出去，那年轻人一旦得知自己骑的竟是那匹多少年来没人骑得了的鼎鼎大名的劣马，准会得意得胀破了肚皮；何况哪怕这马在他胯下连一下都没跳过，他那声「当然」也要比先前更目中无人。再说了，他兴许从此还不要这马了。于是迟疑了一阵，他终于给这马现起了个新名字。

===Original===
“Cloudy, is that horse’s name,” he says.

===Chinese===
「云儿（Cloudy），这马就叫这名儿。」他说。

===Original===
That name sounded sort of pleasing all around, and it fitted the color of the pony mighty well, but then the good points for it would never loom up like the name of Smoky had in the cow country to the north, nor would it ever be mentioned about from state to state and give thrills just at the sound like the name of The Cougar had often done. But then again that horse wasn’t the same no more﻿—he’d went from top cowhorse, to champion bucking horse and all around outlaw, only to fade away in a livery stable, and there for every Tom, Dick, and Harry to ride as they pleased. Cloudy was just a livery plug.

===Chinese===
这名字四下里听着怪受用，配小马的毛色也配得出奇地好；可它的种种好处，永远也起不来那么高的势——「烟儿」那名字当年在北边牧牛地方立起的名头，它比不了；「美洲狮」那名字一州传一州、光听个响儿就教人心里发颤的劲儿，它也比不了。可话又说回来，这马横竖不是从前那匹了——他从顶尖的牧牛马，做到称雄的尥蹶子马、彻头彻尾的劣马，末了在一座出租马房里消磨下去，张三李四王五，谁乐意谁骑。云儿不过是出租马房里一匹拉车的驽马（plug）。

---

			

===Original===
As a raw bronc and then cowhorse, Smoky had been for learning all that could be learned. As The Cougar and outlaw, he’d been for killing and disfiguring every man that gave him the chance. There’d been something that called on him to do his best while on the Rocking R range, and there he went to the top as a cowhorse. Something else, and very different, had stirred his interest while in the arena of the rodeo grounds; he’d shined there as a fighting outlaw, and in a way that’d made all the others seem to be out of sight.

===Chinese===
当生坯野马、后来当牧牛马的那阵子，「烟儿」凡是学得会的，他都要学。当「美洲狮」、当劣马的那阵子，谁给他机会，他就要谁的命、毁谁的相。在摇椅R的牧场上，曾有个什么召唤他拿出全副本事，他在那儿登上了牧牛马的顶儿。另一样全然不同的什么，在竞技场的场子里撩动过他的兴头；他在那儿以一匹能征善斗的劣马大放异彩，放出得叫其余所有的马都成了看不见的影子。

===Original===
He’d had something big to work for, both on the range and in the arena; but now it seemed like as the big livery stable doors closed on him after his first day of use there, that the end of his string had come. He’d sort of felt it in a way, soon as the last car of the bucking horses he’d been with went and disappeared over the skyline. He hadn’t tried to get away, or even snorted when the stable man came in the corral where he’d been left, and led him out.

===Chinese===
在牧场上也好，在场子里也好，他都曾有一桩大事可卖力气；可如今，头一天在出租马房使唤完，大马房的门在他身后一关，倒像是他这一辈子的指望，也到了头。这层他多少早就觉出来了——跟他同行的那些尥蹶子马，最后一节车皮驶过天际线、没了影儿那会儿，他就觉出来了。马房老板走进那口撇下他的畜栏，他没想逃，连个响鼻都没打，就让人牵了出去。

===Original===
He’d followed the man to the big stable, and as he was kept there, he found nothing about the place nor the folks around that suggested anything worth while working for. He was just a horse *there*, a plug that could be rented by the hour or day, and even though all seemed strange and new compared to what he’d been used to, there was nothing in the goings on which could put a spark in his eye.

===Chinese===
他跟着人进了大马房；一住下来他发现，这地方也好，四下的人也好，没一样透着值得卖力气的由头。在那儿他就是*一匹马*，一匹按钟点、按天出租的驽马。比起他过惯的日子，这儿的一切都生疏、都新鲜；可这些来来往往里头，没有一样能往他眼里添进一星火。

===Original===
Maybe it was that his heart was growing old, but anyway, and after getting acquainted some with the place, the pony sort of took things as they come without snorting out his opinions. He was fast getting past caring﻿—his main interest in life soon begin to be only for the manger of hay and the little grain that was fed him when the day’s work was done. One day the stable man came and curried him, that was a new experience for the horse; never had a curry comb ever touched his hide before. Somehow he didn’t mind it, and then come a time when the feel of that performance was looked forward to; it felt near as good as a good roll in the dirt. The currying, his feed of grain, a rest, and to be left alone, had got to be the remains of the mouse colored pony’s ambitions.

===Chinese===
兴许是他心气老了；反正，跟这地方混了个半熟之后，这小马凡事来了就受着，不再打响鼻发表高见。他对什么都快无所谓了——生活里的主要兴头，很快只剩下食槽里那摊干草，和每天活儿干完赏的那一小把料。一天，马房老板来给他梳毛，这对这马是桩新鲜事：打娘胎起，还从没有马梳（curry comb）碰过他的皮毛。不知怎么，他并不嫌；到后来，竟到了盼那套把式的份上——那份舒坦，差不多赶得上在地上美美打个滚。梳梳毛、那顿料、歇口气、没人打搅，就成了这匹鼠灰色小马残剩的全部志气。

===Original===
But he had to work, and earn what hay and care was handed him. He didn’t mind working, but all this aimless chasing around he was took out to do most every day wasn’t at all to that pony’s liking. He’d been broke to doing something useful, and which *had* to be done. Afterwards, and with his bucking, there was a reason; but with these *equestrians*, as they was called, they didn’t seem to know themselves what they wanted to do, or where they wanted to go. They’d just wander around and handle him with a rein in each hand like he was a plow horse. They’d run him up and down streets where the ground was hard on his feet, and let him walk where the going was soft and level. It was no wonder that the end of the day, and the stall at the stable, was looked forward to so much.

===Chinese===
可他总得干活，把人家发给他的草料和照应挣回来。干活他不嫌，只是这般没头没脑地天天被人牵着出去遛，实在不对这小马的脾性。他学本事，是为了干正经有用的活计，是*非干不可*的活计。后来他尥蹶子，也自有其缘由；可这些号称*马术家（equestrians）*的主儿，自己都像不晓得要干什么、要往哪儿去。他们只管由着性子乱转悠，一手拽一根缰，倒把他当成匹拉犁的马。路面硌蹄的街道上，偏夹着他一阵跑；松软平顺的道，倒由着他走。难怪一天到头、马房里那口槽，叫人盼得那么苦。

===Original===
Never before had that horse appreciated his night’s rests as he was now doing. He’d near close his eyes for the peace he’d feel then, and eat his hay and grain slow, the same as though he was fearing that as soon as it was gone, he’d have to be out again, and going. There’d be a short spell thru the night when he’d close his eyes all the way, and his tired mind, like his tired body, would be at rest; and then after a while, when his eyes would open again, he’d clean up what little hay he’d left the night before, and that way, gather all the strength he could for the day’s work that was soon to begin.

===Chinese===
这马从前从没像如今这么领受过一夜的好歇。那阵清净能叫他眼睛眯起，草和料也嚼得慢慢腾腾，倒像生怕一吃完，就又得拉出去赶路。一夜里有一小段时辰，他把眼睛阖了个严实，乏透的心跟乏透的身子一道歇着；过一阵眼再睁开，他把头天夜里省下的那点草拾掇干净，就这样，给眼看就要开头的那天活计，攒下能攒的每一点力气。

===Original===
Near every morning, early, a grey haired man, and sort of stout around the middle, would come. A little “pancake” saddle with flapping iron stirrups, would be put on the pony’s back; and after a lot of hard work and puffing, the equestrian would finally get up and on the horse, and the early morning ride would begin.

===Chinese===
差不多每天一大早，总有个灰白头发、当腰有些发福的男人来。一副小小的「薄煎饼」鞍（pancake saddle），配上扑棱扑棱的铁马镫，往小马背上一搁；那马术家好一番挣扎、好一阵喘，末了总算骑上去坐定，清早一遛就此开场。

===Original===
The man was heavy, and set his saddle mighty awkward, but with all his weight and awkwardness, and as Cloudy got acquainted some with the man, he finally sort of took a liking for him. That one seemed to know where he wanted to go, and when he got there, even though it was no place in perticular, the old fellow would always get down off of him. Sometimes he’d talk to him, and Cloudy would listen﻿—it didn’t matter if he couldn’t make heads or tails of what the talk was about, he just liked the sound of his voice.

===Chinese===
这人分量重，鞍子又搁得要多别扭有多别扭；可别管他多重、多笨，云儿跟他混了个半熟之后，末了倒真有点儿喜欢上他了。这一位像是知道自个儿要去哪儿，到了地头——哪怕那地头压根说不上什么名堂——老人家也总会打他背上下来。有时他跟他说话，云儿就听着——说的什么他一概摸不着边也不要紧，他就是爱听那个嗓音。

===Original===
Them morning rides was always on the outside of town, up some canyon or lane, and Cloudy felt better at them places. Besides, he never was rushed; and if he was put into a trot or a lope, it was done proper and in a way both man and horse enjoyed. Seldom would any sweat ever show after the ride was over and the stable was reached again.

===Chinese===
那些晨遛总在镇子外头，顺着哪条山沟或是车道走，云儿在那类地方心里也松快些。再说，从没人催他；偶尔上一溜小跑、放一程大跑，也做得规规矩矩，人马两舒服。遛完回到马房，他身上几乎见不出一星汗。

===Original===
But the day’s work would be just beginning for Cloudy, and the stable was no more than got into sight, when saddles would be changed and another person, fresh, and aching for a *jaunt*, would get on him and start out on another ride. When he’d be brought back at noon, he’d just have time to eat his grain, when another equestrian would darken the stable door, and ask for Cloudy.

===Chinese===
可对云儿来说，一天的活计这才起头；马房刚进得视野，鞍子就要换人，又一位精精神神、一门心思要*兜一趟风（jaunt）*的主儿跨上去，另开一程。晌午给驮回来，他刚来得及吃上那口料，又一位马术家已经堵在了马房门口，点名要云儿。

===Original===
“I enjoy riding that horse so, don’t you know.”

===Chinese===
「骑那匹马可真享受，您说是吧。」

===Original===
Everybody preferred Cloudy to any horse the stable man had, and being that feller wasn’t running that business for his health, he rented him out every chance he got, and fed him an extra feed of grain so the horse could stand up under the work. Sometimes that horse would be rode till away into the night, then brought in dripping with sweat and often staggering. But the next day his work went on just the same.

===Chinese===
马房里的马，哪匹都比不过云儿招人喜欢；那位老板开买卖又不为养身子板，逮着机会就把他往外租，还多喂他一顿料，好叫他扛得住这份使唤。有时候这马能叫人骑到深更半夜，牵回来时浑身汗透，走道儿直打晃。可第二天，活计照旧。

===Original===
Folks of all ages, sizes, build, and packing from none to a big amount of brains, came and rode Cloudy. Once in a while he’d be handled right and like it was known that a horse has feelings and brains; but most of the time his feelings wasn’t at all considered. No thought would be given that the horse might of already went a long ways, or that he might be tired. But amongst all that rode him, the boys was the worst, and fast running the old pony downhill and towards the end.

===Chinese===
男女老少，高矮胖瘦，脑瓤里的货色从一空到底到满满登登，各路人等都来骑云儿。隔三差五也能碰上个懂行的，拿他当有知觉、有脑筋的牲口对待；可多半时候，他的知觉根本没人放在心上。没人想过这马兴许已经跑出去老远，兴许已经乏了。而在骑他的人群里头，最狠的是那帮小子，撒着欢儿把老马往坡底下、往末路上赶。

===Original===
The most of ’em would start the horse on a high lope, and from the time they got on him till he was brought back, that high lope, instead of being let up on, would most always wind up into a high run. Up and down the side streets they’d race him, loan him to other boys to race him some more, and each would do their best to show off on how fast they could make the tired horse go.

===Chinese===
那帮小子多半一上马就撒开大跑，从骑上去到被牵回来，那大跑不但收不住，十有八九还要长成一溜狂奔。他们骑着他满偏街背巷地赛，又把他借给别的小子接着赛，个个使足了劲儿卖弄，看谁能把这匹乏马催得更快。

===Original===
There was times as the spur, a quirt was layed on the old horse, to make him go faster; when The Cougar heart which had died in him near showed signs of coming back to life again; but the pony’s spirit had dwindled down as the years accumulated, and he couldn’t back the way he felt. He was weary both in mind and body, and no chance was ever given him so as to let either rest; and if once in a while the heart of The Cougar did make a try at coming back it wasn’t for long. The flame would only sputter and go out, and another rap with the quirt would only make him try to do his best once again, as just plain Cloudy, the livery stable plug.

===Chinese===
有几回，马刺、短马鞭（quirt）一齐落到老马身上，逼他再快些；这时节，他那颗死过去的「美洲狮」的心，险些露出还阳的苗头。可这小马的精气神，随着年头一层层摞上去，早熬干了，他撑不起心里那股劲了。他身心两乏，偏又一口喘息也讨不着；偶尔「美洲狮」那颗心当真试着活转来，也活不长。火苗滋啦两下就灭了，再挨一鞭，只会叫他重新打起精神去尽力——作为平平凡凡的云儿，出租马房的那匹驽马。

===Original===
The boys, girls, and grown ups kept a setting on the old horse; and not knowing, but sure and steady, was riding and dragging him down to a death that’d be away ahead of the time when it should come. They’d compared well with a pack of wolves, for like that kind, none of ’em would ever wanted to come within a hundred yards of the horse when he was up and a fighting. None of ’em would ever dreamed of wanting to set on his back when he was The Cougar and hankering to fight and kill, but now and at last he was down; there was no fight in him no more, and like the pack of wolves they compared so well with, they all closed in on him.

===Chinese===
小子们、姑娘们、大人们，轮着班儿在老马背上坐；他们懵懵懂懂，却又稳稳当当，把他骑着、拖着，往一场大大提前的死里送。他们同一群狼相比毫不逊色——因为跟那一路货一样，这马站起来开打的时候，他们谁也别想凑到他百码之内；这马当「美洲狮」、一心想斗想杀的时候，坐他背上这种梦他们谁也做不出来。可如今，他总算倒了；身子里再没一丝斗志，于是就像他们比得那么贴切的那群狼一样，一齐朝他围拢上来。

===Original===
The only difference was, the wolf pack killed their victim quick. They don’t leave the life drag on for days, weeks and months, nor let the victim suffer to finally die slow and by degrees. Then again, the wolf killed to eat and live.

===Chinese===
唯一的不同是：狼群杀猎物，杀得利索。它们不把那条命拖上几天、几个礼拜、几个月，不由着猎物干熬，最后慢慢一寸寸死掉。再说，狼杀生，是为了吃、为了活命。

===Original===
But there was no blame ever attached to these human wolves who was killing the horse only for the pleasure they’d get in riding him, and the fine exercise that went with it. Most of ’em meant well; only they didn’t know. Cloudy, always true in whatever he done, was so willing, no jab of the spur was needed to make him go; and his willingness to do his best that way was often if not always mistaken, and took for granted that he was feeling good and rearing to go.

===Chinese===
可这些披着人皮的狼，杀这匹马不过为了骑他取乐、外带落一身好锻炼，却从来没谁往他们身上安过半句不是。他们大多一片好心；只是不懂罢了。云儿不管干什么都一片实心，情愿得很，不用马刺戳一下就肯走；而这份肯卖力气的情愿，即使不是回回、也常常叫人错认了，想当然当他浑身舒坦、撒着欢儿要跑。

===Original===
They didn’t know the difference between a tired, wore out horse and one that’s fresh and fit to be rode. Then again, there was many who never stopped to realize. To them a horse was just a horse, and they didn’t know nothing about horses. That kind figgered a horse to be like an automobile, always able to go and as fast as was wanted; and instead of stepping on the gas like is done with a car, just give the horse the whip, and that way keep him right on a going.

===Chinese===
他们分不清一匹乏透了的马和一匹精神头十足、正好骑的马。再说，多少人压根没停下来想过。在他们看来，马就是马，他们对马屁也不懂。那种人把马当成一辆汽车，随时候着，要多快给多快；跟汽车不一样的，就是不踩油门儿，只消给马一鞭子，照样叫他一路走下去。

---

			

===Original===
A winter came and scattered the bright fall days four ways. The coming of the long, cold winter, along with the raw winds that swept down from the divide, brought to the folks around a dread of the dreary months that was to follow. Them folks wasn’t for enjoying being out much any more, and instead found a lot of comfort in being where there was a roof over their heads, and a fire roaring between the four walls.

===Chinese===
一个冬天来了，把亮堂堂的秋日刮向四面八方。漫长苦寒的冬天一到，加上打分水岭上扫下来的生冷寒风，叫四下的人对往后那几个月枯索日子先自发怵。那些人再不爱往外跑，反倒觉出许多安适来——头顶有片屋顶，四壁当中一炉火呼呼地烧。

===Original===
The tourists had all left, and scattered back to where they came. “The town was dead,” and many heads was got together a trying to figger ways to break the monotony that’d took hold of the community. For two weeks a cold wind had blowed down off the mountain and once in a while would bring along light flakes of snow that kept a skipping and never seemed to light. The weather was cussed at by some, while others kept busy bringing in wood and coal, and not any had a good word put in for Old Man Winter, not any excepting one, and that one was only an old livery stable plug.

===Chinese===
游客走光了，散回各人的来处。「镇子死掉了」，多少颗脑袋凑成一堆，琢磨着法子破一破罩住全镇的那股单调。一股寒风打山上灌下来，一连刮了两个礼拜，时不时捎几片碎雪，光顾着打旋，总也不肯落。有人咒这天气，有人忙着往屋里搬柴搬煤；冬大爷（Old Man Winter）没从谁嘴里讨得一句好话——只除了一位，而那一位，不过是出租马房里一匹拉车的老驽马。

===Original===
That old plug couldn’t of said anything anyway, but he done better, he *felt* what he couldn’t say. He felt that the coming of winter that way and the evaporating of the tourists and the others, as it came, was all that saved what little life he had left. There was saddle sores on his back, and he’d got to where there was nothing to him but a rack of bones on which a hide hung. That hide was faded from many a sweating, and in spots the hair had wore off and left it bare. His weary legs near buckled under him, and was hardly able to pack the weight he’d reduced to; and another couple of weeks more the old pony would of been done for. He’d long ago been going on his nerve, and that had been fast wearing out on him.

===Chinese===
那匹老驽马反正有话也说不出，可他干得更漂亮——他把说不出的，全都*觉了出来*。他觉出，冬天这么一来，游客之流随之蒸了个干净，恰好救下了他没剩几两的那条命。他背上有鞍伤，一身只剩一副挂皮的骨头架子。那张皮叫汗水沤得褪了色，好几处毛磨光了，光板板露着。四条乏腿在身子底下直打弯儿，几乎驮不动他剩的那点分量；再这么过俩礼拜，这匹老马就该了账了。他早就全凭一口心气硬撑，那口心气也快磨没了。

===Original===
But now, it looked like Old Man Winter had come just in time and saved him from the bone pile. There’d been two weeks when the cold winds howled, whistled thru the cracks of the stable and shook it; and in them two weeks, the old horse had recuperated some till he was able to listen to the howling wind and feel the while that no equestrian would be showing up to interrupt the rest he was needing so bad.

===Chinese===
可这会儿，冬大爷来得正是时候，把他从骨头堆边上捞了回来。寒风怒号的两个礼拜里，风从马房的缝子挤进来，呜呜叫，摇得房子直晃；就在那两个礼拜里，老马将养回来几分，能够听着那怒号的风，一面觉出这工夫再不会有什么马术家露面，来搅和他缺得厉害的那场歇息了。

===Original===
Every person around wondered when that awful wind was going to stop; but with Cloudy, and if he could of, he’d wished that wind would last forever. It’d got to be sweet music to his ears, and he dozed to his heart’s content only to be woke up out of his dream to stare at a fresh forkful of hay once in a while. Then he’d eat a spell, listen to the wind some more, and on the sound of it, go to dozing again. Maybe dreaming of a winter range, somewheres, and far away. Pecos is by him maybe, while he dreams, then other ponies of the Rocking R, and on a ridge watching him is Clint﻿—the only real friend he’d ever knowed.

===Chinese===
周围人人都在纳闷，那阵要命的风几时才肯住；可对云儿——他要是能开口许愿，巴不得那风刮到地老天荒。那声音在他耳朵里成了甜滋滋的音乐，他恣意打盹，只是隔一阵叫梦惊醒，睁眼瞪一口新添的干草。于是吃上一阵，再听听风，听着听着又迷糊过去。兴许正梦见一片冬季牧场，在哪儿呢，远得很。梦里佩科斯兴许就在他身边，跟着是摇椅R旁那些小马；山梁上望着他的，是克林特——他这辈子认下的唯一真朋友。

===Original===
The winter months wore on and Cloudy begin to look like a horse again; then spring come, and the air that came with it got the folks to wanting to be out. One day the gray haired gent who’d rode Cloudy in mornings of the summer before showed up again and was picked on as one steady customer for the pony; then a few days later a young lady came to the stable who “just loved horses,” and asked if she could get Cloudy every afternoon and whenever the weather was fit to ride in.

===Chinese===
冬天的月份一步步磨过去，云儿渐渐又有了马模样；跟着春天来了，随之而来的那口空气，撩得人又想出门。一天，去年夏天早上骑云儿的那位灰白头发老先生又露面了，被定作这小马的一位常客；过几天，又有位年轻小姐来到马房，她「就是稀罕马稀罕得不行」，问能不能每逢天气宜骑，每个下午都由她租云儿。

===Original===
The stable man let her have the horse once and noticing what good care she’d took of him, figgered her as another steady customer for the old horse. With her and the gray haired man showing up every day he allowed how that would be enough work for him, and none of the other equestrians ever got a chance to set on that horse from then on.

===Chinese===
马房老板让她骑了一回，见她把马拾掇得妥妥帖帖，也把她算作这匹老马的一位常客。有她跟那位灰白头发先生天天露面，他估摸这马的活计也就到数了，打那以后，别的马术家再没沾过这匹马的背。

===Original===
A few years before, and if Cloudy had been the kind of a horse folks would want to ride, that pony would of been able to take on a couple more equestrians and stand up under the work easy enough; but now he was getting too old for much more riding, and the stable man realizing that, was trying to make him last as long as he could. But Cloudy was getting stiff mighty fast along the shoulders and front legs, he couldn’t reach out no more in the same stride that’d been his; and instead, whenever a front foot touched the ground for another step, it was like he was placing it on needles, and careful so as not to jar his shoulders and the rest of his body any more than he could help.

===Chinese===
早几年，云儿要真是人人乐意骑的那种马，再添两位客他也轻松扛得住；可如今他上了岁数，经不住多骑了，马房老板心里透亮，只求叫他尽量多撑些日子。无奈云儿的肩头和前腿僵得飞快，再迈不开从前那套步幅了；如今前蹄每回点地预备再迈一步，都像踩在针尖上，小心翼翼，生怕肩头和浑身上下多震着一下半下。

===Original===
There was times when he felt like he wanted to split the breeze the same as he used to, but that feeling was mostly in his heart, and his old legs couldn’t follow up. Them old legs had hit the ground too hard, too many times, and jarred too many riders out of the saddle at the rodeos where he’d performed as a bucking horse. Then the first year of livery stable work where he was jammed around on the town’s hard and rocky streets put the kibosh on him for fair. The old tendons had been called on to do too much.

===Chinese===
有几回他也想再像从前那样剖开风跑一气，可那股劲儿多半留在心上，两条老腿跟不上了。那两条老腿，落地砸得太狠、太勤，在竞技场上把一个个骑手颠出鞍去——他就吃那碗饭。随后出租马房的头一年，在镇上又硬又硌的街面上磕磕碰碰，把他彻底磕残了。那些老筋腱，使唤得过了数。

===Original===
But neither the old gent nor the young lady that was riding him every day noticed the stiffness crawling up on the old horse. He still went, and he still seemed willing to go some more, and far as they could tell he was as good as any four year old. Both took care of him so well that no hint ever came to either of ’em that they was riding an old horse what had along ago earned freedom and a rest for what few years was still his to live.

===Chinese===
可是天天骑他的老先生和年轻小姐，谁也没觉出僵症正悄悄往老马身上爬。他照旧走，瞧着还像有得走；就他们看，他跟哪匹四岁口一样好使。两位把马照应得那么周到，周到得谁也没起过一丝疑心：他们胯下这匹老马，早就挣下了一份自由、挣下了安度余年的权利——不管余下的年头还剩几载。

===Original===
Every afternoon the girl came, her pockets loaded down with lumps of sugar, and refusing help, saddled Cloudy and headed him for a trail from where the scenery around could be seen and well. She’d pet him on the neck and run her fingers thru his mane, and talk while the pony, given plenty of time, would pick his way thru the rocks and brush. She’d let him rest often while in the steepest climbs, and sometimes would get out of the saddle so as to give him a better chance. At them times, she’d reach in the pocket of her white riding habit and get a few lumps of the sugar she’d brought for him.

===Chinese===
每天下午姑娘都来，两只口袋坠满了方糖，不要人搭手，自个儿给云儿备好鞍，把他领上去一条能把四下景致看全看够的小道。她拍他的脖颈，手指头梳他的鬃毛，一路说着话；小马呢，有的是工夫，在石头缝和灌木丛里挑着下脚。爬最陡的坡，她由着他勤歇，有时索性下鞍步行，多给他省一把力气。每逢那阵儿，她就从白骑装的口袋里掏几块糖——特意给他带的。

===Original===
Cloudy hadn’t been much for sugar when it was first introduced to him. He’d sniffed and snorted at the white lump, but the young lady had kept it under his nose till he finally nibbled at it. It didn’t taste so bad, and he’d nibbled at it again, and some more, till came a time as the girl kept a feeding it to him right along he’d got to looking for it. He’d even stop sometimes, look back at her while she was on him, and make it mighty plain that he wanted another one of them white lumps; and when she was by him on the ground he kept a trying to stick his nose in her pockets and reaching for ’em. He knowed where she carried it.

===Chinese===
方糖头回引荐给云儿那阵子，他并不怎么领情。冲那白疙瘩又闻又打响鼻，可年轻小姐把它一直抵在他鼻子底下，他末了还是啃了一小口。味道不赖，于是又啃一口，再啃几口；姑娘一路喂将下去，喂到后来，他竟惦记上这口了。有时候他干脆停住，回头望望背上的人，明明白白表示还想要一块那样的白疙瘩；她步行在他旁边时，他一个劲儿把鼻子往她口袋里扎，伸手够糖——他知道她把糖搁在哪儿。

===Original===
What a surprise it would of been for the cowboys who knowed Cloudy when he was The Cougar, the man killer, to’ve seen him in the act of bumming a young lady for sugar that way; and what a surprise it would of been for that same young lady to’ve learned that not so very long ago that horse would of took her hand and snapped it off at the wrist if that hand had ever come to within reaching distance.

===Chinese===
当年认得「美洲狮」——那匹杀人马——的牛仔们，若是瞧见他这副样子，向一位年轻小姐死乞白赖讨糖吃，该多么吃惊；而那位年轻小姐若是得知，就在不算太久以前，她那只手只要够得着他，他能把它齐着手腕咬下来，又该多么吃惊。

===Original===
It would of been a surprise sure enough, and afterwards, she’d figgered the horse being mean that way would of been on account of rough treatment by someone. She’d been right, even if that someone was only a scrub of a degenerate halfbreed and not fit to be classed amongst humans. Without him coming into the life of that pony there wouldn’t of been no such a horse as The Cougar, and he’d still be known around to the northern country as Smoky, the best cowhorse that ever busted a critter.

===Chinese===
那份吃惊管保货真价实；过后她准会寻思，这马从前那样歹毒，八成是叫什么人作践出来的。她想得对——哪怕那个「什么人」，不过是个蜕了化的、不入流的混血儿，压根不配在人类里头凑数。若不是他闯进这小马的生命，天下本不会有一匹叫「美洲狮」的马；他也仍旧会在北边地界顶着「烟儿」的名声——侍弄过牲口的牧牛马里，顶顶出色的那一匹。

===Original===
But anyway, and whatever had been in the past of the horse that was now better known as Cloudy, didn’t worry the young lady any. To her he was “the sweetest horse” she’d ever seen, and she kept a supplying him with sugar. If she knowed that lumps of sugar wasn’t the best thing there is to feed to a horse, she’d filled her pockets with a handful or so of grain instead, or something that’s more fitting to a horse’s stomach that way; but she didn’t know, and she sure meant well.

===Chinese===
可不管怎么说，这马从前有过什么——如今他叫得更响的名字是云儿——一点不叫年轻小姐操心。在她眼里，他是她见过的「最贴心的马」，糖照旧大把大把供着。她要是知道方糖并不是喂马顶好的东西，就会往口袋里装上一把料，或者别的什么更合马肠胃的东西；可她不知道，而她千真万确是一片好心。

===Original===
Fine warm spring days came, the kind of days when folks and animals alike hunt for a place where the sun shines the best. The last storm of the season had left, and as it went the last of Cloudy’s rest had come to an end. That pony was rearing to go (as best as he could) when the young lady came and saddled him one bright afternoon; and as she’d been cooped up considerable herself, her spirits more than agreed with that of the horse.

===Chinese===
暖洋洋的好春天来了，正是那种人和牲口都一样、要寻块太阳照得最透的地方待着的日子。这一季最后一场风暴走了；它一走，云儿最后的清闲也到了站。一个亮堂堂的下午，年轻小姐来给他备鞍，那小马正铆着劲儿要撒欢儿（就他还能撒的那点儿）；她自己也在屋里闷了太久，那股兴头，跟马的兴头一拍即合，还更有过之。

===Original===
Out of the stable old Cloudy went, his legs hardly feeling the stiffness that was in ’em, and seeming like his hoofs was more for flying and not at all for touching the ground. The old pony acted like he wanted to go so bad that the girl didn’t have the heart to hold him back; besides the stable man had told her one time that it wouldn’t hurt to let him run once in a while, if for a short ways; so, leaning ahead on her saddle, she let the horse go.

===Chinese===
老云儿出了马房，两条腿几乎觉不出里头的僵劲儿，那四只蹄子倒像不是为着落地、是为着飞。老马那副急相，叫姑娘不忍心勒他；再说，马房老板有回跟她提过，偶尔由着他跑一跑、路程短些，不碍什么事；于是她伏低身子贴住鞍子，把缰撒开了给马。

===Original===
Cloudy et up the distance and brought up sudden changes of scenery as mile after mile was covered and left behind. With the warming up of the run, the stiffness went out of his legs. He felt near young again, and was taking the steep hills more like a four year old than the old stove up horse he was. Sweat begin a dripping from him, and as the gait was kept up, that sweat turned to white lather.

===Chinese===
云儿把路程一口一口吃下去，一里又一里撂在身后，景致跟着一幕一幕换了新。跑着跑着身上热了，腿里的僵劲儿也跑了出去。他觉得自己又近乎年轻了，爬那些陡坡，倒像匹四岁口，不像他那匹僵透的老马。汗珠子开始滴答，步子这么一放到底，汗水就翻成了白花花的沫子。

===Original===
His whole hide was soaked and steaming from the heat of his body, but he kept right on a wanting to go, and like the girl, the excitement of the run had got a holt of him till neither realized they was carrying a good thing too far. The girl’s hair was flying in the breeze that was stirred. She’d lost her hat, but she wasn’t caring. To be going and splitting up some more of that breeze had got to the girl’s head, and cheeks flushed and a smiling she was sure getting a heap of joy out of just being alive and a going.

===Chinese===
他周身的皮叫体温蒸得透湿、直冒热气，可他还是一个劲儿要走；跟姑娘一样，这一趟飞奔的兴头攥住了他，俩人谁也没觉出这桩美事做过了头。姑娘的头发在激起来的风里飘。帽子丢了，她也不心疼。一个劲儿往前奔、再剖几口那风，这股痛快冲上了她的脑门；她两颊烧得通红，笑眯眯的，光是这样活着、这样跑着，就叫她受用不尽。

===Original===
The trail followed along a stream and up a canyon; it kept a getting steeper and steeper, and the old horse begin to breathe harder and harder, till finally, his wide open nostrils couldn’t take on enough air to do him no more. He had to slow down or else drop in his tracks, but Cloudy didn’t slow down, and not a sign showed on him that he was wanting to. He was the kind of a horse that never quit and would keep right on a going till his heart stopped.

===Chinese===
小道傍着一条溪，进一道峡谷往上走；越来越陡，老马喘得一口紧似一口，到末了，鼻孔张到兜圆也吸不够那口气了。他得慢下来，不然就得当场栽倒；可云儿不慢，身上也瞧不出一丝想慢的表示。他天生是那种从不认输的马，会一路走到心脏停了跳才拉倒。

===Original===
The girl, not at all realizing, kept a riding and enjoying the fast pace for all she was worth. She might of rode the old pony to his death that afternoon, only, the trail stopped and she couldn’t follow it no further. It had washed out during the spring thaw, and a place ten feet wide and as deep had cut the trail in two.

===Chinese===
姑娘浑然不觉，只管可着劲儿受用那阵快步。那天下午她差一点儿就把这匹老马骑到了头——幸亏小道断了，再走不下去。路叫春汛冲垮了，一道十英尺宽、深也十英尺的口子，把小道拦腰截成两段。

===Original===
She stopped there, and coming out of the trance the fast ride had put her in, she started looking for a place to cross, but there wasn’t any, and the only way left was to go back on the trail she’d come.

===Chinese===
她在那儿停住，从飞驰带出的那场痴迷里醒过来，开始找能过去的道；没有。剩下的唯一一条，就是顺来路折回去。

===Original===
She put her hands on Cloudy’s neck like to tell him how it was “too bad the trail stopped short that way” but she never got to say the words. The feel of the sweat and lather that covered the horse left her dumb, and then she noticed how hard he was breathing.

===Chinese===
她把两只手搭上云儿的脖子，像是要告诉他，「真可惜，路就这么半道断了」；可这话没说出口。摸着满身滚烫的汗和沫子，她哑了口；跟着又察觉出，他喘得有多凶。

===Original===
The thrill of the run had turned to sudden worry and fear for what she might of done, and another sort of excitement took a holt of her as she realized and then wondered what to do. She stepped away from the horse and wide eyed looked at him. She’d never seen a horse shake and quiver all over like that one was doing. He seemed like hardly able to stand up, rocked back and forth like he was going to keel over any minute. Cloudy was “jiggered”7 and his staggering scared her all the more. She must do something, and quick.

===Chinese===
飞奔的快意陡然变作忧惧和后怕，另一种激动攥住了她——她明白了处境，又不知如何是好。她从马身边退开，瞪圆了眼望他。她从没见过一匹马像这样浑身打颤、筛糠。他像连站都站不住了，前摇后晃，眼看说倒就倒。云儿是「跑过了头（jiggered）」7，他一步三晃，越发把她吓着了。她得做点什么，还得快。

===Original===
The first thing that came to her was to try and cool him off before, as she figgered, he fainted from being overheated. She tore at the saddle and worked at the latigos till it was loosened, then she pulled it off and with the blanket throwed it to the ground. Steam raised off the pony’s back, and at the sight of that the girl got excited all the more. Then she spotted the mountain stream below and just a little ways.

===Chinese===
她头一个念头，是趁他还没像她盘算的那样热晕过去，先给他败败火。她扯那副鞍子，解拉蒂戈革条解到肚带松了，跟着连鞍带鞍垫一把拽下来，撂在地上。老马背上腾起白汽，一瞧见那汽，姑娘愈发慌了。这时她望见了山下的溪水，就在不远。

===Original===
She led the horse careful and over to it, and then, thinking steady of quick ways to cool the horse off, she figgered it a good idea to lead him in the water and where it was the deepest. She skipped from boulder to boulder till finally a place was found where the water came up above the pony’s knees, and there she let him stand, while with her cupped hands she splashed the cold snow water on his chest, shoulders, and back.

===Chinese===
她小心翼翼把马牵过去；一面稳住神盘算给马速凉的法子，她觉得领他进水、专往深处走是个好主意。她打一块石头蹦到另一块，末了寻着一处水漫过小马膝盖的所在，就叫他站在那儿，自己拿掬拢的双手，把冰凉的雪水往他胸口、肩头、背上一捧捧撩。

===Original===
A half an hour or so of that, and the horse at last quit quivering, showed signs that he was cooled off and got his breath all OK again. After a while he drank, and then drank some more, and the girl watching him felt sure that the worst was over and that the horse was saved. She smiled, petted him on the neck, and felt relieved at the natural way he’d got to acting again.

===Chinese===
这么着撩了半个钟头上下，马总算不筛糠了，露出凉透了的样儿，那口气也匀匀净净回了位。过一阵他喝了水，又喝了几口；姑娘瞧着，认定最险的关口过去了，马保住了。她笑了，拍拍他的脖颈，见他一举一动重新回了常态，一颗心放宽下来。

===Original===
The sun was hitting for the tall peaks to the west when the girl finally decided Cloudy was all right again and fit to start back. He was good and dry by then and felt cool; she’d kept him in the shade all the while, and being that mountain shade is not at all warm at that time of the year, the old pony was near shivering from the cold by the time the girl led him back to the saddle and put it on him again.

===Chinese===
日头朝西边的峰头顶扑过去时，姑娘终于断定云儿缓妥了，可以动身回程。那时他身上干得透透的，摸着也凉；她一路都叫他待在荫凉里——那个时节，山里的荫凉可半点不暖人——等姑娘把他领回鞍子旁边重新上鞍，这匹老马已经凉得近乎打颤了。

===Original===
The ride back to the stable was like a funeral march as compared with the one starting out. The horse was kept on a slow walk all the way, and every care was taken by the girl so that only the easiest trail was followed. She worried as she rode along and noticed that the horse didn’t seem to be the same as before; his step wasn’t so sure and he’d stumble when there was nothing on the ground for him to stumble on, and then he’d sway like he was weak.

===Chinese===
回马房的一路，比之来时，像一支送葬的进行曲。马一路只迈慢步，姑娘处处小心，专拣最平妥的道走。她一路骑着一路犯愁，瞧出这马不像先前了：脚下没了准头，明明白白地上没绊头，他也磕绊；走两步又一晃，像身上没了力气。

===Original===
It was away after dark when finally the stable was reached, the stable man was there and waiting, and greeting the young lady with a smile he asked:

===Chinese===
天黑透了好一阵，马房才终于到。马房老板正在那儿候着，笑吟吟迎那年轻小姐，问：

===Original===
“Did you water Cloudy before you left?”

===Chinese===
「走之前给云儿饮过水吗？」

===Original===
“No,” says the girl, “but I watered him on the mountain where I turned to come back.”

===Chinese===
「没有，」姑娘说，「不过在山上、我折回来那地方，我让他喝过水了。」

===Original===
“The reason I asked, is because the new stable boy I hired forgot to water him this morning, or he thought I did.”

===Chinese===
「我这么问，是因为我新雇的马房小子今早忘了给他饮水——要不就是他当我喂过了。」

---

			

===Original===
The gray haired man didn’t get to ride Cloudy the next day, nor did anybody else, for that horse was hardly able to even get out of the stall; his legs was like so many sticks of wood and with no more bend in ’em than them same sticks have. His head hung near to the ground, and not a spear of the hay that’d been put in the manger had been touched.

===Chinese===
第二天，灰白头发的老先生没骑上云儿，旁人谁也没骑上——那马连出厩都费了劲；他那四条腿跟几根木头棍子一个样，木头棍子打多弯它们打多弯。脑袋垂得快挨着地，食槽里添的草，一茎也没动。

===Original===
The girl came to the stable that noon, and would of cried at the sight of him, only the stable man came up, and she held the tears back best as she could.

===Chinese===
姑娘晌午来到马房，一见他那样子，眼泪险些掉下来；亏得马房老板正走过来，她才把泪硬生生忍住。

===Original===
“Looks like he’s done for,” says that feller as he came up. He didn’t ask the girl what she’d done, cause a look at the horse told him the whole story better than the girl could of; and as he figgered, a man has to take them chances when he’s renting horses out that way. Besides, the girl looked so downhearted about it that he didn’t have the heart to do any more but try to cheer her up.

===Chinese===
「瞧这样子他是交代了，」那人走近说。他没问姑娘干了什么，因为朝这马望一眼，比姑娘能说上来的还齐全；他也认这个账——干出租马这行，这风险躲不开。再说，姑娘为这事蔫头耷脑的，他也不忍心多话，只想想法子给她宽心。

===Original===
“I’ll doctor him up the best I can, and maybe get him to come out of it a little.”

===Chinese===
「我尽着本事给他治，兴许能把他从这一劫里拽回来一点儿。」

===Original===
The girl took hopes at them words, and her eyes a shining, asked:

===Chinese===
这话叫姑娘生了盼头，她眼睛亮亮的，问：

===Original===
“And can I come and help you?”

===Chinese===
「那我能来搭把手吗？」

===Original===
Every day from then on the time the girl had used a riding Cloudy was spent in the stable and by that horse. Liniments and medicines of all kinds was dug up and bought and used, and as the stable man watched her trying to do her best, he’d only shake his head. He knowed it was no use, and if the horse did come out of it, he’d never come out of it enough to ever be of any use as a saddle horse again.

===Chinese===
打那天起，姑娘原先骑云儿的那份工夫，全花在马房里、花在那匹马身边。各色擦剂药散，翻出来的、买来的，一样样用将上去；马房老板瞧她这样卖力，只有摇头。他知道这是白搭——这马就算缓过来，也缓不到还能当鞍马使的份上了。

===Original===
The horse had been foundered.﻿—The twenty-four hours without water, the hard run and sweating up, and then cooled off sudden in ice cold water, and drinking his fill of that same water, and all at once, had crippled him and stoved him up in a way where he’d be plum useless, only maybe for slow work and hooked to a wagon.

===Chinese===
这马得了蹄叶炎（founder）。——整二十四小时没沾水，一阵狠跑大汗淋漓，跟着骤然泡进冰水里激透，又一口气灌饱了那样的冰水——这一下把他作践残了、作践僵了，残得彻彻底底，兴许只剩慢活儿、套大车还用得上他。

===Original===
A month went by, and the doctoring went on, the girl always a hoping; and then one day she came to the stable to find the horse gone. She hunted up the stable man and finally, after a lot of running around, found him up in the hay loft.

===Chinese===
一个月熬过去了，医治没停，姑娘总怀着指望；后来一天，她来到马房，马没了。她满处寻马房老板，跑了一大圈，末了在草料棚顶上找着了他。

===Original===
“I figgered,” says that feller on finding himself cornered, “that it’d be best to turn him loose. There’s good range up north a ways and thinking it’d do him more good to be loose that way on good feed, I just took him up there.”

===Chinese===
「我琢磨着，」那人被堵了个正着，说，「还是把他放了顶好。北边不远有片好草场，想着叫他撒开放着、吃口好草，对他更养人，我就把他送上那儿去了。」

===Original===
But there was no good range in that country, not for many miles. The stable man had lied to save the girl’s feelings. And instead, realizing that he couldn’t turn the horse loose only maybe to let him starve, and being he couldn’t afford to keep and feed a useless horse, there’d been only one way out. He’d sold him to a man who bought old horses and killed ’em for chicken feed.

===Chinese===
可那地方方圆多少里，压根没有好草场。马房老板撒了个谎，为的是护着姑娘的心。实情是：他明白把马放生，多半不过是叫他慢慢饿死；养一匹没用的马，他又养不起——出路只剩一条。他把马卖给了一个收老马的人，收去宰了做鸡饲料。

## XIV / 十四



===Original===
“Dark Clouds, Then Tall Grass”
===Chinese===
「乌云，而后高草」



===Original===
The man collecting old wore out and crippled horses had come along and led him away. He had a little salt-grass pasture a short distance out of town, and there’s where he took the old horse. He turned him loose amongst a few more old horses, and would keep him there till the time come when some “chicken man” around town would need the carcass of one of the horses to feed to his chickens; then the horse what looked like it had the shortest to live would be killed and hauled away.
===Chinese===
那个收老弱残马的汉子来了，把他牵走了。他在镇外不远有块小小的盐草牧场，老马就被带到了那儿。他把他跟另外几匹老马放在一处散养着，要一直养到镇上哪个「养鸡人（chicken man）」需要一具马尸来喂鸡的时候；到那时，看着最活不成几天的哪匹马，就会被宰掉拉走。



===Original===
It didn’t look like the end was very far for the mouse colored horse. All the work he’d done and the interest he’d had while under the names of Smoky and The Cougar, had stopped being accounted for, and sort of pinched out under the name of Cloudy; and now he had no name. He was just “chicken feed,” and soon, if he stayed in that pasture, all what he’d been and done would be blotted out with the crack of a rifle shot.
===Chinese===
对这匹鼠灰色的马来说，末路看上去已不远了。他以烟儿和「美洲狮」两个名字挣下的那些辛劳与名头，到「云儿」这个名字底下已没人再记账，一点点消磨光了；而如今，他连名字也没了。他只剩个「鸡饲料」的身份；不用多久，只要他还待在那片牧场上，他曾有过的一切、干过的一切，都会随着一声步枪响被抹得干干净净。



===Original===
But the old pony had no hint of that, and as it was he wasn’t for quitting as yet. His old stiff legs was still able to carry him around some, the doctoring he’d got at the stable had helped him more than what had been hoped, and then getting out in a pasture where he could keep moving around as he wanted to was helping him some more. Besides, his old heart was still strong, quite a bit solid meat was covering his ribs, and with the salt and wire grass to graze on he could still make out and mighty well.
===Chinese===
可这匹老小马对此毫无所觉，眼下的他也还没打算认输。他那两条僵硬的老腿还驮得动他四处走走，在马房里挨的那些调治也比指望的更见效，再者，到了牧场上能由着性子来回活动，又让他好了几分。何况他那颗老心脏还挺结实，肋骨上还盖着不算薄的一层实实在在的肉，有盐草和铁线草（wire grass）可啃，他照样能把日子撑下去，还撑得挺不赖。



===Original===
A few weeks went by when once in a while and every few days, one of the old horses he was pasturing with was caught, led out, a rifle shot was heard, and he’d never be seen no more. Other old horses was brought in and they’d pasture on with him till one by one they’d also disappear only to be replaced by more of ’em.
===Chinese===
几个星期过去，隔三差五地，跟他同牧的哪匹老马就被牵出去，接着听见一声枪响，从此再没了影踪。又有别的老马被送进来，跟他一块儿吃草，直到一匹接一匹地同样消失，然后再换上更多的马来。



===Original===
The old mouse colored horse must of looked like he was good to live for a long time yet; anyway, the “chicken horse” man had kept him, maybe for emergency, and so he wouldn’t be out of horses if an order for one came; and that kind was hard to get.
===Chinese===
这匹鼠灰色老马看上去想必还挺能活好一阵子；反正那位养鸡人把他留了下来，兴许是备个急，免得有人上门要马时手里一匹不剩；再说这路马也难得碰上。



===Original===
Then one day, a man came, looked all the old horses over. And finally, like he’d decided, pointed a finger towards the horse that’d last been known as Cloudy. That pony was caught and led out the same way other horses had disappeared, but no rifle shot was heard. Instead, a lot of parleying went on.
===Chinese===
后来有一天，来了个男人，把所有的老马挨个打量了一遍。末了，像是拿定了主意，朝那匹最后叫做云儿的马一指。小马被逮住，照着别马消失前那样被牵了出去，只是没听见枪响。倒是响起了一大片讲价声。



===Original===
Cloudy was led alongside of an old bony something that’d once been a horse. The old rack of bones was hooked onto a light wagon and seeming like hardly able to stand as the eyes of the two men went from him to Cloudy, to sort of figger out which of the two was worth the most, and *how much* the most.
===Chinese===
云儿被牵到一副老骨头架子旁边——那玩意儿从前想必也是匹马。这堆老骨头正套在一辆轻便马车上，看上去连站都快站不住了；两个男人的眼光在他和云儿之间来回打转，掂量着这两个里头哪个更值钱些，又*值多少*钱。



===Original===
Finally the dickering came to an end and seemed like agreeable to both parties. Three dollars to boot was handed, and the trade was made. The rack of bones was unhooked, the harness pulled off of him, and turned loose in the chicken horse pasture. Then Cloudy’s old heart missed a few beats as that same harness was picked up again and throwed over his own back.
===Chinese===
讲价终于到了头，看样子两下里都还满意。一边添了三美元，买卖成了。那堆骨头被卸下车，挽具从他身上剥下来，他也被放归进养鸡人的牧场。接着，那套挽具又被拾起来、搭上了云儿自己的背，这匹老马的心不由得漏跳了几拍。



===Original===
As true a saddle horse, and once hard to set on, as the mouse colored horse had been, the feel of that harness on his back was as much the same as if a shovel or a hayfork had been handed to a cowpuncher with the idea of his using ’em. The old horse felt it a plain disgrace, and snorted as it was buckled around him to stay; but the black whiskered hombre that buckled it on never seemed to notice or care that the horse had no liking for the collar and all the straps.
===Chinese===
这匹鼠灰色的马曾是一匹地地道道的鞍马，当年何等难以骑服；如今这挽具搭上背的滋味，就跟把一把铁锹或干草叉塞到牧牛人手里、要他拿去使唤一模一样。老马觉得这简直是明摆着的丢人现眼，挽具往他身上扣定的时候他直打响鼻；可那个黑胡子汉子只管一件一件地扣，像是压根儿没瞧见、也不在乎这马有多讨厌脖圈和这一身的皮带子。



===Original===
He kept on a fastening the harness, and when that was done, he jerked the old pony around and backed him into the shafts of the same old wagon that the rack of bones had been unhooked out of. Cloudy kept on a snorting and looked on one side and then the other as the shafts of the wagon was raised. If only he could act the way his heart wanted him to; but he didn’t have the strength, the action to put in it, nor the energy no more. The most he could do was to snort, quiver, and shake his head.
===Chinese===
那汉子只管把挽具扣个不停，扣完了，拽着老小马掉过头，倒退着塞进那辆骨头架子刚被卸下来的旧马车的车辕里。车辕抬起来的时候，云儿一个劲儿打响鼻，脑袋这边望望、那边瞧瞧。他要能由着心意行事该多好；可他再没有那份力气、那份身手、那股劲头了。他至多只能打打响鼻，浑身发颤，把脑袋摇了又摇。



===Original===
But, as he was all hooked up and the man jumping in the wagon grabbed his whip, Old Cloudy done his best to try and get back to some of the life and tearing ability that’d once been his. He kicked a couple of times at the rattling thing on wheels and which he was fastened to, then he tried to buck some and finally wound up by wanting to run away; but the harness held and the rattling thing behind came right along wherever he went, and worse yet, he felt the stinging lash of the man’s whip as he fought on and tried to clear himself. Then the jerking of the bit thru his mouth, and with all that to show how useless his fighting and wanting to get away really was, the old pony soon lost heart. He finally settled down to a choppy lope, then a trot that was just as choppy, and at last to a walk.
===Chinese===
可是，等他全套扣齐、那人跳上车抄起鞭子，老云儿还是使出了浑身解数，想找回几分当年那股生气和撒野的本事。他朝那个咯吱作响、把他拴在上头的带轮子的玩意儿踹了几脚，又试着尥了尥蹶子，末了索性想撒腿逃走；可挽具纹丝不松，身后那个咯吱作响的玩意儿他到哪儿跟到哪儿，更糟的是，他一边挣扎着想脱身，一边还得挨那人鞭子上火辣辣的抽。接着嚼子又在嘴里猛扯了几下——这一切都明摆着，他的挣扎和想逃全然白费，老小马很快泄了气。他终于收成一阵颠颠簸簸的大跑，又落成一样颠簸的小跑，最后干脆走成了慢步。



===Original===
Another sting of the whip was felt on his flank, and at the same time, the line was jerked at the bit, and Cloudy, still pulling the wagon, was made to turn up a lane. At the end of the lane was a shack made of old pieces of boards and covered over with the tin of old oilcans. To the right of that and a little ways further was another shack that looked like a mate to the first, only worse, and that one was going to be Cloudy’s place of rest and shelter whenever work was over.
===Chinese===
肋上又挨了火辣辣的一鞭，同时缰绳在嚼子上一扯，拉着车的云儿被拨上了条小巷。巷子尽头是一座用旧木板拼起来的棚屋，顶上盖着旧油桶的铁皮。往右再走一小截，还有一座棚屋，看着跟头一座是一对儿，只是更寒碜——收工以后，那座就是云儿歇脚遮身的地方了。



===Original===
There he was pulled to a stop, unhooked, led to the manger, and tied. The stable door was closed with a bang, and after a while the old horse, still wanting to cling to life regardless of what came, stuck his nose in the manger to nibble on some of what was in it. He reached for a mouthful of what he’d naturally took for hay, and chewed for a spell, but he didn’t chew on it long. There was a musty taste about the long dirty brown stems that didn’t at all fit in with any hay he’d ever et. The kind that’d been put in the manger for him to eat was the same that the livery stable man had used to put in the stalls and bed the horses down with. It was straw, only this was musty straw and wouldn’t even make good bedding for horses.
===Chinese===
到了那儿，他被拽停，卸了套，牵到马槽边拴牢。厩门砰的一声关上了，过了半晌，这匹不管落到什么田地都还想抓住活路的老马，把鼻子探进马槽，去啃里头的东西。他叼了一大口那自然而然当成干草的玩意儿，嚼了一阵，可没嚼多久。那又长又脏的褐色秸秆里透着一股霉味，跟他这辈子吃过的任何干草都对不上号。放进槽里给他吃的这号东西，跟出租马房老板往常撒在马栏里给马铺窝用的是一路货——是秸秆（straw），只不过是发霉的秸秆，连给马当铺草都不够格。



===Original===
Cloudy felt hungry long before the next morning came, and often thru the night he’d nosed into the musty straw with the hopes of finding a few stems that’d do to fill an empty space, but there wasn’t any to be found. The old rack of bones that’d been there before him had looked for some too, and with no better luck. Cloudy’s new owner figgered it cheaper to swap horses with the “chicken man” and give him a few dollars to boot whenever any horse of his give out; he wasn’t going to buy no high-priced hay for no horse. The straw was given to him for the getting and would keep any horse alive and working for at least six months, and then, or whenever the horse would be too weak to go any more, he’d trade him for another. Any kind of a horse, fat or thin, could always be used by the chicken man, and in trade, he’d always take one of the fattest to take the place of the one he’d just starved near to death. That way, year in year out, he’d keep a draining the last of the life of every horse he’d get his claws onto.
===Chinese===
第二天天亮还早得很，云儿就饿了，一夜里他几番把鼻子拱进发霉的秸秆，指望找出几根能填填空膛的，可一根也没找着。比他先来的那堆老骨头也这么找过，运气也没好到哪儿去。云儿的新主人算盘打得好：自个儿哪匹马垮了，就跟养鸡人换一匹、再添上几个美元，这么着最省；他才不打算给哪匹马买什么高价干草呢。秸秆是白捡来的，能让随便哪匹马活着干活至少六个月；到那时候，或者等那马弱得再也走不动了，他就再换一匹。不论肥马瘦马，养鸡人那儿总有用处；换的时候，他回回都挑最肥的一匹，来顶替那匹刚被他饿得半死的。就这么着，年复一年，凡落进他爪子里的马，最后一滴生命的汁水都得被他榨干。



===Original===
His property, and where he starved the horses into making a living for him, took in a couple of acres. Half of that land was rocks, mostly, and where he kept a few chickens. He bought, or stole a little grain for *them*; but they well repaid him. Every time he went to town there was a basket of eggs in his wagon and which he sold well. The other half of this land was cultivated, and where vegetables of all kinds had been made to grow. There’s where the help of a horse was needed, to pull the cultivator or the plow, then the hauling of the vegetables to town, and once there, any odd job that could be got and which would bring a few dollars for the use of the horse and wagon.
===Chinese===
他的这份家业，就是他饿着马替他挣饭吃的地方，拢共两英亩。一半多半是石头地，他就在那儿养了几只鸡。他给*这些鸡*买点儿粮食，或是偷点儿；可鸡给他的回报也不含糊。他每回上镇，车上都搁着一篮鸡蛋，卖得上好价钱。另外一半地开了出来，各色蔬菜都种得活。这正是用得着马的地方：拉中耕机，拉犁，再把蔬菜拉进镇去；到了镇上，凡能揽到的零活，只要靠这马和车能挣几个美元的，他都不放过。



===Original===
It was bright and early the next morning when the work begin for Cloudy. The man showed his teeth in a grin as he looked in the manger while putting the harness on the horse, and noticing the straw in there hadn’t hardly been touched, remarked:
===Chinese===
第二天一大早，云儿的活计就开了头。那人一边往马身上套挽具，一边朝马槽里瞅，瞧见槽里的秸秆几乎没动过，龇牙一笑，说道：



===Original===
“You’ll be eating some of that before you get thru.”
===Chinese===
「等你干完活，总要吃上几口那玩意儿的。」



===Original===
Cloudy was made acquainted with many different kinds of implements and work that day. All was mighty strange and plum against the ways of working which he’d been broke to do. It was pull, and pull, one contraption and then another, back and forth thru furrows, turn at the end and then back again. If he slowed down, or hesitated, wondering what to do, there was the whip always on hand to make him decide and mighty quick.
===Chinese===
那一天，云儿见识了各式各样的农具和活计。样样都透着陌生，跟他从前受调教学会的那套干活路数全然拧着。就是拉呀，拉呀，一样家什接着一样家什，顺着垄沟来回，到头拐个弯，再折回来。他要是慢下来，或是犹豫着不知怎么办才好，鞭子总在手边候着，教他拿定主意，而且快得很。



===Original===
His muscles, having developed under the saddle, used to pack weight, and set that way, wasn’t for getting next to the change very easy. Looking thru a collar and pulling steady was so different to heading off and turning a wild eyed critter. It wasn’t at all like coming out of the chute in front of a grandstand and seeing how many jumps could be put into one; nor didn’t compare even with packing equestrians around. He’d felt some free under the saddle, and even though all of it had been real work, there’d always been something that fitted in and which made him feel natural.
===Chinese===
他的筋骨是在鞍子底下长成的，惯的是驮重物，定的也是那个型，要转这个行当可不容易。从脖圈里望出去、匀着劲儿死拉，跟截住一头瞪圆了眼的牲口再把它拧转来，完全是两码事。这也全然不像从出场栏里冲到看台跟前、看一趟能尥出多少蹶子；就连驮着那些马术家兜圈子都比这个强——那时候鞍子底下的他还有几分自在，虽说干的桩桩都是实打实的活，却总有那么一点合拍的东西在，让他觉得自自然然。



===Original===
But now, with all these straps a hanging onto him, there was a feeling that he was tied down﻿—them straps even seemed to wrap around his heart at times and keep it from beating. And taking all, the strange hard work, the sting of the whiplash on his ribs, nothing fit to eat after he was tired out and the day was over, it was no wonder that the old pony’s heart begin to shrivel up on him.
===Chinese===
可如今，浑身上下挂着这一条条皮带，他只觉得自己被捆死了——这些皮带有时仿佛缠到了他的心上，勒得它跳不动。再想想这一切：生疏的苦活，鞭梢抽在肋上的火辣辣，累散了架、一天到头之后连口像样的吃食都没有——难怪老小马的心要一天天枯萎下去。



===Original===
As the long days run into weeks and the work in the field and in the town got to bearing down on him, the old pony even got so he couldn’t hate no more; abuse or kindness had both got to be the same, and one brought out no more results or show of interest than the other. He went to the jerk of the lines like without realizing; and when he was finally led into the stable when night come the feeling was the same. There he et the musty straw because it was under his nose. He didn’t mind the taste of it, he didn’t mind anything, any more.
===Chinese===
漫长的日子一天天拖成一个个星期，地里和镇上的活计一齐压到他身上，老小马甚至到了连恨都不会恨的地步；虐待也好，恩慈也好，在他这儿都成了一回事，谁也换不来他多一点儿反应、多一点儿兴致。缰绳一扯他就走，跟没了知觉似的；夜里被牵进厩里，滋味也一样。他把发霉的秸秆吃了，不为别的，就因为它在鼻子底下。他不在乎那味儿了，什么都不在乎了，再也不了。



---



			



===Original===
Of the odd jobs that Cloudy’s owner would get to do around town and whenever he could get away from his truck and chicken farm, there was one which he looked forward to the most, and which the thought of made him rub his hands together with pleasure. It was that of scattering the posters advertising The Annual Rodeo, and Celebration, that was pulled off in town and every early fall. But that wasn’t all. There was many other things for him to do at that time for which he could charge without anybody ever finding out whether all he’d been paid to do really had been done.
===Chinese===
云儿的主人每逢能撇下菜园和养鸡场，就会去镇上揽些零活；这些零活里头有一桩他最盼着，一想起就乐得直搓手——那就是到处张贴散发一年一度的牛仔竞技会暨庆典的海报，每年初秋镇上都要办上这么一回。可不止这一桩。那时候他还有好些事可做，钱照收不误，至于收了钱该办的到底办没办，谁也没法查明。



===Original===
That year as usual he was ready, and right on the dot, to take on some more of that kind of work. He’d hooked up the old mouse colored horse and taking a load of vegetables on the way in, stuck around town doing the different kinds of work the rodeo association had furnished him with. He’d be on the go all day and prodding the old horse into a trot, sometimes even if the wagon was loaded.
===Chinese===
那一年他也照旧早作准备，日子一到点儿，就又接下这路活计。他套上那匹鼠灰色老马，进镇时顺路捎上一车蔬菜，然后就泡在镇上，干竞技会协会派给他的种种杂活。他整天马不停蹄，赶着老马颠儿颠儿地小跑，有时车装得满满的也照样催。



===Original===
It’d be away into the night before he’d turn the tired horse towards home. Every day was a great day, *for the man*, there was so many people around to make the town lively; and being most of ’em was strangers, he could get to within talking distance of ’em easy enough, and a few would even stand to have him around for a few minutes at the time.
===Chinese===
总要到夜深了，他才会拨转那匹累坏的马往家走。每一天都是了不起的好日子——*对这汉子来说*——四下里人山人海，把镇子闹得热火朝天；而且这些人多半是外乡客，他轻易就能凑到搭得上话的距离，有几个甚至还能耐着性子容他在跟前待上几分钟。



===Original===
Them strangers had come to see the rodeo. Most of ’em was from other towns around, and mixed in the crowd once in a while could be seen the high-crowned hat of a cowboy who’d come to ride, rope, and bulldog. Then at the Casa Grande Hotel, and registered there, was many cattle buyers from the northern States.
===Chinese===
这些外乡客都是来看竞技会的。多数打周围的镇子来，人群里偶尔还夹杂着一顶高顶阔边帽——那是赶来骑野马、抡套索、扑牛（bulldog）的牛仔。而卡萨格兰德旅馆（Casa Grande Hotel）里，还住着许多从北方各州来的牛贩子，都在那儿登了记。



===Original===
They’d come to bid on the big herds of cattle that was being crowded acrost the border from Mexico; for Pancho Villa and the Yaquis was making it hard for the cattleman of that country. Villa took the cattle to feed his army, while the Yaquis run off whatever Villa overlooked; and the cowman that could, and had any stock left, soon seen where if he wanted to save anything of what he’d worked to accumulate, he’d have to rush whatever that was to the border and get it on American soil mighty quick.
===Chinese===
他们是来竞买那些正被赶着涌过墨西哥边境的大牛群的；因为潘乔·比利亚（Pancho Villa）和雅基人（Yaquis）把那边的养牛人逼得没了活路。比利亚牵牛去喂他的队伍，比利亚漏掉的，雅基人又给轰走；凡是还有办法、还剩下些牲口的牛倌，很快就看明白了：想保住自己苦攒下的那点东西，就得赶紧把它们往边境上赶，越快落到美国地界越好。



===Original===
That’s how come that the stockyards of the border towns was filled with cattle and that the hotels along them same towns was filled with cattle buyers. The Casa Grande Hotel was the most filled on account that along with the business of buying cattle, a little pleasure could be got there afterwards. A rodeo was in that town, and night celebrations; and being that them cattle buyers was still as much cowboys as ever, a good bucking contest and the fun afterwards couldn’t be overlooked, not if it could be helped. “Yep, the town was sure lively.”
===Chinese===
所以边境各镇的牲畜围场里挤满了牛，同一条街上的旅馆里也挤满了牛贩子。卡萨格兰德旅馆塞得最满，因为谈完了买牛的生意，过后还能在那儿找点乐子。镇上正开着竞技会，还有夜里的庆典；这些牛贩子骨子里还跟从前一样是牛仔，一场痛快的尥蹶子比赛加上赛后的热闹，只要躲得开，就断没有错过的道理。「可不是，这镇子真是热闹得很。」



===Original===
Two of the buyers was setting in the lobby of the hotel one morning and a talking on the first day’s event of the rodeo. A telegraph pole stuck up right before their vision and on the edge of the sidewalk, and nailed to that pole was a poster advertising the rodeo, and with a photograph of a bucking horse in action on it, told all about “the great bucking horse and outlaw The Grey Cougar, the only one that could compare, in wickedness and bucking ability, to The Cougar, that once famous man killing horse.”
===Chinese===
一天早晨，两个牛贩子正坐在旅馆大厅里，聊着竞技会头一天的比赛。他们眼前、人行道边上正好竖着一根电线杆，杆子上钉着一张竞技会的海报，海报上印着一匹正尥蹶子的马的照片，把「了不起的尥蹶子马兼劣马『灰美洲狮』」大大吹擂了一番——说它是唯一能在凶悍和尥蹶子本领上，同那匹当年名噪一时的杀人马「美洲狮」相提并论的马。



===Original===
The two went on to talking about the rodeo, and naturally the talk drifted on about The Grey Cougar, and “*how* he could buck.”
===Chinese===
两人接着聊竞技会，话题自然而然就拐到了灰美洲狮身上，聊*他多么*能尥蹶子。



===Original===
“The boys tell me,” says one of the men, “that this Grey Cougar horse couldn’t hold a candle to the real Cougar when it come to bucking and fighting. According to that, the other horse must of been *some* wicked.”
===Chinese===
「弟兄们跟我说，」其中一个道，「这匹灰美洲狮，论尥蹶子、论打架，给正牌的美洲狮提鞋都不配。照这么说，先前那匹马当年准是*相当*凶。」



===Original===
The man was still talking on the subject, when an old mouse colored house, pulling an old wagon loaded down with vegetables, came to a stiff legged stop, and right by the telegraph pole on which the poster telling all about The Grey Cougar was nailed. The man in the lobby grinned a little at the sight of the old horse a standing there like in comparison with the famous grey outlaw, and pointing a finger in his direction, he remarked:
===Chinese===
那人还在这个话头上说着，忽见一匹鼠灰色的老马拉着辆满载蔬菜的旧马车，迈着僵直的腿收住步子，正好停在钉着那张灰美洲狮海报的电线杆旁。大厅里那人瞧见老马站在那儿，活像在跟那匹出了名的灰毛劣马比高下，不由微微一笑，抬手指着他，说道：



===Original===
“There must be the Old Cougar right there, Clint. Anyway he’s got the same color.”
===Chinese===
「那儿站着的准是老美洲狮本尊吧，克林特。反正毛色是一样。」



===Original===
The man called Clint grinned some at the joke, but the grin soon faded away as he kept a looking at the old horse, and noticed the condition he was in. Then he seen the saddle marks that was all over the pony’s back, and he says:
===Chinese===
被称做克林特的那人听了这玩笑也咧了咧嘴，可他盯着那匹老马看下去、瞧清了那副光景之后，笑容很快就敛了。接着他望见小马背上纵横满布的鞍痕，便说道：



===Original===
“You can never tell, that old pony might of been mighty hard to set at one time too﻿—but the way he looks like now, them times are sure done past and gone.”
===Chinese===
「这可说不准，这匹老小马当年兴许也是匹极难骑服的马——可看他眼下这副模样，那样的日子准是早过去了，一去不回了。」



===Original===
“Yep,” agreed the other man, “it’s a miracle that pony can navigate at all﻿—I wonder how it is that this Humane Society hombre that’s sticking around the rodeo grounds don’t happen to notice such as this. I’d like to help hang a feller for driving a horse like that around.”
===Chinese===
「可不是，」另一位应道，「这小马还能走得动路就是个奇迹——真不知那位泡在竞技会场子里的人道协会（Humane Society）汉子，怎么就没瞅见这等事。要我说，赶着这样一匹马到处跑的家伙，吊死他我都愿意搭把手。」



===Original===
The conversation was held up for a spell as the two men watched the bewhiskered man come out of the hotel with an empty basket and climbed the wagon on which the old mouse colored horse was hooked. He grabbed the lines and the whip both at the same time and went to work a putting the horse into a trot.
===Chinese===
话头停了一阵，两人看着那大胡子汉子拎着空篮子出了旅馆，爬上套着鼠灰色老马的马车。他一把同时抓起缰绳和鞭子，动手把马赶进小跑里去。



===Original===
Clint was for getting up as he seen the whip land on the old pony’s hide, but the other man grabbed a hold of his arm and says:
===Chinese===
眼见鞭子落到老小马身上，克林特就要起身，却被另一位一把攥住胳膊，只听他说：



===Original===
“Never mind, old boy, most likely that Humane Society outfit’ll fall on the bolshevik’s neck before he gets very far.”
===Chinese===
「别急，老伙计，那位人道协会的人多半会赶在他跑出多远之前，就扑到这布尔什维克（bolshevik）的脖子上收拾他。」



===Original===
The man called Clint set down again, but he was boiling up inside, and he didn’t at all look pleasant as the conversation was resumed and noticed how his friend turned it to other things and away from the subject of old horses and such. He wasn’t for answering very quick when that same friend went on to talking about that country to the north;﻿—how he’d heard rumors that the Rocking R might be selling out in another year or so. “I wonder why?” he asks.
===Chinese===
被称做克林特的那人重新坐下了，心里却像开了锅，脸上也全不见好看；谈话续上之后，他瞧出朋友怎么把话头引到别处、从老马这类事上岔开了。等那位朋友又谈起北边那片地方——说起他听到风声，摇椅R没准儿一年半载就要整体出盘——他好一阵都没接茬儿。「这是为什么呢？」他朋友问。



===Original===
Clint, turned to his friend and grinning at his idea of changing the subject that way, finally answered: “I guess it’s because Old Tom feels the end a coming, besides he’s getting crowded all around by small outfits, and his range ain’t holding up like it used to.”
===Chinese===
克林特朝朋友转过脸，冲他这番转移话题的机灵劲儿笑了笑，末了才答：「我估摸着，是老汤姆觉得自个儿的大限快到了；再说，四下里全是小牧场挤对他，他那片草场也不比当年顶用了。」



===Original===
“But what are you going to do when the Rocking R sells out? You left that country quite a few times the last few years, and I notice you always go back like there was no other that suited you.”
===Chinese===
「可摇椅R一出盘，你打算怎么办？这几年你离开那片地方也不止一两回了，我瞧你回回都回去，好像天底下再没有第二处合你的意。」



===Original===
“I’ve got that fixed,” says Clint, gradually taking more heart in the new subject, and there he tried to describe some; “You know about where that camp is where I used to break horses when I first started working for the Rocking R? It’s where the outfit used to run their stock horses. Well, I bought that camp from Old Tom Jarvis﻿—that is, I talked him into selling it to me, and four thousand acres of the fine range around to go with it.
===Chinese===
「这个我早安排妥了，」克林特说，渐渐在新话头上来了兴致，便细说起来，「你晓得我头一回给摇椅R干活那阵子，常在那儿驯马的那个营地在哪儿吧？牧场那些工作坐骑从前就撒在那儿。我把那营地从老汤姆·贾维斯手里买下来了——这么说吧，是我劝得他肯卖给我，连同周围四千英亩的上好草场一块儿。」



===Original===
“I’m thinking that this shipment I’m getting together now will be the last Old Tom’ll ever buy, and by the time I get this trainload of Sonora Reds north and delivered to him, I’ll have enough money to make the final payment on my place and still have enough left to buy a few head of cattle and start stocking it.”
===Chinese===
「我琢磨着，我这回张罗的这批牛，该是老汤姆这辈子买的最后一批了；等我把这一整车的索诺拉红牛（Sonora Reds）赶到北边交到他手上，手里的钱就够付清我那块地的尾款，还能剩下几个，买上几头牛，开始往那块地里攒牲口。」



===Original===
Clint often thought of his little place up in the heart of the cow country to the north. He could picture his own cattle ranging there and packing a brand of his own on their slick hides. He’d a long time hoped for the likes, and at last he was getting it. A couple more days now, and he’d be heading north again, and there to stay, this time.
===Chinese===
克林特常常想起北边牧牛地深处他那一小块地方。他能想象得出自家的牛群在那儿撒开吃草，光板的牛皮上烙着自家的印记。这样的日子他盼了许久，如今终于到手了。再过两天，他就又要动身北上了——这一回，去了就不走了。



---



			



===Original===
The last day of the rodeo had come, and Clint was to start with his trainload of stock that night. Him and his friend was setting in the lobby of the hotel that evening a talking and wondering when they’d be seeing one another again, when outside and by the telegraph pole, came the same old mouse colored horse and stopped not an inch from where the two men had seen him a couple of days before.
===Chinese===
竞技会的最后一天到了，克林特当晚就要押着他那列牲口动身。那天傍晚，他和朋友正坐在旅馆大厅里聊着，盘算这一别不知哪天才能再见，忽然门外、就在电线杆边上，来了那匹鼠灰色的老马，收住脚的地方，跟两天前两人瞧见他的地方分毫不差。



===Original===
Both was quick to spot him again this time, and right then, for some reason or other, the conversation died down. The first sight of that old pony hadn’t been forgot, and when he showed up this second time, right before their eyes, he was like reminding ’em, and natural like, set the two men to thinking. That old shadow of a horse told some of the hard knocks of life, of things that was past and gone and which could of been bettered while the bettering could be done.
===Chinese===
这回两人一眼就又认出了他，也不知怎么的，话头当场就低了下去。初见那匹老小马的情景本就没忘，如今他又第二次露面，就杵在他们眼皮底下，像是在提醒他们什么，两人不由得寻思起来。这匹影子似的老马，道尽了生活里的几记重锤，道尽了那些逝去不再的事——那些本可以趁来得及的时候补救的事。



===Original===
It was while the thinking was going on that way, that Clint sort of felt a faint, far away something a knocking and from down the bottom of his think tank. That something was trying hard to come back to life as that man’s eyes kept a going over the pony’s blazed face and bony frame, but it was buried so far underneath so many things that’d been stacked there that the knocking was pretty well muffled up. It’d have to be helped by some sort of a sudden jolt before it could come out on top.
===Chinese===
正这么想着的当口，克林特隐隐觉出有个微弱的、远远的什么东西，正从他思想罐的罐底咚咚敲着。那东西拼命想还过魂来——那人的眼睛一遍遍扫过小马的白章脸和嶙峋的骨架——可它埋在后来堆上去的太多东西底下，敲声给捂得严严实实。非得再猛挨一下子，它才能翻上顶来。



===Original===
The jolt came as the vegetable man got his seat on the wagon and as usual reached for the whip. Clint’s friend a trying to keep him from running out and starting a rompus had tried to draw his interest by asking:
===Chinese===
这一下来的时候，正是卖菜汉在车上坐定、照例伸手去够鞭子的当口。克林特的朋友怕他冲出去闹出一场好戏，正想拿话岔开他的心思，问道：



===Original===
“What’s become of that cowhorse *Smoky*, that used to﻿—?”
===Chinese===
「那匹牧牛马*烟儿*后来怎么样了，就是从前那匹——？」



===Original===
But the question was left for *him* to wonder about, for Clint wasn’t there to answer. Instead the hotel door slammed and only a glimpse of that same cowboy could be seen as he passed by the lobby window. In less than it takes to tell it, he was up on the wagon, took a bulldogging holt of the surprised vegetable man, and by his whiskers, drug him off his seat and down to earth.
===Chinese===
可这问题只留给他*自己*纳闷儿了，克林特早不在那儿答话。旅馆门砰的一声，只剩那个牛仔掠过大厅窗前的一晃影子。说时迟那时快，他已蹿上马车，一个扑牛把式的死抱搂住那吃了一惊的卖菜汉，揪着胡子，把人从座位上拽下来，拖到了地上。



===Original===
The telephone on the desk of the sheriff’s office rang till it near danced a jig, and when that feller lifted the receiver, a female voice was heard to holler: “Somebody is killing somebody else with a whip, by the Casa Grande Hotel. *Hurry! Quick!*”
===Chinese===
治安官（sheriff）办公室桌上的电话响得差不多要自己跳起舞来，那家伙抓起听筒，只听一个女人的嗓门喊道：「有人正在卡萨格兰德旅馆旁边拿鞭子杀人呢！*快！赶紧！*」



===Original===
The sheriff appeared on the scene and took in the goings on at a glance. Like a man who knowed his business, his eyes went to looking for what might of caused the argument as he came. He looked at the old horse whose frame showed thru the hide, then the whip marks on that hide. He knowed horses as well as he did men; and when he noticed more marks of the same whip on the bewhiskered man’s face, he stood his ground, watched, and then grinned.
===Chinese===
治安官到了现场，一眼就把事情的经过看了个大概。像个懂行的，他一路走来，眼睛先去找这场争端的起因。他看了看那匹骨头架子透皮而出的老马，又看了看那张皮上的鞭痕。他识马的本事不输识人；待他又瞧见大胡子汉子脸上更多同一根鞭子抽出的印子，他站定脚跟，看了一阵，咧嘴笑了。



===Original===
“Say, cowboy,” he finally says, “don’t scatter that hombre’s remains too much; you know we got to keep record of that kind the same as if it was a white man, and I don’t want to be looking all over the streets to find out who he *was*.”
===Chinese===
「我说，牛仔，」他末了开口道，「别把那汉子的零碎儿打得四处都是；你知道的，这号人咱们也得像对白人一样留档，我可不想满大街去翻找他*原先*是谁。」



===Original===
Clint turned at the sound of the voice, and sizing up the grinning sheriff, went back to his victim and broke the butt end of the whip over his head; after which he wiped his hands, and proceeded to unhook the old horse off the wagon.
===Chinese===
克林特听见声音回过头，把这位咧着嘴笑的治安官掂量了一下，便回到他那位苦主身上，把鞭子的根把在那人脑袋上折成了两截；随后他抹了抹手，动手把老马从车上卸了下来。



===Original===
That evening was spent in “investigating.” Clint and the sheriff went to the chicken horse man and found out enough from him about the vegetable man and his way of treating horses to put that hombre in a cool place and keep him there for a spell.
===Chinese===
那天晚上都花在「调查」上了。克林特和治安官去找了那位养鸡人，从他嘴里问出足够多关于卖菜汉和他那套待马之道的材料，够把那汉子关进个凉快地方、搁上一阵子了。



===Original===
“I’m glad to’ve caught on to that feller’s doings,” remarks the sheriff as him and Clint went to the livery stable, their next place of investigation.
===Chinese===
「能逮住这家伙的这些勾当，我真高兴，」治安官说；他和克林特正往出租马房去，那是下一处要查的地方。



===Original===
There Clint listened mighty close as he learned a heap about the mouse colored horse when he was known as Cloudy. The stable man went on to tell as far as he knowed about the horse and the whole history of him, and when that pony was known thru the Southwest and many other places, as *The Cougar*, the wickedest bucking horse and fighting outlaw the country had ever layed eyes on.
===Chinese===
在那儿，克林特听得分外仔细，打听到了这匹鼠灰色的马顶着云儿名字那阵子的一大堆事。马房老板把自个儿晓得的都倒了出来，讲这匹马的来龙去脉，讲他还曾以*美洲狮*之名——举国见识过的最凶悍的尥蹶子马、最善斗的劣马——名震西南各地和别处的许多地方。



===Original===
Clint was kinda proud in hearing that. He’d heard of The Cougar and that pony’s bucking ability even up to the Canadian line and acrost it, and to himself he says: “That Smoky horse never did do things halfways.” But he got to wondering, and then asked how come the pony had turned out to be the kind of a horse. That, the stable man didn’t know. It was news to him that the horse had ever been anything else, and as he says:
===Chinese===
克林特听着，心里颇有几分得意。「美洲狮」和这小马的尥蹶子本事，连加拿大边境那儿、连边境以北他都有耳闻。他心里念叨：「烟儿这马，干事从来不留一半。」可他又犯起了嘀咕，接着便问，这马当初是怎么落得成了这么一路马的。这个，马房老板就不知道了。这马从前居然还当过别的什么，对他是桩新闻。只听他说道：



===Original===
“The first that was seen of that horse is when some cowboys found him on the desert, amongst a bunch of wild horses, and packing a saddle. Nobody had ever showed up to claim him, and as that pony had been more than inclined to buck and fight is how come he was sold as a bucking horse﻿—and believe me, old timer,” went on the stable man, a shaking his head, “he was *some* bucking horse.”
===Chinese===
「这马头一回露面，是几个牛仔在沙漠上发现的，混在一群里野马里，背上还驮着副鞍子。从没人出面认领过他；这小马又一心只想尥蹶子、想干架，所以才当尥蹶子马卖了——信我一句，老前辈，」马房老板摇着头接着说，「他可是匹*了不得*的尥蹶子马。」



===Original===
“Well,” says the sheriff, “that’s another clue run to the ground with nothing left of, but the remains.”
===Chinese===
「唉，」治安官说，「又是一条线索追到了底，除了残骸，什么也没剩下。」



===Original===
That night, the big engine was hooked on to the trainload of cattle as to per schedule and started puffing its way on to the north. In the last car, the one next to the caboose, and the least crowded, a space had been partitioned off. In that space was a bale of good hay, a barrel of water, and an old mouse colored horse.
===Chinese===
当天夜里，大车头按点挂上了那一整列牛车，喷着汽往北去了。最后一节车皮——紧挨着守车（caboose）、也最不挤的那节——里头，隔出了一块地方。那块地方里，有一捆上好干草，一桶水，还有一匹鼠灰色的老马。



---



			



===Original===
The winter that came was very different to any the old mouse colored horse had ever put in. The first part of it went by with him like in a trance, not realizing and hardly seeing. His old heart had dwindled down till only a sputtering flame was left, and that threatened to go out with the first hint of any kind of breeze.
===Chinese===
随后来临的这个冬天，跟这匹鼠灰色老马以往熬过的任何一个冬天都不一样。头一段日子，他像是在迷迷糊糊的怔忡里挨过去的，人事不省，几乎什么也看不见。他那颗老心脏已经缩得只剩一豆噼啪欲灭的火苗，但凡有一点儿风吹草动，都有把它吹熄的危险。



===Original===
Clint had got the old horse in a warm box stall, filled the manger full of the best blue joint hay there was, and even bedded him down with more of the same; water was in that same stall and where it could be easy reached, and then that cowboy had bought many a dollar’s worth of condition powders, and other preparations which would near coax life back even in a dead body.
===Chinese===
克林特把老马安置进一间暖和的单马厩（box stall）里，马槽里堆满了顶好的蓝茎草，连铺窝用的都是一样的草；水也搁在那间厩里，一低头就够得着。那牛仔还花了不少美元，买回养膘药粉（condition powders）和种种别的补剂——那些东西，差不多连死尸身上都能把性命哄回来。



===Original===
Two months went by when all seemed kinda hopeless, but Clint worked on and kept a hoping. He’d brought the old horse in the house, and made him a bed by the stove if that would of helped; and far as that goes, he’d of done anything else, just so a spark of life showed in the old pony’s eyes; but he’d done all he could do, and as he’d lay a hand on the old skinny neck and felt of the old hide, he’d cuss and wish for the chance of twisting out of shape who all had been responsible. Then his expression would change, and he’d near bust out crying as he’d think back and compare the old wreck with what that horse had been.
===Chinese===
